### [1] [Validity Domains for Semiclassical Time in Quantum Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37696)
*Gianpaolo Rosati,Sergio L. Cacciatori*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了半经典引力WKB流作为下属量子自由度的时间参考的有效性域，指出Born-Oppenheimer绝热条件应直接在引力组态空间中基于Hamilton-Jacobi矢量场构建，从而将绝热控制与WKB有效性及参考系的量子涨落锐度区分开；在FRW minisuperspace模型中，WKB有效域与绝热域不等价，表明仅存在振荡型半经典分支不足以保证可靠的WKB时间描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上将WKB时间作为先验设定，但其对下属量子系统的时间参考是否可靠缺乏定量判据；需厘清WKB近似有效性、绝热分离条件及参考系量子锐度三者的独立作用与耦合关系。

Method: 在引力组态空间中基于Hamilton-Jacobi矢量场重构Born-Oppenheimer绝热条件，定义三个独立有效性域（WKB域、绝热域、锐度域），并引入退相干条件处理共存相干分支；以闭合FRW minisuperspace耦合均匀标量场为具体模型，对比普通WKB参数与构型空间绝热参数在转向点附近的行为，并采用Airy一致渐近方法消除WKB发散。

Result: 在FRW模型中，靠近引力转向点时，标准WKB控制参数发散，而构型空间绝热参数趋于零；Airy处理虽消除发散，但二者仍不等价——局部WKB参数为O(1)，绝热参数为O(M^{-1/3})；因此WKB有效性域与绝热域严格分离。

Conclusion: 半经典引力WKB流能否提供可靠的时间参考，取决于WKB有效性、绝热性与参考锐度三者交集的有效域D_t；仅存在振荡型半经典分支并不足以保障WKB时间描述的可靠性。

Abstract: We investigate the domain in which a semiclassical gravitational WKB flow can serve as a quantitatively controlled temporal reference for subordinate quantum degrees of freedom. Rather than introducing WKB time at the outset, we formulate the Born--Oppenheimer adiabatic condition directly on gravitational configuration space using the Hamilton--Jacobi vector field. This separates adiabatic control from the local WKB validity of the gravitational branch and from the sharpness of the candidate reference against quantum fluctuations. For a single selected semiclassical branch, these requirements define the validity domain $D_t = D_{\mathrm{WKB}} \cap D_{\mathrm{ad}} \cap D_{\mathrm{sharp}}$, with an additional decoherence condition when coherent branches coexist.In a closed Friedmann--Robertson--Walker minisuperspace coupled to a homogeneous massive scalar mode, the distinction is explicit: approaching the gravitational turning point from the oscillatory region, the ordinary WKB control parameter diverges while the configuration-space adiabatic parameter tends to zero. A uniform Airy treatment of the turning point removes the formal WKB divergence while preserving the inequivalence: the corresponding local control parameter remains of order unity, whereas the configuration-space adiabatic parameter is parametrically suppressed as $O(M^{-1/3})$.Thus Born--Oppenheimer adiabaticity and gravitational WKB validity define inequivalent domains even within the simplest minisuperspace model. We conclude that the existence of an oscillatory semiclassical branch is not sufficient for a reliable WKB temporal description: semiclassical temporal reliability is instead determined by the overlap of the validity domains supporting the reference flow.

</details>


### [2] [Classical traversability of a carbon BTZ wormhole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37704)
*Valerie Tynianska,Alfredo Iorio*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在二维弯曲碳膜上实现的类空间虫洞几何的经典可穿越性，通过反射对称加倍技术构建了无事件视界的平滑可穿越喉部，并发现质量为零的狄拉克准粒子沿类光测地线运动，其穿越行为由角动量阈值决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在凝聚态系统中模拟广义相对论中虫洞等奇异时空结构的可能性，特别是验证其经典可穿越性。

Method: 采用反射对称加倍技术构造类BTZ黑洞外部空间的类虫洞背景几何；将质量为零的狄拉克准粒子视为受度规曲率支配的自旋类光射线；精确求解测地线方程分析轨迹行为。

Result: 发现存在一个由最小喉部半径决定的角动量临界阈值：低于该阈值的轨迹被反射，高于则可穿越；近临界轨迹呈现角坐标对数发散，导致准粒子在喉部多次环绕后逃逸。

Conclusion: 该类比虫洞在经典层面上具有明确的可穿越性判据，其测地线结构为后续介观尺度（如量子输运）研究提供了几何基础。

Abstract: We investigate the classical traversability of an analogue spatial wormhole geometry realized on a two-dimensional curved carbon membrane. The background is obtained by implementing a reflection-symmetric doubling technique on the exterior spatial sector of a non-rotating Bañados-Teitelboim-Zanelli black hole, effectively replacing the event horizon with a smooth traversable throat. Within this framework, massless Dirac quasiparticles propagate as spinorial light rays whose kinematics are governed exclusively by the metric curvature, forcing them to track the null geodesics of the ultrastatic spacetime. By evaluating the exact geodesic equations, we demonstrate that classical traversability is dictated by a sharp binary threshold in the conserved angular momentum, where the minimum throat radius acts as a centrifugal potential barrier that separates traversing paths from reflecting ones. We show that near-critical trajectories experience a logarithmic divergence in their angular evolution, causing the quasiparticles to execute multiple spatial windings around the throat before escaping. These classical ray-tracing configurations establish the necessary geometric backbones for future mesoscopic investigations.

</details>

Abstract: We determine thermodynamic and structural properties of the Coulomb plasma (jellium) in the thin atmosphere of a white dwarf using the path integral Monte Carlo method within a general relativity description of the star.

</details>


### [4] [Draining Without Emptying a Stringy Black Hole: Repetitive Electric Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37713)
*Huai-Fan Li,Xiong-Ying Guo,Ke Wang,Hong Lei*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了GMGHS黑洞在最优条件下重复电Penrose过程的能量提取，发现其有效势曲线在迭代中保持不变；能量回报率可超100%，但能量利用效率不超50%，大部分能量转化为黑洞不可约质量的增加；黑洞性质和粒子参数显著影响提取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索GMGHS黑洞中重复电Penrose过程的最大能量提取潜力及其与RN黑洞的本质差异，理解电荷耗尽极限与能量转化机制。

Method: 理论分析结合数值模拟，分析GMGHS黑洞中粒子运动的有效势、迭代终止条件（逃逸条件与负能粒子存在条件），并系统考察不同参数（如粒子衰变半径、荷质比、比能量及黑洞初值电荷）对能量回报率和利用效率的影响。

Result: GMGHS黑洞中有效势曲线在迭代中不变；迭代无法将电荷耗尽至零；能量回报率可>100%，但能量利用效率≤50%；多数提取能量转化为黑洞不可约质量增量；参数敏感性规律明确；终态黑洞仍带非零质量和电荷并保留可提取能量。

Conclusion: GMGHS黑洞支持高效但受限的能量提取，其物理机制与RN黑洞有本质区别；电荷是维持重复过程的关键约束，能量提取本质上以增大黑洞不可约质量为代价，无法实现净能量无限增益。

Abstract: This paper studies the repetitive electric Penrose process in the Gibbons-Maeda-Garfinkle-Horowitz-Strominger (GMGHS) black hole under the optimal condition for maximum energy extraction. Unlike the Reissner-Nordström (RN) black hole, the effective potential curve of the incident particle in the GMGHS black hole remains unchanged during the iterations. The iteration termination conditions are determined by two aspects: the escape condition requires that particle 0 can overcome the effective potential peak and escape to infinity, and this condition is independent of the black hole charge; the existence condition for negative-energy particle 1 requires the black hole charge to be sufficiently large, indicating that the repetitive process cannot completely consume the charge of the GMGHS black hole to zero. Numerical simulations show that the return on investment of energy extraction can exceed $100\%$, but the energy utilization efficiency does not exceed $50\%$, and most of the extractable energy is converted into an increase in the irreducible mass of the black hole. In addition, a smaller decay radius and a larger absolute value of the charge-to-mass ratio of particle 1 can improve the return on investment and energy utilization efficiency of energy extraction; the larger the specific energy of the incident particle, the smaller the return on investment of energy extraction, while the energy utilization efficiency remains unchanged; for an initially non-extremal black hole, as the initial charge increases, the return on investment of energy extraction increases monotonically, while the energy utilization efficiency first increases and then decreases. After the iterations end, the black hole still retains extractable energy, and neither the charge nor the mass vanishes.

</details>

*Vsevolod Chistiakov,Artur Mingaleev*

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个包含∇μR∇μR和(∇²R)²项的二次引力模型，在特定耦合常数关系下，作用量可写为完全平方形式；通过时间重参数化不变的动力学变量约化，并经非线性变换将约化作用量转化为对应Wiener测度的高斯形式；离散化分析表明仅在7+1维时变Jacobin与动力学变量无关，据此提出用Wiener测度积分形式表示约化模型中观测量期望值的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为构造具有良好数学性质（如可定义路径积分）的量子引力有效模型，探索高阶曲率修正下引力理论的可积结构与随机过程表征。

Method: 对含∇μR∇μR和(∇²R)²项的二次引力模型，在FLRW背景下进行时间重参数化不变的变量约化；引入非线性变量变换使约化作用量变为高斯型；采用时间离散化方法分析变换Jacobin，并确定其与动力学变量无关的维度条件。

Result: 发现仅在7+1维时空下，所构造的离散变量变换的Jacobin不依赖于动力学变量；由此建立约化模型中观测量期望值关于Wiener测度的形式积分表达。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了特定高维二次引力模型与随机过程（Wiener测度）之间的深层联系，为量子引力的路径积分表述提供了新的几何与概率框架。

Abstract: We consider a quadratic gravity model supplemented by the terms $\nabla_μR\nabla^μR$ and $(\nabla^{2}R)^2$. For specific relations among the coupling constants, the action takes a perfect-square form. In the spatially flat FLRW model, we perform a reduction to a dynamical variable invariant under time reparameterizations. We construct a nonlinear change of variable that transforms the reduced action into the Gaussian action associated with the Wiener measure. An analysis of the transformation Jacobian within a time discretization singles out the case of $7+1$-dimensional spacetime, in which the Jacobian of the discrete transformation under consideration is independent of the dynamical variable. On this basis, we propose a formal representation of expectation values of observables in the reduced model as integrals with respect to the Wiener measure.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究圈量子宇宙学不同模型（标准LQC、mLQC-I、mLQC-II）中张量扰动穿越量子反弹的行为及其对原初引力波谱和CMB B模观测的可检验预言，尤其关注初始态依赖性与正则化方案的影响。

Method: 在hybrid和dressed metric两种量子引力背景框架下，结合Starobinsky暴胀势，数值求解张量扰动方程穿越量子反弹；mLQC-I中针对其出现的收缩de Sitter分支，分别采用de Sitter模式函数（dressed metric）和Hankel函数解（hybrid）作为初始态；定义尺度依赖的张量-标量比r(k)=P_T/P_S以分离初始态相关/无关特征，并将功率谱传播至CMB观测窗口。

Result: 对于N_e=60和65，CMB参考尺度上的张量比r_⋆与标准Starobinsky暴胀一致；r(k)在尺度不变区缓慢变化，在较小k处围绕r_⋆振荡；靠近尺度不变区的振荡与绝热阶无关，而更低k处的峰/尖刺则有关；红外区域仍保留正则化依赖，反映长波模式对反弹及前反弹相的记忆；B模功率谱具备潜在可观测性。

Conclusion: mLQC模型在保持与标准暴胀一致的主流观测参数（如r_⋆）的同时，通过r(k)的精细结构（特别是红外振荡与正则化残留）提供了区分不同量子宇宙学模型及初始条件的潜在新探针，增强了圈量子引力理论的可证伪性。

Abstract: We compute the primordial tensor power spectrum in standard loop quantum cosmology and in the modified models mLQC-I and mLQC-II, evolving the tensor perturbations across the bounce in the hybrid and dressed metric prescriptions with a Starobinsky potential. In mLQC-I, the emergent de Sitter contracting branch requires the use of dedicated initial states. We use de Sitter mode function in the dressed metric approach and a Hankel-function solution in the hybrid approach. For inflationary e-foldings $N_e = 60$ and $65$, the pivot-scale tensor-to-scalar ratio agrees with the value of standard inflation with this potential. The scale-dependent ratio $r(k)=\mathcal{P}_T/\mathcal{P}_S$ cancels effects common to the two sectors and separates features that are independent of the initial state from those that depend on it. It varies slowly in the scale-invariant regime and oscillates around $r_\star$ at smaller $k$. The oscillations next to the scale-invariant regime do not depend on the adiabatic order, unlike the peaks and spikes at lower $k$. For a given initial state, the regularization dependence survives in the infrared, where long-wavelength modes retain a record of the bounce and of the pre-bounce branch. We propagate the normalized spectra to the CMB $B$-mode spectrum and discuss observational prospects.

</details>

Abstract: We develop an inverse-design approach to the effective field theory (EFT) of perturbations in scalar-tensor gravity around static and spherically symmetric backgrounds with a timelike scalar-field gradient, taking the odd-parity effective potential as input and reconstructing compatible background and EFT data. As analytically tractable examples, we construct explicit realizations of the Pöschl-Teller potential, for which the quasinormal-mode spectrum can be obtained in closed form. We then study the Regge-Wheeler potential of general relativity and its parametrized deformations, showing how both can be realized within the scalar-tensor EFT. In particular, the latter realizations provide a route to interpreting phenomenological constraints on potential deformations from black hole ringdown observations in terms of the underlying EFT data.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索和检验量子引力（QG）效应，利用四极矩BEC作为探测GUP诱导偏离标准量子力学的实验平台。

Method: 采用二次型GUP（QGUP）和线性-二次型GUP（LQGUP）模型，结合修正的态密度，计算四极矩BEC在有限温度下的凝聚分数、临界温度、物态方程等热力学量的修正。

Result: QQI与GUP的联合作用显著改变BEC的有限温热力学性质；LQGUP通常比QGUP带来更大的修正，但相对大小依赖于参数选择和微扰展开的有效性；基于假定温度分辨率，给出了GUP变形参数的投影上限。

Abstract: We study the thermodynamics of quadrupolar Bose-Einstein condensates in the realm of the generalized uncertainty principle (GUP) for the quest of understanding and testing quantum gravity (QG). Using both quadratic GUP (QGUP) and linear-quadratic GUP (LQGUP) which incorporate modified density of states, we calculate corrections to the condensed fraction, critical temperature, equation of state and other thermodynamic quantities. We find that the intriguing interplay of quadrupole-quadrupole interactions (QQIs) and the GUP may strongly modify the finite-temperature properties of the condensate, with the LQGUP generally predicting larger corrections than the QGUP, although their relative magnitudes depend on the chosen parameters and the validity of the perturbative expansion. We further estimate the sensitivity of the critical temperature to GUP-induced effects by imposing an assumed fractional temperature resolution which provides projected upper bounds on the GUP deformation parameters. Our findings demonstrate that quadrupole-quadrupole interacting Bose gases provide a promising platform for testing GUP-induced deviations from standard quantum mechanics and for probing QG phenomenology through measurements of finite-temperature many-body observables. They also motivate future studies using realistic system-specific parameters.

</details>

Abstract: Five years ago Pook-Kolb, Hennigar and Booth published a series of three papers exploring the evolution of apparent horizons and other marginally outer trapped surfaces during a black hole merger. This was seen to be much more intricate than the well-known "pair-of-pants" merger associated with event horizons. In this paper we show that parameter evolutions of Brill-Lindquist-deSitter initial data mimic many of the features of that evolution. Hence, this analytic intial data is a useful testing ground for ideas about marginally outer trapped surface evolution and spacetime geometry during mergers.

</details>

Abstract: The present study investigates the feasibility of gravitational baryogenesis in a Gauss-Bonnet (GB) modified Randall-Sundrum (RS) type II braneworld cosmology. The modified expansion rate of the Universe in this scenario evolves through three distinct expansion regimes, the GB regime at early times where the expansion rate $H \sim ρ^{1/3}$ falls more slowly than the standard expansion law $H\sim ρ^{1/2}$ of GR, then the RS type era with an enhanced expansion $H \sim ρ$, before finally reducing to the standard expansion law in the low energy limit. However, $\dot{R}>0$ for the RS regime and successful baryogenesis requires either that the decoupling temperature occurs during the GB regime or that the Universe makes a direct transition from the GB regime to the GR regime.

Studies of inflation in GB-braneworld cosmology place constraints on the allowed ranges of the parameters $μ$ and $β$. Guided by these, the asymmetry has been calculated for ranges of $(μ, β, w)$ and it is found that the observed values can be obtained for a value of $β$ within the range $(10^{-2} - 10^{-3})$ for $μ=10^{12}$ GeV, and within the range $(10^{-1}- 10^{-2})$ for $μ=10^{13}$ GeV. All values of $η$ for $μ=10^{14}$ are too high. In contrast to GR where $η$ is zero for a radiation dominated Universe ($w=1/3$), it is non-zero in GB braneworld cosmology.

However, our methods could be straightforwardly extended to more complicated situations. Integrating out the internal quadrupole induces a series of time-dependent finite-size operators with specific numerical coefficients, which we calculate. We find that the static electric quadrupole Love number $k_2$ is negative, and that all finite-size terms are proportional to $k_2$. We further show that resonant excitation induces a leading 2.5 Post-Newtonian term, compared with the 5 Post-Newtonian term in the time-independent case. Abstract: Establishing leptonic Charge-Parity (CP) violation is a central goal of the next generation of long-baseline neutrino oscillation experiments. Abstract: In arXiv:2211.13753 we proposed a novel theoretical approach for non-perturbative QCD, named the inverse problem approach. It extracts non-perturbative quantities by solving a dispersion integral equation as an inverse problem, using perturbative QCD and operator product expansion as inputs. In this work, we apply this approach to the pion and $a_1(1260)$ decay constants, as well as the continuum spectral function. The extracted $f_π= 130.8 \pm 8.1$ MeV agrees with the experimental value, and the reconstructed spectral function reproduces the ALEPH $τ$-decay data from threshold up to $s \simeq 3$ GeV$^2$. The advantages of this approach are as follows: (i) the whole non-perturbative range is solved simultaneously, yielding the ground state, excited states, and the continuum spectrum within a single framework; (ii) it is based on rigorous mathematics, with not only statistical but also systematic uncertainties well controlled and quantifiable; (iii) the regularized solutions exhibit convergence, allowing the precision to be systematically improved with better inputs; (iv) quark-hadron duality emerges naturally at high energy scales, avoiding the low-energy duality assumption and the Borel transformation, and eliminating their associated uncertainties. Finally, the successful physical application of the inverse problem approach demonstrates its broad applicability to non-perturbative quantities.

Abstract: Baryon-number-violating decays with missing energy probe new interactions connecting Standard-Model quarks to light invisible fermions. The calculation includes leading-order two-particle contributions up to twist four and factorisable four-particle terms. Method: (3+1)维粘性流体力学模型，包含QGP体演化中的电磁源项及Cooper-Frye强子化过程的电磁修正；通过调节有效电导率来刻画不同能区的电磁响应。

Abstract: Experimentally observed charge splitting of directed flow, $Δv_1\equiv v_1(h^+)-v_1(h^-)$, exhibits both a sign reversal at midrapidity and the amplitude change from Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=200$ GeV to Pb+Pb at $\sqrt{s_{\rm NN}}=5.02$ TeV. We study this behavior within a unified $(3+1)$-dimensional viscous hydrodynamic model that incorporates an electromagnetic source term in the bulk evolution of the quark-gluon plasma (QGP) and an electromagnetic correction to the Cooper--Frye particlization. The model further predicts the mass-dependent charge splittings for identified pions, kaons, and protons, providing further constraints on their respective relaxation times and the corresponding electromagnetic interaction. Abstract: We calculate the one-loop amplitude for distinct-flavor massless quark scattering in a local BRST-invariant refined Gribov-Zwanziger theory with minimally coupled quarks. The constraint determinant and two dynamical quark flavors are included in the action and in the loop sum. Retaining the refined Gribov-Zwanziger masses in the free field matrix, we combine the mixed self-energy insertion, complete vertex and box corrections, and external-leg residues. The difference between a general linear covariant gauge and a reference gauge vanishes symbolically after removal of the Stueckelberg regulator in the specified dimensional prescription. Abstract: Motivated by near-threshold $J/ψ$ photoproduction measurements, we investigate the gluon contributions to the gravitational form factors (GFFs) of the proton associated with the energy-momentum tensor in quantum chromodynamics. Abstract: We study a minimal $Z_4$-symmetric extension of the Standard Model in which a singlet Dirac fermion $χ$ constitutes dark matter, whose annihilations generate a lepton asymmetry through WIMP leptogenesis. Kinetic equilibrium is maintained mainly by the inelastic conversion $χL\leftrightarrow\barχΨ$. Compared with the near elastic regime, a sizable mass gap suppresses the conversion rate more rapidly as the Universe cools. This naturally drives kinetic decoupling to earlier times, even away from resonance. Starting from the full inelastic collision operator, we derive an effective momentum transfer rate for kinetic decoupling. We then solve the coupled Boltzmann equations for the dark matter abundance, its temperature $T_χ$, and the $B-L$ asymmetry. Early kinetic decoupling can significantly modify the relic abundance, although near resonance this modification can be reduced by annihilation-induced heating. The $B-L$ asymmetry can be affected when asymmetry production overlaps with a substantial departure from kinetic equilibrium. In our scan, the largest deviations occur mainly for $x_{\rm lep}\gtrsim x_{\rm cd}$. After imposing our constraints, asymmetry production typically ends before chemical decoupling, leaving the final asymmetry essentially unchanged. We derive the conversion amplitude from the second-order effective four-Fermi interaction and express it in terms of nuclear responses in the one-body impulse approximation. Using phenomenological nucleon form factors, we retain convection, axial charge, weak magnetism, and the longitudinal pion-pole current in a specified pion-pole-improved prescription. The two physical time orderings and the distinct momentum transfers are derived in the intermediate-state response. We complete its angular reduction and derive the corresponding closure operator, including spin-gradient terms. Both the symmetric (scalar) and antisymmetric (vector) lepton-hadron Lorentz contractions contribute to the spin-averaged, angle-integrated rate. For $0^+\!\to0^+$ transitions we give explicit formulas in terms of two coherent radial amplitudes, without restricting the intermediate nuclei to spin zero. This provides an interface to nuclear calculations of the required nuclear matrix elements. However, in this work, no isotope-specific numerical predictions are attempted. The present treatment uses approximate lepton wavefunctions and recoil-free nuclear propagation. Two-body weak currents and systematic chiral effective-field-theory matching are left for future work.

Method: 基于退化惰性与类惰性希格斯真空的解析界面模型，引入可归一化的SU(2)相对取向场，推导其有效理论，分析单位通量与分数Z弦的局域稳定性，并研究Nambu单极子在界面中的束缚/退禁闭相及其电磁场分布。

Result: 发现界面支持局域稳定的单位与分数Z弦；分数弦携带S²取向场，Nambu单极子表现为连接对称相关等张力Z弦的第二类kink；存在单极子被束缚于分数弦（通量局域化）或退禁闭为界面内涡旋（通量径向弥散）的两个相；电磁磁场均延伸至界面外，且磁荷在相变中守恒。

Conclusion: Z弦与Nambu单极子可在特定希格斯真空界面中获得拓扑与动力学稳定性；其禁闭-退禁闭相变由界面有效理论控制，但不改变整体电磁磁荷，为理解电弱尺度下拓扑缺陷的物理实现提供了新范式。

Abstract: Electroweak $Z$ strings are generally unstable, and their Nambu-monopole endpoints lack topological protection in the bulk. Using degenerate inert and inert-like Higgs vacua as a solvable reference, we show that their interface supports both defects. The analytic interface carries a normalizable $SU(2)$ relative-orientation field. Its effective theory supports locally stable unit-flux $Z$ strings in a one-vacuum phase and fractional $Z$ strings in a two-vacuum phase. The fractional string carries an $S^2$ orientation field; a Nambu monopole is a second kink between symmetry-related, equal-tension $Z$ strings. We identify phases in which Nambu monopoles are confined or deconfined within the interface: in the confined phase, their $Z$ flux is concentrated into fractional strings, whereas in the deconfined phase, the monopole becomes a vortex within the interface and its $Z$ flux spreads radially through the interface. The electromagnetic magnetic field extends outside the interface in both phases. We determine analytic string profiles, effective-theory stability regions, and the unchanged electromagnetic magnetic charge across this confinement transition.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 构建考虑双夸克有限尺寸的理论框架，采用统一的峰高加权半径方案处理基态和径向激发态的非奇异与奇异双夸克，并计算多种Ξ和Ω型双重子的质量谱。

Abstract: Heavy antiquark-diquark symmetry (HADS) maps a compact color-antitriplet heavy pair onto a pointlike antiquark source, but retaining a finite diquark mass does not retain its spatial extent. We develop a finite-size framework that treats the heavy diquark as a composite cluster with an independently determined mass and scale. A unified peak-height-weighted radius prescription applies to ground and radially excited diquarks in nonstrange and strange environments. For 16 selected states of $Ξ_{cc}$, $Ω_{cc}$, $Ξ_{bb}$, and $Ω_{bb}$, the mean absolute mass deviation from the central AL1 three-body spectrum falls from 108 MeV in the point-source limit to 10 MeV, without fitting to baryon levels. The structure-resolution angle defines $δ_{\rm HADS}=1-\langle\cos\hatθ\rangle$, a quantitative, state-dependent measure of the finite-size contribution to HADS breaking. Internal excitation increases this correction, whereas external excitation reduces it, demonstrating that the same heavy source can be resolved differently by distinct surrounding states. Comparisons across reference potentials preserve these trends and the spectral improvement.

The resonance with $J^{PC}=1^{--}$ corresponds to a loosely bound state, while the resonance with $J^{PC}=1^{+-}$ is a virtual state above the threshold. Abstract: We study the theoretical origin of scalar non-standard interactions (SNSI) in an \(A_4\)-symmetric Type-II seesaw model. The model contains an \(SU(2)_L\) scalar triplet \(Δ\) and a real scalar singlet \(S\). After electroweak symmetry breaking, their CP-even neutral fluctuations mix to form two physical scalar states \(φ_1\) and \(φ_2\). These physical scalars couple to both neutrinos and charged leptons, establishing a connection between the neutrino sector and ordinary matter through scalar interactions. This induces a matter-dependent correction to the Type-II seesaw neutrino mass matrix. The SNSI contribution, governed by the relevant Yukawa couplings and the SNSI coefficient, can enhance or suppress the neutrino masses relative to their vacuum values. The model provides a simple and consistent description of how scalar mixing can give rise to SNSI and connects neutrino interactions in matter with the underlying structure of the scalar sector.

One reason may be that these theoretical models either approximate the Coulomb interaction poorly or omit it entirely. For this, we used a custom-developed simulation framework written in C++ and Python. The C++ module integrates the equations of motion for relativistic pions using the Leapfrog algorithm, while the Python post-processing script generates the two-particle spatial source function from the phase-space coordinates. Abstract: We investigate radiative corrections to the QCD $\barθ$ parameter in the minimal left-right symmetric model with a universal seesaw mechanism, where parity symmetry ensures $\barθ=0$ at tree level. We find that the dominant contributions do not decouple and depend sensitively on the mass splittings as well as flavor mixings of the vector-like quarks. For hierarchical vector-like quark masses, the resulting $\barθ$ typically exceeds the current experimental bound from the neutron electric dipole moment. Satisfying this bound requires either nearly degenerate vector-like quark masses or strongly suppressed additional flavor mixing angles. In the former case, the radiative corrections are suppressed by a GIM-like mechanism. For generic mixing parameters, the dimensionless relative mass splittings are constrained to be below approximately $10^{-2}$ and $10^{-4}$ in the up- and down-type vector-like quark sectors, respectively. These results severely restrict the inverted vector-like quark mass hierarchy motivated by the universal seesaw mechanism.

</details>


### [95] [Next-to-leading-order QCD study of $e^+e^- \to η_c + c\bar{c}$ at $B$ factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39718)
*Lu-Yao Zhang,Xu-Chang Zheng,Xing-Gang Wu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在非相对论QCD（NRQCD）框架下，计算了e⁺e⁻ → γ* → η_c + car{c} + X过程的QCD下领头阶（NLO）修正，发现NLO修正使直接截面翻倍，并显著增强角分布和动量分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确理解Belle II实验中η_c伴随开粲强子产生的机制，需建立可靠的理论基准，尤其是色单态贡献的高阶修正。

Method: 采用非相对论QCD（NRQCD）因子化框架，保留色单态贡献及领头阶v（重夸克相对速度）展开，在√s = 10.6 GeV下进行QCD NLO计算，并计入J/ψ与h_c(1P)的辐射衰变馈赠贡献。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了η_c伴随开粲产生的NLO色单态理论基准，为Belle II实验提供关键支持，并与强子对撞中的类粲偶素产生研究形成互补。

Abstract: We study the process $e^+e^- \to γ^\ast \to η_c + c\bar{c} + X$ at next-to-leading order (NLO) in QCD within the framework of nonrelativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization, retaining the color-singlet contribution at leading order in the heavy-quark relative velocity $v$. At the $e^+e^-$ collision energy $\sqrt{s} = 10.6~\mathrm{GeV}$, with $m_c = 1.5~\mathrm{GeV}$ and $μ_R = 2m_c$, the direct cross section increases from $58.7~\mathrm{fb}$ at leading order (LO) to $118~\mathrm{fb}$ at NLO, corresponding to a $K$ factor of approximately $2.0$. Including the radiative feed-down contributions of the $J/ψ$ and $h_c(1P)$ states yields an estimated prompt production rate of $131~\mathrm{fb}$. The NLO corrections substantially enhance both the angular and momentum distributions, while the momentum spectrum retains a similar overall profile. These results establish an NLO color-singlet baseline for $η_c$ production associated with open charm at Belle II, complementary to studies of charmonium production in hadronic collisions.

*Yu-Song Cao,Nick Houston,YanXia Liu,QingZheng Lv,She-Sheng Xue*

Main category: hep-ph

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ALPs是极具理论动机的暗物质候选者，但因其极弱相互作用而难以直接探测。

Method: 将原用于探测随机引力波背景（SGWB）的空间光学晶格钟网络（SOLCN）稍加改造，利用其激光链路探测ALPs诱导的宇宙双折射效应，并通过交叉关联信号分析提高灵敏度。

Result: 在ALP质量范围10^{-18.55}–10^{-14} eV内，尤其在10^{-18}–10^{-14} eV区间，可探测ALP-光子耦合常数g_{aγ}低至10^{-12} GeV^{-1}；采用8 GHz链路频率时，灵敏度可达g_{aγ} ≥ 10^{-16} GeV^{-1}。

Conclusion: SOLCN是一种极具潜力的新平台，能有效拓展低质量ALPs参数空间的实验探测能力，且其灵敏度强烈依赖于网络链路频率。

Abstract: Axion like particles (ALPs) are particularly well motivated candidates for dark matter, and physics beyond the Standard Model more generally. However, direct detection remains extremely challenging due to the weakness of their interactions. In this work, we propose to employ space-based optical lattice clock networks (SOLCN), originally targeted to probe the stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB), to detect axion dark matter through cosmic birefringence via minor modifications to their laser links. Searching for cross-correlated signals then provides significant sensitivity to low mass ALPs as a result of their large correlation lengths. In this work we focus on ALPs in the mass range $10^{-18.55}\text{eV}-10^{-14}\text{eV}$, For ALPs mass region $10^{-18}-10^{-14}\text{eV}$, finding that we can probe down to $g_{aγ}\geq 10^{-12}\,\text{GeV}^{-1}$. Moreover, we find that the SOLCN sensitivity to the ALP-photon coupling depends crucially on the frequency of the network link. With an $8$ GHz frequency link, we then have the potential to reach $g_{aγ}\geq10^{-16}\,\text{GeV}^{-1}$ in this region.

</details>


### [97] [From Colour--Casimir Recoupling to Near-Threshold Scattering: Algebraic Diagnostics and a Pion-Mass Test for the $T_{cc}^{+}$ Channel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39787)
*M. Monemzadeh,N. Tazimi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Tcc+近阈值态作为检验夸克色结构与强子散射关系的理想平台，通过色代数分析、动力学负结果验证及π介子质量测试三方面展开研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Tcc+近阈值态如何反映夸克层面的色结构与强子散射之间的联系，为理解多夸克态提供理论基础。

Method: 采用二维色单态基底（cc̄ūd̄）进行色代数推导；对Coulomb型高斯边和高斯组分哈密顿量进行数值模拟；在不同π介子质量下进行耦合常数外推与Lippmann-Schwinger方程基准测试。

Result: （i）色代数投影精确成立，揭示直接DD*矩阵元仅依赖于类内系数，而隐色-介子混合由Δv决定；（ii）高斯动力学模型中最低紧致态高于DD*阈值148.6 MeV；（iii）π质量变化下未复现格点复极点，仅完成单通道可分势基准研究。

Conclusion: 该工作澄清了色结构投影的代数本质，排除了某些吸引机制，并指出完整耦合道计算与更真实动力学建模的必要性。

Abstract: The near-threshold $T_{cc}^{+}$ is a clean testing ground for how constituent-level colour structure connects to hadronic scattering. We report three results and state what each does and does not establish. (i)~\emph{Colour algebra.} In the two-dimensional colour-singlet basis of $cc\bar u\bar d$, a general pairwise colour operator has direct $DD^*$ matrix element $V_{MM}=-\tfrac43(v_{13}+v_{24})$, containing only the two intra-meson coefficients, while meson--hidden-colour mixing is $V_{MH}^{\rm off}=\tfrac{\sqrt2}{3}Δ_v$ with $Δ_v=v_{14}+v_{23}-v_{12}-v_{34}$; uniform edges give no mixing. The projection is exact within this basis and is checked numerically on 10\,000 random edge assignments. A same-Hamiltonian decomposition shows that it re-expresses the central colour operator ($116.46$ MeV off-diagonal) rather than adding an independent dynamical input. (ii)~\emph{Negative dynamical results.} For Coulomb-type Gaussian edges the hidden-colour diagonal lies below the meson diagonal, so the attractive second-order shift $-2Δ_v^2/(9Δ_H)$ does not apply. In a Gaussian constituent Hamiltonian calibrated to $M_D$ and $M_{D^*}$, the lowest compact level lies $148.6$ MeV above its model $DD^*$ threshold (iii)~\emph{Pion-mass test.} Couplings fixed at the $m_π\simeq280$ MeV lattice pole ($-5.2$ MeV) are carried to $m_π\simeq391$ MeV by changing only the reduced mass and compact-sector gap, both refitted to the lattice $D$ and $D^*$ masses. A one-channel separable Lippmann--Schwinger benchmark ($Λ=650$ MeV, $μ=967.7$ MeV, $C_G\simeq-36.15~\mathrm{GeV}^{-2}$) is calibrated to the $5.2$ MeV pole and serves only as a regulator-sensitivity study. We do not reproduce the complex lattice pole or perform a full coupled-channel calculation.

</details>


### [98] [Spontaneous CP violation and leptogenesis in minimal flavour models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39858)
*H. B. Câmara,F. R. Joaquim,J. R. Rocha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种扩展型I类跷跷板机制，引入一个标量单态，其真空期望值（VEV）自发破缺CP对称性，为轻子CP破坏与宇宙重子不对称性（BAU）提供共同起源；基于Z4离散对称性构建两个最小味模型，预言了特定的CP相位δ≈3π/2、θ23混合角象限及中微子质量下限，并在非共振区研究标量辅助的轻子生成机制，成功解释观测到的BAU。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一解释低能轻子CP破坏与高能轻子生成所需CP破坏，建立共同起源；同时构建可检验的最小味模型以预测中微子振荡参数和无中微子双贝塔衰变可观测量。

Method: 构建含标量单态的I类跷跷板扩展模型，引入Z4离散对称性构造两个最小味模型，其中中微子味结构完全由重马约拉纳质量矩阵决定；在非共振区求解不含味分辨的Boltzmann方程，纳入新标量参与的衰变、散射与洗出过程及其对CP不对称性的贡献。

Result: 两个模型均预言δ≈3π/2、确定的θ23象限，以及正常/倒序质量谱下最轻中微子质量与有效马约拉纳质量mββ分别不低于60 meV / 50 meV；参数空间中存在能成功再生观测BAU的区域。

Conclusion: 标量单态自发破缺CP可统一解释低能与高能CP破坏；所提Z4味模型具有强预测性且可被下一代无中微子双贝塔衰变实验检验；该框架在非共振轻子生成中自洽实现重子不对称性。

Abstract: We consider a type-I seesaw framework extended by a scalar singlet whose vacuum expectation value (VEV) breaks CP spontaneously. The singlet VEV phase provides a common origin for low-energy leptonic CP violation and the high energy CP violation needed to generate the observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe (BAU) through leptogenesis. We identify two minimal flavour models based on a discrete $\mathcal{Z}_4$ symmetry, whose neutrino flavour structure is encoded entirely in the heavy Majorana mass matrix. Both models predict $δ\simeq 3π/2$, a definite octant for the atmospheric $θ_{23}$ mixing angle, and lower bounds of approximately $60~\mathrm{meV}$ ($50~\mathrm{meV}$) on the lightest neutrino mass and the effective Majorana mass $m_{ββ}$ for normal (inverted) ordering, within reach of future neutrinoless double-beta decay searches. We study scalar-singlet assisted leptogenesis in the non-resonant regime, including new decay, scattering, and washout processes involving the heavy scalars, as well as their contributions to the CP asymmetries. By solving the unflavoured Boltzmann equations, we identify the regions of parameter space in which the minimal flavour models successfully account for the observed BAU.

</details>


### [99] [NNLO QCD corrections to direct $J/ψ$ production with open charm at B factories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39895)
*Xiangyu Chang,Chao Zeng,Bin Gong,Jian-Xiong Wang,Cong Li*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高J/ψ伴随开粲过程理论预言精度，缓解NLO与Belle实验数据（尤其是角分布）的偏差，并检验微扰QCD收敛性。

Method: 采用嵌套软-共线减法方法计算包含粲夸克质量效应的NNLO QCD修正，并结合NLO质量外推和ψ(2S)衰变贡献估计总截面。

Result: 在√s=10.6 GeV下获得NNLO截面0.324^{+0.041}_{-0.066} pb，比NLO高18.4%，标度依赖性减小；动量谱峰值附近与Belle数据更吻合，但角分布偏差增大；计入质量外推和ψ(2S)馈赠后总截面约0.59 pb。

Conclusion: NNLO修正显著提升截面精度并改善动量谱描述，但角分布理论与实验差异凸显，需Belle II更高统计精度数据进一步检验。

Abstract: We calculate the next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to direct colour-singlet $J/ψ$ production with open charm at B factories, using nested soft-collinear subtraction for massive partons. At $\sqrt{s}=10.6$ GeV, $m_c=1.5$ GeV and $μ_R=2m_c$, we obtain an approximate NNLO cross section of $0.324^{+0.041}_{-0.066}$ pb, with scale uncertainties from $m_c\leqμ_R\leq4m_c$. The total cross section is $18.4\%$ above NLO and exhibits good perturbative convergence through NNLO, with reduced scale dependence. An NLO-based mass extrapolation to $m_c=1.4$ GeV, combined with estimated $ψ(2S)$ feed-down, raises the rate to about $0.59$ pb. The NNLO correction brings the absolute momentum spectrum near its peak closer to Belle's prompt open-charm data, but shifts the angular shape further away from Belle's prompt charm-associated data. The difference in angular shape motivates more precise measurements at Belle II.

</details>

Result: 给出了slepton在该模型下的完整质量谱表达式，并确认可在该框架下自洽定义R-宇称守恒，即超势保持F = B+L不变。

Conclusion: 该模型不仅提供了可计算的slepton质量谱，还自然支持R-宇称守恒，有利于稳定轻子超伴子并抑制质子衰变等危险过程。

In this model, we can define $R$-Parity conservation, where our superpotential respect the invariance of the quantum number ${\cal F} \equiv B+L$, where $B$ is the baryon number while $L$ is the total lepton number.

### [101] [$P$--wave quarkonia in jets at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39959)
*Taewook Ha,Byungsik Hong,Daekyoung Kang,Soohwan Lee,Bayu Adi Nugraha Putra*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地理论研究了LHC上在喷注内产生P波粲偶素χcJ和底偶素χbJ(nP)的过程，采用碎裂喷注函数框架并在αs的下一阶展开，同时重求和DGLAP对数和阈值双对数，并考虑高态馈送贡献。发现底偶素分布具有近似普适性，而粲偶素分布则对长程矩阵元更敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解P波重夸克偶素在喷注内的产生机制，特别是区分粲偶素与底偶素行为差异的理论根源，并为CMS和LHCb实验提供可检验预测。

Method: 采用碎裂喷注函数（fragmenting jet function）框架，在αs的下一阶（NLO）下计算χcJ和χbJ(nP)在喷注中的产生；重求和DGLAP和阈值双对数；纳入高态馈送贡献；对比不同LDME集合、主量子数n和自旋J的影响。

Result: χbJ(nP)的归一化动量分数分布呈现近似普适形状，对LDME、n和J依赖极弱，源于^3S_1^[8]碎裂通道主导；而χcJ分布形状对LDME敏感，反映^3S_1^[8]与^3P_J^[1]通道相对贡献差异；给出CMS和LHCb运动学区域的预测，并与Pythia 8模拟比较。

Conclusion: 底偶素P波态在喷注中的产生表现出强普适性，适合作为QCD碎裂过程的干净探针；粲偶素则因多种通道竞争而更具模型依赖性；该工作为重夸克偶素产生机制提供了关键理论基准。

Abstract: We present the first systematic theoretical study of the production of $P$-wave charmonium states $χ_{cJ}$ and bottomonium states $χ_{bJ}(nP)$ within jets at the LHC using the fragmenting jet function framework at next-to-leading order in $α_s$. Our calculation resums both collinear DGLAP logarithms and threshold double logarithms in the fragmentation functions and accounts for feeddown contributions from higher quarkonium states. We observe a notable contrast between the charmonium and bottomonium sectors. For $χ_{bJ}(nP)$, the normalized momentum-fraction distributions exhibit a nearly universal shape, with remarkably weak dependence on the choice of long-distance matrix element sets, the radial excitation ($n=1,2,3$), and the total angular momentum ($J=0,1,2$). We attribute this universality to the overwhelming dominance of the ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$ fragmentation channel, while feeddown contributions from higher bottomonium states remain small. In contrast, the shape of $χ_{cJ}$ distributions exhibits a stronger dependence on the matrix-element set, reflecting the different relative contributions of the ${}^3S_1^{[8]}$ and ${}^3P_J^{[1]}$ channels. We provide predictions for the $χ_{cJ}$ and $χ_{bJ}(nP)$ distributions for the CMS and LHCb kinematic selections across a range of jet transverse momenta. We also compare our predictions with \textsc{Pythia}~8 simulations for selected $P$-wave quarkonium states.

</details>


### [102] [The $K T_{cc}(3875)$ Interaction: Bound State, Scattering Observables and Correlation Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40065)
*Wen-Hao Jia,Dao-Cong Lin,Chao Huang,Wei-Hong Liang,Eulogio Oset*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We investigate the interaction of a kaon with the $T_{cc}(3875)$ state, evaluating the scattering amplitude and the correlation function of the system within a molecular picture in which the $T_{cc}(3875)$ is regarded as a $D^* D$ bound state. Since the $KD^*$ and $KD$ interactions dynamically generate the $D_{s1}(2460)$ and $D_{s0}^*(2317)$ resonances, respectively, both of which are significantly bound with respect to their corresponding particle thresholds, the $KT_{cc}(3875)$ interaction is found to be strongly attractive, leading to the formation of two bound states with binding energies of about 63 MeV and 51 MeV, respectively, with respect to the $KT_{cc}(3875)$ threshold. We also determine the scattering observables at threshold, including the scattering length and effective range, and calculate the correlation function. The latter exhibits features similar to those found in previous studies of the scattering of an external particle off a dynamically generated resonance of molecular nature.

### [103] [Off-lightcone Wilson-line operators in gradient flow at next-to-next-to-leading order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40225)
*Xiang-Peng Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于梯度流的规范不变非光锥威尔逊线算符的重整化方案，并计算了从梯度流方案到MS-bar方案的NNLO匹配系数，适用于格点QCD中准部分子分布、准分布振幅及QGP中重夸克输运等物理量的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 格点QCD中计算非光锥威尔逊线算符的矩阵元需要合适的重整化方案；梯度流能压制紫外涨落并降低统计噪声，但需建立其与连续场论方案（如MS-bar）的匹配关系。

Method: 采用一维辅助场形式主义的匹配框架，在小流时间极限下将非局域威尔逊线算符匹配简化为局域流算符匹配，并在QCD中计算了梯度流方案到MS-bar方案的NNLO匹配系数。

Result: 得到了NNLO精度下的匹配系数，可应用于准部分子分布（含自旋）、准分布振幅、QGP中胶子关联函数、pNRQCD中夸克偶素过程及静态势的自旋相关相对论修正。

Conclusion: 该匹配方案为格点上非光锥算符的高精度物理提取提供了可靠、系统且可推广的重整化工具。

Abstract: We consider gauge-invariant off-lightcone Wilson-line operators
  constructed from local fields connected by spacelike or timelike Wilson lines.
  The lattice evaluation of their matrix elements requires
  a suitable renormalization prescription. Gradient flow offers such
  a prescription and, by smoothing
  the fields, suppresses ultraviolet fluctuations and can reduce statistical
  noise. We use the previously established matching framework based on
  the one-dimensional auxiliary-field formalism. In the small-flow-time
  limit, this framework reduces the matching of nonlocal off-lightcone
  Wilson-line operators to that of local current operators.
  We calculate the matching coefficients for these local current operators
  from the gradient-flow scheme to the modified minimal subtraction
  ($\MS$) scheme through next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) in QCD.
  Our results can be applied to lattice calculations of unpolarized and
  polarized quark and gluon quasi-parton distributions, as well as meson
  and baryon quasi-distribution amplitudes.
  They also apply to gluonic correlators entering quarkonium transport
  and heavy-quark momentum diffusion in the quark-gluon plasma (QGP).
  Further applications include quarkonium decay and production in
  potential nonrelativistic QCD (pNRQCD) and
  spin-dependent relativistic corrections to the static potential.

</details>


### [104] [Parity-violating Møller scattering: complete two-loop corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40247)
*Andrzej Czarnecki*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了MOLLER实验中Møller散射的两圈电弱修正，以提高弱电参数测量精度，结果表明两圈修正贡献约1.5%，但未计算项和数据-格点差异仍是主要误差来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高MOLLER实验对电子弱荷及弱混合角测量的理论预测精度，以匹配2.4%的实验精度目标。

Method: 计算费米子与玻色子两圈电弱修正，区分零阶与一阶$1-4\sin^2\theta_W$项；轻夸克真空极化输入来自$e^+e^-$实验数据或格点QCD计算。

Result: 在$\sin^2\hat{\theta}_W(M_Z) = 0.23122$和11 GeV束流下，电子弱荷为0.04546(8)（实验数据）或0.04517(5)（格点），两圈修正占约1.5%；未计算项估计占实验目标的4%，数据与格点差异约占目标的25%。

Conclusion: 两圈电弱修正是关键但非主导误差源；当前理论精度受限于真空极化输入的不确定性，尤其是$e^+e^-$数据与格点QCD结果之间的差异。

Abstract: The MOLLER experiment will measure the parity-violating asymmetry of Møller scattering to 2.4%. We present its fermionic and bosonic two-loop electroweak corrections to the zeroth and first orders in $1-4\sin^2θ_W$. Light quarks enter through vacuum polarization from $e^+e^-$ data or lattice QCD. For $\sin^2\hatθ_W(M_Z) = 0.23122$ and an 11 GeV beam, the weak charge of the electron is $0.04546(8)$ (data) or 0.04517(5) (lattice). Two-loop terms contribute 1.5% of it. Uncomputed terms are estimated at 4% of the experimental goal; the data-lattice difference, about 25% of that goal, limits the prediction.

</details>


### [105] [Heavy-Particle Effective Theory: from On-Shell Formalism to Gravitational Compton Amplitudes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40263)
*Yong-Kang Li,Yi-Ning Wang,Jiang-Hao Yu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于速度投影自旋螺旋变量的重粒子有效理论（HPET）振幅构造方法，通过保留大、小分量实现重参数化不变性，并导出符合幂次计数的HPET振幅；应用于引力时，成功复现Kerr黑洞的自旋诱导多极矩，并验证了经典极限下散射与Compton振幅与文献一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统HPET构造中难以自然保持重参数化不变性，且振幅展开易引入虚假极点；需建立一种能直接关联局域算符、保持对称性并适用于高自旋粒子的系统性振幅构造框架。

Method: 将自旋螺旋变量投影到类时参考速度v^μ的SU(2)小群上，构建包含大、小分量的速度投影振幅；先施加重参数化不变性约束，再对重质量做展开；最后积掉小分量得到满足HPET幂次计数的振幅；利用三定点投影振幅构造型地构造四点引力Compton振幅。

Result: 推导出任意自旋S、等质量两粒子与软无质量玻色子（螺旋度h）耦合的三定点速度投影振幅；构造出无虚假极点（至次领头阶）的HPET引力Compton振幅；证明速度投影最小耦合精确复现Kerr的自旋多极矩至2S阶；树图弹性散射与Compton振幅的经典极限与已有文献一致（接触项除外）。

Conclusion: 速度投影自旋螺旋方法为HPET提供了更自然、对称性保持良好且可推广至高自旋的振幅构造范式，统一了有效理论与散射振幅方法，在经典引力与自旋物理建模中具有重要应用价值。

Abstract: We formulate on-shell heavy-particle effective theory (HPET) amplitudes by projecting the spinor-helicity variables onto the $SU(2)$ little group of a timelike reference velocity $v^μ$. Retaining both large and small components of the velocity-projected spinors gives an intermediate velocity-projected amplitudes in which reparameterization invariance can be imposed before expanding in the inverse heavy-particle mass. Integrating out the small component then yields HPET amplitudes organized by heavy-particle power counting, with a direct correspondence to local operators at fixed order. We determine the three-point velocity-projected amplitudes for two equal-mass particles of arbitrary spin $S$ coupled to a soft massless boson of helicity $h$. Four-point gravitational Compton amplitudes can be constructively obtained using velocity-projected three-point amplitudes, and expanded into HPET amplitudes which is free of spurious poles up to sub-leading order. As a gravitational application, we show that the projected gravitational minimal coupling reproduces the Kerr spin-induced multipoles up to order $2S$, and find the classical limits of the tree-level elastic gravitational scattering and gravitational Compton amplitudes agreed with the literature up to contact terms undetermined by factorization.

</details>


### [106] [Two-loop QCD contribution to the quark-initiated production of a top-antitop quark pair and a Higgs boson at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40266)
*Xiang Chen,Massimiliano Grazzini,Stefan Kallweit*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次直接计算了LHC上顶夸克对伴随希格斯玻色子（t-tbar-H）产生过程的两圈QCD虚修正，聚焦于夸克-反夸克通道，并采用广义大色近似；结合先进多圈数值方法与机器学习技术构建代理振幅，用于精确截面计算，并验证了与以往动力学近似结果的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算t-tbar-H过程的高阶QCD修正对希格斯耦合测量和新物理探针至关重要，但两圈计算技术难度大，此前缺乏直接结果。

Method: 在广义大色近似下，针对q-qbar初态，采用现代多圈积分约化、数值积分及机器学习辅助构建高精度代理振幅。

Result: 获得了t-tbar-H两圈虚修正的首个直接数值结果，与基于动力学近似的先前结果在各自不确定度内一致。

Conclusion: 该工作为t-tbar-H精密理论预言奠定了基础，验证了代理振幅方法在复杂多圈计算中的有效性，支持后续LHC数据分析。

Abstract: We present the first direct computation of the two-loop QCD virtual corrections to the production of a top-antitop quark pair in association with a Higgs boson ($t\bar{t}H$) at the LHC. We focus on the quark-antiquark channel and perform the calculation in the generalised leading-colour approximation. We combine state-of-the-art numerical methods for multiloop calculations with machine learning techniques to construct a surrogate amplitude suitable for accurate cross section computations. We compare our numerical results with those previously obtained using dynamical approximations of the two-loop amplitude, finding agreement within their respective uncertainties.

</details>


### [107] [Probabilistic Energy-Based Neural Networks for Uncertainty-Aware Collider Data Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40268)
*Raymundo Ramos,Ahmed Hammad,Amit Chakraborty,Pyungwon Ko,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于概率能量的神经网络，用于对撞机分析中的不确定性感知分类，通过能量分数识别异常或偏移事件，并在h→τ⁺τ⁻过程的LHC实际分析中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准判别式分类器在面对与训练数据分布不同的事件时仍可能给出高置信度预测，缺乏对分布偏移和系统不确定性的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建一个概率能量型神经网络：输入映射到隐空间，能量定义在类特定嵌入上，以衡量事件与学习到的数据分布的一致性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实的LHC h→τ⁺τ⁻分析中均验证了该方法能有效识别建模误差和系统变化影响的事件，能量分数可用于不确定性感知的事件筛选。

Conclusion: 该能量模型在保持良好分类性能的同时，提升了对噪声、异常及分布偏移事件的识别能力，方法具有通用性，适用于其他对系统和分布不确定性敏感的对撞机分类任务。

Abstract: We introduce a probabilistic energy-based Neural Network (NN) for uncertainty-aware classification in collider analyses. Standard discriminative classifiers can give highly confident predictions even for events that are different from the data used during training. Energy-Based Models provide an alternative by assigning an energy score to each event, which measures how well the event agrees with the learned data distribution. In this framework, a NN maps the input features into a latent space, where the energy is defined with respect to class-specific embeddings. This allows the model to identify noisy, anomalous or shifted events while maintaining good classification performance. We first study the method using controlled synthetic examples and then test it in a realistic collider analysis based on the $h \to τ^+ τ^-$ process at the Large Hadron Collider, where $h$ is the discovered Standard Model Higgs boson. We show that the associated energy score can help identify events affected by mismodelling and/or systematic variations and can thus be used to define uncertainty-aware event selections. Although we use this specific Higgs process as a testbed, the method is general and can be applied to other collider classification problems where robustness against systematic and distributional uncertainties is important.

</details>


### [108] [Two-peak gravitational wave spectra from supercooled two-field phase transitions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40354)
*Daniel Araújo,João Gonçalves,Danny Marfatia*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了双场共形模型中超冷一级相变产生的引力波谱，构建了三个典型玩具模型，推导了两步相变存在的解析判据，并揭示了双场动力学如何生成真正的双峰引力波谱；同时确定了LISA和爱因斯坦望远镜可观测该双峰谱的参数空间区域，并以一个现实的共形暗SU(3)模型为例进行了应用分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超冷一级相变在双场共形模型中产生的引力波谱特征，特别是能否产生可观测的双峰结构，以助于未来引力波探测实验识别新物理信号。

Method: 构建三个代表性双场共形玩具模型，推导两步相变存在的解析判据，结合场论与宇宙学演化模拟，分析引力波谱生成机制，并扫描参数空间以识别LISA与爱因斯坦望远镜可观测区域。

Result: 发现双场动力学可自然导致真实的双峰引力波谱；确定了多个参数区域使得该双峰谱在LISA和爱因斯坦望远镜中具有可观测性；并在共形暗SU(3)模型中验证了该机制的可行性。

Conclusion: 双场共形模型中的超冷一级相变为产生可观测双峰引力波谱提供了新机制，为未来空间与地面引力波探测器提供关键理论指引和信号模板。

Abstract: We study the gravitational wave (GW) spectrum generated by supercooled first-order phase transitions in two-field conformal models. We construct three representative toy models that capture the essential features of these models, derive analytical criteria for the existence of two-step transitions, and show how the two-field dynamics generate a genuine two-peak GW spectrum. We identify regions of parameter space in which two-peak GW spectra are observable at LISA and the Einstein Telescope. By way of illustration, we apply our analysis to a realistic conformal dark SU(3) model.

</details>


### [109] [Hadronic resonances from the 4PI effective action of QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40390)
*Jonas Wessely,Markus Q. Huber*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了4PI有效作用量在研究QCD中共振态中的应用，推导出线性协变规范下至四圈的QCD 4PI有效作用量，并基于其构建二体束缚态核，结合Bethe-Salpeter方程框架实现对ρ、K*和φ介子宽度的计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用4PI有效作用量系统地描述QCD中由衰变道引发的共振态（如介子）

Method: 推导线性协变规范下QCD的4PI有效作用量至四圈；从中提取二体束缚态核；采用物理通道的壳上分解，在Bethe-Salpeter方程框架中实现两体衰变的图解表示

Result: 成功应用于ρ、K*和φ介子，提取了它们的衰变宽度

Conclusion: 4PI有效作用量能自然包含共振态的衰变机制，为QCD非微扰共振态研究提供了自洽的场论工具

Abstract: We discuss the 4PI effective action as a tool to study resonances in QCD. To this end, we present the 4PI effective action of QCD in linear covariant gauges up to four loops and derive from it the kernels for two-body bound states, using mesons as an example. The emergence of resonances via decay channels is built into the formalism at the level of the four-point functions, and we employ an on-shell decomposition of its physical channels to arrive at a diagrammatic representation for physical two-body decays within the Bethe-Salpeter equation framework. We apply this formalism to the $ρ$, $K^*$ and $φ$ mesons and extract their widths.

</details>


### [110] [NNLO Final-State Quark-Pair Corrections in Implicit Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40395)
*Ricardo J. C. Rosado,Adriano Cherchiglia,Marcos Sampaio,Brigitte Hiller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在隐式正则化（IReg）框架下，计算了中性轴矢流（Z）衰变为无质量夸克对的NNLO末态夸克对修正，并在大N_f极限下验证了紫外子发散的递归减除和虚实贡献间红外奇点的相消。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试隐式正则化（IReg）框架下两圈阶计算中紫外子发散递归减除与红外奇点相消机制，并避免维数正规化中γ5和Levi-Civita张量等四维结构解析延拓带来的模糊性与对称性恢复反项。

Method: 采用隐式正则化（IReg）方法，在大N_f极限下计算中性轴矢流Z→qq̄衰变的NNLO末态夸克对修正，不依赖于时空维数的解析延拓；进行重整化与相空间积分。

Result: 重整化与相空间积分后，调节器相关的对数项相消，成功复现了已知的N_f对包含衰变率的贡献；为IReg框架下的规范不变性、重整化一致性与微扰幺正性提供了非平凡的NNLO检验。

Conclusion: IReg作为固定维数正规化方法，在多圈高精度计算中具有优势，可规避维数正规化对四维结构（如γ5、Levi-Civita张量）延拓所引入的歧义与额外反项，提升计算可靠性。

Abstract: We investigate the NNLO final-state quark-pair correction to the decay of a neutral axial-vector current ($Z$) into massless quarks within Implicit Regularization (IReg), in the limit of large $N_f$. Our calculation tests, at two loops, the recursive subtraction of ultraviolet subdivergences and the cancellation of infrared singularities between virtual and real contributions in a framework that does not rely on analytical continuation in spacetime dimension. After renormalization and phase-space integration, the regulator-dependent logarithms cancel and the known $N_f$ contribution to the inclusive rate is recovered. The result provides a non-trivial NNLO check of gauge invariance, renormalization consistency, and perturbative unitarity in IReg. In precision multi-loop calculations, fixed-dimension regularization methods can be advantageous by avoiding the dimensional continuation of intrinsically four-dimensional structures, such as $γ_5$ matrices and Levi-Civita tensors, thereby reducing ambiguities and symmetry-restoring counterterms.

</details>


### [111] [Typed Transverse Momentum Parton distribution function computations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40549)
*Dustin Keller*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了typed-TMD计算框架，以明确表达横动量依赖部分子分布（TMD）提取中的观测语义、参数依赖性和可接受的评估计划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实现中，即使单位和数组维度一致，也不能保证两个实现代表相同的推理问题，因此需要更明确的框架来规范TMD提取过程。

Method: 提出typed-TMD计算框架，显式建模观测映射、参数依赖关系及可行的数值评估计划。

Result: 实现了对TMD提取中关键语义要素（如观测语义、参数依赖）的显式表达与结构化处理。

Conclusion: typed-TMD提升了TMD分析的可复现性与可比性，为高能物理中的参数推断提供了更严谨的计算基础。

Abstract: A transverse-momentum-dependent parton distribution enters an extraction through a specified observation map and a parameter- dependent numerical calculation. Equal units and matching array dimensions do not establish that two implementations represent the same inference problem. We present typed-TMD, a computational framework that makes observation semantics, parameter dependencies, and admissible evaluation plans explicit.

</details>


### [112] [A Lighter QCD-Axion from Small Instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40629)
*Sourav Bera,Susobhan Chattopadhyay,Rick S. Gupta*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出两种新模型，通过在紫外（UV）尺度上同时修改QCD和标准模型味结构，使QCD轴子质量显著低于传统预期；关键机制是UV瞬子对轴子质量和上夸克Yukawa耦合均产生非微扰贡献，当瞬子主导上夸克质量时，轴子可显著变轻；该机制拓展了轴子实验探测范围，尤其利好左偏于传统QCD轴子带的搜寻实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决QCD轴子质量通常被限制在较窄范围的问题，探索其质量显著低于标准预期的可能性，以拓展理论可能性并匹配新型实验探测窗口（如左偏于QCD轴子带的haloscope/helioscope实验）。

Method: 构建两类紫外完成模型：(1) QCD规范群由UV中多个SU(3)群直积破缺而来；(2) 实现部分复合性的复合Higgs模型；在两类模型中系统计入UV瞬子对轴子质量与上夸克Yukawa的非微扰修正，并分析二者竞争关系。

Result: 证明当UV瞬子贡献占上夸克Yukawa主导时，QCD轴子质量可远低于标准预期，使其参数空间可延伸至传统QCD轴子带左侧；两类模型均支持该轻轴子解。

Conclusion: QCD轴子质量并非必须处于传统预期范围内；通过合理构造UV物理（特别是瞬子效应的协同调控），可自然实现轻轴子，为当前及未来轴子实验（尤其是探测左偏区域的实验）提供重要新理论目标。

Abstract: We propose models where the QCD axion mass can be much smaller than the standard expectation. We show that this is possible in models where both QCD and the Standard Model flavor structure get modified in the ultraviolet (UV). In these models, both the axion mass and the up-quark Yukawa can receive sizeable non-perturbative contributions from instantons in the UV. If both these effects are carefully taken into account, the QCD axion is not necessarily heavier than usual expectations and can, in fact, be much lighter. We show that if small instanton contributions account for a large fraction of the up-quark Yukawa, the QCD axion can be lighter than standard expectations whereas it is heavier otherwise. We propose two different models where this condition is realised: (1) one where the QCD gauge group emerges as a subgroup from a product of $SU(3)$ groups in the UV; and (2) a composite Higgs model that also implements partial compositeness. In both these models the QCD axion can lie not only in the region to the right of the QCD axion band but also far to its left. This is especially relevant for a range of proposed haloscope and helioscope experiments that will probe a region mostly to the left of the QCD band.

</details>

### [113] [Creation of the Universe in Lorentzian Classical Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37748)
*Avipsa Chakrabarti,Avik Chakraborty,Martín Molina,Ayan Mukhopadhyay,Salomón Romero*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在洛伦兹引力框架下理解宇宙创生的新机制——'宇宙创生门'，通过允许时空存在引力拼接（非光滑流形），构造了连接多个局域dS区域的旋转对称解；该模型在特定状态方程参数下具有双向测地完备性，初始与终态空间体积有限，并分析了线性扰动谱与宇宙学含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇点定理和暴胀时空的过去不完备性表明，宇宙起源超出半经典引力适用范围；本文旨在不依赖量子引力的前提下，在经典洛伦兹引力中为宇宙‘有始’提供自洽的经典几何描述。

Method: 引入类时完美流体拼接面（gravitational junction），构造旋转对称、多分支（n≥2）、D≥3维的'宇宙创生门'精确解；分析其测地完备性、体积演化、热力学行为；求解线性扰动，区分同质与异质模；探讨初值精细调节与因果响应对时空体积演化的影响。

Result: 1) 构造出满足P=wρ的timelike junction上连接n个局域dS区域的精确解；2) 当w > -(D-3)/(D-2)时，时空双向测地完备，全局时间下初末体积有限；3) 三维扰动含1个混合声模+ (n−1)个相对横模，高维仅存混合声模；4) 有限初体积需精细调节初态；5) 提出允许有界振荡的广义创生判据。

Conclusion: 宇宙可在纯经典洛伦兹引力中实现‘有始’（有限过去体积），无需奇点或量子化；创生门是受引力方程约束的条件化历史，其结构与扰动谱对早期宇宙模型和初始条件问题具有重要启示。

Abstract: The singularity theorems and the past incompleteness of inflationary spacetimes suggest that the beginning of the Universe lies beyond the domain of semiclassical gravity. We show that the creation of the Universe can nevertheless be understood within Lorentzian gravity by allowing gravitational junctions, so that the full spacetime need not be a smooth manifold. We construct rotationally invariant cosmic creation gates joining any finite number $n\geq2$ of locally de Sitter regions in $D\geq3$ spacetime dimensions at a timelike perfect-fluid junction with $P=wρ$. For $w>-(D-3)/(D-2)$, they are geodesically complete in both temporal directions. The total spatial volume approaches finite, positive limits in the infinite past and future on the global-time foliation adapted to the junction fluid. We also construct exact deformed cosmic creation gates through nonlinear homogeneous backreaction that retain the initial volume while the future volume grows without bound. In these solutions, the junction fluid always expands, cooling for $w>0$ and heating for allowed $w<0$. Keeping the bulk regions locally de Sitter, we determine the linearized perturbations: in three dimensions, the generic inhomogeneous sector contains one hybrid sound mode and $n-1$ relative transverse modes; in four and higher dimensions, only the homogeneous hybrid sound mode survives. A finite-volume beginning requires fine-tuned homogeneous and inhomogeneous initial data, while causal response generically leads to unbounded future spatial volume. We also discuss a broader creation criterion allowing bounded past-volume oscillations. The gravitational equations determine conditioned admissible histories. We discuss implications for cosmology.

</details>


### [114] [Black Hole Entropy from Entangled Quantum Reference Frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37750)
*Ludovic Varrin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Donnelly和Freidel的扩展相空间框架下，研究了Schwarzschild黑洞的边缘模式，将其解释为动力学参考系，其相空间是SL(2,R)的余切丛；通过在双曲面点粘合两个外部区域，得到单一正则对，并在两种等价规范（Kuchař与Carlip-Teitelboim）下描述；作者在后一规范下量子化参考系，构造两外部区域的引力纠缠积，并通过限制参考系为因果半群SL⁺(2,R)，利用其Plancherel测度导出与紫外截断无关的Bekenstein–Hawking熵及对数修正项作为纠缠熵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞边缘模式的物理本质及其在量子引力中对熵的微观起源贡献，特别是如何从参考系的动力学和纠缠结构自然导出Bekenstein–Hawking熵及其修正。

Method: 基于扩展相空间方法，将Schwarzschild黑洞边缘模式建模为SL(2,R)上的动力学参考系；通过规范选择（Carlip-Teitelboim变量）实现正则描述；对相对快度与面积变量进行量子化；引入因果性约束（SL⁺(2,R)半群）并应用其Plancherel测度计算纠缠熵。

Result: 成功导出Bekenstein–Hawking熵作为两外部区域间的纠缠熵，并伴随一个与紫外截断无关的对数修正项；确认相对快度即交叉积构造中的时间平移；建立引力纠缠积的明确构造。

Conclusion: 黑洞热力学熵可源于边缘参考系的量子纠缠结构，且其主导项与修正项均可由SL⁺(2,R)半群的调和分析自然得出，无需人为引入紫外截断，支持边缘模式在量子引力熵起源中的核心作用。

Abstract: In the extended phase space of Donnelly and Freidel, the edge modes of a Schwarzschild black hole are dynamical reference frames whose phase space is the cotangent bundle of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{R})$. Gluing the two exterior regions at the bifurcation point leaves a single canonical pair. Within the extended phase space, this pair can be described either by Kuchař's mass and difference of asymptotic times or by the area and the relative rapidity of the two frames, which are two gauge choices of the same system. The latter is the pair of Carlip and Teitelboim, and the relative rapidity is the time shift of the crossed product construction. We quantize the frames in this gauge and construct the gravitational entangling product of the two exteriors. Requiring the frames to be causal restricts them to the semigroup $\mathrm{SL}^+(2,\mathbb{R})$, whose Plancherel measure yields the Bekenstein--Hawking entropy as the entanglement entropy between the two exteriors, together with a logarithmic correction, both independent of the ultraviolet cutoff.

</details>


### [115] [Holomorphic fermions in $\text{AdS}_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37762)
*Calvin Y. -R. Chen,Lukas W. Lindwasser*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了二维反德西特空间中具有特定质量参数的Majorana费米子，发现其存在守恒的对称无迹旋量-张量，并揭示了潜在的共形对称性及手征代数结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维反德西特空间中Majorana费米子在特定质量参数下的对称性与守恒流结构，及其与共形场论的联系。

Method: 利用复坐标下的旋量-张量分析、Laurent级数展开，以及共形Killing旋量-张量的协变表述，参数化经典相空间并分析手征代数。

Result: 发现了对称无迹守恒旋量-张量；获得由任意(反)全纯函数完全参数化的经典相空间；揭示了隐藏的共形对称性迹象；识别出手征代数是平直空间对应代数的严格子代数。

Conclusion: 该模型展现出丰富的几何与代数结构，为理解弯曲时空中的量子场论及AdS/CFT对应提供了新线索。

Abstract: On two-dimensional Anti-de Sitter space, Majorana fermions with a discrete set of finely tuned, non-zero mass parameters possess conserved spinor--tensors that are symmetric and traceless. In complex coordinates, these correspond to pairs of holomorphic and anti-holomorphic half-integer spin currents and admit Laurent series mode expansions reminiscent of standard two-dimensional conformal field theories. We exploit this fact in order to fully parameterise the classical phase space associated with the equation of motion in terms of arbitrary (anti-)holomorphic functions. We further show that this parameterisation can be written covariantly in terms of a conformal Killing spinor--tensor, hinting at a hidden conformal symmetry. We also show they possess chiral algebras which are strict subalgebras of the one present in the corresponding theory on flat space.

</details>


### [116] [The Effective Field Theory Origin of the Fuzzy Sphere Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38702)
*Mykola Dedushenko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将(2+1)D共形场论的模糊球哈密顿量系统性地从S²×ℝ上的局域‘母理论’导出的新框架，并以Ising和易平面铁磁体为例，展示了如何通过积分掉不同自由度得到模糊球模型及其临界行为（包括z=2非相对论临界点），并发现其存在精确单磁子解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解模糊球上非相对论临界现象（如z=2标度）的起源及与局域场论的联系，需建立模糊哈密顿量与连续时空上的母理论之间的系统性对应。

Method: 构建定义在S²×ℝ上的局域母理论（含费米子与标量场），通过路径积分积分掉高能自由度（如高朗道能级、中介场或费米子）来导出模糊球哈密顿量或有效场论；分析对称性破缺、临界流与精确可解性。

Result: 1) 成功将Ising模糊球哈密顿量溯源至含两个带电费米子与实标量φ的母理论；2) 构造复标量Φ母理论，导出易平面铁磁体模糊球模型；3) 首次在模糊球上实现z=2非相对论临界点，并获得其解析解与精确单磁子态。

Conclusion: 模糊球哈密顿量可统一理解为局域母理论的低能有效描述，该框架不仅揭示了离散几何模型与连续场论的深层联系，还为构造和求解新型非相对论临界系统提供了普适工具。

Abstract: Fuzzy sphere Hamiltonians for (2+1)D conformal field theories are usually written directly on the lowest Landau level. We introduce a framework organizing them as descendants of local ``mother theories'' on $S^2\times\mathbb R$. We start with the example of Ising mother theory built from two charged Dirac fermions coupled to a real mediator $φ$. Integrating out $φ$ and the higher Landau levels produces the fuzzy Hamiltonian, while integrating out the fermions gives a conventional local $φ$ effective field theory flowing to the Ising point. Mild modifications of the same theory reach the Gaussian point (tricritical route) and the Lee-Yang continuation. Exploring the space of mother theories, we next promote $φ$ to a complex field $Φ$ and construct a new example, whose descendant is an easy-plane ferromagnet on the fuzzy sphere. Its spontaneous symmetry breaking phase gives a pseudo-Goldstone relativistic scalar, similar to the model introduced by Y.-C. He, while its critical point is non-relativistic, providing the first example of $z=2$ scaling on the fuzzy sphere. Remarkably, it admits exact one-magnon solutions, and the $z=2$ critical point can be found analytically. We then outline a rich landscape of further applications of our approach.

</details>


### [117] [Quiver Lab -- A Computing Tool for Quiver Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37766)
*Zhenghao Zhong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: Quiver Lab 是一个基于浏览器的工具，用于绘制quiver并探索三维N=4超对称理论的模空间，支持计算Higgs/Coulomb分支Hilbert级数、plethystic对数、细化结构及Decay/Fission Hasse图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为研究三维N=4超对称场论的模空间提供直观、易用且功能全面的可视化与计算平台。

Method: 开发基于浏览器的交互式工作区（Quiver Lab），集成代数几何与表示论算法，实现Hilbert级数计算、plethystic对数分析、Hasse图生成等功能，并设计Light与Expert两种用户工作流。

Result: 成功构建Quiver Lab工具，支持多种关键数学物理量的自动计算与可视化，提升相关领域研究效率。

Conclusion: Quiver Lab作为开源计算平台，有效桥接理论物理、代数几何与计算机科学，推动三维N=4理论模空间的探索与教学普及。

Abstract: Quiver Lab is a browser-based workspace for drawing quivers and exploring the moduli spaces of three-dimensional $\mathcal{N} = 4$ theories. It computes Higgs and Coulomb branch Hilbert series, plethystic logarithms, supported refinements, and Decay and Fission Hasse diagrams. This note explains the Light and Expert workflows and the main computational methods

</details>


### [118] [Go with the flow(s): radial de Sitter holography and $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37776)
*Matthew J. Blacker*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了定位于Schwarzschild-de Sitter时空的Wheeler-DeWitt态，并在此基础上提出一种切片无关的世界管全息框架，建立了其与T²及T²+Λ₂截断全息框架的字典关系，从而为dS₄黑洞实现了T²+Λ₂的体实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究de Sitter时空的对偶理论（体-边对应）：一方面从体（bulk）出发理解可能的对偶理论，另一方面从候选对偶理论反推体的性质。

Method: 构建midisuperspace Wheeler-DeWitt态以局域于Schwarzschild-de Sitter解；建立切片无关的世界管全息框架；建立该框架与T²和T²+Λ₂截断全息框架的对应字典；分析平均哈密顿量与T²(+Λ₂)种子能量的关系。

Result: 实现了dS₄黑洞中T²+Λ₂框架的体实现；识别出世界管框架的平均哈密顿量即为T²(+Λ₂)理论的种子能量；指出为复现T²(+Λ₂)能谱需引入切片依赖性，并讨论其代价。

Conclusion: 世界管全息提供了一种连接de Sitter体物理与截断全息模型（尤其是T²+Λ₂）的新路径，但切片无关性与精确能谱复现之间存在根本权衡。

Abstract: What can we learn about a putative dual to de Sitter spacetime from the bulk? Conversely, what can a candidate dual theory teach us about the bulk? In this work, we construct midisuperspace Wheeler-DeWitt states that are localised on the Schwarzschild-de Sitter spacetime. Using these states, we construct a slice-independent worldtube holographic framework in the static patch. We then construct a dictionary between this worldtube proposal and both the $T^2$ and $T^2 + Λ_2$ frameworks for cut-off holography. In doing so, we provide a bulk realisation of $T^2+ Λ_2$ for dS$_4$ black holes. We identify the average Hamiltonian of our worldtube proposal as the seed energy of the $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$ theories. To recover the $T^2 (+ Λ_2)$ energy spectrum as the average Hamiltonian of a worldtube theory, one must make the bulk framework slice-dependent, and we discuss the consequences of this trade-off.

</details>


### [119] [Differential Equations for Dressed "in-in" Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37856)
*Ivo Sachs*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将微分方程和分部积分恒等式的形式主义推广到共形耦合标量场的宇宙学in-in关联函数，将其表示为‘修饰’的平直空间振幅，并算法化地构建相应微分方程，用于分析其积分所得函数类型，以bubble-triangle和exchange图为例进行了演示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统刻画宇宙学in-in关联函数（特别是共形耦合标量场）所对应的特殊函数结构，需发展可算法化、具普适性的解析工具。

Method: 将平直空间振幅的微分方程与分部积分（IBP）方法推广至宇宙学in-in框架，针对共形耦合标量场构造‘修饰’振幅形式，并算法化导出对应微分方程。

Result: 成功为宇宙学bubble-triangle和exchange图建立了有效的微分方程，显著增强了对相关振幅所积分得到的函数类别的识别与分析能力。

Conclusion: 该推广形式主义是强大且可算法化的工具，为解析计算高阶宇宙学关联函数提供了新路径。

Abstract: We extend the formalism of differential equations and Integration-by-Parts identities, to cosmological in-in correlators of conformally coupled scalar fields, that can be formulated as dressed flat space amplitudes. These differential equations turn out to be quite powerful for extracting information about the class of functions which the corresponding amplitudes integrate to. Furthermore, they can be formulated algorithmically. Here we illustrate this for the cosmological bubble- triangle and exchange diagram.

</details>


### [120] [Near-horizon BF bounds for the $t^+$ KK tower on extremal AdS$_5$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37908)
*Xiao-Long Liu,Zheng Miao,Yuezhang Tang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有等角动量的极值带电AdS₅黑洞上，IIB超引力中t⁺型Kaluza-Klein塔所有 horizon harmonics 的带电 Breitenlohner–Freedman (BF) 下界行为，发现其是否满足BF界强烈依赖于电荷与角动量的相对大小，尤其在BPS曲线附近及上下区域表现出不同稳定性特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解AdS背景下带电旋转黑洞的近视界稳定性，特别是KK谱中各模式是否满足带电BF界，这对全息对偶和量子引力中的稳定性问题至关重要。

Method: 基于五维极大超引力（零级）和线性化例外场论（高阶）推导标量方程，系统分析不同电荷-自旋参数下各KK模式的BF界满足情况，特别考察BPS曲线附近的符号变化、离散性效应及高能级模式行为。

Result: 1) 在BPS曲线上所有模式满足BF界；2) 曲线上方（Ω_H>1）总存在违反模式，但有限截断无法覆盖全部；3) 曲线下方（Φ_H>1）仅窄带区域满足BF界，受电荷与自旋离散性控制；4) 大半径固定自旋时，四级KK模式（自旋∝半径）可在U(2)单态均满足BF界的情况下仍发生违反。

Conclusion: 该KK塔的近视界BF稳定性具有丰富相结构，由电荷-自旋比精细调控；当前结果仅针对两导数超引力中的单一KK塔，完整径向稳定性仍是开放问题。

Abstract: We study the charged Breitenlohner--Freedman (BF) bound for all horizon harmonics of the type IIB $t^+$ Kaluza--Klein tower on extremal charged AdS$_5$ black holes with equal rotations. The scalar equation follows from maximal five-dimensional gauged supergravity at level zero and the linearized exceptional-field-theory equations at higher levels. For aligned charge and rotation, a quadratic form in charge and spin changes signature on the curve of supersymmetric (BPS) black holes, where every mode satisfies the bound. Above the curve, where $Ω_H>1$, every black hole has a violating mode, although no finite truncation in level or spin detects violation at every such black hole. Below the curve, where $Φ_H>1$, the BF-passing set is confined to a thin strip and controlled by charge and spin discreteness. At large radius and fixed rotation, a level-four mode with spin proportional to the radius violates the bound even where all U(2) singlets satisfy it. These results concern the near-horizon BF bound of one tower in two-derivative supergravity; full radial stability remains open.

</details>


### [121] [Quotient Quiver Subtraction -- Product Groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38052)
*Sam Bennett,Amihay Hanany,Guhesh Kumaran*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用弦论中的brane系统，通过商箭图减法（quotient quiver subtraction）来系统地对3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ 理论中涌现的U(n)×U(n′)和SU(n)×SU(n′)库仑分支全局对称性进行规范化的组合方法，核心是将幺正规范群对称化为辛群，并通过多个显式例子验证该方法，同时联系Class $\mathcal{S}$ 理论与最大爆破空间（maximal implosion spaces）的三维实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将库仑分支全局对称性的规范操作转化为直观、可计算的组合结构（箭图）操作，弥补现有技术在处理特定非阿贝尔对称性（如SU(n)×SU(n′)）时的系统性不足。

Method: 基于IIB型弦论brane构造，引入商箭图减法；通过两步过程实现对称性规范：先识别涌现的U(n)×U(n′)或SU(n)×SU(n′)库仑分支全局对称性，再经symplectisation将相应幺正规范节点替换为辛群节点，从而完成箭图变换。

Result: 建立了适用于U(n)×U(n′)和SU(n)×SU(n′)对称性的系统性gauging方案；给出了多个具体箭图示例验证其一致性；揭示了该操作与Class $\mathcal{S}$ 理论及三维最大爆破空间实现之间的深层联系。

Conclusion: 商箭图减法是一种强大而普适的工具，能将抽象的对称性规范问题转化为具象的箭图组合操作，拓展了3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ 理论的构造能力与几何理解。

Abstract: Quotient quiver subtraction translates the gauging of Coulomb branch global symmetries into a combinatoric operation on 3d $\mathcal{N}=4$ gauge theories. This work uses brane systems to introduce a systematic method to gauge $\textrm{U}(n)\times\textrm{U}(n')$ and $\textrm{SU}(n)\times\textrm{SU}(n')$ emergent Coulomb branch global symmetries. Similar to other recently-introduced techniques, the gauging operation is a two-step process; in this case, it involves the symplectisation of unitary gauge factors into symplectic groups. A host of explicit examples are used to verify the procedure, and connections are made to Class $\mathcal{S}$ theories and three-dimensional theories realising maximal implosion spaces.

</details>


### [122] [Covering Surfaces, Conical-Defect Continuation, and Multi-Entropy at Arbitrary Genus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38208)
*Chen-Te Ma*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了任意亏格下置换扭相关函数的归一化几何因子，利用双曲均匀化揭示了锥形缺陷指数实部与覆盖空间反常因子的关系，并指出其虚部对应欧拉示性数的Weyl相位；进一步推广至更多分支点、给出高副本指数修正，并阐明全息极限所需的额外条件；并通过一个可约共形/拓扑例子说明大中心荷本身不足以完全确定纠缠结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解任意亏格下置换扭相关函数的几何结构及其在全息纠缠熵中的作用，特别是覆盖空间反常与锥形缺陷指数之间的关系，以及大中心荷极限下纠缠熵的完备性条件。

Method: 采用超几何均匀化方法处理三个均匀分支点情形，分析连续锥形缺陷指数的实部与虚部；将归一化推广至更多分支值，计算高副本指数对几何多熵的修正；并结合共形/拓扑可约模型检验全息极限条件。

Conclusion: 几何因子的完整归一化需同时考虑实部（反常）与虚部（拓扑相位），且全息极限要求超出大中心荷的额外约束；纠缠结构具有深层共形与拓扑耦合特征。

Abstract: We derive normalized geometric factors for permutation-twist correlators at arbitrary genus. For three uniformly ramified branch values, hypergeometric uniformization shows that the real part of the continued conical-defect exponent equals the covering-space anomaly factor. Its imaginary part is an Euler-characteristic Weyl phase. We extend the normalization to additional branch values, derive higher replica-index corrections to the geometric multi-entropy, and state the extra condition required for a holographic limit. A reducible conformal/topological example demonstrates why large central charge alone does not fix the complete entanglement result.

</details>

Motivation: 寻找在熵上优于对称相的带标量毛发的AdS黑洞解，并理解其热力学稳定性与UV性质的关系。

Method: 构造含二次标量–高斯–博内耦合和二次标量势的引力模型，并借助神经网络辅助搜索可行参数空间。

Result: 获得中性AdS₅黑膜解，具有源自由标量毛发、Δ=3的UV维数，且热力学上被熵所优选；该解可完全背反求解。

Conclusion: 标量–高斯–博内耦合可在不改变UV算符维数的前提下诱导对称性破缺与熵驱动的相变，神经网络是探索复杂引力模型的有效工具。

Abstract: We construct neutral AdS$_5$ black branes with source-free scalar hair that is entropically favored over the symmetric phase. A quadratic scalar--Gauss--Bonnet coupling destabilizes the thermal state while leaving the ultraviolet operator dimension fixed at $Δ=3$. The fully backreacted solutions require only a quadratic scalar potential. We describe how a neural-network search led to this simple model.

</details>


### [124] [Circuit complexity of coupled four-mode squeezed states in two-field inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38254)
*Tao Li,Hai-Bing Fu,Lei-Hua Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了双场暴胀中耦合曲率和熵扰动的电路复杂度，通过推导哈密顿量、求解四模压缩态的对振幅，并计算主压缩强度来确定Nielsen电路复杂度；结果表明复杂度在穿越视界后增长，而场间耦合对复杂度影响较小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解双场暴胀中量子涨落的电路复杂度演化，特别是耦合效应对复杂度的影响，为更一般的多场暴胀模型提供基准。

Method: 从二次作用量出发推导完整哈密顿量（含所有对角与非对角二次项），求解四模压缩态的三个耦合对振幅方程，据此计算两个主压缩强度，并代入等权高斯电路公式C₂ₖ=√[2(r₁ₖ²+r₂ₖ²)]得到Nielsen电路复杂度。

Result: 数值结果表明：电路复杂度在视界穿越后增长；场间耦合会改变对振幅和四模压缩态，但对所选等权电路复杂度的影响很小；不同对振幅矩阵可导致相近的复杂度值。

Conclusion: 所计算的高斯电路复杂度对耦合效应不敏感，可作为后续研究更一般双场暴胀模型中高斯电路复杂度的参考基准。

Abstract: We study the circuit complexity of coupled curvature and entropy perturbations in two-field inflation. We start from the quadratic action and derive the full Hamiltonian. The Hamiltonian includes all diagonal and off-diagonal quadratic terms. We then obtain three coupled equations for the pair amplitudes of the four-mode squeezed state. From these amplitudes, we calculate the two principal squeezing strengths. We use them to determine the Nielsen circuit complexity. For an equal-weight Gaussian circuit, the complexity takes the form $\mathcal{C}_{2,k}=\sqrt{2(r_{1k}^{2}+r_{2k}^{2})}$. It depends on the squeezing strengths but not explicitly on the squeezing angles. Our numerical results show that the complexity grows after horizon crossing in both coupled and uncoupled models. Interfield coupling changes the pair amplitudes and the four-mode squeezed state. However, its effect on the circuit complexity is small for the parameters considered. This result shows that different four-mode pair-amplitude matrices can yield similar values of the chosen equal-weight circuit complexity. The present calculation provides a reference for studying Gaussian circuit complexity in more general two-field inflationary models.

</details>


### [125] [Tachyon and massless sectors of the bosonic string effective action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38364)
*Mohammad R. Garousi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了玻色弦有效作用量中非导数tachyon项的结构，通过T对偶性约束确定了六阶导数耦合项，并将耦合分为无质量场部分和tachyon部分；无质量部分耦合常数完全确定，tachyon部分有两个任意常数，且结果与S矩阵展开一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在玻色弦有效作用量中，非导数tachyon仅出现在势能中，而其他耦合涉及tachyon和无质量场的协变导数；为保持T对偶性并匹配S矩阵，需系统确定高阶导数耦合结构。

Method: 利用圆紧化下的T对偶性约束，在特定方案中构建最小耦合基底；结合Buscher规则及其高阶导数修正，通过场重定义分离质量谱，再比对球面级S矩阵元确定耦合常数。

Result: 得到了无质量场部分全部确定的六阶导数耦合；tachyon部分存在两个任意耦合常数的项；四tachyon耦合常数由四tachyon S矩阵六动量阶展开唯一确定；整体结果与两tachyon加两无质量态的S矩阵六动量阶展开精确一致。

Conclusion: T对偶性与S矩阵一致性共同决定了玻色弦有效作用量中六阶导数项的结构：无质量场部分完全固定，tachyon部分保留有限自由度，验证了该框架下低能有效理论的自洽性。

Abstract: Recently, it has been observed that in certain schemes of the bosonic string effective action, the non-derivative tachyon appears only in the tachyon potential, while all other couplings involve covariant derivatives of the tachyon and massless fields. Within these schemes, a minimal basis can be found whose coupling constants are constrained by T-duality upon circle reduction. Using this constraint, one obtains sets of couplings at each order of \(α'\) that are invariant under Buscher rules supplemented by higher-derivative corrections for all base-space fields except the tachyon. In this paper, we observe that consistency of these T-dual sets with S-matrix elements requires an appropriate field redefinition, such that each set becomes invariant under Buscher rules supplemented by higher-derivative corrections for all base-space fields except the metric, dilaton, and tachyon. This separates the couplings into a massless sector and a tachyon sector. We determine the six-derivative couplings in the massless sector, with all coupling constants fixed, and find two T-dual couplings in the tachyon sector with arbitrary coupling constants. We show that the leading-order action and the massless-sector couplings exactly reproduce the expansion of the sphere-level S-matrix element of two tachyons and two massless vertex operators at six-momentum order, and that the coupling constant of the four-tachyon coupling in the tachyon sector is fixed by the expansion of the four-tachyon S-matrix element at six-momentum order.

</details>


### [126] [The Topological String on the Quintic to Genus 83 and the Rank-Two D4-D2-D0 Indices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38473)
*Michael Hecht*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过求解全纯反常方程，结合模性假设与墙穿越方法，计算了高亏格（2–83）和高次数（≤44）的五次Gopakumar-Vafa不变量，并推导出部分PT/DT不变量及D4-D2-D0系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算高亏格、高次数的GV不变量，以深化对弦论中镜像对称与BPS态计数的理解，并填补现有计算在高阶情形下的空白。

Method: 利用全纯反常方程积分求解；引入秩一模性与秩二电荷共轭假设构建边界与剩余墙穿越方程组；辅以计算机辅助扩展墙穿越分析；结合低亏格输入与（条件性）秩二mock模性进行外推。

Result: 获得亏格2–83、次数≤44的GV不变量；确定36个极点秩二D4-D2-D0系数中的22个；完成次数≤32的完整GV、PT与DT数据表；给出亏格最高达216的部分墙穿越不变量；提出亏格84–85及剩余极点系数的条件性候选值。

Conclusion: 该方法显著拓展了高亏格GV不变量的可计算范围，验证并强化了模性与墙穿越结构在BPS计数中的核心作用，为后续构造完整mock模形式及理解高阶量子修正提供了关键数据支撑。

Abstract: We compute quintic Gopakumar-Vafa invariants for genera 2-83 and degrees up to 44 by integrating the holomorphic anomaly equations. Under rank-one modularity for the required Gieseker invariants and rank-two charge conjugation, a coupled system of boundary and surplus wall-crossing equations fixes the holomorphic ambiguities and 22 of the 36 polar rank-two D4-D2-D0 coefficients, without assuming any rank-two index. A computer-assisted extension of wall crossing supplies additional equations and the boundary datum needed to close the recursion through genus 83. With known low-genus input, the GV data are complete through degree 32 and yield PT and DT tables. Selected higher-genus wall-crossing invariants reach genus 216; 33 entries require additional hypotheses.
