Retrospectively selected extrapolations give conditional candidates at genera 84 and 85 and, with rank-two mock modularity, for the remaining polar coefficients.

</details>


### [127] [Resurgence and Modularity in the First Painlevé Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38584)
*Kohei Iwaki,Noam Tamarin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了第一类Painlevé方程的非线性Stokes映射与tau函数模变换之间的精确关系，通过分析WKB和拓扑递归框架下的等单性系统，明确了不同旋转角度下参数变换对应的模群生成元S和T作用，并利用Rogers五项恒等式验证了tau函数在绕无穷远点一周后的单值性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Painlevé方程解在复平面上绕无穷远点的全局解析行为，特别是非线性Stokes现象与模对称性的深层联系。

Method: 结合精确WKB分析、拓扑递归假设与等单性系统理论，追踪椭圆谱曲线同调基在自变量旋转下的变化，比较不同Borel方向上的Stokes乘子，并推导tau函数在模变换下的显式变换规律。

Result: 建立了三个非线性Stokes映射的复合等于模群ST生成元复合的恒等式；给出了8π/5旋转对应T作用、2π/5旋转对应S作用的显式参数变换；确定了tau函数变换中的乘性因子及归一化常数；并证实这些因子通过Rogers dilogarithm的五项恒等式相互抵消，保障单值性。

Conclusion: 第一类Painlevé方程的tau函数及其超越解在绕无穷远点一周后保持单值性，其内在对称性由模群SL(2,ℤ)精确刻画，且该结构可通过Stokes几何与椭圆曲线同调演化统一描述。

Abstract: For the first Painlevé equation, we relate three nonlinear Stokes maps associated with a full turn around infinity to modular transformations of the two-parameter tau function through the identity $\boldsymbol S_π\circ\boldsymbol S_0\circ \boldsymbol S_{-π}=\mathsf S\circ\mathsf T$. Here the subscripts label Borel directions, while $\mathsf S$ and $\mathsf T$ denote the modular generators. Working under the analytic assumptions of the topological recursion and exact WKB constructions, we compare Stokes multipliers of the associated isomonodromy system while tracking the homology basis of its elliptic spectral curve under rotations of the independent variable. For the sectors considered, we identify the known parameter transformation induced by an $8π/5$ rotation with the modular $T$-action and derive a new explicit parameter relation realizing the $S$-action for a $2π/5$ rotation. We also determine the multiplicative factors in the corresponding tau-function relations, including their normalization constants. Together, these results give an explicit realization of single-valuedness after one full turn around infinity for both the Painlevé transcendent and its tau function, with the tau-function prefactors cancelling by Abel's five-term identity for the Rogers dilogarithm.

</details>


### [128] [The Non-Relativistic Limit of Five-Dimensional Boson Stars and Scale Invariance Violation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38606)
*Eduard Kim,Oleg Kondrashov,Emin Nugaev,Yakov Shnir*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了(4+1)维广义相对论中球对称玻色子星的非相对论极限，发现其对应的牛顿理论具有精确的尺度不变性，从而产生具有固定U(1)荷和零结合能的临界构型；进一步分析了相对论与引力修正如何破坏该对称性，并通过与Wick-Cutkosky模型对比，量化了这些修正对能量-电荷关系和运动学稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高维玻色子星在非相对论极限下的尺度对称性及其破缺机制，揭示临界构型的物理起源。

Method: 分析(4+1)维爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登系统在非相对论极限下的行为，结合尺度对称性分析与Wick-Cutkosky模型对比，研究相对论和引力修正的影响。

Result: 发现非相对论极限下存在精确尺度不变性，导致临界玻色子星具有固定U(1)荷和零结合能；相对论和引力修正破坏该对称性，使解的简并被解除，并决定远离临界极限时玻色子星的行为。

Conclusion: 弱尺度对称性破缺是决定高维玻色子星结构与稳定性的关键因素，为理解其非相对论极限下的物理提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the non-relativistic limit of spherically symmetric boson stars in (4+1)-dimensional general relativity (GR). In this limit, the resulting Newtonian theory exhibits an exact scale invariance, leading to a critical boson star configuration with a fixed global U(1) charge and vanishing binding energy. We identify the origin of these properties in the restored scale symmetry and analyze how relativistic and gravitational corrections break this symmetry. By comparing the full (4+1)-dimensional Einstein-Klein-Gordon system with the Wick-Cutkosky model, we quantify the effects of these corrections on the energy-charge relation and the kinematic stability of the configurations. Our results demonstrate how weak violation of scale invariance lifts the degeneracy of the non-relativistic solutions and determines the behavior of boson stars away from the critical limit.

</details>


### [129] [Conformal Spectrum of (Super) Chiral Ising and XY Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38614)
*Oleg Antipin,Jahmall Bersini,Jacob Hafjall,Giulia Muco,Francesco Sannino*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过半经典方法在下一阶精度下确定了临界 Gross-Neveu-Yukawa 和 Nambu-Jona-Lasinio-Yukawa 模型中复合算符的标度维数谱，并揭示了其洛伦兹结构；在超对称预期出现的参数点上，得到了全谱的闭式表达式，为红外区涌现超共形动力学提供了进一步证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究临界 Gross-Neveu-Yukawa 和 NJL-Yukawa 模型中复合算符的标度维数谱，验证红外区是否涌现超对称性与超共形动力学。

Method: 采用半经典方法，在下一阶精度（next-to-leading order）下计算标度维数谱，并解析算符的洛伦兹结构；在特定参数下推导全谱的闭式表达式。

Result: 确定了两类模型在各自手性 Ising 和手性 XY 普适类中的标度维数谱；在超对称参数点获得闭式解；算符被组织成超共形多重态。

Conclusion: 结果支持在红外固定点处存在涌现的超共形对称性，强化了强耦合量子场论中对称性增强的可能性。

Abstract: We determine the spectrum of scaling dimensions of composite operators in the critical Gross-Neveu-Yukawa and Nambu-Jona-Lasinio-Yukawa models through a next-to-leading order semiclassical computation. Their infrared fixed points belong to the chiral Ising and chiral XY universality classes, respectively. At next-to-leading order, we resolve the Lorentz structure of the operator spectrum. For the parameter values at which supersymmetry is expected to emerge in the infrared, we obtain a closed-form expression encoding the full spectrum within the semiclassical methodology. The resulting organization of operators into superconformal multiplets provides further evidence for emergent superconformal dynamics.

</details>


### [130] [Bootstrapping Wilson lines in Critical Bosonic QED$_3$ Part 1: Large-$N$ endpoint correlators and CFT data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38663)
*Jun Maeda,Toshiki Onagi,Soichiro Shimamori*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了临界玻色子QED₃中带一般电荷q的共形Wilson线，结合四点图计算与端点交叉方法，得到了端点维度、缺陷g函数及最低非单态Wilson线原初算符的维度和OPE系数，并发现体谱与Wilson线谱在每个非单态味通道中至1/N阶一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解临界玻色子QED₃中带电荷q的共形Wilson线的缺陷物理及其与体理论谱的对应关系。

Method: 结合四点图计算与保持SU(N)味结构完整性的端点交叉方程，在大N极限下计算四端点及混合关联函数至1/N次领头阶。

Result: 获得端点维度、缺陷g函数、非单态Wilson线原初算符的维度与OPE系数；发现体谱与Wilson线谱在所有非单态味通道中至1/N阶一致，且在简并能级上对应OPE加权平均反常维度。

Conclusion: 共形Wilson线在大N下可精确刻画，其缺陷谱与体理论高自旋谱存在系统性对应，揭示了缺陷CFT中体-边界一致性在强耦合下的深层实现。

Abstract: We study the conformal Wilson line with general charge $q$ in critical bosonic QED$_3$ with $N$ complex scalar flavors by combining diagrammatic four-point calculations with endpoint crossing. We formulate the endpoint crossing equations with the full $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ flavor structure, keeping positivity manifest. For arbitrary positive integer charge $q$ held fixed at large $N$, we obtain closed-form expressions for four-endpoint correlators and mixed correlators involving two bulk Hubbard--Stratonovich fields through first subleading order in $1/N$. These results determine the endpoint dimension, the defect $g$-function, and the dimensions and endpoint OPE coefficients of the lowest non-singlet Wilson-line primaries. Moreover, combining the diagrammatic correlator with endpoint crossing, somewhat surprisingly, we find agreement between the bulk and Wilson-line spectra in every non-singlet flavor channel through order $1/N$. At degenerate levels, this agreement concerns the OPE-weighted averages of anomalous dimensions probed by the endpoint correlator. For unit charge, we independently reproduce these averages from the known bulk higher-spin spectrum.

</details>


### [131] [Topological interfaces between universes and generalized families of 2d QFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38680)
*Pinak Banerjee,Yuhan Gai,Alonso Perez-Lona,Daniel Robbins,Subham Roy,Eric Sharpe,with an appendix by Xingyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究具有拓扑点算子的二维量子场论，指出其可分解为多个“宇宙”，并利用多融合范畴描述其有限对称性；通过分类不同“宇宙”间的拓扑界面（I/II/III型），揭示对称性结构，并将结果与广义量子场论族建立对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解具有拓扑点算子的二维量子场论的分解结构及其对称性本质，尤其是非可逆对称性在多宇宙场景下的作用。

Method: 运用多融合范畴理论建模有限对称性，引入作用函子刻画平凡作用对称性与宇宙间拓扑界面；结合orbifold构造、2-群扩展及RG流构造不同类型的宇宙对；并与Brennan-Intriligator广义QFT族建立对应。

Result: 建立了三类宇宙对（Type I/II/III）的分类框架：I型由可逆界面连接，II型仅含非可逆界面，III型无界面连接；给出了orbifold和2-群扩展等具体构造，并提出与广义QFT族的物理对应。

Conclusion: 多融合范畴是描述分解型二维QFT对称性的自然语言；宇宙间的拓扑界面类型编码了对称性结构；该框架统一解释了多种构造并拓展了广义QFT族的物理内涵。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quantum field theories with topological point operators decompose into universes. Just as fusion categories have played an important role in understanding non-invertible symmetries, multi-fusion categories play an analogous role for the symmetries of such decomposing theories. We describe a finite symmetry as a multi-fusion category together with its action functor, which keeps track of trivially acting symmetries and of the topological interfaces between universes. Relative to a chosen symmetry, we classify pairs of universes by the topological interfaces it contains, which reflect the structure of the multi-fusion category: universes in different indecomposable summands are joined by no interface (Type~III), while within a summand they are related by invertible (Type~I) or only non-invertible (Type~II) interfaces. For orbifolds with a trivially acting abelian normal subgroup, we derive the full inherited symmetry, which characterizes the operators between universes found case by case by Robbins, Sharpe and Vandermeulen and yields pairs of Types~I and~II but never of Type~III. We construct Type~III pairs instead from extensions of $2$-group type and from renormalization group flows out of a common ultraviolet theory. Finally, we propose a correspondence with the generalized families of quantum field theories of Brennan and Intriligator, in which equivariant families correspond to Type~I pairs and categorical ones to Type~II pairs whose gauging preserves the symmetry, family anomalies appear as universes carrying different symmetry-protected topological phases, and Type~III pairs can realize families over a discrete parameter. An appendix by Xingyang Yu works out the higher-charge Schwinger model as a Lagrangian example.

</details>


### [132] [Unifying energy-entropy bounds in semiclassical gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38713)
*Jackson R. Fliss,Andrew Rolph*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新的引力能量-熵条件（GEECs），统一并强化了量子聚焦猜想（QFC）和抹平零能量条件（SNEC），且在经典极限下退化为零能量条件（NEC）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QFC和SNEC虽各自强大，但互不蕴含，且均不退化为经典NEC，亟需更统一、更强的约束框架。

Method: 定义GEEC新框架，将其施加于广泛应力张量界，并结合有效场论思想与测试案例约束参数空间。

Result: 构造出一族显式GEEC，能同时推出QFC和SNEC，并在经典极限下还原为NEC。

Conclusion: GEEC是半经典引力中能量与熵约束的有力候选者，有望成为该领域更基础的普适原理。

Abstract: We revisit bounds on energy and entropy in semiclassical gravity, aiming to strengthen and unify established bounds, in particular the quantum focussing conjecture (QFC) and the smeared null energy condition (SNEC). Both the QFC and SNEC have important applications and are the strongest in their respective hierarchies; however, neither implies the other, nor reduces to the null energy condition (NEC) in the classical limit. To that end, we define a new class of bounds dubbed gravitational energy-entropy conditions (GEECs) that imply both the QFC and SNEC, and reduce to the NEC classically. We construct an explicit family of GEECs by imposing the defining criteria on a broad set of stress tensor bounds, and constrain its remaining parameter space through test cases, in an effective field theory-inspired approach. Our GEECs are powerful candidate bounds on energy and entropy in semiclassical gravity.

</details>


### [133] [Collective Bootstrap for Matrix Quantum Mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38716)
*Xianlong Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于环路空间的集体自举框架，用于求解大N矩阵量子力学的基态能量，通过半定规划给出下界，并在多个模型中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决大N矩阵量子力学中基态能量计算的困难，避免将含动量的矩作为独立变量处理，并提高截断精度和适用性。

Method: 构建环路空间中的集体自举框架，利用环路拼接与分裂操作组织动能约束，结合位力定理、Schur补和二次环路乘积松弛，将非线性约束优化转化为半定规划；再通过序列半定优化启发式方法搜索满足平面因子化的构型。

Result: 在单、双、三矩阵模型中，所得基态能量和环路期望值与解析解及主场优化结果高度一致；框架支持更高词长截断，适用于更多矩阵模型（如BFSS和BMN模型的玻色子部分）。

Conclusion: 该框架为大N矩阵量子力学提供了一种系统、高效且可扩展的数值求解方法，尤其适合难以解析处理的复杂模型。

Abstract: We develop a collective bootstrap framework for large $N$ matrix quantum mechanics formulated directly in the loop space of single trace coordinate moments. Loop joining and splitting operations organize the kinetic constraints systematically and avoid treating momentum containing moments as independent variables. Employing the virial theorem, a Schur complement, and a relaxation of quadratic products of loops, we recast the nonlinear constrained optimization of the collective energy into a semidefinite program, which yields a lower bound on the planar ground state energy at every truncation. A sequential semidefinite optimization heuristic then searches for configurations satisfying planar factorization and estimates the ground state energy and loop expectation values. Applications to one-, two-, and three-matrix models exhibit close agreement with analytic solutions and master field optimization. By working exclusively with coordinate moments, the framework permits truncations at higher word length and offers a promising approach to models with more matrices, including the bosonic sectors of the BFSS and BMN models.

</details>


### [134] [Elliptic master integrals and the one-loop cosmological wavefunction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38725)
*Amara McCune,Matthew D. Schwartz*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过自举方法精确计算了共形耦合标量单圈三角图的宇宙学波函数系数，发现其并非椭圆函数，而是由多重对数函数（dilogarithms）构成；椭圆周期在计算中意外相消，原因在于环动量积分被积函数在椭圆部分的留数对应于平直空间振幅（有理函数）。相比之下，单圈四边形图即使每顶点仅一个外腿也不具多对数性质，其阈值不连续性涉及椭圆积分，并在正四面体动量构型下引入K3曲面的周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于宇宙学波函数的圈修正与散射振幅所需积分类似，而共形耦合标量单圈三角图的主积分是椭圆的，人们预期其波函数系数也应为椭圆函数，但该文旨在检验这一预期是否成立。

Method: 采用自举（bootstrap）方法精确计算单圈三角图波函数系数，并分析其解析结构；同时研究单圈四边形图的阈值不连续性及积分几何性质（如K3曲面周期）。

Result: 单圈三角图波函数系数精确表达为多重对数函数（dilogarithms），椭圆周期完全抵消；而单圈四边形图不具多对数性质，其阈值不连续性含椭圆积分，且在正四面体动量下其被积函数涉及K3曲面周期。

Conclusion: 宇宙学波函数的解析结构比预期更简单（三角图退化为多对数），但也更丰富（四边形图引入K3几何），揭示了宇宙学关联函数与高维代数几何之间的深层联系。

Abstract: Loop corrections to the cosmological wavefunction involve similar integrals to those required for scattering amplitudes. For the one-loop triangle of a conformally coupled scalar, the master integrals are elliptic, and so the triangle wavefunction coefficient was expected to be elliptic as well. Using a bootstrap approach, we compute this triangle coefficient exactly and show that it is not: the elliptic periods cancel, and the flat-space wavefunction coefficient is a combination of dilogarithms. The cancellation occurs because the residue of the loop integrand on the elliptic sector is a flat-space amplitude, rational in the loop momentum. The one-loop box, by contrast, is not polylogarithmic, even with a single external leg at each site. Its threshold discontinuity involves elliptic integrals, which we also give in closed form. For regular-tetrahedron momenta the periods of a K3 surface enter its loop integrand.

</details>


### [135] [From pq-webs to magnetic quivers and back I: dictionary and deformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38769)
*Sebastiano Garavaglia,Sara Pasquetti,Palash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了五维N=1理论与三维N=4磁单极子夸克之间的精确参数字典，将五维味质量与瞬子耦合映射为磁单极子夸克的Fayet-Iliopoulos参数，并通过超共形指数和球面配分函数验证了该字典的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立五维N=1理论与三维N=4磁单极子夸克之间的定量联系，以便利用三维可观测量精确研究五维理论在各种变形下的行为。

Method: 提出并验证一个精确的参数字典，将五维理论中的 flavour 质量和瞬子耦合映射到三维磁单极子夸克的 FI 参数；结合超共形指数、squashed-sphere 配分函数及 Higgsing 分析方法进行检验。

Result: 成功构建并验证了五维与三维参数间的精确对应关系；展示了如何用三维可观测量（如配分函数）重构五维物理并遍历完整变形网络。

Conclusion: 磁单极子夸克不仅是描述五维理论的有用工具，更可作为定量研究其变形响应的可靠框架；参数字典的建立使得双向物理信息提取成为可能。

Abstract: This is the first of two companion papers in which we study five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories engineered by $(p,q)$ five-brane webs and their associated three-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=4$ magnetic quivers. We focus on the $N+\mathrm{SU}(N)_0^{k-1}+N$ box quiver theories. We propose an exact parameter dictionary relating the five-dimensional flavour masses and instanton couplings to the Fayet-Iliopoulos parameters of the corresponding strong-coupling magnetic quiver.
  We test this dictionary through a broad class of deformations, including mass deformations, weak-coupling limits, and vacuum expectation values. On the five-dimensional side, we implement these flows directly at the level of the superconformal index. The dictionary fixes the corresponding FI deformations and monopole VEVs. We analyse them in the magnetic-quiver squashed-sphere partition function using a new method that tracks Higgsing, decoupled sectors, and infrared theories. Our results promote magnetic quivers to tools for the quantitative study of how the five-dimensional superconformal index responds to deformations. Once the parameter dictionary is known, exact three-dimensional observables can be used to reconstruct the corresponding five-dimensional physics and to navigate the full deformation network in both directions.

</details>


### [136] [From pq-webs to magnetic quivers and back II: distributional indices and 5d theories breaking bad](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38770)
*Sebastiano Garavaglia,Sara Pasquetti,Palash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究五维N=1理论及其三维磁单极子夸克，探讨了当Higgs机制过度时五维超共形指数的分布特性，并建立了其与三维'坏'和'破缺'理论的类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于前一篇论文提出的N+SU(N)_0^{k-1}+N理论与其磁单极子夸克在无限耦合下的精确参数字典，本文旨在研究nilpotent moment-map VEV形变及其三维对应物。

Method: 利用五维(p,q)五膜网及其关联的三维磁单极子夸克框架，结合Hanany-Witten移动和广义toric图的镶嵌，分析Higgs级联及不同框架间的等价性。

Result: 发现当Higgs化过度时，五维超共形指数呈现分布性，这与三维中'坏'和'破缺'理论的特征一致；并给出了五膜网对这些结果的物理解释。

Conclusion: 五维理论中的过度Higgs化导致超共形指数的分布行为，揭示了五维与三维理论间深层的结构对应关系，特别是通过广义toric图的镶嵌和Hanany-Witten移动体现。

Abstract: This is the second of two companion papers in which we study five-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories engineered by $(p,q)$ five-brane webs and their associated three-dimensional magnetic quivers. Leveraging the exact parameter dictionary between the $N+\mathrm{SU}(N)_0^{k-1}+N$ theories and their magnetic quivers at infinite coupling, proposed in the first paper, we investigate nilpotent moment-map VEV deformations and their three-dimensional counterparts. We show that, when the Higgsing is excessive, the five-dimensional superconformal index becomes distributional, mirroring a characteristic feature of bad and broken theories in three dimensions. We also develop a five-brane web interpretation of these results, identifying the Hanany-Witten moves associated with the Higgsing cascade and relating inequivalent frames to distinct tessellations of the generalised toric diagram.

</details>


### [137] [Holography and localization in fractional field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38806)
*Gianluca Calcagni,Iván Salvador-García*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种将分数场论局部化的方法：一是通过推广Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓定理，构建全息对偶的高维局域理论；二是提出‘谱局部化’方法，引入质量连续谱以保持局域性，并兼容fakeon机制与经典化程序，从而为分数量子引力提供更易处理的框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数场论虽有望用于引力相互作用的量子化，但其非局域算符导致理论处理困难，亟需发展有效的局域化方法。

Method: 1）推广Caffarelli-Silvestre延拓定理至含多尺度动能项与非局域相互作用的自伴分数场论（以标量场为例），建立边界非局域场与高维体空间中两个局域标量场的全息对偶；2）提出‘谱局部化’新方案，不增加维度，而是引入质量连续谱实现局域化，并兼容fakeon和经典化程序。

Result: 成功构建了两种局域化方案：第一种给出明确的全息对偶结构，但难以纳入局域非线性势；第二种谱局部化方案自然容纳fakeon机制，适用于分数量子引力，并便于与二次量子引力比较分析。

Conclusion: 谱局部化是一种更灵活、更具物理适用性的局域化策略，为分数量子引力的可重整性、因果性及经典极限研究提供了新路径。

Abstract: Fractional field theory is a promising framework wherein to quantize the gravitational interaction but the presence of nonlocal operators can make its handling difficult. With the purpose of easing this problem, we recast the theory as a local dual in two ways. First, we generalize the Caffarelli--Silvestre extension theorem to self-adjoint fractional field theories with multi-scale kinetic terms and nonlocal interactions, taking the example of a scalar field. The latter is reinterpreted as a nonlocal boundary degree of freedom holographically dual to a local theory of two scalars living in a higher-dimensional bulk. However, inclusion of a local non-linear potential is not straightforward. We therefore propose an alternative picture where the nonlocal theory is not extended to a higher-dimensional space but is localized at the price of introducing a continuum of masses. This \emph{spectral localization} allows for a natural implementation of the fakeon prescription and the related classicization procedure, already employed in local theories with higher-derivative and complex ghosts. Applications to fractional quantum gravity and its comparison with quadratic quantum gravity are discussed.

</details>


### [138] [Gravitational Reconstruction in $AdS_{4}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38887)
*David A. Lowe,Yiru Wang,Juanyi Yang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在AdS4时空背景下，从边界上守恒且无迹的应力张量出发，重构线性化的引力扰动，并推广了先前的实洛伦兹构造；通过边界光锥截面积分、边界改进恢复Fefferman-Graham规范，并利用位置空间广义函数处理一般类空动量数据，最终为Bondi-Metzner-Sachs对称性和天球全息提供了平坦空间极限的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展先前在实洛伦兹情形下的AdS4引力扰动重构方法，建立更普适的、适用于任意类空边界动量（含非零及零频率）的线性化引力重建框架，并探索其向平直空间的可控极限，以支撑BMS对称性与天球全息的研究。

Method: 基于边界共形平坦度规上的守恒无迹应力张量，采用光锥截面积分法处理无类空傅里叶支撑的数据；通过边界改进项恢复Fefferman-Graham规范；对一般类空动量情形，引入位置空间显式广义函数作用于实应力数据，构造边界核并分析收敛条件。

Result: 实现了AdS4中各类线性引力扰动（含零频极限）的完整边界重构；明确了边界核的构造方式与收敛充分条件；证明了边界改进可同时保持规范条件（FG）与物理可归一化性；导出了支持BMS和天球全息的可控平坦空间极限。

Conclusion: 边界应力张量足以完全编码AdS4中的线性引力自由度，且该编码具有良好的解析结构与极限行为，为AdS/CFT中引力动力学的边界描述及渐近对称性研究提供了严格而普适的工具。

Abstract: We reconstruct linearized gravitational perturbations in $AdS_{4}$ from a conserved, traceless stress tensor on a fixed conformally flat boundary metric, extending our earlier real Lorentzian construction. For admissible data with no spacelike Fourier support in a flat conformal frame, the physical response reduces to an integral over boundary light-cone cuts. Boundary improvements restore Fefferman--Graham (FG) gauge while preserving normalizability and the linearized Weyl tensor. We then reconstruct perturbations with general spacelike boundary momentum, including nonzero frequency and its zero-frequency limit, using explicit position-space generalized functions on real stress data. Boundary kernels are constructed for normalizable perturbations and sufficient conditions are derived for the reconstruction to converge. The results motivate controlled flat-space limits relevant to Bondi--Metzner--Sachs symmetries and celestial holography.

</details>


### [139] [Generalized Symmetries of QED$_4$ on Non-Spin Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38907)
*Michele Del Zotto,Vivek Saxena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了非自旋四维流形上无质量QED的广义对称性，揭示了磁1-形式对称性在引力背景下的扭曲特性及其与自旋^c结构求和相关的混合反常，并讨论了其在不同相和手征缺陷中的物理后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义对称性对时空切向结构敏感；在非自旋四维定向流形上，光子成为spin^c联络，路径积分需对所有spin^c结构求和，这引发对磁对称性如何受引力影响的新理解需求。

Method: 基于spin^c几何、广义对称性理论、反常分类（如约化定向配边）、指标定理及拓扑场论（如spin^c Hall TQFT与WRT理论）进行分析，并以复射影平面为具体示例验证。

Result: 发现磁1-形式对称性被w2扭曲；存在二阶磁-引力混合反常，仅在spin^c结构求和时显现；chiral缺陷具有引力与电磁响应；spin^c Hall TQFT可抵消电磁反常但残留framing反常，需中性WRT层补偿；该framing问题亦存在于自旋流形上的QED。

Conclusion: 无质量QED在非自旋四维流形上的对称性结构深刻依赖于spin^c几何与引力背景，其反常行为统一由spin^c结构求和与拓扑配边分类所刻画，对理解量子场论在弯曲时空中的基本性质具有重要意义。

Abstract: Generalized symmetries are sensitive to the tangential structure of spacetime. We study this interplay for massless QED on oriented non-spin four-manifolds, where the photon becomes a spin$^c$ connection and the path integral sums over spin$^c$ structures. The shifted flux quantization extends the magnetic 1-form symmetry into a $w_2$-twisted 1-form symmetry: its $2π$ rotation acts as a gravitational sign, fixed by the normal bundle of the surface the operator wraps. The obstruction to treating it as an ordinary magnetic 1-form symmetry is an order-two mixed magnetic-gravitational anomaly, which is classified by reduced oriented bordism and detected on the Wu manifold. In a fixed spin$^c$ sector, this anomaly is removable by a counterterm, so it is a property of the sum over spin$^c$ structures. We discuss its consequences for gapped, Coulomb, and Higgs phases. The chiral anomaly is governed by the index of the spin$^c$ Dirac operator, so rational chiral defects carry gravitational as well as electromagnetic responses. A spin$^c$ Hall topological field theory cancels the electromagnetic part but leaves a framing anomaly. At the level of anomaly inflow, this is canceled for every rational angle by stacking a neutral, generally noninvertible Witten-Reshetikhin-Turaev layer. The same framing subtlety affects the chiral defects of QED on curved spin manifolds. The complex projective plane illustrates each of these features explicitly.

</details>


### [140] [Are Black Hole and Cosmological Horizon Thermodynamics Necessarily Inequivalent?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39048)
*Zachary J. Hoelscher*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了宇宙学视界热力学与黑洞热力学在量子引力理论中的等价性问题，通过Nariai极限下的带正宇宙常数的黑洞模型，发现二者在半经典近似下局部物理难以区分，暗示其熵可能具有共同的微观起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有观点认为，在一致且紫外完备的量子引力理论中，宇宙学视界与黑洞热力学通常不等价，本文旨在寻找反例以检验该猜想，并受弦论中黑洞具有良好性质而宇宙学视界缺乏类似特性的启发。

Method: 采用含单个非旋转黑洞和正宇宙常数的宇宙作为玩具模型，在Nariai极限（黑洞质量达最大值）下，结合严格宇称不变的量子场论，于半经典框架中分析黑洞性质与宇宙学视界附近物理的可区分性。

Result: 在忽略量子涨落导致的Nariai精确平衡不稳定性的半经典近似下，观测者无法轻易区分黑洞视界与宇宙学视界附近的物理行为。

Conclusion: 该结果虽未证明二者在所有宇宙中普遍等价，但表明它们不可能完全无关；并提出：任何试图描述正宇宙常数下黑洞的量子引力理论，都应为该极限下宇宙学熵与黑洞熵提供相关的微观起源。

Abstract: It has recently been argued that cosmological horizon thermodynamics and black hole thermodynamics are generically inequivalent in any consistent, UV complete theory of quantum gravity, where it was suggested that this could be falsified by the identification of a counterexample. This conjecture was motivated in part by noting that cosmological horizons do not incorporate many of the convenient features found in the black holes described by string theory. In this work, we consider as a toy model a universe with a single non-rotating black hole and a positive cosmological constant. We work in the Nariai limit where the mass of the black hole is taken to its maximum possible value, and where we also assert that the quantum field theory living on this background is strictly parity invariant. In the semi-classical regime where we neglect instability of the exact Nariai equilibrium under quantum fluctuations, we find that the observer cannot readily distinguish the physics in the neighborhood of the black hole horizon from the physics in the neighborhood of the cosmological horizon. This does not prove that the two are generically equivalent in any universe, though it does suggest that they cannot be entirely unrelated in general. We propose that any consistent theory of quantum gravity that aims to describe black holes in a universe with a positive cosmological constant should provide a related microscopic origin for the leading semiclassical cosmological entropy and black hole entropy in the neighborhood of the particular limit considered here.

</details>


### [141] [Deformed Amplituhedra and Interpolating Anomalous Dimensions on the Coulomb Branch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39052)
*Francesco Calisto,Subramanya Hegde,Martín Lagares,Elia Mazzucchelli*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了N=4超杨-米尔斯理论库仑分支上平面四点散射振幅，推广了变形Amplituhedron构造至一般外动能学，并通过协正定性约束确定实几何与振幅分子结构；在特定三参数质量构型下，利用四维微分方程解析计算了一、二圈积分，验证了红外极限下的指数化行为，并揭示了‘行走反常维度’可由尖点与八边形反常维度全阶确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论在库仑分支（即存在质量尺度）下的散射振幅结构，特别是多尺度、多参数质量运动学下的解析性质与反常维度插值行为。

Method: 推广变形Amplituhedron到六参数嵌入空间Gram矩阵描述的通用外动能学；利用逆Gram矩阵的copositivity（协正定性）约束刻画实几何并限制二圈规范形式分子；针对三参数质量构型，在四维直接求解微分方程以获得含多重对数函数的解析积分结果。

Result: 确认二圈振幅分子仅由两个平面双盒图结构张成；获得一、二圈解析表达式；发现多尺度红外极限下振幅指数化；明确一、二圈插值系数；提出‘行走反常维度’可由尖点与八边形反常维度全阶唯一确定。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了多质量参数下N=4 SYM四点振幅的几何与解析框架，不仅验证了Amplituhedron在非零质量情形的适用性，还为理解反常维度的普适结构及全阶动力学提供了新线索。

Abstract: We study planar four-point scattering amplitudes on the Coulomb branch of $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory, focusing on multi-parameter families of massive kinematics. We extend the deformed Amplituhedron construction of \cite{Arkani-Hamed:2023epq,Flieger:2025ekn} to general external kinematics characterized by a six-parameter embedding-space Gram matrix. We show that copositivity constraints on the inverse Gram matrix control the real geometry and restrict the numerator of the two-loop canonical form to the span of the two planar double-box structures expected for the scattering amplitude. We then specialize to a three-parameter massive configuration and evaluate the relevant one- and two-loop integrals using differential equations directly in four dimensions, obtaining analytic expressions in terms of multiple polylogarithms. We investigate multiscale infrared limits of the proposed two-loop amplitude and find exponentiation through two loops. The kinematic family contains the limits governed by the cusp and octagon anomalous dimensions and interpolates between them through the walking anomalous dimension introduced in~\cite{Alday:2026cpk}. Our result reproduces the one-loop interpolation and fixes the two-loop coefficients in their proposed all-loop ansatz. Interestingly, our results suggest that the walking anomalous dimension may be entirely determined through all loop orders by the cusp and octagon anomalous dimensions.

</details>


### [142] [Regularized Master-Field Approximation for Mass-Deformed Supersymmetric Yang-Mills Matrix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39101)
*Yue Lei,Reishi Maeta*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文应用正则化主场近似（RMA）方法研究了含三、四、十个矩阵的质量变形超对称杨-米尔斯型矩阵模型，验证了RMA能定量复现模糊球解及其相变，并揭示狄拉克算子零模在某些情况下会导致模糊球构型失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展高效计算方法以处理大-N 矩阵模型，并检验其在超对称矩阵模型中对经典模糊球解及其量子稳定性刻画的能力。

Method: 采用正则化主场近似（RMA）方法，应用于具有三、四、十个矩阵的质量变形超对称杨-米尔斯型矩阵模型。

Result: RMA成功定量复现了各模型中的模糊球经典解及其相变行为；发现狄拉克算子零模可在特定参数下导致模糊球构型失稳。

Conclusion: RMA是一种适用于多矩阵、复杂权函数的大-N 矩阵模型的有效数值工具，能可靠捕捉经典解与量子不稳定性机制。

Abstract: The regularized master-field approximation (RMA) is an efficient computational method for large-$N$ reduced matrix models, including those with multiple matrices or complex integration weights. In this work, we apply the RMA to mass-deformed supersymmetric Yang--Mills-type matrix models with three, four, and ten matrices. All three models admit fuzzy-sphere configurations as classical solutions. We show that the RMA quantitatively reproduces these fuzzy-sphere solutions and their breakdown, and that zero modes of the Dirac operator can destabilize fuzzy-sphere configurations in certain cases.

</details>


### [143] [virasoro_blocks: Software for the 2d bootstrap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39106)
*David Poland*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍了virasoro_blocks软件，用于高效、高精度地计算Virasoro共形块（尤其适用于数值bootstrap），通过最优插值减少Kac极点数量，并支持任意精度、奇异中心荷（如最小模型、c=1）及多种扩展（如全局块、N=1超共形块、环面特征标等）；并用其获得标量能隙与平均交换维数的紧致bootstrap界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Zamolodchikov递归给出的Virasoro块虽具解析形式，但含大量Kac极点，严重阻碍其在半定规划（SDP）类数值bootstrap中的应用；需开发更鲁棒、高精度、广适配的计算工具。

Method: 基于Zamolodchikov递归，在椭圆nome展开下提取正前因子与关于交换权的有理函数；采用最优插值技术显著减少极点数量以提升SDP稳定性；支持任意精度运算，可处理奇异中心荷（如c=1及最小模型）；并扩展至全局块、N=1超共形块、环面字符与环面单点块。

Result: 实现了开源软件virasoro_blocks；在仅假设标量Virasoro原初算符条件下，得到趋近Liouville理论预测区间[(c−1)/16, (c−1)/12]的标量能隙上界；并计算了多个中心荷下相同标量四点函数中平均交换维数的约束，其边界结构丰富，诸多已知二维CFT位于或邻近该边界。

Conclusion: virasoro_blocks为二维共形场论的数值bootstrap提供了高效、稳健且通用的新工具，显著拓展了可处理的理论范围（如临界模型、c=1），所获新约束揭示了二维CFT空间的深层结构。

Abstract: We introduce virasoro_blocks, software that computes Virasoro conformal blocks at any central charge in a form suitable for the numerical bootstrap. At each finite order in the elliptic nome, Zamolodchikov's recursion gives the block as a positive prefactor times a rational function of the exchanged weight. However, its many Kac poles make it difficult to use in semidefinite programming approaches to the bootstrap. In this software we use optimal interpolation to reduce the number of poles while keeping high accuracy. The software works in arbitrary precision and handles singular central charges, including the minimal models and $c=1$. It also computes global blocks, $N=1$ superconformal blocks, torus characters, and torus one-point blocks. We illustrate it with two applications. Assuming only scalar Virasoro primaries, we compute bounds on the scalar gap that approach the window of Liouville solutions between $(c-1)/16$ and $(c-1)/12$. We also bound the average exchanged dimension in four-point functions of identical scalars at several central charges. These bounds have a rich structure, and many known 2d CFTs lie on or near their boundary.

</details>


### [144] [Ortho-symplectic interface line operators in $\mathcal{N}=4$ SYM theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39207)
*Yasuyuki Hatsuda,Tadashi Okazaki*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了正交和辛规范群的4维N=4超对称杨-米尔斯理论中半BPS界面处的线算符，提出了NS5型和D5型界面下威尔逊线与涡旋线在S对偶作用下的对偶性，并通过线缺陷半指数匹配提供了强证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索4维N=4 SYM理论中不同规范群（正交/辛）下半BPS界面处线算符的分类与对偶结构，以深化对S对偶性和边界/界面物理的理解。

Method: 基于半BPS界面的分类（NS5型与D5型），分析线算符的电/磁荷标记方式，并利用线缺陷半指数（half-index）作为对偶性检验工具，比较对偶线算符的指标匹配。

Result: 明确了NS5型界面线算符由两侧规范群的电/磁荷对标记，D5型则由单侧保留规范群的电/磁荷标记；提出并验证了威尔逊线与涡旋线在S对偶下的对应关系，且其半指数完全匹配。

Conclusion: 证实了正交/辛规范群下N=4 SYM中界面线算符存在S对偶性，拓展了AdS/CFT与边界场论中对偶性的适用范围，并为更一般规范群的界面物理提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We study the line operators at the half-BPS interfaces in 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills (SYM) theories of orthogonal and symplectic gauge groups. For the NS5-type interfaces, line operators are labeled by pairs of electric or magnetic charges under the gauge groups on either side of the interface, whereas for the D5-type interfaces they are labeled by electric or magnetic charges under the single gauge group preserved by the interface. We propose dualities between the Wilson lines and vortex lines at the interfaces under the action of S-duality. As highly non-trivial evidence of the dualities on the junctions of the line operators and the interfaces, we find matching of the line defect half-indices for the dual pairs.

</details>


### [145] [Schwinger--Keldysh worldline effective field theory for quantum black holes: dissipation, noise and decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39243)
*Hajime Kobayashi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个用于量子黑洞的Schwinger-Keldysh世界线有效场论，统一处理耗散、涨落与退相干，并通过因果响应核与对称噪声核刻画约化动力学；其谱满足正定性约束和Kubo-Martin-Schwinger热对称性，噪声核经Hubbard-Stratonovich变换可视为驱动度规与世界线的高斯源；应用于后牛顿近似下的施瓦西双星系统，导出Planck压制的完整相对加速度噪声，并保持质心动量守恒；该噪声核亦给出支撑空间叠加态的四极矩退相干泛函，并复现Danielson-Satishchandran-Wald的视界诱导标度律；非平衡情形下响应与噪声仍为独立匹配参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述量子黑洞在实时演化中的耗散、量子涨落与退相干效应，需超越传统虚时方法，建立能自然包含开放量子系统特性的有效理论框架。

Method: 基于Schwinger-Keldysh闭合时间路径形式主义构建世界线有效场论；引入因果响应核与对称噪声核表征约化动力学；利用Hubbard-Stratonovich变换将噪声核转化为高斯随机源；通过吸收匹配（absorptive matching）连接到黑洞视界物理。

Result: 1) 推导出后牛顿近似下双黑洞系统的Planck压制、动量守恒的相对加速度噪声；2) 同一噪声核导出空间叠加态的四极矩退相干泛函，并复现DSW视界诱导退相干标度；3) 明确非平衡下响应与噪声为独立匹配数据；4) 提出向微观量子结构与空间延展粗粒化描述拓展的路径。

Conclusion: Schwinger-Keldysh世界线EFT为量子引力中开放系统动力学提供了自洽框架，将黑洞热力学、随机引力波辐射与量子退相干统一于单一实时间影响泛函中，且具备向更普适量子时空结构推广的潜力。

Abstract: We formulate a Schwinger--Keldysh worldline effective field theory for quantum black holes that treats dissipation, fluctuations, and decoherence within one real-time influence functional. The reduced dynamics is encoded in causal response and symmetric noise kernels. Their spectra obey positivity constraints, while thermal Kubo--Martin--Schwinger symmetry relates them through the fluctuation--dissipation relation. A Hubbard--Stratonovich representation turns the noise kernel into a Gaussian source driving the resolved metric and worldline. For a leading post-Newtonian Schwarzschild binary, direct and metric-mediated stochastic forces combine into the complete relative-acceleration noise at this order, preserve center-of-mass momentum balance, and remain Planck suppressed. The same noise kernel gives the quadrupolar decoherence functional for a supported spatial superposition and reproduces the Danielson--Satishchandran--Wald horizon-induced scaling after absorptive matching. Away from thermal equilibrium, response and noise remain independent matching data. We discuss extensions to microscopic quantum structure in the worldline kernels, matching to spatially extended coarse-grained descriptions, and gravitational splitting.

</details>


### [146] [Quantum field theory with scale-dependent weighted measures: nonlinear momentum representations, vacuum fluctuations, and causal structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39285)
*S. González-Salud,R. Cartas-Fuentevilla*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了通过动量空间加权测度构建自由量子场的方法，引入独立的能量尺度以在低动量下恢复常规理论；提出了一族由无量纲谱函数表征的模型，包含欧几里得Snyder代数对应的空间权重作为特例，并讨论其与修正色散关系、非线性动量坐标及协变谱权重的联系；对复Klein-Gordon场，导出了等时对易关系、守恒加权观测量、真空涨落和任意空间维度下的两点函数，明确了紫外有限性条件，并通过普通Pauli-Jordan分布的空间卷积分析了非局域因果结构；还给出了相互作用推广示例及核函数与振幅的数值图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决量子场论在紫外区的发散问题并探索普朗克尺度下的新物理，需构建能自然截断紫外行为且保持合理对称性和因果结构的自由场模型。

Method: 基于动量空间加权测度方法，引入独立能量尺度和无量纲谱函数构造自由场；采用协变谱权重（利用类时四速度定义）、分析修正色散关系与非线性动量坐标；严格推导复Klein-Gordon场的等时代数、守恒量、真空期望值及两点函数；通过空间卷积刻画非局域因果结构，并给出紫外有限性判据。

Result: 获得一族具紫外截断特性的自由量子场模型，其中Snyder代数情形为其特例；导出完整可观测量代数与守恒律；明确两点函数紫外有限的条件；揭示非局域因果结构源于Pauli-Jordan分布的空间卷积；提供相互作用推广形式及核与振幅的数值结果。

Conclusion: 动量空间加权测度方法可系统构建兼具紫外有限性、协变性与物理合理性的自由量子场模型，为研究量子引力效应下的场论提供了新框架。

Abstract: We reconsider the construction of free quantum fields through momentum-space weighted measures, introducing an independent energy scale that restores the conventional theory at low momenta. The resulting family is characterized by a dimensionless spectral function and includes, as a distinguished case, the spatial weight associated with the Euclidean Snyder algebra. We discuss its relation to modified dispersion relations and nonlinear momentum coordinates, and introduce an observer-covariant spectral weight defined using a timelike four-velocity. For the complex Klein-Gordon field we obtain the equal-time algebra, conserved weighted observables, vacuum fluctuations, and two-point functions in arbitrary spatial dimension. The ultraviolet conditions for their finiteness are established, and the nonlocal causal structure is analyzed in terms of a spatial convolution of the ordinary Pauli-Jordan distribution. An illustrative interacting extension and numerical profiles of the kernels and amplitudes are also presented.

</details>


### [147] [Does SO(2,2) symmetry lead to an additional U(1) symmetry in 5-dimensional Einstein-scalar theories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39400)
*Saeedeh Sadeghian*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过李对称方法研究了五维含标量场的爱因斯坦方程，构造了一个依赖θ和ψ坐标的解，从而反驳了Sadeghian等人关于SO(2,2)对称性下额外U(1)对称性的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Sadeghian等人在五维含标量场爱因斯坦理论中提出的SO(2,2)对称性是否必然导致额外U(1)对称性的猜想。

Method: 采用李对称方法求解含五维标量场的爱因斯坦方程。

Result: 找到了一个显式依赖θ和ψ坐标的解，作为反例，证伪了该猜想。

Conclusion: 在五维含标量场情形下，SO(2,2)对称性不足以保证额外U(1)对称性成立，原猜想不成立。

Abstract: Solving Einstein equations including the matter fields in dimensions higher than four is computationally challenging. However, the existence of symmetries facilitates these calculations. Among these symmetries, we can mention the SO(2,2) symmetry that arises in the near-horizon geometries of extremal black holes with vanishing horizon area in four dimensions \cite{EVH/CFT} and higher dimensions \cite{Sadeghian:2015}. Solutions of Einstein theories including scalar and Maxwell fields with SO(2,2) symmetry have been classified in four, five, and higher dimensions \cite{Sadeghian:2014}. For the Einstein-Maxwell-Lambda theory, a theorem states that SO(2,2) symmetry leads to an additional U(1) symmetry and therefore unique axisymmetric solutions. Nevertheless, in the presence of a scalar field, this symmetry group is not large enough in five dimensions to guarantee solution uniqueness, and the additional U(1) symmetry remains a conjecture in \cite{Sadeghian:2014}. Here, we study the Einstein equations of theories with a five-dimensional scalar field using the Lie symmetry method for solving partial differential equations. We challenge the conjecture in \cite{Sadeghian:2014} and find a solution that depends on both $θ$ and $ψ$ coordinates as a counterexample to that conjecture.

</details>


### [148] [Absorption of bound supernova axion-like particles by the neutron star weakens the heating limits on their photon coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39402)
*Probit J Kalita,Subha Mitra,Bharat Kumar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文重新评估了轴子类粒子（ALPs）在超新星中产生并束缚于中子星后对老中子星再加热的效应，指出先前研究忽略了ALP被中子星吸收的关键过程；作者发现吸收远快于衰变，仅极小比例（10^{-9}–10^{-5}）的ALP能存活并贡献加热，但仍可在PSR J2144−3933中排除特定质量-耦合窗口（m_a ≈ 7.7–260 MeV），该窗口受核子轫致辐射率、物态方程及π/Δ共振等多因素影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（Bose et al.）忽略了束缚ALP轨道穿越中子星时被强烈吸收的过程，高估了其衰变产热贡献，导致对ALP-光子耦合的限制过强；本文旨在修正这一遗漏，给出更准确的天体物理约束。

Method: 作者自行计算了原中子星演化、ALP产生率、广义相对论轨道动力学及逆核子轫致辐射吸收过程；系统考察了12种原中子星演化模型、3种归一化到相同超新星能量损失的吸收率、含/不含Δ(1232)共振的物态方程，并纳入π转换与Δ共振对产生和吸收的修正。

Result: ALP一旦轨道进入中子星，数分钟内即被完全吸收，远早于衰变；仅初始位于星外、近圆轨道的ALP可幸存，贡献加热不足10^{-4}；PSR J2144−3933仍可排除m_a ≈ 7.7–260 MeV的ALP参数窗口，其下边界g_{aγ}比无吸收情形高280–700倍；该窗口对吸收率和最终星半径敏感，且需ALP-光子直接耦合抵消π混合诱导耦合。

Conclusion: 吸收效应极大削弱ALP再加热机制，但未完全消除其可观测性；修正后的约束仍具显著物理意义，且可能已被现有超新星伽马射线观测所覆盖；未来需结合更精确的核物理输入与多信使观测进一步检验。

Abstract: Axion-like particles (ALPs) that couple to protons are produced in a core-collapse supernova, and the slow ones remain bound to the new neutron star. Bose, Ganguly, Kumar, and Raj [arXiv:2610.30255] proposed that the photons from the decay of this bound cloud reheat old neutron stars, and they derived strong limits on the ALP-photon coupling. They neglected the absorption of the ALPs by the star, but a bound ALP on an orbit that enters the star and reaches 50 km crosses it about $6\times 10^{18}$ times over the 333 Myr age of PSR J2144$-$3933. We show that inverse nucleon bremsstrahlung in the cold star absorbs every ALP whose orbit enters it within minutes, long before it decays, so only ALPs born outside the final star on nearly circular orbits survive; they carry $9\times 10^{-9} - 6\times 10^{-5}$ of the heating. We compute the protoneutron-star evolution, the production, the general-relativistic orbits and the absorption ourselves, for twelve protoneutron-star evolutions, three nucleon-bremsstrahlung rates normalized to the same supernova energy loss of light ALPs, and equations of state with and without $Δ(1232)$ isobars. PSR J2144$-$3933 still excludes a window at $m_a\simeq 7.7-196$\ MeV, with a lower edge in the photon coupling $280-700$ times higher than without absorption. Pion conversion and the $Δ(1232)$ resonance, which raise the production of surviving ALPs more than their absorption, widen this window to $m_a\simeq$ $7.7-260$\ MeV. The window depends at similar levels on the bremsstrahlung rate and on the radius of the final star. It requires a direct photon coupling that cancels the one induced by ALP-pion mixing, and existing supernova gamma-ray limits may already cover it.

</details>


### [149] [Carrollian CFTs and the Light Front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39450)
*Arjun Bagchi,Kinjal Banerjee,Sourish Banerjee,Meghaa AK*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过四维庞加莱代数与三维共形Carroll代数的同构性，建立了光前物理与Carroll共形场论（CFT）之间的联系，并构建了连接大质量体散射的新Carroll CFT表示，计算了两点函数，给出了三维Carroll CFT与四维渐近平坦时空中的光前理论之间的映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Carroll对称性在渐近平坦时空（AFS）的全息对偶中起关键作用，且自然出现在光前；但其与光前物理及大质量体散射的具体联系尚需厘清。

Method: 利用四维庞加莱代数与三维共形Carroll代数的同构性，结合光前理论的已有见解，构造新的Carroll CFT表示，并计算两点函数，建立3d Carroll CFT与4d光前理论之间的映射。

Result: 构建了连接大质量体散射的新Carroll CFT表示，计算出相应的两点函数，并确立了3d Carroll CFT与4d渐近平坦时空中的光前理论之间的明确对应关系。

Conclusion: Carroll对称性不仅是AFS全息的数学基础，亦可作为统一描述光前动力学与大质量散射的对称性框架；该工作为Carroll CFT提供了新的物理实现和应用路径。

Abstract: Carrollian symmetries play an important role in building holographic duals to asymptotically flat spacetimes (AFS). This construction hinges on the isomorphism between the (asymptotic) isometries of AFS and the conformal Carroll algebra. Carroll symmetries also naturally arise on the lightfront, as co-dimension one subalgebras in the Poincare algebra written in lightcone coordinates. In this paper, building on the isomorphism between four dimensional (4d) Poincare and 3d conformal Carroll algebras, we make a connection between physics on the lightfront and Carrollian CFTs. Using insights from the lightfront literature, we construct new representations of Carroll CFTs that connect to massive bulk scattering. We compute two-point functions and build a map between 3d Carroll CFTs and 4d lightfront theories living in AFS.

</details>


### [150] [Large-$N$ limit of chiral quiver theories: free energy, supersymmetric indices and operator counting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39569)
*Edoardo Colombo,Hyojoong Kim,Nakwoo Kim,Hoseob Shin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文找到了具有M理论对偶的三维N=2手性陈-西蒙斯-物质理论在大N极限下S3自由能鞍点方程的第一个解析解，验证了其自由能按N^{3/2}标度律，并与全息、算符计数等方法预言一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决手性N=2 Chern-Simons-matter理论大N极限下矩阵模型长程力未抵消导致的N^{3/2}标度律张力问题。

Method: 求解大N极限下S^3自由能的鞍点方程，结合全息对偶、超共形指数与拓扑扭曲指数分析，并辅以有限N数值验证。

Result: 获得首个解析解，确认自由能严格满足N^{3/2}标度；与AdS4×Q^{1,1,1}/Z_k全息预言完全匹配；推广至S^2×S^1上的超共形及拓扑扭曲指数；支持算符计数猜想。

Conclusion: 该解析解不仅解决了长期存在的标度律矛盾，还统一验证了多种对偶方法（全息、算符计数、数值模拟）在手性模型中的有效性，显著拓展了相关猜想的适用范围。

Abstract: We find the first analytic solution to the large-$N$ saddle point equations for the $S^3$ free energy of a chiral $\mathcal N=2$ Chern-Simons-matter theory with an M-theory dual. Focusing on the chiral quiver dual to AdS$_4\times Q^{1,1,1}/\mathbb Z_{\rm k}$, we show how the long-range forces of the matrix model cancel at large $N$, resolving a long-standing tension with the expected $N^{3/2}$ scaling. The resulting free energy precisely matches the holographic prediction for all assignments of trial R-charges. Our results extend to the superconformal and topologically twisted indices on $S^2\times S^1$, whose large-$N$ and Cardy limit is determined by the same saddle. The solution agrees with the predictions of the operator counting method, giving strong evidence for this conjecture beyond non-chiral quivers. We support the analytic solution with a finite-$N$ numerical analysis for multiple assignments of R-charges, extending recent numerical results obtained for this quiver.

</details>


### [151] [Superstring Field Theory with Double Background Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39594)
*Chris Hull*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的超弦场论，包含物理超弦和影子超弦两个解耦部分；并推广了该理论，使两部分可分别在不同共形场论背景下展开，且各自对背景的边缘形变保持不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决超弦场论中背景依赖性问题，并探索物理超弦与影子 sector 的对称结构及其独立背景展开的可能性。

Method: 基于共形场论构造超弦场论，引入对称作用量形式，并推广至两个独立背景下的边际形变不变性框架。

Result: 构建了具有物理与影子 sector 对称性的超弦场论；进一步推广得到两 sector 可分别在不同背景上展开、且各自对边际形变保持不变的新理论。

Conclusion: 该理论不仅揭示了超弦场论中潜在的双重结构，还提供了背景无关性更广义的实现方式，为弦论基础与全息原理研究提供新视角。

Abstract: A recently constructed superstring field theory has two decoupled sectors, the physical superstring and a shadow sector with a second copy of the superstring. These are both expanded around the same background conformal field theory and the theory is independent of marginal deformations of this common background. A form of the action is presented that is symmetric between the two sectors. A generalisation of this theory is given in which the two sectors can be expanded around two different backgrounds, and the theory is independent under separate marginal deformations of the two background conformal field theories.

</details>


### [152] [Majorana Neutrino Physics and Matter-Antimatter Asymmetry in Type IIA String Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39804)
*Yang Liu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在Type IIA弦理论框架下，利用欧几里得D2-瞬子机制统一解释中微子马约拉纳质量与重子不对称性，通过匹配实验数据验证了正常质量序的跷跷板主导机制，并给出无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将中微子质量起源（马约拉纳质量）与宇宙正反物质不对称（重子数产生）在弦理论中统一解释，并检验其是否符合量子引力约束（Swampland准则）。

Method: 基于$T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$型IIA弦论紧化模型，引入具Sp(2) Chan-Paton对称性的欧几里得D2-瞬子，同时生成跷跷板机制与Weinberg算符；通过实验数据（如中微子质量平方差）匹配确定风味向量和参数空间。

Result: 确认跷跷板主导、正常质量序为唯一符合观测的解；给出重子不对称参数η_B ≈ 8.6×10⁻¹¹（与观测值同量级）；预测无中微子双贝塔衰变有效质量⟨m_ββ⟩ = 2.594 meV；参数扫描表明质量等级可被容纳但非理论预言。

Conclusion: 该弦论构造提供了中微子质量与重子不对称性的自洽量子引力相容实现，构成存在性证明，但风味结构未从第一性原理导出，且质量等级未被预测。

Abstract: We study Majorana neutrino masses and the matter-antimatter asymmetry in Type IIA string theory on a $T^6/(\mathbb{Z}_2 \times \mathbb{Z}_2)$ orientifold with intersecting D6-branes. Euclidean D2-instantons with $\text{Sp}(2)$ Chan-Paton symmetry generate both the seesaw mechanism and the Weinberg operator. The flavour vectors are not computed from a compactification but fixed by matching, so our results are an existence proof. Matching the measured mass-squared differences selects the seesaw-dominated case with normal ordering. Swampland criteria constrain the instanton action and the reheating temperature. A benchmark example reproduces the neutrino parameters, gives $η_B \approx 8.6 \times 10^{-11}$, within an order of magnitude of observation, and predicts $\langle m_{ββ} \rangle =2.594 \text{meV}$. A parameter scan shows the mass hierarchy is accommodated rather than predicted.

</details>


### [153] [On functional classes in higher-spin theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39851)
*Yu. A. Tatarenko*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了d维Vasiliev理论中理想因子分解问题，通过精确描述高自旋代数A及其扭曲伴随模A^{tw}作为特定函数空间，提出了基于Moyal星积的自由级因子分解方案，并探讨了其与局域性问题的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决d维Vasiliev理论中理想因子分解所需的高自旋代数A及其扭曲伴随模A^{tw}的精确定义问题，并厘清其与理论局域性的关系。

Method: 利用Moyal星积结构，对高自旋代数A和其扭曲伴随模A^{tw}建模为特定函数空间，施加代数闭合性与模作用条件，并结合辅助约束推导出自由级因子化程序。

Result: 得到了具有简单代数形式的容许函数条件；确认了Soloviev先前考虑的A对应函数空间；发现某些非局域表达式因A^{tw}性质而发散，而自旋局域表达式则良定义。

Conclusion: 所建立的函数类为Vasiliev理论中自由级因子分解提供了严格数学基础，并揭示了其与局域性（特别是自旋局域性）之间的内在联系。

Abstract: The factorization over the ideal in the $d$-dimensional Vasiliev theory requires an accurate description of the higher-spin algebra $\mathcal{A}$ and its twisted adjoint module $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ as certain functional spaces. It is shown that the conditions of $\mathcal{A}$ to be closed under action of the Moyal star product, and of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ to be a star-product $\mathcal{A}$-module along with some auxiliary conditions lead to properly defined free-level factorization procedure. The conditions on admissible functions have simple algebraic form; in the case of $\mathcal{A}$ the analogous functional space has been considered by Soloviev \cite{Soloviev:2013iba}. The relation of the obtained functional classes to the locality problem in the Vasiliev theory is discussed. In particular it is observed that certain non-local expressions diverge due to properties of $\mathcal{A}^{tw}$ while the spin-local expressions are well-defined.

</details>


### [154] [Initial conditions leave IR imprints in quantum cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39965)
*Manishankar Ailiga,Akash Mondal,Gaurav Narain*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了洛伦兹符号下ℝ×S³几何的引力路径积分及S³上的度规涨落，引入一族由参数c和ω表征的协变边界作用量，分析其对宇宙波函数一阶量子修正的影响；发现ω需为半整数，对应多个c依赖的共轭动量分支，其中部分对应dS、Hartle-Hawking和隧穿宇宙初态；仅dS与HH鞍点具良好涨落行为，并利用c作为紫外调节器得到有限波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子宇宙学中边界条件对波函数及其量子修正（尤其一环）的影响，特别是如何通过协变边界项调控紫外发散与鞍点选择。

Method: 在ℝ×S³洛伦兹背景上构造含涨落的爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量路径积分；引入含参数c和ω的无限族协变边界作用量以约束尺度因子与涨落的边界行为；通过鞍点近似与一环计算，结合UV正则化（c起调节器作用）和反常抵消项，系统求解波函数。

Result: ω必须取半整数值；出现多个c依赖的初始共轭动量分支，对应不同宇宙初态（dS、HH、隧穿）；仅dS与HH鞍点具良定义涨落；c可调节一环行列式极点位置并吸收UV发散；加入适当反常项后获得UV有限的无边界与dS波函数；波函数在红外区既发散又敏感于初边条件。

Conclusion: 协变边界参数c不仅作为紫外调节器，还深刻影响量子宇宙初态的选择与物理可观测量的有限性；边界条件并非任意，其选择直接决定鞍点稳定性、量子修正的可计算性及红外行为。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational path integral of $\mathbb{R} \times S^3$ geometries in Lorentzian signature along with the metric fluctuations which live on the $S^3$ hypersurfaces. Each metric configuration is characterised by lapse, scale factor and fluctuations, and is weighted by the exponential of the Einstein-Hilbert action. A particular boundary requirement for the scale-factor is achievable via an infinite family of covariant boundary actions, each restricting the boundary behaviour of the fluctuation field which cannot be arbitrarily chosen. The paper explores the effect of this infinite family of covariant boundary actions on the wavefunction of Universe to one-loop. These boundary actions are characterized by parameters $c$ and $ω$. Dominant contribution of initial momentum in the path-integral restricts $ω$ to be half-integer, leading to an infinite family of $c$-dependent conjugate momenta. In special case for fixed $ω$, four such $c$-dependent dominant branches of conjugate momenta emerge: two lead to deSitter, one leads to Hartle-Hawking and one to the tunnelling Universe. For all these $c$-dependent initial conjugate momenta, the lapse-saddles are relevant, however only, one of deSitter saddle and Hartle Hawking saddle have well-behaved perturbations. The one-loop wavefunction at these well-behaved saddles is computed systematically by isolating the UV-divergences, where the covariant boundary parameter $c$ acts as a UV-regulator and also helps in shifting the poles of one-loop determinant, away from the real $q_f$ axis for dS saddles, where $q_f$ is the final size of the Universe. UV-finite wavefunction for no-boundary and dS is obtained by adding suitable counterterms. The infrared behaviour of these wavefunctions show not only IR-divergences but also sensitivity toward the initial boundary choices.

</details>


### [155] [The Effective Kerr-Compton Tree Amplitude from Black Hole Perturbation Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40101)
*N. Emil J. Bjerrum-Bohr,Gang Chen,Konstantinos Papadimos,Nabha Shah,Chenliang Su*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在后闵可夫斯基有效场论框架下构造克尔黑洞对引力波散射的树级康普顿振幅的新方法，利用黑洞微扰理论中的波散射函数确定接触项，并推导出最高至六阶自旋的显式表达式，验证了已知结果并重现了一圈尾项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算依赖于振幅的物理一致性，缺乏系统性确定高阶自旋接触项的方法；需将黑洞微扰理论与有效场论更紧密地结合以提升精度和普适性。

Method: 基于黑洞微扰理论中的波散射函数，迭代构造树级康普顿振幅，约束并唯一确定后闵可夫斯基有效场论中的高阶自旋接触项。

Result: 得到了最高至六阶自旋的显式振幅表达式，揭示了复超越结构，并首次发现六阶自旋处存在主导耗散型接触项；成功复现文献中已知结果及一阶圈尾效应。

Conclusion: 该方法为高阶自旋引力散射提供了系统、自洽且可推广的构造路径，弥合了有效场论与黑洞微扰理论之间的关键鸿沟。

Abstract: We develop a new method to construct the tree-level Compton amplitude for gravitational-wave scattering off a Kerr black hole in post-Minkowskian effective field theory. Building on previous computations that required physical consistency of the amplitude, the method uses wave scattering functions from black hole perturbation theory to uniquely determine additional contact terms. We illustrate the iterative procedure by deriving explicit expressions up to sixth order in spin, including previously unknown sectors at the fifth and sixth orders in spin. The amplitude features complex transcendental structures and a leading dissipative contact term at sixth order in spin. We verify our result against known results in the literature and reproduce the one-loop tail result from black hole perturbation theory.

</details>


### [156] [$\mathcal{N}=4$ Super Yang-Mills Flux Tube in AdS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40156)
*Victor Gorbenko,Orr Sela,Brian R. Zielazienski*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₄中N=4超杨-米尔斯理论边界上的超对称Wilson线，作为全息弦与禁闭通量管之间联系的模型。通过分析弱耦合和强耦合下的缺陷谱及超共形指数，建立了规范理论算符与全息弦世界面激发之间的对应关系，并支持从指数增长的规范理论谱到类Cardy弦世界面谱的平滑过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索全息弦与禁闭通量管之间的联系，构建一个既非完全可积又保持足够超对称性的模型，以更贴近真实禁闭规范理论。

Method: 在平面极限下，分析边界超对称Wilson线缺陷系统的弱/强耦合谱及超共形指数，建立规范理论算符与全息弦世界面激发之间的映射。

Result: 发现世界面轴子在强耦合下对应于双粒子态；实现了规范理论谱向类Cardy弦谱的平滑过渡；提出了非可积超对称模型用于研究禁闭理论中的谱重组。

Conclusion: 该模型为理解禁闭规范理论中由全息原理引发的谱结构重整提供了可控的非可积超对称框架。

Abstract: We study a supersymmetric Wilson line on the boundary of $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills theory in AdS$_4$, or equivalently on a half-space, as a model for exploring the connection between holographic strings and confining flux tubes. The boundary breaks four-dimensional conformal symmetry and is expected to remove integrability, bringing this system closer to a confining string while preserving enough supersymmetry to constrain its dynamics. Working in the planar limit, we analyze the defect spectrum at weak and strong coupling and compute the superconformal index, thereby relating the perturbative gauge-theory description to the worldsheet excitations of a long holographic string. These complementary perspectives allow us to propose a map between operators in the two regimes, under which the worldsheet axion, in particular, becomes a two-particle state at strong coupling. The resulting picture supports a smooth interpolation from an exponentially growing gauge-theory spectrum to the Cardy-like spectrum of a string worldsheet, providing a non-integrable supersymmetric model for investigating the spectral reorganization expected in confining gauge theories.

</details>


### [157] [A density calculus for quantum field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40174)
*Tanay Kibe,Pratik Roy*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Haagerup非交换L^p空间，旨在将密度矩阵、分数幂和Schatten范数等常见计算推广到缺乏内在迹的III型局部可观测量代数中，并以手征电流的相干激发为例验证了夹心Rényi散度的单调性和Rényi量子零能量条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将密度矩阵、分数幂和Schatten范数等常见计算方法扩展到缺乏内在迹的III型局部可观测量代数中。

Method: 首先在矩阵代数上构建Haagerup非交换L^p空间，明确展示交叉积、其迹和对偶作用，再推广至一般冯·诺依曼代数；重点讨论正规态由Haagerup密度表示的方式，以及交叉积迹与用于计算期望值的标准Haagerup泛函之间的区别。

Result: 计算了手征电流相干激发的夹心Rényi散度，验证了其在限制下的单调性及所有实数阶α>1下的Rényi量子零能量条件。

Conclusion: Haagerup非交换L^p空间为处理III型代数中的量子信息问题提供了有力框架，且相关性质（如单调性与能量条件）在具体物理模型中得到验证。

Abstract: These notes provide an introduction to Haagerup noncommutative $L^p$ spaces for quantum field theorists. The aim is to explain how the familiar calculus of density matrices, fractional powers, and Schatten norms extends to local observable algebras of type~III, where an intrinsic trace is unavailable. We develop the construction first for a matrix algebra, making the crossed product, its trace, and the dual action explicit, before passing to general von Neumann algebras. Particular attention is paid to the representation of normal states by Haagerup densities and to the distinction between the crossed-product trace and the canonical Haagerup functional used to compute expectation values. As a concrete application, we calculate the sandwiched Rényi divergence for a coherent excitation of a chiral current, verifying monotonicity under restriction and the Rényi quantum null energy condition at every real order $α>1$. The presentation assumes familiarity with quantum mechanics and elementary quantum field theory, while introducing the operator-algebraic concepts needed for the construction and calculation.

</details>


### [158] [Quantum curves of 6d SCFTs on a -3 curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40203)
*Minsung Kim,Sung-Soo Kim,Xin Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了6维N=(1,0)超共形场论中半BPS缺陷与量子Seiberg-Witten曲线的关系，利用广义爆破方程计算缺陷存在下的配分函数，并提出以椭圆差分方程形式表达的量子曲线，应用于su(3)、g2及so(N)等理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解6维N=(1,0) SCFT中半BPS缺陷的结构及其对量子Seiberg-Witten几何的影响，拓展低维类比中的量子曲线概念到6维情形。

Method: 使用广义爆破方程计算R^4×T^2上含codimension-4和codimension-2缺陷的配分函数；取Nekrasov-Shatashvili极限，从中导出作为椭圆差分方程的量子Seiberg-Witten曲线。

Result: 为最小su(3)、g2、so(N)等6d SCFT构建了对应的量子Seiberg-Witten曲线；并推广至带Z2扭转的圆紧化su(3)理论。

Conclusion: 量子Seiberg-Witten曲线可自然推广至6维N=(1,0) SCFT，其由缺陷配分函数的NS极限所编码，为高维量子几何提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study half-BPS defects and quantum Seiberg-Witten curves in 6d $ \mathcal{N}=(1,0) $ SCFTs on a $ -3 $ curve. We compute the partition functions in the presence of codimension-4 and codimension-2 defects on $ \mathbb{R}^4 \times T^2 $ using generalized blowup equations. We then propose quantum Seiberg-Witten curves as elliptic difference equations satisfied by the Nekrasov-Shatashvili limits of the defect partition functions. We apply this construction to the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory, and to the $ \mathfrak{g}_2 $ and $ \mathfrak{so}(N) $ gauge theories on a Higgsing tree whose endpoint is the minimal $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ theory. We also investigate defects and the quantum curve of the $ \mathfrak{su}(3) $ gauge theory compactified on a circle with a $ \mathbb{Z}_2 $ twist.

</details>


### [159] [Competition and coexistence of baryonic condensates in AdS hardwall model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40244)
*Akash Singh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究冷密QCD中二夸克与双重子凝聚是否共存，利用五维AdS硬壁模型并考虑完全背景反作用，发现二者仅在特定维度窗口内共存，且该窗口随电荷增大而拓宽；相图显示低温下共存态出现在禁闭相，高温窄带则为解禁闭单凝聚态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究冷密QCD中不同重子数破缺序参量（如二夸克与双重重子凝聚）能否共存，而非互斥，以深化对强相互作用物质相结构的理解。

Method: 采用五维AdS硬壁模型，引入两个带U(1)_B电荷的标量场（分别对应二夸克类和双重重子类算符），并对其引力背景进行完全自洽反作用计算；系统扫描不同维度Δ₂与电荷q₂参数空间，并分析μ_B-T相图。

Result: 二夸克型（q₁=2, Δ₁=3）与另一标量算符仅在狭窄的Δ₂区间内同时凝聚；该区间随q₂增大而拓宽，当两算符重合时消失；该共存行为在禁闭与解禁闭相中均存在；在μ_B-T平面上，双凝聚态稳定于低温禁闭区，其上方为一窄带高温解禁闭单凝聚区。

Conclusion: 二夸克与双重重子凝聚并非必然互斥，可在特定参数范围内共存，表明冷密QCD可能存在比传统单序参量描述更丰富的相结构；共存窗口的存在及其在相图中的位置凸显了电荷与维度对强耦合多体凝聚的竞争与协同作用。

Abstract: Cold dense QCD admits several order parameters for baryon-number breaking, among them diquark and dibaryon condensates. We ask whether they exclude one another or coexist, using a five-dimensional AdS hardwall model with full backreaction in which two charged scalars, dual to operators of charges $q_i$ and dimensions $Δ_i$, couple to a common $U(1)_B$ gauge field. With the diquark-like operator fixed at $q_1=2$, $Δ_1=3$, both operators condense only within a narrow window of $Δ_2$, which widens with $q_2$ and closes when the operators coincide. Outside the window, only a single condensate forms. The structure persists in both confined and deconfined phases. In the $μ_B$-$T$ plane, confined matter with both condensates is preferred at low temperature, below a narrow band of deconfined matter with one condensate.

</details>


### [160] [$\mathcal{N}=2$ conformal higher-spin actions in harmonic superspace](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40274)
*Evgeny Ivanov,Nikita Zaigraev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在谐波超空间中构建了4D, N=2超共形高自旋规范多重态的线性化Weyl型离壳作用量，并验证其超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性；基于Grassmann谐波解析性与半解析性原理，提出新的几何框架，并探讨推广至非线性水平及任意N=2超引力背景的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续前期关于N=2共形超引力的工作，旨在为N=2超共形高自旋规范理论建立系统、几何清晰的线性化离壳形式，并探索其向非线性理论和一般超引力背景推广的可能性。

Method: 在谐波超空间中，利用Grassmann谐波解析性原理确定预势及其变换规律，利用Grassmann半解析性原理构造不变作用量与广义N=2超Weyl张量；检验超共形不变性与线性化规范不变性，并分析在一般N=2超引力背景下的适用条件。

Result: 成功构造出N=2超共形高自旋规范多重态的线性化Weyl型离壳作用量；明确了预势结构与变换由谐波解析性完全决定；发现线性化高自旋规范不变性仅与N=2 Weyl平坦超背景相容。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅提供了N=2超共形高自旋理论的新几何框架，还为未来构建完整非线性理论及在弯曲超背景中的推广奠定了基础。

Abstract: We construct linearized $4D, \mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-type off-shell actions in harmonic superspace for the superconformal higher-spin gauge multiplets introduced in arXiv:2404.19016. This work is a followup of our arXiv:2511.16325, where an analogous construction was carried out for conformal $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity. We check the superconformal invariance of the new actions and their linearized gauge invariance. As the characteristic features of our formulation, the structure of the underlying prepotentials and their gauge and superconformal transformations are entirely determined by the fundamental \textit{principle of Grassmann harmonic analyticity}, while the construction of the invariant actions and of the generalized $\mathcal{N}=2$ super-Weyl tensors relies upon the \textit{principle of Grassmann half-analyticity}. This provides not only a new geometric framework for the linearized $\mathcal{N}=2$ superconformal gauge higher-spin theories, but also suggests possible ways of promoting them to the full nonlinear level. We consider a generalization to an arbitrary $\mathcal{N}=2$ supergravity background and show that the linearized spin-$\mathbf{s}$ gauge invariance, without radical modifications of the gauge transformation law and/or harmonic zero-curvature equations, is consistent only with $\mathcal{N}=2$ Weyl-flat super-backgrounds.

</details>


### [161] [Bounds on the Diagonal Degree of the Biadjoint Scalar Amplitude Matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40355)
*Freddy Cachazo,Rajbir Longia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了双伴随标量理论中部分振幅矩阵M_n的对角子矩阵的最大非奇异尺寸（即对角度Δ_n），证明其相对于CHY秩(n-3)!呈指数衰减，从而否定了存在临界n_c使得当n>n_c时总存在满秩对角子矩阵的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在KLT公式中使用最少项数，需寻找M_n中最大秩的对角子矩阵；此前有猜测存在临界n_c使n>n_c时总存在满秩对角子矩阵。

Method: 通过组合分析与渐近估计方法，推导Δ_n的上下界，证明其相对于(n-3)!呈指数衰减。

Result: 证明Δ_n/(n-3)! = exp(-Θ(n))，并给出显式界：exp(-1.5n) ≤ Δ_n/(n-3)! ≤ exp[-0.01n+O(1)]。

Conclusion: 否定了存在临界n_c的猜想，表明大n下满秩对角子矩阵几乎不存在，对KLT构造的优化有重要启示。

Abstract: Biadjoint scalar theory partial amplitudes, $m_n(α,β)$, define an $(n-1)!/2\times (n-1)!/2$ matrix, $M_n$, with rows and columns labeled by orderings. The CHY construction of $M_n$ implies that its rank is $(n-3)!$. Invertible submatrices of maximal rank enter in the KLT construction relating graviton amplitudes to gluon amplitudes. Motivated by finding the KLT formula with the smallest number of terms, one of the authors posed the question of whether there is a critical $n_c$ such that for all $n>n_c$ there exists a diagonal submatrix of $M_n$ of rank $(n-3)!$. In this work we obtain bounds on the size of the largest non-singular diagonal submatrix of $M_n$, which we call its diagonal degree $Δ_n$, and we answer the question in the negative. We prove that the diagonal degree is exponentially suppressed relative to the CHY rank: $Δ_n/(n-3)! = \exp(-Θ(n))$ as $n\to \infty$. More explicitly, we establish the bounds $\exp(-1.5n)\leqΔ_n/(n-3)!\leq \exp \left[ -0.01n+O(1)\right]$.

</details>


### [162] [Exact holographic superfluid at the Bogomolny point: transport, large charge, and Rényi entropies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40371)
*Siqing Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了AdS₅中Abelian Higgs模型在探针极限下的解析解及其在不同AdS背景下的物理含义，揭示了其与对偶共形场论中超流、电荷态及Rényi熵等现象的精确对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找强耦合超流体全息模型的解析解，并统一理解其在不同AdS切片下对偶CFT中的物理意义。

Method: 利用标量质量取Breitenlohner-Freedman下界、四次耦合取Bogomolny值的特殊参数条件，在静态无电荷AdS₅背景下求得节点less探针极限凝聚相的闭式解，并分析其线性稳定性及激发谱。

Result: 在平面黑膜、全局AdS₅和双曲切片下分别得到超流温度行为（严格满足Gorter-Casimir两流律）、大电荷基态能量与标度维数、以及计算球面Rényi熵的带毛黑洞均匀相；发现额外激发塔源于探针部分的红外不动点。

Conclusion: 该模型在特定参数下提供了一个罕见的、可解析求解且线性稳定的全息超流模型，能精确刻画多种CFT物理问题，拓展了对强耦合相变与信息熵的理解。

Abstract: The Abelian Higgs model in AdS$_5$, in the probe limit, is a standard holographic model of a strongly coupled superfluid, usually solved numerically. When the scalar mass saturates the Breitenlohner-Freedman bound and the quartic coupling takes the Bogomolny value, its condensed phase can be found in closed form on every static, uncharged AdS$_5$ background with maximally symmetric spatial sections. We prove that it is linearly stable, as is any nodeless probe-limit condensate in the standard quantization with a nonnegative quartic coupling and no superflow. On each slicing it answers a different question about the dual conformal field theory. On the planar black brane it is the superfluid at all temperatures below $T_c$: the superfluid fraction follows the Gorter-Casimir two-fluid law exactly, and the speed and the attenuation of sound are found in closed form. In global AdS$_5$ it describes a state of charge $Q$ on $\mathbb{R}\times S^3$, arguably the lowest-energy one, and gives its scaling dimension in closed form at all $Q$, classically in the bulk. Its excitations include towers that the superfluid effective theory does not describe; they come from an infrared fixed point of the probe sector. The hyperbolic slicing gives in closed form a homogeneous hairy phase of the black holes that compute the charged Rényi entropies of a ball. Its condensate does not vanish at the entangling surface. When sourced by operators inserted there, the phase sets in at Rényi index exactly one at zero chemical potential, as found for a neutral scalar.

</details>


### [163] [Breaking of integrability in elliptic deformations of AdS3 strings with mixed flux](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40386)
*Ian Le Meur,Fiona K. Seibold*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了在IIB型超引力中由混合NSNS和RR通量支撑的$AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$超弦的椭圆形变，并研究其可积性；发现Wess-Zumino项在椭圆形变下破坏可积性，而Lax分析识别出的可积情形下树图S矩阵满足经典Yang-Baxter方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在混合NSNS和RR通量下$AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$超弦椭圆形变的可积性，特别是Wess-Zumino项对可积性的影响。

Method: 通过构造平坦Lax联络和计算树图世界面S矩阵，从两个角度分析可积性。

Result: 对于一般椭圆参数和NSNS通量，发现违反可积性所需的弹性的非弹性$2 \rightarrow 2$散射振幅；在Lax分析识别出的可积情形下，这些非弹性振幅消失，且树图S矩阵满足经典Yang-Baxter方程；Wess-Zumino项在椭圆形变下破坏可积性。

Conclusion: Wess-Zumino项虽在有理与三角模型中保持可积性，但在椭圆形变中导致不可积；仅特定参数下系统仍可积，且其S矩阵具备经典可积结构。

Abstract: We construct elliptic deformations of the $AdS_3 \times S^3 \times T^4$ superstring supported by mixed NSNS and RR fluxes in type IIB supergravity and investigate their integrability. The NSNS sector includes a Wess-Zumino term, and imposing that the theory preserves 8 supersymmetries also requires the presence of a B-field coupling the deformed $AdS_3$ and $S^3$ factors. We explore integrability from two different perspectives: through the construction of a flat Lax connection and the computation of the tree-level worldsheet S-matrix. For generic elliptic parameters and NSNS flux, we find inelastic $2 \rightarrow 2$ amplitudes, violating the elasticity required by integrability. In the integrable cases as identified by the Lax analysis, these inelastic amplitudes vanish and the tree-level S-matrix satisfies the classical Yang-Baxter equation. In particular, the addition of a Wess-Zumino term, which preserves integrability in the rational and trigonometric models, breaks integrability for elliptic deformations.

</details>


### [164] [Holographic duals of JT gravity at finite cutoff: the classical case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40391)
*Andreas Belaey,Thomas G. Mertens,Lorenzo Russo,Alex Tarana*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在BF理论框架下构建了具有有限截断边界的JT引力模型，并通过动力学约化得到边界上的一维系统，从而几何化地理解了边界相空间自由度，进一步推导出有限截断下全息Schwarzian描述的闭式表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有限截断下JT引力的边界相空间结构及其对体时空局域性和时空基本结构的启示。

Method: 采用一阶BF形式构建带有限截断边界和端界膜的JT引力，通过动力学约化得到边界一维系统，并几何化分析其相空间自由度。

Result: 获得了有限截断下JT引力全息Schwarzian描述的闭式相空间表达式，明确了其对应于AdS₂中有限截断曲线嵌入的自由度。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解JT引力中边界自由度与体时空局域性的关系提供了几何基础，并为后续量子化研究铺平道路。

Abstract: We formulate JT gravity with finite-cutoff boundaries and end-of-the-world branes in the first-order BF formulation. We dynamically reduce the BF model into a one-dimensional system living at the boundaries, generalizing earlier constructions. We obtain in particular a geometrical understanding of the phase space degrees of freedom associated to each of these boundaries. We leverage these results to probe bulk locality and the underlying structure of spacetime. Within our framework, we are able to derive a closed-form expression for the holographic Schwarzian description of JT gravity at finite-cutoff in phase space. The resulting degrees of freedom describe the embedding of the finite-cutoff curve in AdS$_2$. A quantization of these systems is presented in a companion paper.

</details>


### [165] [A Simplified Born Rule for Cosmological Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40412)
*Fan Zhu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文证明了平直时空中的等时in-in关联子中所有双边界极点都会抵消，从而导出一个简化的Born规则，并引入嵌入式波函数系数、Born多面体等新概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学关联子是原初宇宙的自然可观测量，通过Born规则与波函数系数相关联；但传统计算中存在复杂的双边界极点问题，需系统性理解其抵消机制与几何结构。

Method: 基于平直时空中的等时in-in形式框架，对任意图（含局域导数相互作用）严格证明双边界极点在空间动量积分前即完全相消；进而定义嵌入式波函数系数，推导简化Born规则；针对共形耦合Tr(φ³)理论，建立森林切割展开与Born多面体的几何构造。

Result: 1) 任意图下双边界极点普适性抵消；2) 提出嵌入式波函数系数与简化Born规则；3) 对Tr(φ³)理论得到均匀加权森林切割展开；4) 构造Born多面体，其典范函数精确复现归一化关联子；5) 导出对应弦论型公式。

Conclusion: 双边界极点的自动抵消揭示了宇宙学关联子内在的简洁性与几何本质；Born多面体为高能宇宙学提供了新的组合几何与计算范式，连接量子场论、引力和弦论。

Abstract: Cosmological correlators are natural observables of the primordial universe and are related to wavefunction coefficients by the Born rule. For equal-time in-in correlators in flat spacetime, we prove that all double-boundary poles cancel for arbitrary graphs, including in theories with local derivative interactions. These cancellations occur before integration over spatial loop momenta and yield a $\textit{simplified Born rule}$ in terms of what we call embedded wavefunction coefficients. The resulting expansion reveals which physical poles can occur together while keeping the interaction dependence explicit. For conformally coupled $\operatorname{Tr}(φ^3)$ theory in flat spacetime, the simplified Born rule takes the form of a uniformly weighted forest-cut expansion. From its pole combinatorics, we construct what we call the $\textit{Born polytope}$, whose canonical function reproduces the normalized correlator for arbitrary graphs after matching its facet supports to the physical energies. The same geometry also yields a stringy formula for correlators.

</details>


### [166] [Gravity between two masses beyond Newton's law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40462)
*Zhengwen Liu,Xing-Bo Yuan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过构建静态相互作用能框架，首次在广义相对论中精确推导了任意质量比下两体静态引力相互作用至牛顿常数12阶的展开，并给出超越Schwarzschild试探体极限的首阶自作用（backreaction）的全阶闭式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从爱因斯坦理论中提取两体静态引力相互作用仍是一个基础性解析难题，尤其需克服引力非线性导致的无穷阶场耦合。

Method: 以静态相互作用能为出发点，采用精确单体背景（精确求解一个天体的自作用）、利用定态性简化另一天体所需场变量，并结合积分响应恒等式揭示不同耦合模态间的抵消机制；再辅以互补展开获得有限阶下的完整质量依赖关系。

Result: 得到了第九至第十二阶牛顿常数精度下的静态相互作用结果；推导出首个超越试探体近似的、对引力耦合常数全阶有效的backreaction闭式解。

Conclusion: 该工作弥合了试探粒子极限与等质量极限之间的理论鸿沟，揭示了广义相对论中隐藏的解析简洁性，为轨道动力学建模与引力波波形生成提供了关键解析基石。

Abstract: Newton's law of universal gravitation describes the attraction between two masses through an inverse-distance potential. In Einstein's theory gravity gravitates: each mass reshapes the spacetime geometry through which the other acts. More than a century later, extracting the two-body interaction from this coupled geometry remains a fundamental analytical challenge. Even without motion or radiation, the static sector of the interaction harbours an infinite hierarchy of field couplings: the purest laboratory for gravitational non-linearity. Here we determine the static interaction from ninth to twelfth order in Newton's constant for arbitrary mass ratios, and derive a closed expression for the first backreaction beyond the Schwarzschild test-body limit to all orders in the gravitational coupling. We achieve both advances by formulating the problem around the stationary interaction energy. An exact one-body background resums one source's self-interaction, stationarity reduces the fields required from the other, and integrated response identities reveal cancellations across the coupled modes. Complementary expansions then resolve the complete mass dependence at finite order. Together, these results bridge the gap between an infinitesimal probe and comparable masses, exposing hidden analytical simplicity within Einstein's theory and providing essential building blocks for modelling orbital dynamics and gravitational-wave generation.

</details>


### [167] [Generalised $Ω$-backgrounds in twisted $N=4$ super Yang-Mills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40465)
*Elisa Iris Marieni,Itamar Yaakov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文引入了4维N=4超杨-米尔斯理论的刚性超引力背景，推广了其拓扑扭曲及N=2理论的Ω-形变，并推导了三种不等价拓扑扭曲下的扭超对称变分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广N=4超杨-米尔斯理论的拓扑扭曲和Ω-形变，构建更一般的刚性超引力背景。

Method: 推导N=4 Weyl多重态和耦合到刚性玻色背景的N=4物质多重态的扭超对称变分。

Result: 得到了三种不等价拓扑扭曲下的扭超对称变分，并讨论了其他更奇异的背景。

Conclusion: 成功构造了推广的刚性超引力背景，为N=4理论的拓扑扭曲研究提供了新框架。

Abstract: We introduce rigid supergravity backgrounds for 4d $\mathcal{N}=4$ super Yang-Mills which deform its topological twists and generalise the $Ω$-deformation of $\mathcal{N}=2$ gauge theories. We derive the twisted supersymmetry variations of the $\mathcal{N}=4$ Weyl multiplet, and the $\mathcal{N}=4$ matter multiplet coupled to a rigid bosonic background, for each of the three inequivalent topological twists. Other, more exotic backgrounds are also discussed.

</details>


### [168] [Localization in Seven-Dimensional Supergravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40514)
*Florian Gaar,Michèle Levi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将相对等变局域化方法扩展到七维最大Cartan规范超引力，通过构造适应自旋子现实性条件变化的双线性系统，实现了对AdS7黑洞作用量的局域化计算，并推导出电荷关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 七维超引力为研究强耦合六维超共形理论提供了全息途径，但其独特的自旋子双线性几何和大质量高形式动力学使得系统分析和求解极具挑战性。

Method: 采用相对等变局域化方法，在七维最大Cartan规范超引力中构建基于场强的欧几里得作用量与Killing-自旋子方程；设计适配自旋子现实性条件（由辛-马约拉纳变为马约拉纳）的双线性系统；利用3-形式自偶性确定高形式定点数据，并在填充流形单固定点处进行局域化。

Result: 成功恢复一般超对称AdS7黑洞的作用量；证明了超对称Killing流与R-对称性联合作用下规范协变Killing-自旋子的不变性；导出线性化学势约束；通过约束熵泛函极值及实正定熵要求，得到非线性电荷关系。

Conclusion: 该方法无需显式求解场方程即可获得超对称可观测量，揭示了七维超引力中AdS7黑洞热力学与全息对偶的深层结构，且所得非线性电荷关系等价于洛伦兹情形下的视界极值条件。

Abstract: Seven-dimensional supergravity provides holographic access to strongly coupled six-dimensional superconformal theories through its highest-dimensional supersymmetric AdS vacua. Its unique spinor-bilinear geometry and massive higher-form dynamics make systematic analysis and solution-finding distinctively challenging. Relative equivariant localization can obtain supersymmetric observables in odd dimensions from fixed-point data on an auxiliary even-dimensional filling, without an explicit solution. We extend this approach to seven dimensions in maximal Cartan-gauged supergravity with all gauge fields active. We formulate the Euclidean action and Killing-spinor equations in terms of field strengths, and construct a bilinear system adapted to the change in spinor reality conditions from symplectic-Majorana to Majorana. On the non-null gauged active spinor branch, independent, oppositely R-charged spinors are paired by Majorana conjugation on the same complex background. One algebraic gauging correction and two algebraic bilinear projections suffice to extend the equal-charge sector to independent Cartan charges, and the underlying efficient reorganization also applies in the Lorentzian theory. Massive 3-form self-duality fixes the quartic anomaly class and determines the higher-form fixed-point datum from Abelian moment maps. Localization at the single fixed point of the filling recovers the general supersymmetric AdS$_7$ black-hole action, without extending the solution. We prove gauge-covariant Killing-spinor invariance under the combined supersymmetric Killing flow and R-symmetry action, deriving the linear chemical-potential constraint. Extremizing the constrained entropy functional and requiring real positive stationary entropy yields the nonlinear charge relation within the Euclidean analysis, equivalent to Lorentzian horizon extremality for the known spherical black-hole family.

</details>


### [169] [Thermodynamics of Swirling Black Holes and Kerr-Taub-NUT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40529)
*Ernesto Frodden,Diego Hidalgo,Francisco Manzano*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了KS和AKS黑洞解的热力学第一定律，并重新审视了KTN时空的第一定律；利用诺特定理和表面荷方法计算了含弦状缺陷的KS与KTN黑洞的质量和角动量，得到有限的准局域荷，且不依赖非平坦渐近区域；发现KS和KTN的熵不满足四分之一面积律，因其拓扑不同，需新几何框架；而无弦状缺陷的AKS黑洞熵则符合标准面积律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对含弦状缺陷（如KS、KTN）和无缺陷（如AKS）的新型旋转黑洞，传统热力学第一定律与熵公式是否适用尚不明确，需基于对称性原理建立自洽的荷定义与第一定律。

Method: 基于诺特定理，采用并改进表面荷方法，在存在轴向锥形奇点（弦状缺陷）的KS与KTN时空中计算准局域质量与角动量；对AKS时空则结合Christodoulou-Ruffini公式与表面荷方法交叉验证。

Result: KS与KTN黑洞的质量与角动量可被有限、准局域地定义，其熵偏离A/4；而AKS黑洞熵严格满足A/4，且两种质量计算方法结果一致。

Conclusion: 弦状缺陷显著改变黑洞热力学结构，使标准熵公式失效；只有在无缺陷、可正则化的时空（如AKS）中，经典黑洞热力学才完全成立；这提示需发展适配非平凡拓扑的广义几何热力学框架。

Abstract: We establish the first law of black hole thermodynamics for the recently discovered Kerr swirling (KS) and accelerated Kerr swirling (AKS) black hole solutions. We also revisit the first law for the Kerr-Taub-NUT (KTN) spacetime. Guided by Noether's original insight that global symmetries give rise to conservation laws, we employ and adapt the surface charge method for computing the associated charges (mass and angular momentum) in the presence of spacetime string-like defects (conical singularities) along the axes of both KS and KTN black holes. We obtain finite integrated charges in a quasi-local manner, without relying on the non-flat asymptotic regions characteristic of these spacetimes. We then establish a first law without introducing independent stringy terms unrelated to symmetries. For both KS and KTN, the quantities that play the role of the black hole entropy differ from the simple geometrical rule of one-fourth of the horizon area. This mismatch arises because we keep first-law charges associated with conservation laws. It indicates that standard formulas may not be naively used for black holes with string-like defects; they have a different topology and suggest the need for a new geometrical framework. In stark contrast, for the AKS spacetime, which can be regularized outside the singularity and lacks string-like defects, we find that the charges are consistent with a first law having the usual one-fourth of the area entropy.
