We first determine the mass using the Christodoulou-Ruffini formula and then check its agreement with the result obtained from the surface charge method.

</details>


### [170] [On Geometric Observables in Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40615)
*Manthos Karydas,Murat Kologlu,Allic Sivaramakrishnan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了微扰量子引力中的几何可观测量（如距离和时间），构建了具有引力修饰的坐标不变量，并计算了其相关函数中的主导引力子效应，建立了实验测量与天球操作之间的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何量（如距离和时间）在经典引力中是基本概念，但在量子引力中，其引力子修正尚未被充分探索。

Method: 构建具有引力修饰的距离和时间可观测量以建模实际测量（包括时空涨落情形），确保坐标不变性；通过取渐近极限得到记忆效应和Shapiro时间延迟算符；计算几何可观测量关联函数中的主导阶引力子修正。

Result: 明确了有限距离几何可观测量如何演变为渐近极限下的记忆效应和Shapiro时间延迟算符，并计算了其关联函数中主导的引力子修正效应。

Conclusion: 本文识别出一类能连接实验测量与天球操作的几何可观测量，为量子引力可观测效应提供了新路径。

Abstract: Measures of geometry like distance and time are staples of classical gravity. While geometric quantities arise broadly in quantum gravity, their graviton corrections are less explored. In this paper, we study geometric observables in perturbative quantum gravity. We form distance and time observables that model measurements, including in the presence of spacetime fluctuations. Gravitational dressing ensures these constructions are coordinate invariant. Then we show how these finite-distance observables become memory and Shapiro time delay operators upon taking asymptotic limits. We compute leading graviton effects in correlators of geometric observables throughout. Altogether, our results identify a class of geometric observables that link experiments and operations on the celestial sphere.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Complex Langevin kernels for real-time SU(N) lattice gauge theory in 3+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37800)
*Kirill Boguslavski,Paul Hotzy,David I. Müller*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出并测试了针对3+1维实时间SU(N)格点规范理论的改进型复朗之万（CL）动力学，引入多种新核函数（如各向异性核、轮廓依赖相位核、局域场依赖核），显著延长了稳定模拟的时间尺度，并推广了CL正确性判据，为非阿贝尔规范理论的实时间模拟提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时间非平衡量子场论（尤其是非阿贝尔规范理论）因符号问题难以用传统蒙特卡洛方法模拟，复朗之万方法是重要候选，但其在高维规范理论中存在收敛性与稳定性挑战，亟需适配格点规范理论的改进方案。

Method: 提出并实现三类针对格点规范理论定制的复朗之万核函数：各向异性核（基准）、轮廓依赖相位核、基于迹化plaquette的局域场依赖核；在矩形Schwinger-Keldysh时间轮廓上进行SU(2)纯规范模拟；推广CL正确性判据至带核动力学，以漂移模长为诊断工具。

Result: 在g=0.5下，各向异性核支持稳定模拟至实时间2β；相位核延至3β；局域场依赖核达3.5β；首次将此类核成功应用于3+1维格点规范理论；建立了可验证的CL收敛性评估框架。

Conclusion: 所提核修正显著提升了复朗之万方法在实时间非阿贝尔规范理论模拟中的稳定性与可达时序，为Minkowski空间中可控的非平衡研究奠定了基础，并将用于后续不等时关联函数计算。

Abstract: We investigate complex Langevin (CL) dynamics for real-time SU($N$) gauge theories in 3+1 dimensions and introduce new kernel modifications tailored for lattice gauge theory (LGT). Using the anisotropic kernel as a baseline, we demonstrate that on a rectangular regularized Schwinger-Keldysh time contour, stable simulations are possible for pure SU(2) gauge theory up to a real-time extent of $2β$ at a bare coupling $g=0.5$, where $β$ denotes the inverse temperature. We show that a contour-dependent phase kernel extends this reach to $3β$, while a local, field-dependent kernel based on traced plaquettes achieves $3.5β$. These constructions represent the first application of such kernels to LGTs, especially in 3+1 dimensions. To validate the approach, we generalize the CL correctness criterion based on the drift magnitude to kernelled dynamics, providing a practical tool to assess correct convergence. Our results substantially advance the applicability of CL to real-time simulations in Minkowski space, establishing a framework for controlled studies of non-Abelian gauge theories at unprecedented time scales. A forthcoming paper applies these developments to the computation of unequal-time correlation functions.

</details>


### [172] [Agentic AI for QCD perturbation theory or: how I learned to stop worrying and love the bot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37960)
*Erik Lundstrum*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用AI编码代理进行微扰量子色动力学（QCD）计算的可行性与有效性，强调其模块化、可验证性特点与AI代理能力高度契合，并展示了方法在单圈和双圈计算中的应用及高效迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码代理擅长处理可模块化分解且每步可验证的计算任务，而微扰QCD计算恰好具备这两点特性，因此值得探索其在该领域的应用。

Method: 设计指令与验证机制，指导AI代理完成一阶和二阶微扰QCD计算，并重点预防常见错误。

Result: 成功实现AI代理完成首个微扰计算后，其指令可高效迁移到其他相似计算任务，在数小时内完成并验证，用户干预极少。

Conclusion: AI编码代理非常适合执行微扰QCD计算，具有高可靠性、强可迁移性和低人工成本优势。

Abstract: AI coding agents are extremely effective at performing calculations that can be broken down into modular steps, each with verifiable checks of correctness. Perturbative QCD calculations satisfy both of these properties and we find that they are well-suited to solution by agentic AI systems. We discuss our methods for instructing and verifying the work of agents in one- and two-loop perturbative calculations, with a focus on pre-empting common mistakes. We find that once an agent has successfully completed one perturbative calculation, its instructions transfer very well to other similar calculations which can then be completed and verified in a matter of hours with little effort from the user.

</details>


### [173] [Hot and dense QCD equation of state from the complex Langevin equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38466)
*Michael Mandl,Dénes Sexty,Daniel Unterhuber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过首次在物理π介子质量下进行的复Langevin模拟，研究了QCD状态方程在高于交叉温度及高重子化学势下的行为，并给出了能量、熵、焓密度及比热容随重子化学势和温度变化的连续极限外推结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高温高重子化学势下QCD状态方程，弥补传统格点QCD在有限密度下遇到的符号问题。

Method: 采用复Langevin方法，在物理π介子质量下进行格点模拟，并进行连续极限外推。

Result: 获得了能量密度、熵密度、焓密度和比热容在高温高重子化学势区域的连续极限结果。

Conclusion: 复Langevin方法在物理点成功应用于有限密度QCD，为研究强相互作用物质的热力学性质提供了新途径。

Abstract: We discuss the QCD equation of state at temperatures above the crossover and for high baryon chemical potentials, obtained via the first ever complex Langevin simulations at the physical pion mass. In particular, we present continuum-extrapolated results for the energy, entropy, and enthalpy densities, as well as for the specific heat capacity as a function of baryon chemical potential and temperature.

</details>


### [174] [Enhanced Signal and Excited-State Control in Nonlocal Lattice Correlators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39944)
*Shi-Yi Zhong,Jian-Hui Zhang,Dian-Jun Zhao*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过非局域与局域关联函数比值来提取部分子物理量的新拟合策略，显著改善了信噪比、减小激发态污染，并提升了精度、稳定性和可及动量范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法受限于随欧氏距离和强子动量增大而急剧恶化的信噪比，以及激发态污染问题。

Method: 构造非局域关联函数与局域关联函数的比值，以投影出主导基态动力学，从而获得更干净、对激发态更不敏感的信号。

Result: 在夸克/胶子PDF、夸克GPD和TMDPDF上的验证表明，结果与传统三点/两点比值拟合一致，但精度和稳定性显著提升，且可拓展至更大强子动量；对非正向矩阵元（如GPD）分析大幅简化。

Conclusion: 该方法具有一般性，适用于各类狄拉克结构、动量结构、连通与非连通贡献，可直接应用于现有格点QCD程序，推动高精度强子结构研究。

Abstract: Nonlocal correlation functions underpin lattice calculations of parton physics. However, their extraction is limited by a rapidly deteriorating signal-to-noise ratio with increasing Euclidean separation and hadron momentum, and by excited-state contamination. We propose a new fitting strategy that enhances control over both: by taking the ratio of nonlocal to local correlators, we can project out the dominant ground-state dynamics with a signal that is much cleaner and far less sensitive to excited states than the conventional approach. We demonstrate the method on the quark and gluon PDF, the quark GPD and TMDPDF. The results are consistent with those from the conventional three-point/two-point ratio fit but exhibit markedly improved precision and stability, and extend the accessible range to larger hadron momenta. For nonforward matrix elements such as GPDs, our strategy greatly simplifies the analysis procedure. Our method applies to general Dirac and momentum structures, for connected and disconnected contributions, and extends naturally to other nonlocal correlators, making it immediately deployable in existing lattice programs toward precision hadron structure.

</details>


### [175] [Weak (quasi-)PDFs from the lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40208)
*Patrick Jenny,Axel Maas,Sofie Martins,Georg Wieland*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文使用格点方法研究了希格斯粒子和W/Z玻色子的电弱部分子分布函数（PDFs），计算了准PDF并初步得到PDF，结果显示出符合规范不变性预期的有趣结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来对撞机将探测粒子的电弱子结构，而准确描述该结构可能需要类似QCD的部分子分布函数（PDFs），即电弱PDFs。

Method: 在简化标准模型框架下，采用格点方法计算希格斯和W/Z玻色子的准PDF，并在一阶近似下推导其PDF。

Result: 尽管存在不确定性，结果展现出与规范不变性预期一致的有趣结构。

Conclusion: 电弱PDFs可通过格点方法进行探索，为未来高能对撞机实验提供理论支持。

Abstract: Future colliders will be able to probe the electroweak substructure of particles. An accurate description of this substructure may necessitate electroweak PDFs, similarly to QCD. We investigate therefore the Higgs and W/Z PDFs in a reduced Standard Model setup using lattice methods, determining the quasi-PDFs, and, to lowest order, the resulting PDFs. While uncertainties remain, the results show interesting structures in line with expectations from gauge invariance.

</details>


### [176] [Complex potential and the thermal width of quarkonium in quark-gluon plasma](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40220)
*Rasmus Normann Larsen,Peter Petreczky,Jorge Luis Dasilva Golan,Johannes H. Weber*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文研究了2+1味QCD在非零温度下的重夸克-反夸克复势，指出其虚部与热夸克偶素宽度密切相关，并据此估算高温下粲偶素和底偶素的热宽度，结果与T矩阵分析进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接通过格点QCD计算夸克偶素关联函数在高温下尚不可行，因此需要寻找替代方法来估算热宽度。

Method: 基于格点2+1味QCD模拟，分析复势的虚部，并建立其与热夸克偶素宽度的关系，进而估算粲偶素和底偶素的热宽度。

Result: 对部分激发态夸克偶素，本文估算的热宽度小于T矩阵分析的结果。

Conclusion: 复势虚部可作为估算高温下夸克偶素热宽度的有效工具，且在某些激发态上与T矩阵分析存在差异，提示不同理论方法间需进一步对照与理解。

Abstract: We study the complex heavy quark anti-quark potential in lattice 2+1 flavor QCD at non-zero temperature. We point out a close relation between the imaginary part of the potential and the thermal quarkonium width. Using this relation, we estimate the thermal width of charmonia and bottomonia at high temperatures, which are not yet accessible with direct lattice QCD calculations of the quarkonium correlation functions and compare these to the results of the T-matrix analysis. For some of the excited quarkonia states, our estimates of the thermal width are smaller than the ones obtained in the T-matrix analysis.

</details>

### [177] [Identifying Scientifically Interesting Galaxies with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37747)
*Yixuan Cheng,Nolan Koblischke,Jo Bovy*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用预训练的视觉-语言模型（如Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite）根据自然语言指令直接评估天文图像科学价值的方法，无需标注样本，即可在哈勃望远镜图像集中高效识别高科学价值的异常星系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常检测方法侧重统计罕见性，但最罕见的未必最具科学价值；亟需一种能依据科学兴趣而非单纯稀有性对图像排序的新范式。

Method: 使用预训练视觉-语言模型（Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite）直接对Hubble图像按自然语言定义的‘科学兴趣’打分排序，不进行任何微调或目标域训练。

Result: 在2万张图像中，98.2%的167个已知参考异常落入前~2000名；成功复现引力透镜、水母星系候选体、喷流星系等；发布102个高分新候选体；推理成本约$5.05/万图。

Conclusion: 开箱即用的视觉-语言模型可在无监督、零样本条件下，有效替代传统异常检测，实现面向科学价值的大规模图像排序。

Abstract: Anomaly searches in large astronomical image datasets aim to identify rare objects for follow-up investigation. Yet the objects that are most statistically unusual are not necessarily those of greatest scientific relevance. We investigate whether pretrained vision-language models can directly assess scientific interest from natural-language instructions, enabling galaxy images to be ranked without labeled examples. We evaluate models on a 20,000-image Hubble Space Telescope sample that includes 167 reference anomalies previously identified through machine learning and expert inspection. By scoring images for scientific interest, Gemini 3.1 Flash-Lite places 98.2% of the reference anomalies within the highest-ranked ~2,000 images, at a present-day inference cost of approximately $5.05 per 10,000 images. We recover known gravitational lenses, jellyfish-galaxy candidates, and galaxies with visible jets, and release 102 high-scoring candidates for further investigation. These results show that an off-the-shelf vision-language model, without any training on the target data, offers a practical way to rank large image datasets by scientific relevance rather than rarity. The project code is available at https://github.com/TheCheng2005/Galaxy-Anomaly-Search/

</details>


### [178] [NORI: a GPU-accelerated code for simulating self-gravitating and self-interacting dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37768)
*Oliver Zier,Ruediger Pakmor,Xuejian Shen,Rongrong Liu,Lucas Schleuss,Mark Vogelsberger,Lars Hernquist*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了NORI，一种用于模拟自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）系统的高效N体数值代码，支持CPU和GPU并行计算，采用蒙特卡洛方法处理弹性各向同性自相互作用，在保持精度的同时显著提升计算速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统N体模拟SIDM的引力热坍缩计算成本高，需极短时间步长和大量粒子，限制了高分辨率模拟的可行性。

Method: 开发了NORI代码，基于蒙特卡洛方法处理SIDM弹性散射；采用全系统粒子副本与Morton序树结构避免MPI通信；固定评估顺序保证CPU/GPU结果位级一致；顺序处理散射事件以精确守恒动量与能量；使用全局时间步长提高效率。

Result: NORI在单个四A100 GPU节点上比112核CPU节点运行的AREPO-2快约60倍/时间步；对完整核心坍缩模拟（中心密度达10³–10⁴ρₛ），加速比约30；成功用10⁸粒子模拟至中心密度10⁴ρₛ；支持高达~2×10⁹粒子的孤立SIDM模拟。

Conclusion: NORI是一种高效、精确、可扩展的SIDM专用N体模拟工具，适用于孤立系统如引力热坍缩和星系团碰撞研究。

Abstract: Self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) can form cores in dark matter haloes and later drive their gravothermal collapse, raising their central densities far above those of NFW haloes. Simulating this collapse is computationally demanding, requiring increasingly short time-steps and a large particle number to resolve the shrinking core. We present NORI, an $N$-body code that runs on CPUs and GPUs and simulates isolated self-gravitating systems with elastic, isotropic self-interactions using a Monte-Carlo method. To avoid MPI communication in the tree walks, each compute node in CPU mode, and each GPU or group of GPUs in GPU mode, holds a copy of all particles and its own Morton-ordered tree. A fixed evaluation order makes the results bitwise identical on CPUs and GPUs. Applying scattering events sequentially conserves the momentum and energy of each pair to round-off and allows multiple scatterings per particle per step. With global time-steps in both codes, NORI is several times faster on CPUs than our AREPO-2 implementation. One four-A100 GPU node is up to 60 times faster per time-step than AREPO-2 on a 112-core CPU node. For a full core-collapse simulation, where AREPO-2 uses individual time-steps, the speed-up is $\sim 30$ for the central density to reach $10^3$--$10^4$ times the NFW scale density, $ρ_{\rm s}$. We follow a halo with $10^8$ particles within $r_{200}$ to a central density of $10^4\,ρ_{\rm s}$. Within the limit of about $2\times10^9$ particles set by data replication, NORI provides an efficient tool for isolated SIDM simulations, including gravothermal collapse and cluster collisions.

</details>


### [179] [Focal plane modal estimation of high-order wavefront drifts on coronagraphic testbeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38014)
*Saikrishna Manojkumar,Susan F. Redmond,Leonid Pogorelyuk,Christine L. Page,Garreth Ruane,Niyati Desai,Kerri Cahoy*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模态暗区维护（MDZM）算法，利用扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）估计高阶波前漂移模式，并直接生成变形镜（DM）控制指令，无需电场共轭（EFC）及其正则化参数，在仿真和NASA JPL的十年调查测试台（DST）上实现了36小时以上约3×10⁻¹⁰的归一化强度稳定度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足未来宜居世界天文台（HWO）对长时观测下波前稳定性的严苛要求，需克服热与机械不稳定性引起的慢变高阶波前漂移，从而维持高对比度成像能力。传统暗区维护（DZM）方法存在信息利用不充分、依赖EFC等缺陷。

Method: 提出基于扩展卡尔曼滤波（EKF）的模态暗区维护（MDZM）算法，在变形镜执行器基底下估计波前漂移模态；利用探针图像对全焦平面信息进行融合；直接输出DM控制指令，规避EFC及正则化参数；在DST硬件平台和FALCO仿真中验证，涵盖窄带与10%宽带传感场景。

Result: 在DST上实现超36小时、归一化强度约3×10⁻¹⁰的稳定暗区；相比DZM更接近光子极限；提供系统本征漂移估计能力；宽带（10%）仿真显示其性能仍优于窄带DZM。

Conclusion: MDZM是一种更鲁棒、自适应且无需人为调参的高阶波前传感与控制新范式，显著提升长期高对比度成像稳定性，具备向HWO等未来任务推广的潜力。

Abstract: The upcoming Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO) requires precise wavefront stability over long observation periods to directly image Exo-Earths, which are 10 orders of magnitude dimmer than their host star. Dark zone maintenance (DZM) has been proposed as a focal plane high-order wavefront sensing and control (HOWFSC) strategy to mitigate thermal and mechanical instabilities that cause slow-evolving high-order wavefront drifts, which ultimately degrade contrast. Here we present a modal dark zone maintenance (MDZM) algorithm that uses an Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) to estimate the drifting wavefront modes in the basis of the deformable mirror (DM) actuators used for control. The estimator derives information across the entire focal plane from probe-pair images, addressing deficiencies of DZM, and directly obtains a DM command, eliminating the need for Electric Field Conjugation (EFC) and its regularization parameter. MDZM has been demonstrated in simulation and in hardware on the Decadal Survey Testbed (DST) at NASA JPL. These experiments have maintained a normalized intensity (NI) of $\sim 3 \times 10^{-10}$ for over 36 hours, and have reduced the gap to the photon-derived theoretical bounds compared to DZM. MDZM also provides an estimate of naturally occurring drift on the testbed, offering potential system identification capabilities. To evaluate the robustness of MDZM, a parameter scan is conducted on DST, spanning exposure times and drift rates. Additionally, realistic observing scenarios for HWO will involve broadband images with a 20% bandwidth, rather than the 2.5% narrowband images previously used in MDZM testbed demonstrations. Therefore, we tested MDZM in simulation using broadband sensing images with a 10% bandwidth, using the FALCO software package with a model based on DST, and compared its performance against MDZM runs using narrowband images.

</details>


### [180] [Experimental Demonstration of Double-Fourier Interferometer Imaging for Future Space-Based Far-Infrared Observatories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38105)
*Jeremy P. Scott,Locke D. Spencer,Chris Benson,David Naylor,Berke Ricketti,Dinula De Silva,Vince Weiler*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文通过实验室测试平台验证了双傅里叶干涉仪（DFI）在远红外波段（0.3–2.1 THz）的关键观测技术，成功实现了孔径合成成像、空间依赖光谱分辨及宽视场成像，证实DFI是面向未来空间远红外天文台的成熟可行技术。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远红外（FIR）天文观测受限于衍射极限导致的空间分辨率不足，亟需新技术提升分辨率与光谱成像能力。

Method: 构建0.3–2.1 THz实验室DFI测试平台，开展系统建模、强度不平衡/非线性相位误差/相位中心偏移等关键校准，并实验验证一维/二维孔径合成、带光谱滤波的扩展源探测、以及探测器阵列多像素联合宽场成像。

Result: 成功恢复目标几何结构；探测到空间相关的光谱变化；实现宽视场成像；实验数据与理论预测高度一致，偏差源于光学像差和透视效应。

Conclusion: DFI架构已在实验室层面得到全面验证，具备高分辨率、宽带谱成像与宽视场能力，是一项面向未来空间FIR望远镜高度成熟且极具潜力的技术。

Abstract: Far-infrared (FIR) astronomy is crucial for observing star formation, active galactic nuclei, and protoplanetary systems, but its effectiveness is severely limited by poor diffraction-limited spatial resolution. To overcome this limitation, a double-Fourier interferometer (DFI), which couples a spatial interferometer with a Fourier transform spectrometer, has been proposed to deliver high-resolution broadband spectral imaging. Despite various conceptual and prototype studies, a comprehensive demonstration of DFI capabilities has been lacking. In this study, we present the validation of key DFI observing techniques using a laboratory testbed operating in the 0.3 to 2.1 THz optical bandwidth. We detail the instrument's ideal response alongside necessary calibrations for intensity imbalances, non-linear phase errors, and phase-center offsets. Our experimental results successfully demonstrate aperture synthesis for both one- and two-dimensional targets, accurately recovering source geometries. Furthermore, we prove the instrument's sensitivity to spatially dependent spectral variations by utilizing a low-pass optical filter on extended sources. Finally, we validate wide-field imaging capabilities by combining data from independent pixels in a detector array, each sensitive to different regions of the source plane. The experimental data show significant agreement with theoretical predictions, with minor deviations attributed to optical aberrations and perspective effects. These laboratory demonstrations validate the DFI architecture as a highly capable and mature technology for future space-based FIR observatories.

</details>


### [181] [Model of the instrumental polarization induced by a rotating HWP with anti-reflective sub-wavelength structures for CMB polarimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38165)
*Ryota Takaku,Hiroaki Imada,Guillaume Patanchon,Yuki Sakurai*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文研究了用于宇宙微波背景（CMB）偏振测量的多层蓝宝石半波片（HWP）及其亚波长结构（SWS）对仪器偏振的影响，发现SWS不对称类型对关键性能指标影响不大，而透射率提升更有利于抑制HWP引起的仪器偏振。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来CMB偏振实验需严格控制仪器系统误差，尤其是由旋转半波片（HWP）非理想电磁响应引发的HWP同步仪器偏振。

Method: 采用严格耦合波分析（RCWA）对带亚波长结构（SWS）的多层蓝宝石HWP进行建模与仿真，比较了四种不同SWS不对称构型（含一个实际加工几何）的透射率、偏振效率及仪器偏振响应。

Result: 三种不对称SWS模型在透射率、偏振效率和仪器偏振响应上无显著差异；第四谐波仪器偏振典型值约10^{-4}（CMB频段），可被抑制；实际加工SWS因增强相位延迟，其更高透射率对应更低仪器偏振。

Conclusion: 提升总透射率（含实际SWS形貌效应）比精细调控SWS不对称性更能有效降低HWP诱导的仪器偏振。

Abstract: Future CMB polarization experiments require precise control of instrumental systematics in the search for primordial $B$-mode polarization. A rotating half-wave plate (HWP) provides effective polarization modulation, but its non-ideal electromagnetic response can generate HWP-synchronous instrumental polarization. In this work, we characterize the response of a multi-layer sapphire HWP with anti-reflective sub-wavelength structures (SWS) using rigorous coupled-wave analysis. For three asymmetric SWS models, we find no substantial differences in the transmittance, polarization efficiency, or instrumental-polarization response that can be attributed to the type of SWS asymmetry. The fourth-harmonic instrumental polarization relevant to CMB polarization reconstruction is typically of order $10^{-4}$ in the CMB bands, which is expected to be mitigated. We further evaluate an actually fabricated SWS geometry, whose asymmetry enhances the retardance, and compare its response with that of an idealized model having the same type of asymmetry. Comparison of the realistic SWS with the idealized model shows that higher transmittance tends to be associated with lower instrumental polarization. These results suggest that improving the total transmission, including the effect of the fabricated SWS profile, may be more important for reducing HWP-induced instrumental polarization than tuning the SWS asymmetry itself.

</details>


### [182] [Characterisation of the ILMT 4K x 4K TDI CCD imager](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38362)
*Gokul singh Mehra,Bhavya Ailawadhi,Talat Akhunov,Monalisa Dubey,Naveen Dukiya,Sara Filali,Paul Hickson,Priyanshi Kumari,Kuntal Misra,Vibhore Negi,Kumar Pranshu,Jean Surdej,Sarvesh Kumar Yadav*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文通过两种方法测量了ILMT望远镜所用4K×4K CCD的增益（约3.9 e/ADU）和读出噪声（约6 e），利用晨昏时段非均匀照明图像，在TDI模式下实现参数标定，提升CCD性能理解和校准精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于ILMT是天顶指向液体镜面望远镜，其CCD工作在TDI模式，需准确测定增益和读出噪声以实现精确光度校准和性能评估。

Method: 提出两种基于晨昏非均匀照明CCD图像的参数测量方法：一是沿水平梯度最小的行计算光子噪声方差；二是将图像划分为背景相近区域，分析区域间流量比的涨落；同时利用零曝光偏置帧独立测量读出噪声，并建立信号方差与平均通量的线性关系求解增益。

Result: 两种方法均得出增益约为3.9 e/ADU；由偏置帧测得读出噪声为1.55 ADU（约6 e）。

Conclusion: 所获参数有助于深入理解CCD性能，支撑后续高精度数据处理与科学分析。

Abstract: In this work, we determine the gain and read-out noise of the 4Kx4K CCD imager used with the 4-m International Liquid Mirror Telescope (ILMT). Due to the zenith-pointing characteristic of the telescope, the CCD operates in the Time Delay Integration (TDI) mode for image acquisition. The CCD frames obtained during rising twilight have non-uniform illumination and can potentially be used to calculate important CCD parameters using two approaches. The first approach directly measures variance in flux along rows with minimum horizontal gradient, despite the strong vertical illumination gradient. The second method divides the CCD frame into regions exposed to similar background illumination and analyses the flux ratio variation between the two regions. Mathematically, we establish a linear relationship between the signal variance and average flux, from which gain is determined, while the read-out noise (RON) is measured independently from the bias frames. Both methods provide a gain value of approximately 3.9 e/ADU, while the RON measured from bias frames is 1.55 ADU (~ 6 e). Estimation of these parameters provides a better understanding of the CCD performance and allows for an accurate calibration.

</details>


### [183] [PyMsOfa 2.0: A Pure-Python Implementation of the IAU Standards of Fundamental Astronomy Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38410)
*Dongjie Tan,Jianghui Ji,Jiacheng Liu*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: PyMsOfa 2.0 是一个纯 Python 实现的 SOFA 天文算法库，包含全部 247 个官方例程，支持跨平台、批量处理和高精度验证，已用于 Gaia 和 CHES 等实际天文任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足天体测量计算对权威、可复现算法的需求，同时克服原 SOFA 库仅提供 Fortran 77 和 ANSI C 版本带来的语言兼容性和部署限制。

Method: 将 SOFA C 库全部 247 个官方例程逐行重写为纯 Python（基于 NumPy），不依赖外部编译库；保留所有原始常数、级数与系数；增加非官方但经验证的扩展模型（如 IAU 2006$_{J_2}$/2000A$_{R26}$）；全面对比 C 版本进行精度验证。

Result: 实现跨平台（Linux/Windows/macOS）一致运行；支持 NumPy 数组批量运算；绝大多数例程达机器精度，其余满足 SOFA 文档标称容差；已成功应用于 Gaia 数据归算与 CHES 任务。

Conclusion: PyMsOfa 2.0 提供了一个高保真、易用、可移植且可扩展的 Python 天体测量标准算法库，填补了 SOFA 在现代科学计算生态中的关键空白。

Abstract: Astrometric calculations require authoritative algorithms so that every result is correct and reproducible. The Standards of Fundamental Astronomy (SOFA) service of the International Astronomical Union (IAU) provides such algorithms, officially released only in Fortran 77 and ANSI C. In an earlier paper we presented the first version of PyMsOfa, a Python package wrapping the SOFA C library. Here we present PyMsOfa 2.0, which re-implements the complete SOFA library in pure Python with NumPy. All 247 official SOFA routines are ported directly to Python, requiring no compilation of any external C library and running identically on Linux, Windows, and macOS. The routines operate on NumPy arrays, so arguments can be processed in batch, which is convenient for survey pipelines and Monte Carlo simulations. The package also provides an extension module with validated models not yet part of the official SOFA release, such as the improved precession--nutation model IAU 2006$_{J_2}$/2000A$_{R26}$. The reimplementation reproduces the official SOFA algorithms: all constants, series, and coefficients are taken unchanged from the SOFA source files, and routine names differ only by the pym prefix. We verify every routine against the official SOFA C release: most reproduce the C results to machine precision, the remainder within the accuracy tolerance stated in the SOFA documentation. The package has been applied to Gaia astrometric reduction and used in astrometric studies of the Closeby Habitable Exoplanet Survey (CHES) mission, and is available through PyPI, GitHub, and Zenodo.

</details>


### [184] [Shock detection in meshless finite mass hydrodynamic schemes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38700)
*Ludwig M. Böss,Frederik Groth,Klaus Dolag,Damiano Caprioli*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对无网格有限质量（MFM）流体动力学方法的新型激波探测与曲面重建算法，显著提升了宇宙结构形成模拟中激波识别与能量耗散、宇宙线加速预测的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拉格朗日方法（如SPH）在模拟宇宙结构形成中的复杂激波（如重叠、碰撞、强剪切背景下的激波）方面长期劣于欧拉或类拉格朗日方法，亟需改进。

Method: 开发并实现了首个面向MFM方法的激波探测算法，包含激波面重建；通过解析解和传统SPH激波探测方法进行精度验证；将其应用于宇宙结构形成的数值模拟。

Result: 新算法在激波识别精度上优于传统SPH激波探测方法，并在宇宙结构形成模拟中给出了更可靠的激波能量耗散率和宇宙线加速预测。

Conclusion: 该工作成功弥补了MFM等完全拉格朗日方法在激波建模方面的短板，为高保真宇宙学流体模拟提供了关键工具。

Abstract: Shocks are prevalent in a multitude of astrophysical plasmas. They are essential in the dissipation of gravitational potential energy during structure formation, where they channel it into heat, turbulence, magnetic fields, and particle acceleration. Accurately capturing shocks in simulations of cosmological structure formation is a considerable challenge, due to the complexity of overlapping and colliding shocks, embedded in a medium experiencing complex shear flows. Fully Lagrangian hydrodynamical methods have historically been inferior in this regard compared to Eulerian or pseudo-Lagrangian methods. This work aims to remedy this discrepancy. We present the first efforts to implement a shockfinder algorithm with shock surface reconstruction for meshless finite mass (MFM) hydrodynamic schemes. We show its accuracy compared to analytic predictions and a legacy smoothed particle hydrodynamics (SPH)- based shock-finding method. We apply this new shock-finding method to simulations of cosmic structure formation to obtain improved predictions for shock energy dissipation and cosmic ray (CR) acceleration.

</details>


### [185] [lenscape: Fast, High-resolution $Δχ^2$ Maps of Microlensing Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38764)
*Michael Hippke*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了lenscape，一个开源Python包，用于加速行星微透镜分析中的Δχ²映射，并通过多阶段邻域传播和自适应轮廓细化来抑制局部最小值伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行星微透镜分析中，构建Δχ²映射是主要计算瓶颈，因为每个网格点都需要完整的光变曲线优化及有限源放大率计算。

Method: 提出lenscape工具，采用多阶段邻域传播与自适应轮廓细化技术加速Δχ²映射生成并减少局部最小值干扰。

Result: 在MOA-2022-BLG-091事件中，lenscape在台式机上仅用14分钟复现了50×50的已发表Δχ²图，并在两小时内生成更高分辨率的400×400图。

Conclusion: lenscape显著提升了行星微透镜参数空间扫描的效率与可靠性，为高分辨率Δχ²映射提供了实用、开源的解决方案。

Abstract: Planetary microlensing analyses rely on $Δχ^2$ maps across the binary-lens separation $s$ and mass ratio $q$ to locate degenerate solutions and bound parameter uncertainties. Because every grid point requires a full light-curve optimization with finite-source magnifications, constructing these maps is often the primary computational bottleneck in event modeling. Here I present lenscape, an open-source Python package that accelerates $Δχ^2$ mapping and suppresses local-minimum artifacts through multi-stage neighbor propagation and adaptive contour refinement. Applied to the planetary event MOA-2022-BLG-091, lenscape reproduces the published $50\times50$ map in 14 minutes and generates a $400\times400$ map in under two hours on a desktop computer.

</details>


### [186] [UnEZ: A Fully Unsupervised Self-Templating Neural Network for Redshift and Line Strength Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38816)
*Asha Abd,Karl Glazebrook,Colin Jacobs*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为UnEZ的全无监督、物理启发的机器学习模型，用于在无需红移或谱线强度标签的情况下预测星系光谱的红移和谱线强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着光谱巡天规模扩大，传统依赖模板光谱或人工红移目录的方法难以覆盖大样本中星系的多样性，导致计算负担重且泛化能力差。

Method: UnEZ采用编码器-解码器结构：编码器预测发射/吸收线的振幅与宽度；固定确定性解码器生成唯一模板、估计红移、红移置信度曲线及光谱模型，全程无监督且不依赖任何标签或预设模板。

Result: 在SDSS DR18数据集上验证，SNR > 10时离群率0.459%，NMAD为1.237×10⁻⁴；SNR > 3时离群率3.89%，NMAD为1.471×10⁻⁴，性能媲美当前有监督红移估计算法，并可反向生成模板光谱。

Conclusion: UnEZ证明了全无监督神经网络能从光谱数据中自然涌现红移等物理属性，提供了一种可扩展、免模板、免标签的新范式，适用于未来大规模巡天。

Abstract: As spectroscopic surveys grow, estimating galaxy properties such as metallicity, star formation rate, and redshift becomes computationally cumbersome. Methods of estimating such properties rely on template spectra or curated redshift catalogs, which fail to represent the diversity of objects seen in large-scale surveys. We present UnEZ, a fully unsupervised, physics-informed machine learning model that can predict spectroscopic redshifts and line strengths with no prior redshift or line strength labels. An encoder predicts emission and absorption line amplitudes/widths, and a fixed deterministic decoder constructs a unique template, estimated redshift, redshift confidence curve, and spectral model. UnEZ was tested on the Sloan Digital Sky Survey (SDSS) Data Release 18 due to its large dataset and thoroughly vetted redshift catalog which serves as an independent benchmark. Despite the lack of templates, line strength labels, or redshift labels, UnEZ's performance competes with modern redshift estimation algorithms. At SNR > 10, UnEZ achieves an outlier fraction of 0.459% and a normalized median absolute deviation (NMAD) of $1.237 \times 10^{-4}$. At SNR > 3, UnEZ achieves an outlier fraction of 3.89% and an NMAD of $1.471 \times 10^{-4}$. Furthermore, we demonstrate how UnEZ's line strength and redshift predictions can be utilized for various purposes such as constructing template spectra required in traditional redshift estimation algorithms. UnEZ illustrates how a fully unsupervised neural network can allow redshift, among other galaxy properties, to emerge naturally from the spectra themselves, providing a template-free, label-free approach that scales to the demands of future surveys.

</details>


### [187] [SuperCrude EoS: A Simple, Analytical, and Reasonably Accurate Equation of State for Astrophysical Fluid Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38848)
*Paul C. Duffell,Dillon Hasenour*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: SuperCrude是一种简化的天体物理状态方程，用解析近似替代查表法，在多数参数区域内精度接近helmeos，但在某些区域（如简并、气体与辐射效应相当）有效绝热指数误差约几百分比，温度估算误差达数十百分比；其解析特性保证了严格的热力学一致性，但与核反应网络耦合时核合成产额不如helmeos，需进一步提升温度精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为爆炸性天体物理模拟提供一种既简洁又物理合理、且计算高效的状态方程，避免传统插值数据表的复杂性和低效性。

Method: 构建基于物理启发的紧凑解析近似公式来替代高精度插值数据表（如helmeos），确保热力学一致性，并通过多种数值测试验证其在宽广参数空间中的性能。

Result: 在大部分参数区域，SuperCrude的有效绝热指数精度与helmeos相当；但在简并-气体-辐射竞争区域，其有效绝热指数误差约几百分比，温度估算误差达数十百分比；在白矮星热核爆炸等测试中表现良好，但与刚性核反应网络耦合时核合成产额偏低。

Conclusion: SuperCrude是一种高效、热力学自洽的状态方程，适用于大尺度爆炸性天体物理模拟，但温度精度尚需改进以提升核合成预测能力。

Abstract: We present SuperCrude, a vastly simplified astrophysical equation of state that captures the most important physics needed for explosive astrophysics, but using compact, physically-motivated analytical approximations rather than precise interpolated data tables. We present a number of code tests demonstrating the performance of SuperCrude in a wide range of parameter space. In large regions of parameter space, SuperCrude is precisely accurate to within the accuracy level of helmeos. For some regions (especially where degeneracy, gas, and radiation are comparable) SuperCrude has errors in its effective adiabatic index on the order of a few percent. The solver's estimate of the temperature is less accurate, on the order of tens of percent. Because the solver is analytic, SuperCrude is designed in such a way to guarantee exact thermodynamic consistency. We incorporate SuperCrude into some hydrodynamic and astrophysical test problems, including the thermonuclear explosion of a white dwarf star. When coupled with a stiff nuclear network, SuperCrude does not achieve the same nucleosynthetic yields as helmeos, motivating future work to improve the temperature accuracy further.

</details>


### [188] [Flow-based neural networks for scalable high resolution imaging of VLBI observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38978)
*Lev Kats,Alexey Goldin,Andrey S. Andrianov*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于小波流（WaveletFlow）神经网络架构的贝叶斯采样方法，用于提升甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）成像的像素规模与统计信息量，在M87和HL Tau观测数据上验证了其相比CLEAN算法在保持图像保真度的同时提供更丰富统计推断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统贝叶斯VLBI成像方法（包括基于流的神经网络）随恢复特征数量增加而计算代价高昂，难以扩展至高像素图像分布；需兼顾高分辨率成像与统计可靠性。

Method: 采用WaveletFlow神经网络架构，利用小波变换提升建模图像分布的可扩展性，实现更高像素数的后验图像采样；应用于模拟数据、RadioAstron对M87的存档观测及ALMA对HL Tau的观测。

Result: 在M87和HL Tau数据上重建图像保真度与现有方法相当，但提供了比CLEAN更丰富的统计信息（如像素级不确定性、相关性等），支持对致密天体（如活动星系核）的深入统计分析。

Conclusion: WaveletFlow有效缓解了贝叶斯VLBI成像的可扩展性瓶颈，为高分辨率干涉成像提供了兼具精度与统计严谨性的新范式。

Abstract: Bayesian sampling methods in very-long-baseline interferometry enable reliable imaging of astronomical sources with a record high angular resolution. However, even after the transition from classical MCMC methods to flow-based neural networks, they remain computationally expensive as the number of recovered features grows. We used WaveletFlow neural network architecture to increase the number of pixels in the recovered image distribution, addressing the scaling issues of previous models. We applied the algorithm to both model data and archive RadioAstron project observations of M87 and ALMA telescope observations of HL Tau. While yielding comparable fidelity of the individual images, the algorithm provides more statistical information compared to CLEAN, which may be crucial for future research on compact astronomical objects such as active galactic nuclei.

</details>


### [189] [The Korean Space Telescope (KST): A Dedicated Space Observatory for the Dark Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39094)
*M. James Jee,Ho Seong Hwang,Dongwook Lim,Minjin Kim,Myungshin Im,Sungwook E. Hong,Ho-Gyu Lee,Soung-Chul Yang,David Parkinson,Arman Shafieloo,Jun-Qian Jiang,Haeng Pal Heo,Dongok Ryu,Hagyong Kihm,Hyung Yun Noh,Wooyong Kang,Youngsun Kim*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: KST是一个1.4米宽视场空间望远镜，专用于暗物质与暗能量研究，具备可见光成像与近红外无狭缝光谱观测能力，通过‘婚礼蛋糕’双层巡天策略协同解决暗能量动力学、暗物质本质及引力波暗标准烛光三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前暗物理领域的三大关键问题：暗能量的动力学行为、暗物质的本质，以及利用引力波暗标准烛光作为独立宇宙学标尺。

Method: 采用1.4米口径宽视场空间望远镜，配备衍射极限可见光成像与同步近红外（R~100）无狭缝光谱仪；实施‘婚礼蛋糕’双层巡天策略（2000 deg²宽场+10 deg²星系团深场），结合弱引力透镜、重子声学振荡和近红外标准化Ia型超新星哈勃图等多探针方法。

Result: 预期在暗能量枢轴红移处达到σ(w_p) ~ 0.03精度；绘制从星系团核心至银河系卫星星系尺度的暗物质分布图以检验冷、温及自相互作用暗物质模型；构建深度近红外宿主星系星表以实现无需距离阶梯的H₀测量；同时产出涵盖星系演化、活动星系核、系外行星和银河系的遗产科学数据。

Conclusion: KST作为韩国首个专用天体物理空间任务，凭借其独特观测能力与互补性设计，在暗宇宙研究中占据不可替代地位，并与Rubin、Euclid、Roman等大型巡天项目形成协同。

Abstract: The Korean Space Telescope (KST) is a next-generation 1.4-meter wide-field space observatory, a dedicated probe of the dark sector. Its single instrument provides diffraction-limited visible imaging (~0.15 arcsec in the optical) together with simultaneous near-infrared (R ~ 100) slitless spectrophotometry, so that every detected source is observed in the NIR by the survey itself, with spectral information delivered at a resolution set by its brightness (full R ~ 100 typing at the bright end, synthetic medium-band or broadband photometry below it) and with no separate follow-up, a capability decisive for time-domain science. With it, KST addresses the three outstanding questions of dark physics in concert: the dynamics of dark energy, the nature of dark matter, and gravitational-wave dark sirens as independent cosmological rulers. KST combines measurements of expansion and structure growth to test dark-energy models. A two-tiered "Wedding Cake" strategy (2,000 deg^2 Wide plus 10 deg^2 cluster-targeted Deep Survey) combines weak lensing, baryon acoustic oscillations, and a near-infrared-standardized Type Ia supernova Hubble diagram to constrain dark energy at its pivot redshift (sigma(w_p) ~ 0.03); maps dark matter from cluster cores to galactic substructure to test cold dark matter against warm and self-interacting alternatives; and builds a deep near-infrared host catalog for a distance-ladder-independent H_0 from dark sirens. The same data yield a Legacy Science portfolio (galaxy evolution, active galactic nuclei, exoplanets, and the Milky Way) at no additional observing cost. As Korea's first dedicated astrophysics space mission, KST occupies a niche complementary to the Vera C. Rubin Observatory, Euclid, and Roman.

</details>


### [190] [Tabular Foundation Models Dramatically Outperform Bespoke Photometric Redshift Estimators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39332)
*Biprateep Dey,Joshua S. Speagle*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出使用表格基础模型（TFMs）进行光度红移估计，仅需少量上下文样本即可超越传统定制化模型，尤其在标定数据稀缺时优势显著，为未来成像巡天提供高效、抗偏置的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光度红移估计依赖于稀缺且存在观测选择偏差的光谱训练数据，亟需更高效、鲁棒的方法来缓解数据偏差问题。

Method: 利用表格基础模型（TFMs）通过上下文学习直接预测完整的红移概率分布，无需微调，仅用少量（如1000个）上下文样本进行推理。

Result: 在标准基准数据集上，TFMs在代表性与偏差训练数据下均系统性优于定制化模型；即使基线模型额外加入10,000条标定光谱，TFMs仍保持优势；仅用1000个上下文样本即可媲美或超越使用40,000个样本训练的基线模型。

Conclusion: TFMs提供了一种样本高效、抗选择偏差的光度红移估计新范式，适用于未来大规模成像巡天任务。

Abstract: Estimating galaxy redshifts (a proxy for distance) from imaging data alone, also known as photometric redshifts (photo-$z$'s), is essential for studies of extragalactic astrophysics and cosmology. However, data-driven estimators rely on spectroscopic training datasets, which are scarce and systematically biased toward bright and easily observed galaxies. In this work, we show that tabular foundation models (TFMs), whose synthetic pretraining amounts to a learned prior over regression problems, can outperform bespoke estimators using only in-context learning. Evaluating on a widely used benchmark dataset, we find that TFMs predicting full probability distributions in a single forward pass systematically outperform bespoke estimators across both representative and biased training data regimes, matching or exceeding baselines trained on over $40{,}000$ galaxies with just $1{,}000$ training context examples. We also show that the TFM advantage is largest when little to no calibration data are available, and that it persists even when the bespoke baselines are supplemented with up to $10{,}000$ representative calibration spectra. This shows that TFMs offer a sample-efficient framework for upcoming imaging-only astronomical surveys, where observational selection bias can be mitigated simply by including a small representative calibration sample in context.

</details>


### [191] [Towards an Astronomical Use of New-Generation Geodetic Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39656)
*Víctor Pérez-Díez*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文开发了一套针对VGOS数据的天文校准与成像流程，实现了对多个AGN的多频段偏振成像，并利用结果改进了测地VLBI观测精度，体现了VGOS在天文学与大地测量学中的双重价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VGOS虽为大地测量设计，但其宽频覆盖与对致密AGN的常规观测为天体物理研究提供了新机遇；同时，源结构效应影响测地VLBI精度，需建模校正。

Method: 构建了从VGOS相关器输出到全偏振校准可见度的处理流程，包括线-圆偏振转换、仪器相位/幅度校准、考虑电离层色散的新宽带全局条纹拟合算法，以及正则化最大似然成像；再将重建模型用于修正测地VLBI中的结构相关误差。

Result: 成功对1803+784等6个AGN进行多频强度与偏振成像，测得频率依赖的核偏移、谱指数和法拉第旋转特性；1803+784核偏移指数kr=0.73^{+0.12}_{-0.19}；模型校正显著降低强结构源（如3C418）的闭合延迟散射和残差。

Conclusion: VGOS不仅可作为高精度多频VLBI天文观测平台，其天文建模成果还可反哺大地测量，提升地球定向参数与参考架精度，实现跨学科协同增益。

Abstract: This thesis explores the astronomical use of observations from the VLBI Global Observing System (VGOS), a next-generation geodetic network designed to improve the precision of terrestrial and celestial reference frames and Earth orientation parameters. Although primarily conceived for geodesy, its wide frequency coverage and regular monitoring of compact active galactic nuclei (AGN) provide valuable opportunities for astrophysics.
  We develop a calibration pipeline that transforms VGOS correlator outputs into full-polarization, calibrated visibilities suitable for astronomical imaging. The procedure combines linear-to-circular polarization conversion, instrumental phase and amplitude calibration, and a new wideband global fringe-fitting algorithm that accounts for dispersive ionospheric effects. Regularized maximum likelihood imaging is then used to reconstruct multi-frequency intensity and polarimetric images.
  The pipeline is applied to the blazar 1803+784 and five additional AGN: 3C418, 0059+581, 0613+570, 1849+670, and 2229+695. We measure frequency-dependent core shifts, spectral indices, and Faraday rotation properties. For 1803+784, the fitted core-shift index is $k_r = 0.73^{+0.12}_{-0.19}$, suggesting departures from a simple conical jet in energy equipartition.
  Finally, we apply the reconstructed source models to correct structure-dependent effects in geodetic VLBI observables. The correction reduces closure-delay scatter and post-fit residuals for strongly structured sources, particularly 3C418, while its effectiveness depends on source variability and the temporal proximity of the imaging observations.
  These results demonstrate the dual scientific value of VGOS as a powerful multi-frequency astronomical VLBI instrument and as a means of improving geodetic measurements.

</details>


### [192] [Search for Cosmic Rays in GRANDProto300](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39720)
*Jolan Lavoisier,Aurélien Benoit-Lévy,Kumiko Kotera,Mauricio Bustamante,Marion Guelfand,Nathan Lebas,Takashi Sako,Xishui Tian,for the GRAND Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: GRANDProto300 是 GRAND 实验的原型阵列，旨在通过自主无线电探测验证超高能宇宙线（10^17–10^18.5 eV）的探测技术；本文介绍了其宇宙线搜索流程（含四步滤波）及一个基于CNN的噪声/信号识别模型，并报告了在2025年7–10月实测数据上的效率与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证自主无线电探测技术对超高能宇宙线（10^17–10^18.5 eV）的有效性，以研究银河系到河外宇宙线源的过渡区域。

Method: 构建包含46–63个天线的GRANDProto300原型阵列；设计四步滤波流水线（剔除时-空聚集噪声、偏振筛选、天顶角筛选、触发延迟筛选）；开发并训练基于CNN的信号识别模型，使用模拟和真实宇宙线信号数据。

Result: 在2025年7–10月实测数据上评估了滤波流水线的效率；CNN模型在模拟和真实数据上均展现出良好识别准确率。

Conclusion: GRANDProto300已稳定运行并产出有效数据；所提滤波流程与CNN识别方法为后续GRAND大规模阵列提供了关键技术验证与可行方案。

Abstract: GRANDProto300 is a prototype array of the GRAND experiment, designed to validate the technique of autonomous radio-detection of astroparticles by detecting cosmic rays with energies between 10$^{17} - 10^{18.5}$ eV. This observation will further enable the study of the Galactic-to-extragalactic source transition region. Between November 2024 and June 2025, 46 antennas were operational, and since July 2025, 63 antennas have been collecting data stably. We present here our cosmic-ray search pipeline, that involves several filtering steps: (1) exclusion of clustered (in time and space) noise events, (2) polarization cut, (3) zenith arrival cut, and (4) delay in expected trigger cut. The efficiency is evaluated on the latest batch of data from July 2025 to October 2025. Additionally, we present an identification layer that is being developed: a CNN-based algorithm to discriminate between noise and cosmic-ray signals, using signal traces. We present the accuracy of our model, trained both on simulations and on real cosmic-ray signal datasets.

</details>


### [193] [The Radio Neutrino Observatory in Greenland (RNO-G) - System Design and Instrumentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39740)
*Nils Heyer for the RNO-G Collaboration,Steven Barwick,AJ Nielsen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: RNO-G项目开发了新型电热熔装置，用于在格陵兰冰盖中精确部署LPDA天线，显著提升定位精度，并完成了实地测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提高RNO-G探测宇宙中EeV级中微子时天线的部署精度与效率，需解决传统安装方式导致的位置与方向不确定性问题。

Method: 设计并应用新型电热熔装置安装LPDA天线，实现对天线在冰层中x/y方向±2 cm及旋转方向±1°的精确定位；同时开展Summit Station现场测试验证系统性能。

Result: 新电子系统成功简化了天线部署流程，并将定位不确定性降低至±2 cm（x/y）和±1°（旋转）；实地测试证实其可行性与稳定性。

Conclusion: 该电子系统及电热熔技术为RNO-G高精度无线电探测提供了可靠硬件支撑，提升了未来中微子事件重建的空间分辨率与探测灵敏度。

Abstract: The Radio Neutrino Observatory in Greenland (RNO-G) is designed to measure cosmic neutrinos in the EeV range through an array of radio antennas detecting nanosecond-long radio flashes. In this contribution, the RNO-G electronic system is outlined, illustrating how signals are measured from the antennas to the recorded pulses. We present new instrumentation developments, focusing on electric melters to install LPDA antennas into the Greenlandic ice sheet. The new system will simplify deployment and reduce positioning uncertainty to $\pm$2cm (x/y) and $\pm$1°(rotational). We report on field tests from the RNO-G site at Summit Station.

</details>


### [194] [Robust statistical significance for Gravitational Wave inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39785)
*Alexandre Sébastien Göttel*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出使用证据网络（evidence networks）来解决引力波数据分析中贝叶斯因子（Bayes factors）在统计解释性、噪声鲁棒性和大规模事件分析中的偏差与不确定性问题，实现高速、高精度、无似然（likelihood-free）的模型比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯因子在引力波事件级和总体级分析中广泛使用，但其统计意义受限于：（1）噪声下分布未知，难以量化显著性；（2）嵌套采样输出随事件数增加引入累积偏差；（3）约20%含瞬态噪声事件中结果不可靠；（4）依赖高斯平稳噪声假设。

Method: 引入证据网络——一种基于学习数据集间可区分性的深度生成模型，可快速（每秒达10^5次）推断贝叶斯因子，并构建不同信号假设下的贝叶斯因子背景分布以评估统计显著性。

Result: 实现了对最多10^4个事件的无偏总体参数推断；验证了方法在瞬态噪声存在下的鲁棒性；为摆脱高斯平稳噪声假设的似然无关（likelihood-free）分析奠定基础。

Conclusion: 证据网络提供了一种高效、可扩展、统计严谨且更稳健的贝叶斯因子计算框架，有望成为下一代引力波数据统计分析的核心工具。

Abstract: The field of gravitational-wave data analysis heavily relies on Bayes factors to make statements about astrophysical effects in events detected by the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA collaboration. This is true on an event level, for example when discussing the potential detection of precession or eccentricity, as well as on a population level. For the latter, perhaps the main limiting factor is the reliance on the output from event-level nested sampling analyses, which is known to introduce growing uncertainties and biases as the number of detections increases. In both cases, while Bayes factors are useful when comparing different models, because the underlying distribution of Bayes factors in noise is unknown, it is often not possible to use them to make quantifiable statistical statements. Their statistical meaning is also questionable in the at least $20\%$ of gravitational-wave events that contain transient noise artifacts. This paper shows that it is possible to use evidence networks, which can infer Bayes factors after learning the distinguishability between datasets, to solve all of the aforementioned problems. By being able to calculate up to $10^5$ Bayes factors per second, we can construct background distributions of Bayes factors under different signal assumptions to estimate significance. We show that the high precision achieved allows us to scale the results from up to $10^4$ events with no discernable bias on population-level quantities, while laying the foundations for a likelihood-free method that does not rely on the assumption of Gaussian and stationary noise.

</details>


### [195] [Preparation of a celestial field for precise VLBI observations of the JUICE spacecraft: a test case of the Venus flyby](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39925)
*Krisztina Perger,Sándor Frey,Giuseppe Cimò,Dominic Dirkx,Jasper Edwards,Sam Fayolle,Judit Fogasy,Krisztina É. Gabányi,Leonid I. Gurvits,Máté Krezinger,Guifré Molera Calvés,Vidhya Pallichadath,Noor Masdiana Md Said,Bert Vermeersen*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为支持JUICE任务（木星冰卫星探测器）在2025年8月金星飞越期间的高精度轨道测定，开展的VLBI相位参考校准源搜寻与定位工作；通过EVN在8.4 GHz观测了9个候选源和3个RFC源，成功精确定位其中4个ICC候选源和全部3个RFC源（精度达亚毫角秒），虽未发现适合作为VLBI相位参考的亮致密源，但新测位置与流量可用于后续飞越时航天器信号检测验证，并发现两个源具有特殊射电结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持JUICE任务金星引力助推阶段的高精度航天器状态矢量重建，需在黄道附近补充致密射电源以增强甚长基线干涉测量（VLBI）的相位参考能力，因现有天球参考架（ICRF）在该区域密度不足。

Method: 采用欧洲VLBI网（EVN）在8.4 GHz开展‘摆动式’相位参考VLBI观测，针对JUICE金星飞越路径附近的9个在波束内校准候选源（ICC）和3个来自射电基本星表（RFC）的已知亮致密源，通过相对天体测量将其位置与邻近ICRF源关联。

Result: 成功探测并成像4个ICC候选源和全部3个RFC源，坐标测定精度达亚毫角秒（sub-mas）；但所有ICC源均因太暗或空间展宽而不适合作为VLBI相位参考校准源；新获得的位置与流量数据可用于后续金星飞越时JUICE航天器信号的检测验证；进一步分析揭示J0843+1826呈毫角秒尺度分辨结构，J0843+1814为一青年射电星系，可能属于星系团成员。

Conclusion: 尽管本次观测未获得理想的VLBI相位参考校准源，但显著提升了JUICE任务关键路径附近天球参考架的覆盖与精度，所获源参数具备实际任务支持价值；同时拓展了对特定射电源物理性质的认知。

Abstract: The Jupiter Icy Moons Explorer mission, launched in 2023, is on its way to the Jovian system, to arrive in 2031. The Planetary Radio Interferometry and Doppler Experiment performs radio astronomical observations of the spacecraft already in its interplanetary cruise phase. Since the sky density of potential compact radio sources suitable as phase-reference calibrators in very long baseline interferometry observations is low, densifying the celestial reference frame along the Ecliptic is an essential task in support of JUICE. In preparation for the JUICE gravity assist manoeuvre at Venus in August 2025, to support the accurate reconstruction of the spacecraft state vector, we selected in-beam calibrator source candidates along the JUICE trajectory. Additionally, we attempted to link known nearby bright compact reference sources from the Radio Fundamental Catalog to the International Celestial Reference Frame. We performed nodding-style phase-referencing VLBI observations with the European VLBI Network at 8.4GHz, targeting nine in-beam calibrator candidate and three RFC sources in the celestial vicinity of JUICE's trajectory during its Venus flyby. We linked their positions to nearby ICRF sources by means of relative astrometry. Four of the ICC targets and all RFC sources were detected and imaged with the EVN, and their coordinates determined with typically sub-mas precision. Although none of the targeted ICC sources proved suitable as VLBI phase-reference calibrators because of being too faint or resolved, their newly-determined positions and flux densities could in principle be used to verify the detection of the spacecraft at the time of the actual flyby around Venus. During the detailed analysis of the ICC targets, we found that the structure of one of them, J0843+1826 is resolved on mas scale, and the other one, J0843+1814 is a young radio galaxy, likely a member of a cluster.

</details>


### [196] [Identifying narrow-band mutual coupling resonances in SKA-low stations using frequency-independent current basis functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40008)
*John Cumner,Oscar S. D. O'Hara,Quentin Gueuning,Anthony Brown,Fred Dulwich,Oskar Zetterstrom*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过构建频响无关电流基函数来识别孔径阵列中互耦引起的窄带谱特征的方法，以减轻其对21-cm功率谱测量（如SKA-low）的干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧密排列天线单元间的互耦会在嵌入单元方向图（EEPs）中引入窄带谱特征，威胁21-cm功率谱（尤其是宇宙再电离EoR信号）的精确测量。

Method: 利用频率相关基函数的奇异值分解（SVD），构造频率无关的电流基函数，从而识别互耦导致的窄带共振特征；在三单元线性阵列和SKA低频阵伪随机布局站上进行了验证。

Result: 在SKA-log-periodic伪随机站中发现多个强窄带共振（如50.8 MHz和83.6 MHz），被动天线耦合功率可接近或超过激励天线；模拟延迟功率谱显示含共振的频段会导致显著前景泄漏；最终识别出两段无共振污染的干净频带：59.2–76.2 MHz和84.2–103.3 MHz。

Conclusion: 所提方法可有效识别并规避互耦引起的窄带干扰，为SKA-low等低频射电望远镜在EoR观测中选取干净频带提供依据。

Abstract: Mutual coupling between closely spaced elements in aperture arrays introduces narrow-band spectral features in the Embedded Element Patterns (EEPs), threatening measurements of the 21-cm power spectrum with instruments such as SKA-low. We detail a method to identify such features through the construction of frequency-independent current basis functions, utilising a singular value decomposition of frequency-dependent basis functions. We demonstrate the method on a three-element linear array and full pseudo-random station of SKA log-periodic antennas and examine the current power induced by mutual coupling. For the pseudo-random layout station we note many narrow-band resonance features, including strong resonances at 50.8~MHz and 83.6~MHz, where the coupled power on the passive antennas can approach or exceed that of the excited antenna. Simulated delay power spectra for the SKA-low core show that observing in frequency bands including resonance features causes large foreground leakage within the delay power spectra, obscuring the EoR window. For the pseudo-random layout station with SKALA4.0 we identify two significant frequency bands from 59.2--76.2~MHz and 84.2--103.3~MHz which are free of contamination from current resonances, preserving the EoR window in these frequency ranges.

</details>


### [197] [Temporal regularization of petal modes in SCAO systems: The Soft-Limiter approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40184)
*Guido Agapito,Jueqi Lin,Carmelo Arcidiacono,Giulia Carlà,Enrico Pinna,Fabio Rossi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MMSE Soft-Limiter的新型控制器，用于在极大望远镜（ELT）的单共轭自适应光学（SCAO）系统中有效抑制由分段镜面引起的‘岛效应’（Island Effect），即差分活塞（petal modes）累积导致点扩散函数（PSF）退化的问题；该方法在保持连续模式校正能力的同时，显著提升金字塔波前传感器（PWFS）对差分活塞的鲁棒性，尤其在中等至较差视宁度（0.6″–1.2″）下将活塞误差限制在单个条纹内，避免锁相丢失，并为慢速拼接传感器留出整数模糊度解析空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决ELT中因分段主镜和支撑结构导致的 pupil fragmentation 引起的差分活塞（petal modes）在SCAO系统中难以被PWFS有效探测的问题，防止由此引发的‘岛效应’——即未校正的活塞误差累积、PSF严重劣化。

Method: 提出一种确定性、轻量级的MMSE Soft-Limiter控制器：基于von Kármán湍流先验构建子空间MMSE估计器，并引入仅作用于相对petal自由度的连续时间正则化（即带泄漏增益的漏积分器）；通过SPECULA端到端仿真验证其性能，并理论推导泄漏增益g的有效操作窗口（g ≲ g_eff/π）。

Result: 在ANDES/ELT仿真中，经典积分器在视宁度≥1.0″时失锁并跨多条纹漂移，而MMSE Soft-Limiter在0.6″–1.2″全范围内将差分活塞严格约束于单个条纹内；在恶劣湍流下有效抑制petal偏移及其科学波段影响，且不牺牲良好视宁度下的连续校正性能，亦无边缘促动器耦合等空间抑制方法带来的Strehl损失；同时明确了泄漏增益窗口随视宁度变窄、临界点约在1.0″处，超出后整数模糊度解析完全依赖慢速拼接通道。

Conclusion: MMSE Soft-Limiter是一种兼顾实时性与物理一致性的高效petal控制方案，为ELT的PWFS主导SCAO系统提供了稳健的差分活塞管理策略，并揭示了快慢双通道协同设计的关键约束——即在强湍流下，整数相位模糊度解析必须由独立慢速拼接系统承担，这对ELT波前传感架构设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Extremely large telescopes (ELTs) rely on segmented mirrors and thick support structures that fragment the pupil. In single-conjugate AO (SCAO), this produces differential pistons, or petal modes. The Pyramid Wavefront Sensor (PWFS), baseline for ANDES, MICADO, HARMONI, and GMT-NGSAO, has poor sensitivity to these modes in the visible under realistic residual turbulence. We aim to prevent the "Island Effect", where poorly observed differential pistons accumulate and degrade the PSF, without compromising continuous-mode correction. We propose the Minimum Mean Square Error (MMSE) Soft-Limiter, a deterministic, lightweight controller combining a subspace MMSE estimator derived from von Kármán priors with a continuous temporal regularization. It acts as a leaky integrator applied only to the relative petal degrees of freedom. End-to-end SPECULA simulations of ELT/ANDES show that, while a classical integrator loses lock and wanders across multiple fringes at seeing $\ge 1.0''$, the MMSE Soft-Limiter confines the differential piston within a single fringe over the $0.6-1.2''$ range. It bounds the petal excursion and its science-band impact under severe turbulence, while keeping optimal continuous-mode correction at good seeing, without the Strehl penalty of spatial mitigation such as Edge Actuator Coupling. We derive and verify the operating window for the leakage gain, $g \lesssim g_{\mathrm{eff}}/π$: below it, the fast loop retains genuine physical steps up to $λ_{WFS}/2$ with a small droop. This maintains a stable modulo-$λ_{WFS}$ wavefront on the fast sensor, letting a slower phasing sensor resolve integer ambiguities. As the window narrows with turbulence, its boundaries cross near $1.0''$ seeing; beyond this, retaining physical discontinuities becomes the sole responsibility of the phasing channel, a key design driver for segmented ELTs.

</details>


### [198] [Model-Based Retrieval of Four Years of JWST/NIRCam Wavefronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40187)
*Shrishmoy Ray,Benjamin Pope,Louis Desdoigts,Hayden Greer,Milo Langker,Peter Tuthill,Marshall Perrin,Anand Sivaramakrishnan,Alexandra Greenbaum,Laurent Pueyo,Max Charles*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了camino，一个基于JAX和dLUX的开源算法，用于建模NIRCam仪器的波前，显著提高了对JWST离焦图像数据拟合的精度，并重建了自科学运行开始至2026年初的波前历史。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高JWST镜面段表面与对准监测的精度，支持高保真度数据恢复，并推动开放、可集成、物理驱动的预测性校准方法。

Method: 开发了基于JAX和可微分Fresnel光学代码dLUX的开源算法camino，采用正则化最大似然推断，并以全部NIRCam波前传感数据为条件进行训练。

Result: 将离焦数据拟合的约化卡方值从60–65显著降低至12–13，并成功重建了JWST自科学运行起至2026年初的波前历史。

Conclusion: camino作为当前STScI相位检索算法的并行开源替代方案，使更广泛的科学界能获取高精度波前估计，并直接集成到科学分析流程中，朝向无需专用参考星的预测性校准迈出关键一步。

Abstract: The fidelity of data recovered by the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) depends on precise monitoring of the mirror segments' surfaces and alignment. To this end, every few days the observatory takes pairs of defocused images of a reference star with its NIRCam instrument; a closed-source phase retrieval package is then employed to infer the wavefront. We here present camino (Computational Aberration Modelling and Inference for NIRCam Observations), an open-source algorithm to model NIRCam built in JAX using the differentiable Fresnel optics code dLUX and regularised maximum likelihood inference that has been conditioned on the entire archive of NIRCam wavefront sensing data. We typically improve the reduced chi-squared in fits to the defocused data from 60-65 to 12-13. We reconstruct the wavefront history of JWST from the beginning of science operations to early 2026. This open source implementation operates in parallel to the current STScI phase retrieval algorithm, allowing access by the broader scientific community to high-accuracy wavefront estimation and enabling its direct integration into science-analysis workflows. This represents a major step towards the ultimate goal of predictive calibration methods based on physics-based forward modelling, obviating the need for time-consuming observation of contemporaneous dedicated reference stars.

</details>


### [199] [posterior-landscape: morphological feature finding in two-dimensional probability densities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40272)
*Michele Mancarella,Viola De Renzis,Alessandro Agapito*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: Posterior-landscape 是一个 Python 框架，用于识别和表征二维概率密度中的形态特征，融合拓扑数据分析、密度脊估计与尺度空间图像分析，支持贝叶斯推断中不确定性传播，并应用于引力波群体重建以提取有宇宙学意义的结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统识别和解释贝叶斯后验分布（尤其是引力波群体重建）中的形态特征，并评估其宇宙学信息含量，亟需一种能量化不确定性、连接多维与一维结构、支持跨后验比较的工具。

Method: 结合拓扑数据分析、统计密度脊估计和尺度空间图像分析，提取二维密度中的局域浓度、谷、脊及斜率变化；在贝叶斯框架下传播特征恢复、几何与对应关系的不确定性；建立二维结构与一维边缘分布特征的关联，并量化其与其他参数的相关性。

Result: 在可控密度上验证有效；成功应用于引力波群体重建，实现了对群体特征的系统识别、物理解释与宇宙学相关性评估；生成了可用于模型预测与观测重建之间形态比较的标准化目录。

Conclusion: Posterior-landscape 提供了一种鲁棒、可解释且可推广的形态分析范式，弥合了高维贝叶斯推断与物理可解释特征提取之间的鸿沟，尤其适用于多信使天文学等复杂概率建模场景。

Abstract: We present posterior-landscape, a Python framework to identify and characterize morphological features in two-dimensional probability densities. The framework combines concepts from topological data analysis, statistical density-ridge estimation, and scale-space image analysis. It extracts localized concentrations, valleys, ridges, and changes of slope; for Bayesian reconstructions, it propagates uncertainty in feature recovery, geometry, and correspondence across posteriors. The framework also connects two-dimensional structures to features in one-dimensional marginals, and quantifies their correlation with other inferred parameters. We validate the method on controlled densities, and demonstrate its use on gravitational-wave population reconstructions, where it provides a systematic way to identify and interpret population features and assess which ones carry information relevant for cosmology. The resulting catalogues also offer a common morphological representation for future comparisons between predictions and population reconstructions.

</details>


### [200] [Measuring Precipitable Water Vapor through Dual-Band GPS at Rubin Observatory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40363)
*Anja Weyant,W. M. Wood-Vasey,Elana K. Urbach,Sara Gelven,Marcos Tamargo-Arizmedi*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了利用GNSS信号延迟测量智利Rubin天文台上空可降水量（PWV）的初步结果，验证了方法的准确性与精度，并分析了PWV的季节性、日变化及其与风速的关系，以支持LSST高精度天文观测需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面天文观测中，大气可降水量（PWV）是波长>7000 Å观测的主要系统误差源，尤其对超新星宇宙学等高精度科学目标影响显著；Rubin天文台虽地处干燥地区，但仍需连续、高时间分辨率的PWV监测。

Method: 采用GNSS信号延迟反演PWV，通过与国际GNSS服务（IGS）结果比对验证方法；在Allegheny天文台完成接收机（Trimble Net R9）调试后部署至Rubin天文台，自2025年9月5日起持续观测。

Result: 测得南半球春季（9–11月）平均PWV为3.0 kg/m²，夏季（12–2月）为5.4 kg/m²；PWV存在明显日变化，振幅1.2–3.8 kg/m²，且与东西向风速高度相关；GNSS反演方法达到0.9 kg/m²准确度和0.36 kg/m²精度。

Conclusion: 基于GNSS的PWV监测方法具备高精度、高时间分辨率和全天候能力，可有效支撑LSST等对大气校正要求严苛的天文科学项目。

Abstract: Precipitable water vapor (PWV) in Earth's atmosphere is a known source of systematic error in ground-based astronomical measurements, particularly at wavelengths greater than 7,000 angstroms. PWV produces wavelength-dependent extinction in the spectral energy distributions of observed sources. For high-precision applications, such as supernova cosmology, the transmission function must be measured accurately and precisely for each observation. The Vera C. Rubin Observatory is located in the Chilean Andes, a region known for dry conditions. However, even at this location, time-variable absorption by water vapor is a factor. Signal delays from Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) offer a robust, easy, 24-hour monitoring of PWV. In this paper we present measurements from the first six months of GNSS-PWV at Rubin Observatory. We verified our analysis by replicating results from the International GNSS Service (IGS), demonstrating high consistency. We then installed and commissioned a Trimble Net R9 receiver at Allegheny Observatory for a commissioning run before it was relocated to Rubin Observatory. At Rubin Observatory we have been continuously measuring PWV since 2025 September 5. We find average PWV levels of 3.0 kg/m$^2$ in Southern Hemisphere spring (September to November) and 5.4 kg/m$^2$ in summer (December to February). The PWV at Rubin also has a clear daily variation with an amplitude of 1.2-3.8 kg/m$^2$ that is highly correlated with the east-west wind strength. We conclude that the proposed GNSS-based processing method achieves an accuracy of 0.9 kg/m$^2$ with 0.36 kg/m$^2$ precision, confirming the capability for continuous, high-temporal-resolution PWV monitoring to support demanding LSST science cases.

</details>


### [201] [Tailoring Infra-Red Filters for Global Mapping of Enceladus' Thermal Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40456)
*D. G. Lyster,C. J. A. Howett,N. E. Bowles,K. Nowicki,R. Evans,T. J. Warren*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Enceladus Thermal Mapper (ETM)仪器的初步可行性与设计研究，旨在为土卫二提供高分辨率连续热成像，以深入理解其地质活动与潜在宜居性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 土卫二拥有地下海洋和活跃的冰火山活动，是天体生物学研究的关键目标；但卡西尼任务受限于其绕土星轨道，仅能提供有限的高分辨率飞越热数据，亟需专用绕轨热成像设备。

Method: 基于TechDemoSat-1、火星气候探测仪和月球探路者等任务的技术积累，设计了一款轻量、低功耗、384×288像素传感器的热成像仪（ETM），运行于土卫二轨道，工作高度150 km，分辨率达80 m/pixel；并构建数字滤光组件模型以优化热辐射测量性能。

Result: ETM可实现土卫二表面（含冬季极区低至45 K）的连续高分辨率热测绘，显著提升对其全球传导热损失的约束能力，并更精准刻画与定位其活跃的南极地形。

Conclusion: ETM将为理解土卫二的地质演化机制和评估其宜居性提供关键、全面的温度数据支撑，具备重要科学价值与工程可行性。

Abstract: We present the results of an initial study into the feasibility and design of the Enceladus Thermal Mapper (ETM) instrument, designed to provide detailed temperature data for Enceladus, an icy moon of Saturn identified by the Cassini Mission as a prime candidate for astrobiological study due to its subsurface ocean and active cryovolcanism. Building on heritage from previous missions including TechDemoSat-1, Mars Climate Sounder, and Lunar Trailblazer, ETM aims to measure temperature variations across Enceladus' surface, including at the poles during the extreme cold of winter, when they can reach as low as 45 K. Where Cassini provided limited high-resolution flyby data of Enceladus due to its Saturn-centric orbit, ETM, in orbit around the icy moon will offer continuous high-resolution thermal mapping with a 384 x 288-pixel sensor, capable of 80 m/pixel resolution from 150 km altitude. This will improve constraints on the Enceladus' global conductive heat loss, and better characterise and contextualise its active South Polar Terrain. To optimise the instrument's filters for thermal emission measurements, a digital model of the filter assembly was developed. This lightweight, low-power instrument is capable of significantly advancing the understanding of Enceladus' geological behaviour and evaluating its potential habitability by providing comprehensive temperature data essential for interpreting its evolution.

</details>

### [202] [Hot off the Stacks: X-rays in Romulus25, Part I - Comparisons to Observations from eRosita](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37763)
*N. Nicole Sanchez,Andrew Benson,Gwen Rudie,Ben Oppenheimer,Hannah Bish,Emily Silich,Phil Hopkins,John ZuHone,Michael Tremmel,Tom Quinn*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用Romulus25宇宙学模拟数据，前向建模软X射线观测，分析了750多个星系热晕介质（CGM）的X射线表面亮度与光度，并与eROSITA等观测结果对比，发现模拟结果整体偏亮，尤其在内晕区域；引入零金属丰度辐射模型后显著降低X射线光度，改善与观测（尤其是银河系质量星系）的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释近期eROSITA巡天观测到的银河系质量及以上星系热CGM X射线辐射特征，并检验Romulus25模拟中热CGM物理模型（特别是缺乏金属线冷却）对X射线辐射预测的影响。

Method: 基于Romulus25模拟中750多个恒星质量10–11.5 M⊙的星系，使用pyXSIM和SIXTE前向建模0.5–2.0 keV软X射线观测，提取热CGM（T>10⁶ K）的表面亮度剖面和X射线光度L_X；按恒星质量分组堆叠比较，并尝试零金属丰度辐射模型以评估金属冷却缺失的影响。

Result: Romulus25模拟的平均X射线表面亮度剖面比Chadayammuri et al. (2022a) 和Zhang et al. (2024a) 观测更亮，尤其在内CGM；平均L_X也高于Zhang et al. (2024b) 等观测及EAGLE/TNG/SIMBA模拟，仅在银河系质量端基本一致；采用零金属丰度模型后，L_X下降近一个量级，显著改善与观测（尤其银河系质量星系）的符合程度。

Conclusion: Romulus25模拟中缺失金属线冷却导致热CGM X射线辐射被高估，尤其在较高质量星系中；零金属模型虽为简化，但表明金属丰度和冷却物理对X射线CGM诊断至关重要；未来模拟需纳入更精细的热力学与辐射过程以匹配X射线观测。

Abstract: Recent observations from the from Spectrum Roentgen Gamma (SRG)/eROSITA all-sky survey data have helped to illuminate the hot circumgalactic medium (CGM) around galaxies at Milky Way-masses and above. In our study, we explore the hot X-ray emitting (T \textgreater 10$^6$ K) component of the CGM in over 750 galaxies from the Romulus25 cosmological simulation (Tremmel et al. 2017) between 10.0 < log(M$_*/M_{\odot}$) < 11.5. We forward-model soft X-ray [0.5 - 2.0] keV observations of our simulated galaxies using pyXSIM and SIXTE to measure the X-ray surface brightness (SB) profiles of the hot CGM. We then compare the average X-ray surface brightness profiles of six stellar-mass selected sub-samples to compare to the observational results from Chadayammuri et al. (2022a) and Zhang et al. 2024a. Broadly, we find that our stacked SB profiles are too bright compared to observations from Chadayammuri et al. (2022a) and Zhang et al.(2024a) particularly in the inner CGM. Additionally, we calculate the X-ray luminosity (L$_χ$) of the hot CGM for each galaxy and compare the average L$_χ$ across stellar mass to observations from Zhang et al. (2024b) as well as measurements from the EAGLE, TNG, SIMBA simulations. Across stellar mass, the average L$_χ$ of galaxies from Romulus25 are brighter than observations except at the lower, Milky Way-mass end where we are in relative agreement with both observations and most other simulations. We also consider a zero-metallicity emission model, because Romulus25 does not have metal-line cooling, finding the average L$_χ$ decreases by nearly an order-of-magnitude, which better matches observations particularly in the Milky Way-mass regime.

</details>


### [203] [Resolving the thermal component of stellar feedback with the "anchor-and-refine" scheme in galactic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37767)
*Chengzhe Li,Hui Li,Wei Cui,Federico Marinacci,Laura Sales,Mark Vogelsberger,Paul Torrey*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为“anchor-and-refine”的新型恒星反馈方案，通过锚定恒星粒子所在网格并根据是否解析超新星冷却半径自适应选择热能注入或动量沉积，从而更真实地模拟热气体演化与星系风形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有星系尺度模拟常无法解析超新星冷却半径，导致基于动量的子网格模型低估热气体作用，难以准确刻画恒星反馈对星系物质循环的调控。

Method: 提出‘anchor-and-refine’反馈方案：在恒星粒子所在网格（host cell）设锚点防止其位移，并保留热反馈储库；若冷却半径被解析，则全部能量以热能形式注入该网格；否则细化该网格，并将终端动量沉积到周围气体网格中。

Result: 理想化测试与高分辨模拟结果一致，成功再现超级气泡性质并保持高温内区；在孤立银河系质量星系模拟中，生成更精细X射线辐射结构、清晰明亮的中心中平面，并获得更平滑的恒星形成历史；热气体主导外流质量加载（z>1 kpc时质量加载因子约0.2），维持稳态能量驱动风，而冷外流随高度下降。

Conclusion: 该方案通过更好保持热气体结构及热/动能分配，显著改进了有限分辨率下对热气体、恒星形成和反馈的建模能力，适用于未来星系尺度及宇宙学zoom-in模拟。

Abstract: (abridged) Stellar feedback regulates galactic baryon cycles, but galaxy-scale simulations often cannot resolve supernova cooling radii. Momentum-based subgrid models can consequently underresolve the hot gas around young stars. We develop an "anchor-and-refine" feedback scheme that anchors a host cell near each star particle, preventing its displacement and preserving a thermal-feedback reservoir. When the cooling radius is resolved, all supernova energy is injected into the host cell as thermal energy; otherwise, the host cell is refined and terminal momentum is deposited into surrounding gas cells. Idealized tests agree with high-resolution simulations, yielding consistent superbubble properties and preserving hot interiors without artificial cooling. In an isolated Milky Way-mass galaxy, the scheme captures finer X-ray-emitting structures and a sharply defined, X-ray-bright central mid-plane. It also produces a smoother star formation history than pure hybrid feedback. Hot gas dominates outflow mass loading, with the mass-loading factor plateauing at approximately 0.2 at heights z>1 kpc. Phase decomposition indicates that hot gas sustains a steady, energy-driven wind, whereas cooler outflows decline with height because of radiative cooling and gravitational fallback. These results highlight the role of thermal feedback in driving galactic winds. By preserving hot superbubble interiors and the partition of thermal and kinetic energy, anchor-and-refine improves the treatment of hot gas, star formation, and feedback at limited resolution, supporting future applications to galaxy-scale and cosmological zoom-in simulations.

</details>


### [204] [Extra! Extra! X-ray Emission!: X-rays in Romulus25, Part II - Physical Drivers of X-ray Emission in the CGM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37769)
*N. Nicole Sanchez,Andrew Benson,Gwen Rudie,Ben Oppenheimer,Hannah Bish,John ZuHone,Michael Tremmel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过Romulus25和PIONEER模拟，揭示了银河系质量星系周围热CGM（T > 10^5.5 K）的X射线辐射主要由热气体质量和其平均金属丰度共同决定；首次指出金属丰度偏高的CGM可能系统性地偏高X射线观测亮度，从而影响对真实气体质量的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确表征银河系质量星系周围百万开尔文量级的热星系周介质（CGM）对理解星系演化至关重要，但其X射线辐射的物理起源尚不清晰。

Method: 基于Romulus25（R25）宇宙学模拟与PIONEER高分辨率zoom-in模拟，分析热CGM气体质量（M_gas, T>10^5.5K）、X射线光度（ε_χ）及平均金属丰度（Z̄）之间的相关性，并结合恒星与AGN反馈能量注入机制进行物理解释。

Result: 发现：1）热CGM气体质量与X射线光度强相关；2）二者均与热CGM平均金属丰度存在次级相关；3）热气体质量由恒星与AGN反馈总能量共同决定；4）相同质量下，金属丰度越高，软X射线辐射越亮。

Conclusion: 金属丰度是影响MW质量星系X射线观测亮度的关键因素，高金属丰度CGM会导致X射线观测对气体质量的高估，需在观测解释中予以校正。

Abstract: Characterizing the hot (T ~ 10$^{6}$ K) circumgalactic medium (CGM) around Milky Way-mass (MW-mass) galaxies is critical to a full understanding of galaxy evolution. In this second paper of the "X-rays in Romulus25" series, we unpack the specific physical processes giving rise to the presence of hot X-ray emitting gas in the halos of galaxies from the Romulus25 (R25) simulation, focusing on L$_{*}$ galaxies. We determine that a strong correlation appears between the mass of hot CGM gas, M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$, and the X-ray emissivity, $ε_χ$, measured in the first paper of this series, (Sanchez et al. 2026a, submitted). There is also a secondary correlation between M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$ and $ε_χ$ with the average metallicity of the hot CGM gas, $\bar{Z}_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$. We use the well-studied PIONEER simulations from Sanchez et al. (2019), a set of zoom-in MW-mass galaxies run with the genetic modification technique of Roth et al. (2016) and the same physics as R25, to interpret these correlations. We determine that the presence of hot X-ray emitting gas is determined by two key processes: 1) the quantity of hot gas mass in the CGM, M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$, is set by the combined total energy injection of $both$ stellar and AGN feedback, and 2) together the average temperature and metallicity of the hot CGM changes the soft X-ray spectrum of the gas resulting in brighter metal-rich CGM and dimmer metal-poor CGM for the same quantity of M$_{gas, T > 10^{5.5} K}$. These results indicate, for the first time, that metal-rich gas halos may bias X-ray observations of MW-mass galaxies.

</details>


### [205] [Not so dead after all: young stars in the massive S0 NGC 3115](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37774)
*João P. V. Benedetti,Alexandre Vazdekis,Anna Ferré-Mateu,Elham Eftekhari,Alberto Rodríguez-Ardila,Rogério Riffel*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过紫外光谱分析发现，传统上被认为是'红且死亡'的早期型星系NGC 3115中心存在约0.69%的年轻恒星成分，该成分随半径增加迅速下降，并与动力学结构相关，表明其源于内部物质循环驱动的残余恒星形成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验年轻恒星成分在早型星系（尤其是透镜状星系）中是否普遍存在及其空间分布特征，挑战‘红且死亡’的传统认知。

Method: 利用SOAR/Goodman紫外光谱和线指数拟合，结合单主星族+年轻成分（过去1 Gyr恒定恒星形成）的恒星形成历史模型，并使用UltraNest进行贝叶斯参数推断，获取年龄、金属丰度、[Mg/Fe]及年轻恒星质量分数的后验分布。

Result: 年轻恒星质量分数在星系中心达0.69^{+0.09}_{-0.15}%，1 kpc外降为零；该成分与压力支撑区域的动力学结构强相关；排除了极端水平分支星等替代解释。

Conclusion: 年轻恒星在‘红且死亡’的早型星系中普遍存在，其空间梯度一致表明残余恒星形成是普遍现象，对理解此类星系的重子循环具有关键意义。

Abstract: Context: Massive early-type galaxies (ETGs) are traditionally considered "red-and-dead" based on optical observations. However, ultraviolet (UV) spectroscopy can uncover a hidden population of young stars with a sub-one-per cent mass fraction. Aims: We measure the young stellar mass fraction in NGC 3115, a massive isolated lenticular galaxy, to test the ubiquity and spatial distribution of this component beyond previously studied ellipticals. Methods: Using SOAR/Goodman spectra and line-strength fitting, we modelled the star-formation history with a single bulk stellar population plus a young component (constant star-formation over the last 1 Gyr). We used ultranest to derive posterior distributions for age, metallicity, [Mg/Fe], and the young stellar mass fraction. Results: By tracking spatial variations in U-band indices, we found the young stellar mass fraction peaks at $0.69^{+0.09}_{-0.15}\%$ in the centre, dropping to zero beyond 1 kpc. This young component correlates with kinematic structures, dominating in the pressure-supported region. We interpret this as residual central star formation fueled by internal processes, such as recycled stellar material. Alternative UV-bright models (e.g., extreme horizontal branch stars) yielded poorer fits and unphysically high mass fractions. Conclusions: Young stars in old, "red-and-dead" galaxies are more common than expected. Their presence across various ETGs with similar gradients indicates that residual star formation is a ubiquitous feature, offering key constraints on the baryon cycle in these galaxies.

</details>


### [206] [Ejected and Rejected: Strong Scatterings in Dense Star Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37775)
*Adam Distler,Nicholas C. Stone*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了星团中强散射事件对天体动力学演化的影响，特别是对恒星抛射和极端质量比旋进（EMRI）的抑制作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解碰撞星系系统的演化对于预测致密星团的整体演化及潮汐撕裂事件、极端质量比旋进（EMRI）、恒星抛射等损耗锥现象的发生率至关重要。弱散射主导大部分演化，但罕见的强散射可能带来定性不同的结果，需系统评估其天体物理重要性。

Method: 推导各向同性、球对称分布函数（代表球状星团和核星团）中速度扰动的精确微分率分布，并确定分布边界与近似率标度；进一步推广至核星团中能量和角动量扰动率的估算，并应用于（i）恒星抛射和（ii）EMRI“抵消”过程的研究。

Result: 发现抛射率在某些参数区间因强扰动分布饱和及弱散射极限失效而变得复杂，可能增强损耗锥屏蔽效应；同时发现大量原EMRI事件会被强散射显著抵消，表明强散射对真实EMRI率有不可忽略的影响。

Conclusion: 强散射在星团动力学中具有关键但常被低估的作用，尤其对EMRI形成率和恒星抛射机制影响显著，亟需后续工作定量评估其整体效应。

Abstract: Understanding the evolution of collisional stellar systems is key to predicting both the bulk evolution of dense star clusters and also the rates of tidal disruption events, extreme mass ratio inspirals (EMRIs), stellar ejections, and other loss cone phenomena. Although a majority of the evolution is dictated by weak, small-angle scatterings, rare close encounters resulting in strong scatterings can sometimes produce qualitatively different outcomes. To systematically determine the astrophysical importance of strong scatterings, we derive the exact differential rate distribution of velocity kicks in isotropic and spherically symmetric distribution functions representing globular and nuclear star clusters, while also deriving the distribution boundaries and approximate rate scalings. We then extend this formalism to derive approximate rates of energy and angular momentum kicks for objects in the nuclear star cluster, and then apply this framework to study (i) ejections and (ii) ``cancellation'' of extreme mass ratio inspirals. We find that ejection rates are complicated in some regimes due to the saturation of the strong kick distribution and the breakdown of the weak limit, likely enhancing the effect of loss cone shielding. We also determine that a sizable fraction of proto-EMRIs will likely be canceled by strong scatterings, highlighting the need for future work to determine to what degree strong scatterings impact the true EMRI rate.

</details>


### [207] [Simulation-Informed Dust Masses in Typical $6<z<14$ JWST Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37821)
*Jed McKinney,Dhruv T. Zimmerman,Desika Narayanan,Caitlin M. Casey,Julian B. Munoz,Paul Torrey,Hui Li,Federico Marinacci*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用恒星半光半径和紫外/光学消光推断高红移星系尘埃质量的新方法，并基于宇宙学模拟验证其有效性，应用于JWST观测的6<z<14星系，得出尘埃质量为10³–10⁵ M⊙，支持超新星早期增丰与热气体中尘埃高效摧毁的图景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接探测z>8典型星系中的尘埃目前不可行，但紫外/光学谱光度数据暗示其存在，亟需间接可靠的方法来估算高红移星系尘埃质量。

Method: 基于尘埃粒子在宇宙学zoom-in模拟中实时演化的结果，构建恒星半光半径与UV/optical消光联合约束尘埃质量的新方法，并校准其与红移、恒星质量的依赖关系。

Result: 该方法在低红移重现观测趋势，但在高红shift偏离银河系标定因子达~1.5 dex；应用于JWST数据（6<z<14）得到M_dust≈10³–10⁵ M⊙，与ALMA上限一致。

Conclusion: 高红移星系尘埃可能主要由超新星早期产生，随后在星系晕热气体中被高效摧毁；所提方法及标定表可为未来观测提供宽范围尘埃质量估算工具。

Abstract: Direct detections of dust in typical $z>8$ galaxies remain untenable, despite the presence of dust suggested by rest-frame UV/optical spectro-photometry. Informed by cosmological zoom-in simulations where dust particles evolve on-the-fly, we present a novel method for inferring dust masses in high-redshift galaxies using stellar half-light radii and UV/optical attenuation. Our methods reproduce observed trends at lower redshift, ultimately diverging from Milky Way-based calibration factors by up to $\sim1.5$ dex. We apply this method to observed JWST galaxies from $6<z<14$, and find dust masses in the range $M_{\rm dust}\sim10^3-10^5\,M_\odot$ that are fully consistent with upper limits from ALMA, and suggest an early dust enrichment period by supernovae followed by efficient grain destruction in the hot gas. Future observations may use our tabulated calibration to infer dust masses over a wide range of stellar masses and redshifts.

</details>


### [208] [No Significant Excess in Typical Variability in CLAS+ Changing-Look AGN Candidates in Rubin DP2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37876)
*Lilianne Nakazono*

Main category: astro-ph.GA
