TL;DR: 本文利用Rubin Data Preview 2（DP2）数据，检验变脸活动星系核（CLAGN）在光谱转变后是否仍表现出增强的短时标光学变异性，结果未发现各状态（turn-on/turn-off/intermediate）在r/i波段存在显著中位振幅超出，但中间态r波段振幅分布更宽，暗示可能存在高振幅子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验CLAGN在光谱转变后是否仍保持增强的短时标光学变异性，以理解其物理机制。

Method: 基于Rubin DP2数据，对比CLAS+ CLAGN候选体与光度宁静类星体对照样本的峰峰值星等振幅（逐波段），分析不同状态及时间延迟下的中位|Δmag|差异和振幅分布宽度。

Result: 未发现任何状态在r或i波段有显著中位振幅超出；r波段中间态振幅分布显著宽于对照组（经多重比较校正后）。

Conclusion: CLAGN在光谱转变后短期内未普遍表现出增强的光学变异性，但中间态r波段更宽的振幅分布可能暗示存在一个高变幅子群体。

Abstract: Rubin Data Preview 2 (DP2) samples the CLAS+ changing-look AGN (CLAGN) candidates $\sim1,000-9,000$ days after their comparison epochs, so it can test whether these objects retain enhanced short-term optical variability after their spectral transitions. Comparing peak-to-peak amplitude in magnitudes against a photometrically quiescent quasar control, one band at a time, we do not detect a significant median-amplitude excess for any state (turn-on, turn-off, intermediate) in either of two bands, nor a different in the median $\,|\,Δ\text{mag}\,|\,$ as function of time lag. The $r$-band CLAS+ amplitude distributions are wider than the control's, with the intermediate state formally significant after multiple-comparison correction. This could hint for a high-amplitude subpopulation.

</details>


### [209] [The Carousel Lens III: Joint lens convergence and source kinematic modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37971)
*William Sheu,Xiaosheng Huang,Tania M. Barone,Fuyan Bian,Aleksandar Cikota,Karl Glazebrook,Tesla Jeltema,Evan Odell,Jackson H. O'Donnell,Linus Upson,Felipe Urcelay,Sean Xu*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于积分场光谱数据立方体的强引力透镜前向建模新方法，利用透镜弧的分辨运动学信息，显著提升了爱因斯坦半径和质量分布幂律斜率的测量精度，并揭示了源星系的旋转特性与黑洞质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强引力透镜分析中，透镜弧的空间分辨运动学信息尚未被充分挖掘；传统成像建模受限于系统误差，需引入独立、高精度的建模手段。

Method: 构建端到端前向建模框架，从透镜收敛轮廓及源星系的视线速度、速度弥散和面亮度剖面出发，重建[OII]发射线积分场光谱数据立方体；利用VLT/MUSE深度观测对Carousel Lens系统中z=1.432的四重成像星系进行建模。

Result: 测得爱因斯坦半径θ_E = 12.816″±0.006″、对数质量坡度γ = 1.412±0.002，统计误差比传统扁平发射线图模型减小2–2.5倍；发现源星系具有强旋转，位于恒星形成主序上，中心黑洞质量log M_BH/M⊙ = 7.89±0.51。

Conclusion: 数据立方体级前向建模是一种实用且独立于传统图像建模的新途径，适用于当前系统误差主导的强透镜研究，并可推广至同系统其他透镜源及其他透镜系统。

Abstract: We introduce a new, independent modeling component to the Carousel Lens series by exploiting the spatially-resolved kinematics contained within the lensed arcs, which so far has remained rather underexplored in strong-lensing analyses. We present an end-to-end forward-modeling framework that reconstructs an integral-field spectroscopic datacube from a parameterization of the lens convergence profile together with the line-of-sight velocity, velocity dispersion, and surface brightness profiles of the unlensed source. The kinematic structure of the source acts as an additional set of ray-traceable markers distributed across every lensed image, inaccessible from imaging data alone. Furthermore, by generating an [OII] emission-line datacube, we isolate a single source redshift and remove lens and line-of-sight light contamination. We apply this framework to deep VLT/MUSE observations of the Carousel Lens, modeling the [OII] emission of the quadruply-imaged star-forming galaxy at $z_{\rm s} = 1.432$ that anchors the system's cosmography constraints. We measure an Einstein radius $θ_{\rm E} = 12.816''^{+0.005}_{-0.007}$ and a logarithmic slope $γ= 1.412^{+0.002}_{-0.002}$, tightening the statistical uncertainties on both by roughly a factor of $2-2.5$ relative to the corresponding flat emission-line map model. We find that the source galaxy exhibits a strong rotation, lies confidently along the star-forming main sequence, and has an inferred central black hole mass of $\log M_{\rm BH}/M_{\odot} = 7.89 \pm 0.51$. These results establish datacube-level forward modeling as a practical and independent route to traditional image-based lens models in the current systematics-limited regime, and one that extends naturally to this system's other lensed sources and to other lens systems.

</details>


### [210] [Tidal shells as clocks of galaxy mergers and tests of cosmological models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38035)
*Ivana Ebrová,Michal Bílek,Elisabeth Sola,Pierre-Alain Duc,Ana Lalović*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文比较了观测和宇宙学模拟中恒星壳层系统的差异，发现模拟预测存在大量>100kpc的巨型壳层，但实际观测中罕见；进而聚焦于并合历史的对比，利用壳层识别方法估计近邻大质量长椭球旋转星系的并合时间，并与模拟结果比较；同时介绍利用米兰科维奇望远镜开展更深成像以扩大样本的进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星壳层记录了星系并合历史及宿主星系引力势，但观测与模拟在壳层尺度上存在明显差异，需系统比较以检验理论模型。

Method: 采用壳层识别方法估计近邻大质量长椭球旋转星系的并合时间，并与宇宙学模拟中的并合历史进行对比；同时利用米兰科维奇望远镜开展深度成像观测以扩充样本。

Result: 模拟预测存在大量>100kpc的巨型壳层，但观测中极为罕见；初步对比显示观测估计的并合时间与模拟存在一定差异；新观测正扩大高质量壳层样本。

Conclusion: 观测与模拟在壳层尺度上存在重要差异，提示当前星系形成模型可能在并合动力学或壳层演化方面存在不足；扩大观测样本将有助于更可靠地约束并合历史和暗物质分布。

Abstract: Stellar shells-concentric arc-like tidal features formed in galaxy mergers-provide a valuable record of recent galaxy assembly histories encoding both the merger event and the gravitational potential of the host galaxy. We compare shell systems in observations and cosmological simulations from two complementary perspectives. First, we show that simulations predict a substantial population of very large (>100kpc) shells that are rarely observed, revealing a potentially important discrepancy between simulations and reality. Because the scales of observed and simulated shell systems differ significantly, we focus on a comparison of merger histories. Using a shell-identification method, we estimate merger times for a sample of nearby massive prolate rotators and compare them with merger histories derived from cosmological simulations. We also present ongoing efforts to expand the sample of deeply imaged prolate rotators using observations from the Milankovic telescope, paving the way for future studies based on larger observational samples.

</details>


### [211] [Turbulent gas-rich discs at high redshift: Chaotic orbits and rapid bulge formation at high gas fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38159)
*Johan M. Villa-Alatorre,Joss Bland-Hawthorn,Thor Tepper-Garcia,Oscar Agertz,Christoph Federrath*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Nexus数值模拟框架，研究了高红移盘星系中恒星棒的轨道特性，发现气体比例高的星系中混沌轨道占比显著上升，并导致棒结构在约1 Gyr内坍缩形成旋转支撑的核球。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移星系中恒星棒的形成与演化机制，尤其是其轨道动力学特性与气体含量的关系。

Method: 采用Nexus N体/流体动力学模拟，分析不同气体比例（f_gas）下星系中棒支持轨道的性质，统计混沌轨道占比（f_chaos），并追踪轨道演化及势场变化。

Result: 当f_gas > 60%时，混沌轨道占比超60%；f_gas ≈ 80–100%时，棒在≈1 Gyr内坍缩为旋转支撑的扁椭球核球，且坍缩紧随f_chaos ≥ 50%之后；势场快速变陡，混沌轨道更易偏离平均场；不同质量粒子径向分布一致。

Conclusion: 高气体比例导致中心势场快速演化，激发高混沌轨道比例，进而通过‘剧烈弛豫’使恒星棒坍缩形成旋转核球，这为早期星系核球形成提供新机制。

Abstract: Using the \nexus\ N-body/hydrodynamic framework, we show that stellar bars that emerge in high-redshift disc galaxies are different in their orbital characteristics compared to their low-redshift counterparts. We identify bar-supporting orbits and, among them, find the fraction of chaotic orbits compared to regular orbits ($f_{\rm chaos}$). This fraction rises above 60\% for models with $f_{\rm gas} > 60 \%$; for $f_{\rm gas} \lesssim 60\%$, we observe chaotic fractions close to zero. The emerging bars seen in the $f_{\rm gas}= 80\%$ and $100\%$ simulations collapse in $\lesssim$1 Gyr to form central bulges supported by rotation, and this collapse follows soon after the chaotic fraction starts to dominate ($f_{\rm chaos} \gtrsim 50\%)$. Moreover, the gravitational potential in those simulations steepens rapidly over that period, and by integrating the chaotic orbits, we find that orbits that deviate from the evolving mean field are more likely to be chaotic. Hence, we argue that the irregularity associated with the rapidly evolving, central potentials drives higher chaotic fractions in these gas-rich bars. As a result, the emergent bars collapse through `violent relaxation' to form oblate rotating bulges. Supporting evidence for this picture comes from the fact that particles of different mass share identical radial profiles, a natural consequence of the rapid relaxation process.

</details>


### [212] [Unveiling the Shock-Excited Molecular Environment in the Orion Protostar HOPS 370](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38192)
*Somnath Dutta,Koshvendra Singh,Devendra K Ojha,Saurabh Sharma,Tarak Chand,Manojit Chakraborty,Kartik Gokhe,Joe P. Ninan,Jyotirmoy Dey*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用TANSPEC光谱仪对深嵌入原恒星源HOPS 370进行了近红外K波段光谱观测，通过H₂分子转动-振动发射线分析其物理条件和激发机制，发现H₂辐射主要来自与原恒星外流相关的暖冲击加热气体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解深嵌入原恒星（Class 0/I）的物理环境与激发动理学，特别是区分热激发与紫外荧光等不同机制。

Method: 利用TANSPEC在3.6-m DOT望远镜上获取HOPS 370的K波段光谱；基于H₂ 1–0 Q(3)与S(1)线比确定消光；利用多条H₂谱线构建激发图，推导激发温度、柱密度和正仲氢比；结合S-与Q支谱线诊断激发机制。

Result: 测得A_V = 23.24 ± 1.25 mag，激发温度T_ex = 2078 ± 215 K，H₂柱密度N(H₂) = (1.93 ± 0.93)×10²⁰ cm⁻²，正仲氢比=2.45 ± 0.21；谱线比F(2–1 S(1))/F(1–0 S(1)) = 0.12 ± 0.01支持热激发主导。

Conclusion: HOPS 370中的H₂辐射起源于受冲击加热的暖气体，很可能与其原恒星外流相关；S支与Q支联合H₂光谱分析是研究深嵌入原恒星系统物理条件和激发历史的有效手段。

Abstract: We present near-infrared K-band ($1.94$--$2.52~μ$m) spectroscopy of the deeply embedded protostellar source HOPS\,370, obtained with the TIFR--ARIES Near Infrared Spectrometer (TANSPEC) on the 3.6-m Devasthal Optical Telescope (DOT). The spectrum is dominated by prominent molecular hydrogen ($\rm H_2$) ro-vibrational emission, with multiple transitions detected from the $1$--$0$ S, $2$--$1$ S, and $1$--$0$ Q branches. Using the intrinsically linked $\rm H_2$ $1$--$0$ Q(3) and $1$--$0$ S(1) transitions, which arise from the same upper energy level, we derive a visual extinction of $A_V = 23.24 \pm 1.25$ mag, corresponding to $A_K = 2.50 \pm 0.13$ mag, consistent with the embedded Class~0/I nature of the source. The diagnostic line ratio $F(2$--$1~{\rm S}(1))/F(1$--$0~{\rm S}(1)) = 0.12 \pm 0.01$ is consistent with thermal excitation and disfavors ultraviolet fluorescence as the dominant excitation mechanism. Assuming optically thin emission and local thermodynamic equilibrium, we derive an $\rm H_2$ excitation diagram that yields an excitation temperature of $2078 \pm 215$ K and a molecular hydrogen column density of $(1.93 \pm 0.93)\times10^{20}$ cm$^{-2}$. We further derive an ortho-to-para ratio of $2.45 \pm 0.21$, indicating substantial shock-driven thermal processing while remaining below the high-temperature equilibrium value of 3. Taken together, these diagnostics indicate that the molecular hydrogen emission in HOPS\,370 originates predominantly in warm, shock-heated gas most likely associated with its protostellar outflow. Our results demonstrate the diagnostic power of combined S- and Q-branch $\rm H_2$ spectroscopy for constraining the physical conditions and excitation history of deeply embedded protostellar systems.

</details>


### [213] [Age-Resolved Vertical Motions in the Milky Way Warp: A Localized Enhancement in Intermediate-Age Populations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38235)
*Xiaoyue Zhou,Xiaodian Chen,Haifeng Wang,Shu Wang,Zhihong He,Licai Deng*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用APOGEE DR19光谱和Gaia天体测量数据，通过分析不同年龄红巨星的垂直速度（V_Z）分布，研究银河系盘翘曲的演化。结果发现，中等年龄（2–6 Gyr）恒星在9–12 kpc半径处表现出比年轻恒星显著更高的平均V_Z，暗示其翘曲起始半径更小，可能反映翘曲随时间演化或不同恒星种群垂直结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定银河系翘曲如何演化可约束星系盘的形成与动力学演化过程。

Method: 基于APOGEE DR19光谱估计222,355颗红巨星的年龄，结合Gaia天体测量和几何距离，对其中168,824颗星按0–10 Gyr分为五个年龄组，分析其翘曲相关的径向垂直速度（V_Z）分布。

Result: 在9 ≤ R < 12 kpc区域，2–4 Gyr和4–6 Gyr红巨星的平均V_Z比0–2 Gyr年轻群体分别高1.48±0.46和1.49±0.52 km s⁻¹；而在R ≈ 12–13 kpc处，所有0–6 Gyr群体的V_Z分布一致；该中间年龄增强效应在稳健性检验中持续存在。

Conclusion: 中等年龄恒星翘曲起始半径可能更小，其V_Z差异或源于早期扰动与当前翘曲的叠加效应，或反映不同恒星种群垂直结构的本征差异。

Abstract: Determining how the Milky Way warp evolves provides constraints on the formation and dynamical evolution of galactic disks. Using Apache Point Observatory Galactic Evolution Experiment Data Release 19 (APOGEE DR19) spectroscopy, we estimate ages for 222,355 red giants. With Gaia astrometry and geometric distances, we analyze 168,824 of these stars in five age bins spanning $0$--$10$ Gyr. The warp-related radial vertical-velocity ($V_Z$) profile of young red giants ($0$--$2$ Gyr) is broadly consistent with those of classical Cepheids and young open clusters. Over $9\leq R<12$ kpc, we find that the mean $V_Z$ values of the intermediate-age red giants ($2$--$4$ and $4$--$6$ Gyr) are $1.48\pm0.46$ and $1.49\pm0.52$ km s$^{-1}$ higher than that of the young population, respectively. By contrast, at $R\simeq12$--$13$ kpc, the $V_Z$ profiles of the $0$--$6$ Gyr red giants are consistent within their uncertainties. The intermediate-age enhancement persists across our robustness tests and may reflect smaller effective kinematic warp onset radii for the intermediate-age populations. The observed velocity differences may arise from the combined effects of earlier perturbations and the present-day warp, or from differences in vertical structure among stellar populations.

</details>


### [214] [The Influence of Hubble Expansion on the Future Merger of the Milky Way and Andromeda](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38490)
*Yufan Zhou,Tianchi Zhang,Shihong Liao,Zhiyuan Li,Lan Wang,David Benisty*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了哈勃膨胀对银河系与仙女座星系（M31）未来并合的影响，通过在不同宇宙学模型（纯牛顿、ΛCDM、w0waCDM）下进行理想化N体模拟，发现当M31切向速度较高（>66.5 km/s）时，哈勃膨胀会显著延迟并合时间甚至改变系统最终命运，但在低速或加入M33和LMC后该效应减弱；实际影响取决于未来更精确的质量和自行观测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多数星系并合的理想化模拟忽略哈勃膨胀，但在本星系群等引力主导区域，哈勃膨胀仍存在且已在部分研究中被证明重要，因此需定量评估其对MW–M31并合的影响。

Method: 开展一系列理想化N体模拟，涵盖纯牛顿、ΛCDM和w0waCDM宇宙学模型，并考虑当前观测约束下的不同星系质量和自行参数；还扩展至包含M33和大麦哲伦云（LMC）的多体情形。

Result: 在M31切向速度较低时，哈勃膨胀效应可忽略；当速度高于66.5 km/s时，ΛCDM模拟相比牛顿模拟明显延迟并合时间，甚至可能改变并合结局；w0waCDM中该延迟较弱；加入M33和LMC后差异减小。

Conclusion: 哈勃膨胀对高切向速度星系并合有可观测影响，但其在MW–M31并合中的实际作用尚待更精确的星系质量与自行测量来确认。

Abstract: Most existing idealized simulations of galaxy mergers neglect Hubble expansion, as the local Hubble flow is curved by overdensities and the expansion effect is weak at these scales. However, even in the gravity-dominated Local Group (LG), the expansion itself still persists, and it has been shown important in some LG-related studies. Here we take the future merger of the Milky Way (MW) and Andromeda (M31) as a case study to investigate the differences between simulations with and without Hubble expansion. We perform a suite of idealized N-body simulations of the MW--M31 system under pure Newtonian, $Λ$CDM, and $w_0w_a$CDM scenarios, testing different masses and proper motions of galaxies informed by current observational constraints. At low M31 tangential velocities, especially in cases where the two galaxies merge within a Hubble time, the expansion effect is negligible; while at high velocities (>66.5 km/s), the $Λ$CDM simulations introduce a noticeable delay in the merger time compared to the Newtonian ones and may even alter the final fate of the system, but the caveat here is that the isolated-pair idealization and the assumed expansion history break down. The delay is also present but less pronounced in the $w_0w_a$CDM runs. When M33 and the Large Magellanic Cloud are included, the differences in merger times are reduced. In summary, Hubble expansion can play a noticeable role for galaxy mergers with high tangential velocities, but its actual influence on the MW--M31 merger depends on the precise masses and proper motions to be determined in the future.

</details>


### [215] [GRAVITY-RESOLVE: A comprehensive image reconstruction tool for Galactic Center observations with GRAVITY](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38589)
*K. Abd El Dayem,N. Aimar,P. Amaro-Seoane,B. Aytaç,A. Berdeu,J. -P. Berger,G. Bourdarot,W. Brandner,Y. Cao,C. Correia,R. Davies,D. Defrère,A. Drescher,F. Eisenhauer,T. A. Enßlin,M. Fabricius,H. Feuchtgruber,S. Flesch,N. M. Förster Schreiber,A. Foschi,Q. Fournier,P. Garcia,R. Garcia Lopez,R. Genzel,S. Gillessen,F. Gonté,X. Haubois,S. F. Hönig,M. Houllé,S. Joharle,J. Kammerer,A. Kaufer,P. Kervella,J. Knollmüller,L. Kreidberg,L. Labadie,R. Laugier,S. Lacour,O. Lai,J. -B. Le Bouquin,J. Leftley,R. Li,B. Lopez,D. Lutz,F. Mang,A. Mérand,F. Millour,M. Montargès,N. Morujão,E. Nehiba,H. Nowacki,M. Nowak,J. Osorno,T. Ott,S. Pappert,T. Paumard,C. Paladini,K. Perraut,G. Perrin,R. Petrov,P. O. Petrucci,N. Pourré,S. Rabien,D. C. Ribeiro,S. Robbe-Dubois,M. Sadun Bordoni,J. Sánchez Bermúdez,J. Sauter,J. Scigliuto,J. Shangguan,T. T. Shimizu,J. Stadler,F. Soulez,C. Straubmeier,E. Sturm,M. Subroweit,C. Sykes,L. J. Tacconi,P. Thevenet,F. Thiel,V. Thoss,I. Urso,F. Vincent,S. von Fellenberg,J. Woillez,G. Zins*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了GRAVITY-RESOLVE G^R图像重建代码的改进与应用，显著提升了VLTI/GRAVITY在银河系中心的近红外成像能力，包括提高极限星等（K波段约20.0等）、加速计算（提速至少10倍）、支持多点联合重建（NIR拼接），并成功发现两颗新恒星S303和S304。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升ESO VLTI/GRAVITY仪器在银河系中心的成像能力，特别是增强重建软件的深度、速度和多指向联合处理能力。

Method: 修订并开发GRAVITY-RESOLVE G^R图像重建代码，集成GRAVITY+的faint模式；利用闭合相位和可见度振幅进行图像重建；开展注入测试评估极限星等；实现九个独立数据集的联合重建（NIR拼接）。

Result: 首次基于GRAVITY+faint数据用G^R重建出图像；实现409.6 mas × 409.6 mas（≈3390 AU × 3390 AU）尺度的NIR拼接；极限星等达m_K ≈ 20.0（25 mas半径内）；发现两颗新恒星S303和S304；计算耗时降低至少10倍。

Conclusion: G^R是一种快速、精确且高深度的图像重建工具，显著拓展了GRAVITY在银河系中心 faint star 探测与高分辨成像的应用潜力。

Abstract: We aim to improve and extend the imaging capabilities of the GRAVITY beam combiner instrument of ESOs VLTI with respect to reconstruction software in the Galactic Center. We revise and develop the image reconstruction code GRAVITY-RESOLVE $\text{G}^{\text{R}}$, which has been specifically developed to reconstruct stars in the Galactic Center with GRAVITY. The main objectives are to increase the limiting magnitude in GRAVITY images, reduce its computational runtime, and allow for a joint reconstruction of multiple, spatially separate datasets/pointings on sky. We apply $\text{G}^{\text{R}}$ to recent GRAVITY data, featuring the implemented GRAVITY-faint mode as part of GRAVITY+, the upgrade of GRAVITY. We reconstruct visibility phases and amplitudes and present the first GRAVITY image reconstructed with $\text{G}^{\text{R}}$ from these quantities. We demonstrate NIR mosaicking with $\text{G}^{\text{R}}$ by jointly reconstructing nine separately acquired datasets of 2025 into a single image on a scale of $\simeq 3390\ \text{AU}\times 3390\ \text{AU}$, corresponding to $409.6\ \text{mas}\times 409.6\ \text{mas}$. With dedicated injection tests of point sources into GRAVITY data, we estimate the limiting magnitude to be $m_{\text{K}}\ \simeq\ 20.0$ within a radius of $25\ \text{mas}$ around the field center when reconstructing from closure phases and visibility amplitudes. We report on the discovery of two new faint stars in the Galactic Center: S303 and S304. Finally, we find a decrease in computational runtime by a factor of at least $\sim10$. With $\text{G}^{\text{R}}$, we demonstrate GRAVITYs capabilities for NIR imaging. We show that $\text{G}^{\text{R}}$ is a fast, precise, and deep image reconstruction tool for Galactic Center observations, enabling the discovery of even more faint stars.

</details>


### [216] [The nature of the Norma arm as a bar-driven star-forming shock front](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38607)
*Hillary Davis,Joss Bland-Hawthorn,Thor Tepper-Garcia,Oscar Agertz,Elizabeth Iles*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过新一代N体-流体动力学模拟，提出银河系中与年轻大质量恒星相关的脉泽并非位于“人马臂”，而是位于银河系棒状结构第一象限的激波前沿（即尘埃带），表明该尘埃带具有恒星形成活动，并据此测定了银河系棒的关键参数：半长轴约4.3 kpc、半短轴约2.3 kpc、倾角约20度、模式速度≤40 km s⁻¹ kpc⁻¹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决银河系“人马臂”脉泽位置归属争议，厘清其是否属于螺旋臂还是棒结构轨道；深化对银河系中心棒结构及其恒星形成性质的理解。

Method: 采用新一代N体-流体动力学数值模拟，将观测到的百万年量级脉泽位置与模拟中棒结构的激波前沿（尘埃带）进行匹配分析，并反演棒的几何与运动学参数。

Result: 确认脉泽位于银河系棒的第一象限激波前沿/尘埃带，证实该区域存在恒星形成活动；推得棒的半长轴a ≈ 4.3 ± 0.2 kpc、半短轴b ≈ 2.3 ± 0.2 kpc、倾角φ = 20⁺⁵₋₂ deg、模式速度Ωₚ ≲ 40 km s⁻¹ kpc⁻¹。

Conclusion: 银河系棒是长而慢且正在恒星形成的结构；传统所称的‘人马臂’脉泽段实为棒尘埃带，挑战了银河系四旋臂模型的既有认知，需重新评估棒-旋臂相互作用及整体结构。

Abstract: The Milky Way is commonly depicted as having four major spiral arms, including the Norma-Outer arm, and a central stellar bar whose properties remain debated. Masers associated with young high-mass stars have been used to trace a segment of the "Norma arm" extending from one end of the Galaxy's central stellar bar. Recent work suggests that these masers may reside along a spiral arm or occupy the dominant x1 stellar orbits that give the bar its shape. We now use a new generation of N-body, hydrodynamic simulations to argue that these million-year old masers instead trace the Galactic bar's Quadrant I shock front or, equivalently, its dust lane. This implies that the bar dust-lane is star-forming, shedding new light on our understanding of star formation processes in the Milky Way. By associating the masers with the simulated shock front, we determine the bar's parameters to be: a ~ 4.3 +/- 0.2 kpc, b ~ 2.3 +/- 0.2 kpc, phi = 20 (+5/-2) deg, Omega_p <~ 40 km s^-1 kpc^-1, representing the bar's semi-major and semi-minor half-lengths, inclination angle and pattern speed, respectively. This raises questions about the Milky Way's spiral structure and suggests that the Galactic bar is long, slow and star-forming.

</details>


### [217] [Kinematic methods of spiral nature identification: limitations and applicability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38633)
*Valeria S. Kostiuk,V. D. Zozulia,A. A. Marchuk,A. S. Gusev,I. V. Chugunov,A. A. Smirnova*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过玩具模型和TNG50模拟数据，评估了两种运动学方法（相位反转法和速度谐波主导法）在区分星系旋臂形成机制（如密度波、运动学螺旋、动力学螺旋和重复模式）中的有效性，并以NGC3938为例进行了实证分析，提出一种结合多种独立检验的旋臂结构分类策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系旋臂的本质仍存在争议，多种形成机制难以通过观测区分，亟需可靠的动力学诊断方法。

Method: 采用相位反转法和速度谐波主导法，在玩具模型与TNG50模拟数据上进行系统测试，并应用于观测目标NGC3938。

Result: 相位反转法对棒共振位置估计高度依赖取向，对旋臂可靠性不足；速度谐波主导法更稳健，能区分不同旋臂类型，且对旋转曲线不确定性不敏感；NGC3938分析支持重复模式场景。

Conclusion: 应联合多种独立运动学检验来分类旋臂结构，该策略有望在更大样本中验证并揭示旋臂形成机制的多样性。

Abstract: The nature of spiral arms in galaxies remains debated, with several competing formation scenarios, including density waves, kinematic spirals, dynamic spirals, and recurrent modes, that are difficult to distinguish observationally. We tested two kinematic methods - the phase reversal method and the velocity harmonic dominance method - using toy models and mock data from TNG50 simulations. For bar resonances, the phase reversal method is strongly orientation-dependent and yields reliable corotation estimates only when the bar is aligned with the disc minor axis. For spiral arms, the phase reversal method does not always yield reliable results and should be used in tondem with another kinematic method. The velocity harmonic dominance method is more robust: it distinguishes between density wave, kinematic, and dynamic spiral patterns, although it still shows some sensitivity to orientation. Uncertainties in the rotation curve do not strongly affect its results. We also apply both methods to the observed grand-design spiral galaxy NGC3938 as an illustrative example; the results suggest the presence of multiple spiral modes, consistent with the recurrent modes scenario and this interpretation relies on different indirect evidence. Based on our findings, we propose a strategy for classifying spiral structure in observed galaxies that combines multiple independent tests. Applying this analysis to an extended sample will allow us to validate its predictive power and to investigate the diversity of spiral formation scenarios.

</details>


### [218] [Chemodynamical Study and Evidence for Hot Compact Companions to Blue Straggler Stars in Berkeley 31](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38654)
*Deniz Cennet Çınar,D. Bisht,Ing-Guey Jiang,K. Belwal,Selçuk Bilir*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用Gaia DR3天体测量、Gaia-ESO巡天DR5.1光谱及多波段测光数据，对疏散星团Berkeley 31进行了详细研究，确定其基本参数、恒星种群及银河系轨道，并识别出大量蓝离散星（BSS）和黄离散星（YSS）候选体，其中部分BSS表现出紫外超量，暗示存在致密热伴星；结果表明该星团为年龄约3.7 Gyr、金属丰度偏低的外盘星团，其轨道演化显示适度的向外迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解疏散星团Berkeley 31的基本性质、恒星种群特征及其在银河系中的动力学演化历史，特别是其化学与轨道演化的关联。

Method: 结合Gaia DR3天体测量（自行与视差）、Gaia-ESO Survey DR5.1光谱（金属丰度、α元素丰度、视向速度）以及多波段测光（含Swift/UVOT紫外数据），进行成员星证认、等龄线拟合、化学钟年龄估计、SED建模及轨道积分分析。

Result: 确认329颗可能成员星；测定距离6024±181 pc、色余E(B-V)=0.148±0.047 mag、[Fe/H]=−0.36±0.04 dex、[α/Fe]=0.21±0.04 dex、年龄3.7±0.2 Gyr；发现62颗BSS和2颗YSS（其中48颗BSS和2颗YSS为新发现）；2颗BSS显示紫外超量，分别对应热白矮星与前身ELM白矮星伴星；轨道分析得导引半径12.29±0.29 kpc、偏心率0.11±0.01，显示适度向外迁移。

Conclusion: Berkeley 31是一个典型的中年、贫金属外盘疏散星团，其恒星种群特征与其银河系轨道演化一致，支持其起源于更内侧区域并经历向外迁移的过程。

Abstract: We present a detailed study of the open cluster Berkeley 31 using Gaia DR3 astrometry, Gaia-ESO Survey DR5.1 spectroscopy, and multi-wavelength photometry. We identify 329 probable cluster members from their proper motions and trigonometric parallaxes and use them to examine the cluster's fundamental parameters, stellar populations, and Galactic orbit. The cluster is located at a distance of $6024 \pm 181$ pc, with a color excess of $E(B-V)=0.148 \pm 0.047$ mag, and has a mean metallicity of [Fe/H] $=-0.36 \pm 0.04$ dex and $\langle [α/{\rm Fe}] \rangle=0.21 \pm 0.04$ dex. Isochrone fitting gives an age of $3.7 \pm 0.2$ Gyr, consistent with the independent chemical-clock estimate of $3.72 \pm 1.42$ Gyr. The systemic radial velocity is $57.89 \pm 0.08$ km s$^{-1}$. We find that about 38% of the MS stars are unresolved binary candidates with $q \gtrsim 0.5$. We identify 62 BSSs and 2 YSS candidates, including 48 BSSs and both YSSs not previously reported in the literature. We constructed multi-wavelength SEDs for these stars, including Swift/UVOT data for 28 BSSs. Two BSSs show clear ultraviolet excesses and are consistent with compact hot companions, one with a hot WD and the other with a pre-ELM WD. The present orbital parameters give a guiding radius of $12.29 \pm 0.29$ kpc and an eccentricity of $0.11 \pm 0.01$. Comparison with the estimated chemical birth radius indicates a moderate outward shift of the guiding center, with the present orbital position also contributing to the radial displacement. These results consistently describe Berkeley 31 as an intermediate-age, metal-poor outer-disk cluster and place its stellar populations in the context of its Galactic dynamical history.

</details>


### [219] [On The Scaling Relations Between Black Holes And The Galaxy/Bulge Mass And Their Connections With The Black Hole Occupation Fraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38699)
*Emanuele Contini,J. K. Jang,Jinsu Rhee,Changjo Seo,Sukyoung K. Yi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用半解析模型FEGA25和YS200宇宙学模拟，研究黑洞-宿主星系标度关系（如MBH–M⋆和MBH–Mbulge）对黑洞质量阈值、星系形态和红移的依赖性，并探讨其与黑洞占据分数（BHOF）的关系；结果表明标度关系在低质量端受选择效应显著影响，高质量端趋于稳定，且形态和红移演化不明显；BHOF与标度关系提供互补信息，模型在z=0与观测一致，但预测更低质量端存在更大弥散和弯曲。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞与宿主星系协同演化机制，厘清观测中因黑洞质量探测阈值、星系形态分类及红移演化带来的系统偏差，并评估黑洞占据分数（BHOF）对标度关系解释力的补充作用。

Method: 基于半解析模型FEGA25与YS200宇宙学模拟，分析不同黑洞质量截断下的MBH–M⋆和MBH–Mbulge关系，按星系形态分类并考察z=0–3演化；与NewCluster流体动力学zoom-in模拟对比；联合分析BHOF与标度关系的统计关联与偏差来源。

Result: BH质量截断主要影响低质量端关系，高质量端收敛；MBH–M⋆关系随星系核球占比增强而上移；z=0–3无显著系统演化；BHOF与标度关系互补——低BHOF常伴更大选择偏移，但映射非唯一；FEGA25在z=0与观测一致，但预测低质量端弥散增大、关系弯曲。

Conclusion: 黑洞-星系标度关系的观测表征高度依赖于样本选择（尤其是BH质量阈值）和宿主形态，其红移不变性暗示统计层面的协同生长较稳健；BHOF是理解标度关系偏差不可或缺的独立维度，单一标度关系不足以约束BH种群真实分布。

Abstract: We investigate the black hole (BH)--host galaxy scaling relations predicted by the semianalytic model \texttt{FEGA25}, focusing on their dependence on BH mass threshold, galaxy morphology, and redshift, and on their connection with the BH occupation fraction (BHOF). Using the YS200 cosmological simulation as our reference volume, we study the $M_{\rm BH}$--$M_{\star}$ and $M_{\rm BH}$--$M_{\rm bulge}$ relations for different BH mass selections and compare their redshift evolution with the hydrodynamical zoom-in simulation \texttt{NewCluster}. We find that BH mass cuts primarily affect the low-mass regime, while the selected relations progressively converge toward the intrinsic no-cut relations at higher host masses. The $M_{\rm BH}$--$M_{\star}$ relation also depends systematically on galaxy morphology, with increasingly bulge-dominated systems preferentially occupying higher stellar masses. Over $z=0$--$3$, FEGA25 shows no clear systematic evolution of either scaling relation, consistent with a broadly preserved statistical connection between BH and host-galaxy growth. NewCluster shows no clear monotonic evolution either, although it exhibits larger scatter, particularly toward high host masses. We further show that BHOF and BH--host scaling relations encode complementary information. A low BHOF generally corresponds to a stronger selection-induced offset, but the mapping is not unique: substantially different occupation fractions can yield similar median scaling relations, and at fixed BHOF the bias depends on the host property and underlying BH mass distribution. At $z=0$, \texttt{FEGA25} is consistent with available observational constraints for both stellar- and bulge-mass relations, while predicting increased scatter and bending toward lower host masses.

</details>


### [220] [The coupled evolution of nuclear star clusters and intermediate-mass black holes in a dwarf-galaxy merger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38767)
*Christian Partmann,Rachel S. Somerville,Michaela Hirschmann,Antti Rantala,Natalia Lahén*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究通过数值模拟探究了矮星系并合过程中核星团（NSCs）与中等质量黑洞（IMBHs）的共同演化，发现NSCs能显著促进IMBH双星的形成、硬化与并合，并增强气体吸积，从而克服IMBH并合中的输运与硬化瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低质量星系中核星团（NSCs）与中等质量黑洞（IMBHs）可能共存，并协同促进IMBH的并合与增长；但缺乏对二者耦合动力学的高分辨率模拟研究。

Method: 采用GRIFFIN（多相ISM与全采样初始恒星质量函数）与KETJU（正则化后牛顿黑洞动力学）联合模型，模拟两个含10^3 M⊙ IMBH及10^6 M⊙ NSC的气体丰富矮星系的等质量并合；黑洞建模为无反馈吸积沉降粒子。

Result: 有NSC时，IMBH在数Myr内形成双星、约340 Myr后并合；NSC在~500 Myr内并合；IMBH质量分别增长至1.1×10^5和4.1×10^4 M⊙；并合引发~175 km/s反冲，使残余黑洞脱离NSC但未脱离星系；NSC演化为更大、旋转、具平坦核的结构；潮汐撕裂率在并合前上升；该并合事件LISA可观测至高红移。

Conclusion: NSCs可有效消除低质量星系中IMBH并合所面临的输运与硬化瓶颈，是IMBH成长与引力波源形成的关键促进机制。

Abstract: Nuclear star clusters (NSCs) and intermediate-mass black holes (IMBHs) are expected to coexist in low-mass galaxies and might together facilitate the coalescence and growth of IMBHs. We simulate an equal-mass, gas-rich dwarf-galaxy merger in which each progenitor hosts a $10^3\,\rm M_\odot$ IMBH embedded in a $10^6\,\rm M_\odot$ NSC to study their co-evolution. The simulation model combines the multiphase interstellar medium and fully sampled initial stellar mass function of GRIFFIN at $4\,\rm M_\odot$ gas resolution with the regularised post-Newtonian black hole dynamics of KETJU. Black holes are modelled as accreting sink particles without feedback. Without NSCs, $10^3$-$10^4\,\rm M_\odot$ IMBHs sink only to separations of $50$-$200\,\rm pc$ and capture no gas. With NSCs, the clusters coalesce within ${\sim} 500\,\rm Myr$, the IMBHs form a binary within a few Myr, and scattering of mainly old NSC stars drives them to merger ${\sim} 340\,\rm Myr$ later. The NSCs also promote gas capture, growing the black holes to $1.1\times10^5$ and $4.1\times10^4\,\rm M_\odot$. Gas capture is suppressed during the merger-induced starburst by stellar feedback and the motion of the NSCs relative to the gas, and proceeds in brief supernova-regulated episodes once the NSCs have settled in the centre. Scattering by the IMBH binary and the merger recoil of ${\sim} 175\,\rm km\,s^{-1}$, which removes the remnant from the NSC but not from the galaxy, leave a larger, rotating NSC with a flat core. The stellar tidal disruption rate rises towards coalescence, and the merger is detectable with LISA out to high redshift. NSCs thus remove the delivery and hardening bottlenecks of IMBH mergers in low-mass galaxies.

</details>


### [221] [ARRAKIHS: The observability of tidally stripped satellites in a Milky Way-like halo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38779)
*Abram Pérez-Herrero,Y. Revaz,S. Roca-Fàbrega,S. T. Nagesh,J. Preston,D. Erkal,D. Herranz Muñoz,O. Agertz,M. Akhlaghi,R. Bieri,A. Camazón-Pinilla,J. Cenarro,A. P. Cooper,H. Domínguez Sánchez,A. Fattahi,R. Feldmann,A. Font,M. A. Gómez-Flechoso,E. Gaztañaga,M. Güdel,R. Guzmán,E. Illés,R. Infante-Sainz,C. Kintziger,A. Marín-Franch,D. Martínez-Delgado,B. Moore,K. Nagamine,P. Papaderos,P. Renard Guiral,A. H. Riley,R. Rodríguez-Cardoso,S. Serrano,S. Shen,E. Sola,F. Torradeflot,M. Wiesmann*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过高分辨率N体模拟，研究了符合ΛCDM框架和本星系群观测的矮星系在银河系势场中产生的潮汐流的演化与可探测性，发现潮汐特征的可观测性主要取决于矮星系的恒星质量、轨道偏心率（e₀ ≳ 0.75更易产生可观测延展结构）及演化阶段；中等质量（10⁸–10⁹ M⊙）矮星系最易产生可观测潮汐面积，而低质量（10⁶–10⁷ M⊙）系统可能被剥离却无显著可探潮汐信号；可探测面积随时间呈非单调演化，且暗物质晕核型（尖峰vs平坦）影响微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深场成像巡天正不断发现银河系类似星系周围的低表面亮度恒星流，这些流可追溯其被瓦解矮星系前身的轨道演化与内部结构；本文旨在基于ΛCDM框架和本星系群观测约束，系统评估矮星系潮汐特征的演化规律与当前/未来巡天（如ARRAKIHS、LSST、Euclid）的可探测性。

Method: 在静态银河系势场（含盘、核球与暗物质晕）中开展高分辨率N体模拟，矮星系模型包含Plummer恒星分布与尖峰/平坦型广义NFW暗物质晕；每0.5 Gyr生成理想化面亮度图，持续10 Gyr；以μlim=29–32 mag arcsec⁻²为极限面亮度，量化潮汐特征的投影可探测面积。

Result: 潮汐特征可探测性最强依赖于前身星系恒星质量、轨道偏心率代理e₀及演化阶段；e₀ ≳ 0.75的径向轨道更易产生延展可探测潮汐结构；可探测面积主导来自M⋆=10⁸–10⁹ M⊙前身星系，而M⋆=10⁶–10⁷ M⊙系统常剥离但无显著可探信号；可探测面积随时间非单调变化，反映流形成、相混合与弥散物质变暗的竞争；尖峰与平坦暗晕模型间差异小于轨道多样性引起的散射。

Conclusion: 矮星系潮汐流的可探测性具有强选择性，当前及下一代深度巡天将优先揭示中等质量、高偏心率轨道矮星系的遗迹；暗物质晕内核结构对宏观可探测性影响甚微，轨道动力学与恒星质量是主导因素；该结果有助于解释观测到的恒星流数量分布，并为ΛCDM框架下矮星系演化建模提供关键约束。

Abstract: Deep imaging surveys are revealing increasing numbers of low surface brightness stellar streams around Milky Way-like galaxies. These streams trace the orbital evolution and internal structure of their disrupted dwarf galaxy progenitors. We investigate the evolution and observability of tidal features from dwarf galaxies consistent with the $Λ$CDM framework and Local Group observations. We performed high-resolution $N$-body simulations of dwarf galaxies orbiting in a static Milky Way-like potential comprising a disc, bulge, and dark matter halo. Each dwarf comprised a Plummer stellar distribution embedded in a cuspy or cored generalised Navarro--Frenk--White dark matter halo. Idealised integrated surface brightness maps were constructed every 0.5 Gyr over 10 Gyr, and detectability was quantified through the projected area of stellar light brighter than limiting surface brightnesses, $μ_{\rm lim}=29$--$32$ mag arcsec$^{-2}$, chosen to span depths relevant to ARRAKIHS, LSST, and Euclid. The detectability of tidal features depends most strongly on progenitor stellar mass, orbital configuration, parameterised by the eccentricity proxy $e_0$, and evolutionary stage. Across this range, extended tidal debris is predominantly recovered for initially more radial configurations, mainly those with $e_0 \gtrsim 0.75$. In the simulated grid, detectable area is dominated by $M_\star=10^8$--$10^9 M_\odot$ progenitors, whereas $M_\star=10^6$--$10^7 M_\odot$ systems can undergo stripping without detectable debris. The detectable area evolves non-monotonically with time, reflecting the competition between stream formation, phase mixing, and the fading of diffuse debris below the adopted surface brightness limits. Cuspy and cored progenitors show similar evolution of the detectable area, with differences smaller than the scatter induced by orbital diversity.

</details>


### [222] [The extragalactic science of the KMOS VVVX-GalCen spectroscopic survey II: Discovery of a galaxy cluster at z = 0.089 behind the Galactic plane](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38811)
*E. O. Schmidt,C. Valotto,C. Villalon,L. D. Baravalle,J. L. Nilo-Castellón,M. V. Alonso,M. Soto,M. Gómez,D. Minniti,W. Weidmann,D. Mast,C. Obasi,D. Alvarez Rocha,S. Federle,R. K. Saito,V. D. Ivanov,M. G. Navarro,Z. Guo,A. Pichel,I. Petralia,V. Fermiano,A. Luna,M. Lad,J. Osses,R. Pignata,V. Valdivia Rojas,P. Marchant Cortes,E. G. Rivera,G. Ferrero,N. Masetti,J. Borissova,J. G. Fernández-Trincado,C. Cáceres,F. Nogueras-Lara,D. Galdeano,F. Fortuni*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用VLT/KMOS近红外光谱观测，确认了银河系遮蔽区（ZoA）内一个被尘埃遮挡的星系团，包含14个成员星系，红移z≈0.089，动力学质量约1.9×10¹⁴ M⊙，属贫星系团。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系遮蔽区（ZoA）是本地宇宙星系普查中最大的观测空白，仅靠测光无法判别其中探测到的星系过密区是否为真实物理结构还是视线方向投影。

Method: 基于VVV NIRGC III星表识别出19个星系候选体，使用VLT/KMOS在HK波段获取其中16个的近红外光谱，并通过互相关方法测量径向速度；结合红移、速度弥散和红序一致性判断物理关联性。

Result: 14个星系构成致密结构，平均红移z=0.089±0.001，速度弥散σ_v=560^{+110}_{-120} km/s，推得动力学质量M_{200}=1.9^{+0.7}_{-1.1}×10¹⁴ M⊙；13个成员落在近红外红序2σ范围内；另2个为前景星系。

Conclusion: 近红外光谱可有效穿透银河系消光，确认ZoA中真实星系团的存在并实现动力学刻画，弥补光学/测光巡天的局限。

Abstract: The Zone of Avoidance (ZoA) continues to represent the most significant incompleteness in the extragalactic census of the Local Universe. It is evident that photometric surveys alone are insufficient to establish whether the galaxy overdensities detected therein are physical systems or projections along the line of sight. We present the first spectroscopic results of the extragalactic science area of the KMOS VVVX-GalCen Spectroscopic Survey, demonstrating how near-infrared (NIR) spectroscopy can reveal and dynamically characterise galaxy clusters hidden by the Milky Way. A photometric overdensity of 19 galaxy candidates was identified in the VVV NIRGC III catalogue, centred at $(l,b)=(274.05^\circ,+2.85^\circ)$ with a mean interstellar extinction of $A_{K_s}=0.2$ mag; 16 of these candidates were observed with VLT/KMOS in the $HK$ grating and yielded spectra with reliable cross-correlation radial velocities. A total of 14 galaxies form a compact structure with a mean redshift of $\langle z \rangle = 0.089 \pm 0.001$, which corresponds to a mean radial velocity $\langle cz \rangle = 26660 \pm 170$ km/s, while two are foreground galaxies. The rest-frame line-of-sight velocity dispersion is $σ_v = 560^{+110}_{-120}$ km/s, implying an indicative dynamical mass of $M_{200} = 1.9^{+0.7}_{-1.1} \times 10^{14} M_\odot$ (based on members within the cluster core $\sim 0.4 r_{200}$) and placing the system in the poor-cluster regime. Of these 14 cluster members, 13 also lie within the $2σ$ scatter of the fitted near-IR red sequence. The other two galaxies are foreground objects that lie outside this range.

</details>


### [223] [Environmental Quenching of Satellite Galaxies out to z~0.4 with GAMA and DEVILS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38836)
*Megan Oxland,Matías Bravo,Laura Parker,Luke Davies,Sabine Bellstedt*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究利用GAMA和DEVILS巡天数据，以一致的方法分析卫星星系在落入星系群/团后恒星形成率的演化，发现其相对于主序星系以约0.14 dex/Gyr速率持续下降，且该趋势在z~0–0.4（约4.5 Gyr）内无显著红移演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同巡天和红移下对卫星星系环境致钝（environmental quenching）的直接比较受限于样本选择、环境度量及性质推导方法的差异，亟需统一分析框架。

Method: 选取高完备度的GAMA（z~0）与DEVILS（z~0.4）巡天中卫星星系样本，采用一致方法提取星系性质与环境参数；利用投影相空间作为入轨时间代理，量化钝化比例演化，并追踪其相对于恒星形成主序（SFMS）的演化轨迹；进一步结合目视形态分类分析。

Result: 卫星星系在入轨后恒星形成率相对于SFMS以~0.14 dex/Gyr稳定下降；早型与晚型卫星均呈现相似衰减趋势；GAMA与DEVILS间未见显著红移演化，表明过去~4.5 Gyr内群/团环境对卫星星系恒星形成的压制效应基本恒定。

Conclusion: 环境致钝过程在z~0–0.4期间具有时序一致性与形态普适性，支持‘渐进式剥离’等模型，且群/团环境对卫星星系恒星形成的抑制作用在近45亿年来保持稳定。

Abstract: While numerous studies have shown that satellite galaxies experience declining star formation rates after infall, direct comparisons across different surveys and redshifts are complicated by varying sample selections, environmental metrics, and techniques used to derive galaxy properties. In this work, we select satellite galaxies from the highly complete Galaxy and Mass Assembly (GAMA) Survey and the Deep Extragalactic VIsible Legacy Survey (DEVILS) to investigate environmental quenching out to z~0.4. These surveys derive galaxy properties and environments in a consistent manner, minimizing systematic biases and enabling a robust comparison across redshift. Using projected phase space as a proxy for infall time, we quantify the evolution of the quenched fraction and trace how satellites evolve relative to the star-forming main sequence (SFMS). We find consistent trends between GAMA and DEVILS, with satellites exhibiting a steady decline in star formation relative to the SFMS at a rate of ~0.14 dex/Gyr following infall. Extending this analysis to include visual morphology, we find similar declining trends for both early- and late-type systems. Overall, we find no significant redshift evolution between GAMA and DEVILS, indicating that group and cluster environments have a comparable impact on the star formation activity of satellite galaxies over the past ~4.5 Gyr.

</details>


### [224] [Odds Estimation for Supermassive Black Hole Binaries in Local Core Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38886)
*C. J. Harris,Kayhan Gültekin*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过结合IllustrisTNG模拟与半解析核清除模型，构建了基于可观测参数预测近邻星系中是否存在束缚超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHB）的统计模型，并在模拟数据上取得高精度；应用于观测样本后，识别出8个高概率宿主星系，其中M86被特别指出为强候选体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHBs）是星系并合的自然产物，但在近邻星系中直接探测仍极富挑战性，亟需可靠、可操作的观测判据来筛选候选体。

Method: 融合IllustrisTNG宇宙学模拟中的大质量星系组装历史与半解析核清除模型；构建符合观测星系恒星质量函数的核星系样本；模拟SMBHB从动力摩擦、恒星硬化到引力波辐射的完整演化；采用逻辑回归，以恒星质量缺失、恒星质量、中心密度、中心速度弥散和有效半径五个可观测量作为预测变量。

Result: 模型在模拟数据上表现优异：伪R²达0.51±0.03，准确率0.95±0.01，精确率0.92±0.03，召回率0.82±0.04；预测未并合双星典型分离约0.3 pc，仅约0.3%落入脉冲星计时阵频段；应用于观测核心星系，识别出8个SMBHB宿主候选体，其中M86（NGC 4406）被独立方法交叉验证为强候选体。

Conclusion: 基于多维核心星系观测参数的统计模型可有效提升SMBHB搜寻效率；M86等候选体应作为后续高分辨率观测（如甚长基线干涉测量）的重点目标，以期实现首个确凿的近邻SMBHB探测。

Abstract: Supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHBs) are expected products of massive galaxy mergers, but identifying binaries in nearby galaxies remains challenging. We combine simulated massive-galaxy assembly histories from IllustrisTNG with a semi-analytic core-scouring model to connect observable core galaxy properties to the probability of hosting a bound SMBHB at z=0. We (i) construct a core galaxy population using an observationally motivated galaxy stellar mass function, (ii) model binary evolution through dynamical friction, stellar hardening, and gravitational-wave emission, and (iii) perform logistic regressions with five observable predictors: stellar mass deficit, stellar mass, central density, central velocity dispersion, and effective radius. The resulting models perform well on simulated systems, achieving a pseudo-$R^2$ of $0.51\pm0.03$, accuracy $0.95\pm0.01$, precision $0.92\pm0.03$, and recall $0.82\pm0.04$. Uncoalesced binaries are typically compact, with separations peaking near 0.3 pc, though only $\sim0.3\%$ emit in the pulsar timing array band. Applying the model to observed core galaxies, we identify eight nearby galaxies more likely than not to host SMBHBs. Of those eight host candidates, we note Messier 86 (NGC 4406) as a particularly likely example that was similarly identified using independent means and therefore warrants future follow-up.

</details>


### [225] [Detailed chemo-dynamical characterization of four elusive Milky Way star clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38923)
*D. A. Alvarez Garay,E. Ceccarelli,D. Massari,M. Bellazzini,A. Mucciarelli,M. Libralato,E. Pancino,A. Avdeeva,M. Echeveste,E. I. Leitinger,S. Nedhath,S. Rani,E. Reggiani,N. Sanna,S. Saracino,L. Steinbauer,A. Turchi,E. Dodd,E. R. Garro,C. Zerbinati*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用VLT的X-shooter光谱仪对四个难以观测的银河系恒星系统（BH 140、Garro 01、BLISS 1和Kim 3）进行了化学动力学分析，首次获得了它们的视向速度、金属丰度和α元素丰度，并结合轨道分析探讨其起源（吸积或原位形成）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示这些新发现的暗弱恒星系统的运动学与化学性质，有助于理解其本质及其在银河系演化历史中的作用。

Method: 使用VLT/X-shooter中分辨率光谱获取四系统的视向速度与化学丰度（[Fe/H]、α元素），并开展轨道积分以分析其轨道参数（如z_max、E、L_Z），进而判断其起源。

Result: BH 140、Garro 01、BLISS 1为相对富金属（[Fe/H]≈−0.7）且α增强系统；Kim 3则显著贫金属（[Fe/H]≈−1.8）。轨道分析表明Garro 01很可能原位形成；BH 140虽轨道偏心率高、α略低，仍可能原位形成；BLISS 1明确为吸积系统但无已知母体，其化学性质却似原位；Kim 3轨道特征与Elqui流相关。

Conclusion: 这四个系统起源多样：Garro 01极可能原位形成；BH 140和BLISS 1存在原位与吸积起源的张力；Kim 3很可能源自吸积的Elqui流。结果凸显银河系恒星系统形成历史的复杂性。

Abstract: A variety of faint and observationally challenging stellar systems has been discovered in the Milky Way in the last decades. Unveiling their kinematic and chemical properties is crucial to understand their nature and to assess their role in the history of our Galaxy. In this paper, we present a chemo-dynamical analysis of four elusive stellar systems (BH 140, Garro 01, BLISS 1, and Kim 3) using spectra collected with the X-shooter medium-resolution spectrograph at the VLT. We derived precise line-of-sight velocities and chemical abundances for all targets. Specifically, we provide the first spectroscopic characterization of metallicity and α-element content for BH 140, the first comprehensive chemical characterization for BLISS 1 and Garro 01, and the first spectroscopic metallicity determination for Kim 3. BH 140, Garro 01 and BLISS 1 are relatively metal-rich systems with [Fe/H] ~ -0.7 dex and have enhanced α-element abundances, whereas Kim 3 is notably metal-poor with [Fe/H] ~ -1.8 dex. We also performed an orbital analysis to investigate their origin as accreted or in situ systems. We found that Garro 01 and BH 140 have orbits confined within < 1.5 kpc from the Galactic plane, the former having also a remarkably circular orbit. They were likely born in situ, although the high orbital eccentricity of BH 140 and its mild deficiency in α-elements leave other possibilities open. Although undoubtedly accreted, BLISS 1 does not exhibit a clear dynamical association with any known progenitor, lying in a high energy (E) - high vertical angular momentum (LZ ) prograde orbit reaching a maximum height from the Galactic plane of zmax ~ 8 kpc. Its chemical properties seem to favor an in situ origin, which might be connected with the Aleph sub-structure. Finally, we tentatively link the high energy and low LZ orbit of Kim 3 to the Elqui stream.

</details>


### [226] [3-Methoxy-1-propanol Rotational Spectroscopy and Radio Astronomical Search toward NGC 6334I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38934)
*D. Archie Stewart,Laurent Margulès,Roman A. Motiyenko,Brett A. McGuire*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了3-甲氧基-1-丙醇在NGC 6334I星云MM1原恒星源中的探测，通过实验测定其旋转谱并模拟其在该源物理条件下的光谱，但未在ALMA观测数据中发现其谱线，给出了柱密度上限为7.5×10¹⁵ cm⁻²。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测更大甲氧基化醇类分子（如3-甲氧基-1-丙醇）在恒星形成区的存在，以理解复杂有机分子的化学演化规律。

Method: 测量3-甲氧基-1-丙醇最稳定构象体的厘米波、毫米波和亚毫米波段旋转谱，结合实验旋转参数，在MM1源物理条件下模拟其旋转光谱，并与ALMA观测数据比对。

Result: 未在ALMA数据中识别出3-甲氧基-1-丙醇的任何谱线，确定其柱密度上限为7.5×10¹⁵ cm⁻²；其柱密度下降趋势与甲氧基甲醇→2-甲氧基乙醇一致。

Conclusion: 目前尚无法确认3-甲氧基-1-丙醇在MM1源中存在，其丰度显著低于更小的甲氧基醇，支持复杂有机分子丰度随分子尺寸增大而降低的趋势。

Abstract: The methoxy functionalized alcohol species methoxymethanol and 2-methoxyethanol have recently been identified toward the MM1 protostellar source in the NGC 6334I massive star-forming region. Here we report measurements of portions of the centimeter, millimeter, and sub-millimeter regions of the rotational spectrum of the most stable conformer of 3-methoxy-1-propanol in order to probe the presence of even larger methoxylated alcohols toward the source. The experimental rotational parameters determined from these spectra were used to simulate the rotational spectrum of 3-methoxy-1-propanol under the physical conditions of the MM1 source and compared to the same ALMA observational dataset of the source in which 2-methoxyethanol was first discovered. No 3-methoxy-1-propanol transitions are identified in the data, and an upper limit on its column density toward the source was determined to be $7.5 \cdot 10^{15} \text{ cm}^{-2}$. When considering the series of these three methoxy alcohols, the decrease in column density going from 2-methoxyethanol to 3-methoxy-1-propanol is consistent with how the column density of 2-methoxyethanol decreases compared to methoxymethanol.

</details>


### [227] [The origin of UV emission in extremely low-mass satellite galaxies: Insights from Leo T](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39390)
*S Amrutha,Mousumi Das,Daniel Vaz,Jarle Brinchmann*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用UVIT数据对矮星系Leo T进行了远紫外（FUV）研究，识别并表征了30个FUV星团及若干FUV发射恒星，发现其缺乏大质量O/B型星，恒星种群以贫金属的主序星、蓝环星等为主；FUV辐射主要来自<1 Gyr年轻恒星，且星团分布暗示曾受银河系潮汐作用影响；尽管探测到CO气体，但分子云与星团无空间关联，星形成效率极低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leo T是已知质量最低、仍含气体并有近期恒星形成的银河系卫星星系，是研究极低质量、低金属丰度环境下紫外波段恒星形成的理想实验室。

Method: 利用紫外线成像望远镜（UVIT）开展远紫外（FUV）观测，结合HST和MUSE恒星证认与光谱数据，提取FUV星团并测定其面积、光度和恒星形成率（SFR）；对比FUV分布与H I及CO分布；分析（NUV−r）颜色轮廓以约束恒星年龄；估算全局SFR与分子云相关星形成效率（SFE）。

Result: 识别出30个FUV星团，确认其中无大质量O/B型星；FUV恒星包括主序星、蓝环星、水平分支星和发射线星，其中上主序与发射线星集中在光学中心，下主序与蓝环星更弥散；5/6颗有MUSE光谱的FUV恒星为极贫金属（[Fe/H] ≤ −3）；NUV−r色指数表明<185 pc内紫外辐射源于<1 Gyr年轻恒星；CO云与FUV星团无空间关联，SFE极低；星团空间分布呈现方向性，暗示曾受银河系潮汐扰动。

Conclusion: Leo T的恒星形成活动由低质量、极贫金属恒星主导，缺乏大质量星团和高效分子云恒星形成，其FUV辐射特征与极端低质量、低金属丰度及外部潮汐相互作用密切相关。

Abstract: Leo T, a Milky Way (MW) satellite, is the lowest-mass gas-rich galaxy known to show recent star formation, making it an excellent laboratory for investigating star formation in the ultraviolet (UV) regime within a low-density, metal-poor environment. We present a far-UV (FUV) study using Ultraviolet Imaging Telescope (UVIT) data, aiming to characterize FUV star-forming clusters (SFCs), identify FUV-emitting stars, constrain their properties, and explore star formation in an extremely low-mass, metal-poor galaxy. We examined the galaxy's FUV extent and compared it with the H I distribution. We extracted thirty SFCs and derived their properties, including area, luminosity, and star formation rate (SFR), and compared them with previous studies. FUV-emitting stars and their stellar parameters were identified through cross-matching with catalogs from the Hubble Space Telescope and the Multi Unit Spectroscopic Explorer (MUSE). We also estimated the global SFR and star formation efficiencies (SFEs) associated with previously identified CO clouds. The FUV luminosities of the SFCs confirm the absence of massive O and B-type stars in Leo T. The FUV-emitting stellar population consists of main-sequence (MS) stars, blue loop (BL) stars, horizontal branch stars, and emission-line (EM) stars. The upper MS and EM stars are concentrated near the optical center, whereas the lower MS and BL stars are more spatially extended. We find that five out of the six FUV-emitting stars with MUSE spectroscopy are extremely metal-poor ([Fe/H] $\leq$ -3). The (NUV - r) color profile indicates that the UV emission is dominated by stellar populations younger than 1 Gyr out to a radius $\lt$ 185 pc. Despite the recent detection of CO(1-0) emission, no SFCs are associated with the molecular clouds, which exhibit extremely low SFEs. The spatial distribution of the SFCs suggests a directional interaction with the MW.

</details>


### [228] [Probing Multi-formation Channels of Dark Matter-Deficient Galaxies in a Simulated Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39431)
*Sanghyeon Han,Ho Seong Hwang,Jaehyun Lee*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: This paper uses the IllustrisTNG100 simulation to identify two formation channels for dark matter-deficient galaxies (DMDGs): 'acquired' (losing dark matter later) and 'inborn' (forming with little dark matter from the start), differing in dynamics, environment, and star formation history.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To understand the formation mechanisms of dark matter-deficient galaxies (DMDGs), which challenge standard galaxy formation paradigms and may arise through distinct physical pathways.

Method: Analysis of the IllustrisTNG100 cosmological hydrodynamical simulation; DMDGs defined as galaxies with dark matter-to-stellar mass ratio < 1; classification into 'acquired' and 'inborn' based on evolutionary history; comparison of structural, kinematic, environmental, and chemical properties.

Result: Two distinct DMDG populations identified: acquired DMDGs show strong temporal variations and form near neighbors in overdense regions; inborn DMDGs have mild evolution, form stars in situ from pre-enriched gas, and may assemble via tidal interactions or filamentary gas flows.

Conclusion: There are at least two physically distinct formation channels for DMDGs—acquired and inborn—highlighting diverse pathways to dark matter deficiency and enriching our understanding of extreme galaxy formation.

Abstract: We investigate the formation and evolution of dark matter-deficient galaxies (DMDGs) using the IllustrisTNG100 cosmological hydrodynamical simulation. We define DMDGs as systems with a dark matter-to-stellar mass ratio below unity, and find two formation channels. In the first, galaxies that initially form within massive dark matter halos lose the majority of their dark matter and transition into DMDGs (acquired DMDGs). In the second, galaxies form with little or no dark matter from the beginning (inborn DMDGs). The two populations show distinct physical properties. Acquired DMDGs exhibit significant temporal variations in mass content, size, and kinematics, whereas inborn DMDGs show mild evolution. The former population tends to form closer to their nearest neighbor galaxies in large-scale overdense regions than the latter population does. We find that most stars in the inborn DMDGs form in situ. Their precursor gas is already metal-enriched and associated with other galaxies before formation. Tidal interactions and filamentary gas flows may contribute to the assembly of compact gas clumps, suggesting multiple formation pathways for inborn DMDGs. Our findings support the idea that there are at least two distinct channels to make galaxies dark matter deficient, which is important for understanding the formation mechanisms of these extreme systems.

</details>


### [229] [The Peculiar Astrometric Behaviour and Radio Structure of the Quasar 1524-136 Revealed with Very Long Baseline Interferometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39603)
*Angelina Osetrova,Sándor Frey,Oleg Titov,Alexey Melnikov,Panna Gelle*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过分析VLBI和Gaia数据，发现射电类星体1524-136的位置突变源于X波段核心亮度相对喷流热点显著增强，导致射电位置测量峰值从热点转移到核心，揭示了喷流结构变化对天体参考系稳定性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释射电类星体1524-136在1990年代至2010年代间高达0.1角秒以上的异常天体测量位置偏移，并评估其对VLBI构建的天球参考系稳定性的影响。

Method: 综合分析历史VLBI天体测量与成像数据（特别是X波段~8.4 GHz）以及Gaia光学天体测量数据，定位位置偏移的物理起源。

Result: 确认位置偏移由X波段核心区域相对于北喷流末端热点的显著增亮所致，致VLBI测得的亮度峰值从热点迁移至核心。

Conclusion: 此类因喷流结构演化导致的射电源位置表观变化可能影响其他射电天体的天体测量精度，需深入理解其天体物理机制以保障VLBI天球参考系的长期稳定性。

Abstract: According to astrometric very long baseline interferometry (VLBI) observations accurate to sub-milliarcsecond levels, the radio quasar 1524-136 (J1526-1351) exhibited an exceptionally large shift, more than 0.1", between the 1990s and 2010s. This luminous quasar is known for its extended (~0.4"), highly asymmetric and non-collinear jet structure. Here we analyse historical VLBI astrometric and imaging data, as well as Gaia optical astrometric information, to find the cause of the sudden apparent change in the position. At the X band (~8.4 GHz frequency), where radio positions are conventionally measured, the core region brightened substantially relative to the hotspot at the end of the northern jet. As a result, the brightness peak shifted from the hotspot to the core, causing the VLBI position to shift accordingly. Similar changes could affect the astrometric positions of other radio sources. A better understanding of the astrophysical reasons behind them is important for maintaining the stability of the VLBI-based celestial reference frame.

</details>


### [230] [Hoag-type Galaxies: A Photometric Study of the Cores and Rings of Thirty Systems from the DESI Legacy Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39629)
*Jingshuo Yang,Cheng Li*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过DESI成像数据识别出30个Hoag型星系，分析其核心与环的结构和颜色特性，发现核心多为红序早型星系但略偏蓝、更致密；环颜色更广、部分偏蓝；多数为核心星系，暗示环境利于环长期存续，但环性质多样性表明可能有多种形成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hoag型星系稀有，以往研究受限于样本量小，本文旨在利用大视场巡天数据扩大样本，系统刻画其核心与环的物理性质及环境特征，以探讨其形成与演化机制。

Method: 基于DESI成像，人工检验两个机器学习环星系星表，筛选出30个Hoag型星系；利用Legacy Survey g/r/z波段图像，减去二维核心模型后测量环性质；将核心与质量匹配的SDSS早型星系对比，并结合SDSS群组信息分析环境分布；构建简化核心+环模型估算轨道稳定性。

Result: 核心主要位于红序、遵循早型星系质量关系但整体更蓝、更小、表面密度更高、浓度和Sérsic指数更低；环颜色范围更宽、延伸至蓝云；16个有群信息者中14个（87.5%）是中心星系，显著高于对照组（~64%）；环表面密度与归一化宽度随核心+内晕/环质量比增大而减小；多数核心R90位于模型最外稳定圆轨道内，环常跨越不稳定区。

Conclusion: Hoag型星系核心可能含一定内禀演化（如核球缓慢增长），但高致密性排除纯弥散伪核球起源；高中心星系比例暗示低环境扰动有利于环气体保留与长期稳定；环性质多样性支持多通道形成机制共存。

Abstract: Hoag-type galaxies contain a smooth central core surrounded by a detached outer ring, but their rarity has limited previous studies mainly to individual systems. We identify thirty galaxies with apparent Hoag-type morphology in DESI imaging by visually inspecting two machine-learning ring-galaxy catalogues and analyse their core and ring properties using DESI Legacy Imaging Surveys $g$, $r$, and $z$ images. We measure the rings after subtracting two-dimensional core models and compare the cores with matched SDSS galaxies. The cores lie predominantly on the red sequence, whereas the rings span a broader colour range extending toward the blue cloud. The cores broadly follow early-type mass scaling relations, but their mass-matched distributions tend towards slightly bluer colours, smaller sizes, higher stellar-mass surface densities, and lower concentrations and Sérsic indices. Of the 16 systems with SDSS group information, 14 (87.5 per cent) are centrals, compared with $\sim64\%$ in the mass-matched early-type control sample. The halo-mass and large-scale overdensity distributions become much more similar after central--satellite status is also matched. Ring stellar-mass surface density and normalized width decrease with increasing core-plus-inner-halo to ring mass ratio. Most core $R_{90}$ values lie inside the outermost stable circular orbit of a simplified core-plus-ring model, while the rings frequently extend across its unstable interval. The slightly bluer core colours, lower concentrations, and lower Sérsic indices suggest a possible secular contribution, but the compact, dense cores imply more complex structural assembly than a simple diffuse-pseudobulge origin. The high central fraction may favour gas retention and ring survival by reducing satellite-specific environmental processing. The diversity of ring properties nevertheless leaves room for multiple formation pathways.

</details>


### [231] [Detection of HNCOH+ in the interstellar medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39654)
*M. Rey-Montejo,I. Jimenez-Serra,M. Sanz-Novo,V. Lattanzi,S. Thorwirth,V. M. Rivilla,S. Zeng,L. Colzi,D. San Andres,A. Megias,A. Martinez-Henares,M. A. Requena-Torres,S. Martin,B. Tercero,P. de Vicente*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了在银河系中心分子云G+0.693-0.027中首次探测到亚稳态离子HNCOH⁺，其丰度高于热力学更稳定的同分异构体H₂NCO⁺，挑战了星际分子丰度由热力学稳定性主导的传统认知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HNCOH⁺此前被理论预测存在于星际介质中，但一直未被观测证实；同时，其与已探测到的H₂NCO⁺的相对丰度关系可检验当前星际化学模型。

Method: 利用Yebes 40-m、IRAM 30-m和APEX望远镜对G+0.693-0.027开展超深宽带谱线巡天（31–275 GHz，带宽~150 GHz），结合分子跃迁谱线识别与柱密度拟合分析。

Result: 首次在ISM中探测到HNCOH⁺，柱密度为(9.9±0.5)×10¹² cm⁻²，相对H₂丰度为(7.3⁺¹·³₋₁·₀)×10⁻¹¹；其与H₂NCO⁺的丰度比为3.8⁺⁰·⁵₋₀·₄。

Conclusion: HNCOH⁺的探测扩展了含异氰酸酯基团（–NCO）的星际分子清单，并表明星际同分异构体丰度受动力学控制强于热力学稳定性，支持近期动力学模型的预测。

Abstract: Only five out of the $\sim$350 molecules detected in the interstellar medium (ISM) carry the isocyanate functional group (NCO), the essential substructure of peptide bonds in proteins. Among them are isocyanic acid (HNCO) and its most stable N-protonated isomer H$_2$NCO$^+$. Previous studies have predicted that the O-protonated isomer HNCOH$^+$ should be present in the ISM; yet this species has remained elusive. We search for the metastable HNCOH$^+$ towards the Galactic Center molecular cloud G+0.693-0.027, where H$_2$NCO$^+$ has already been detected. We use an ultra-deep broadband spectral survey carried out towards G+0.693-0.027 with the Yebes 40-m, IRAM 30-m and APEX telescopes covering nearly 150 GHz of bandwidth between 31 and 275 GHz. We report the first detection of HNCOH$^+$ in the ISM with a column density of $N$ = (9.9 $\pm$ 0.5)$\times$10$^{12}$ cm$^{-2}$, which corresponds to a molecular abundance relative to H$_2$ of (7.3$^{+1.3} _{-1.0}$)$\times$10$^{-11}$. With the measured H$_2$NCO$^+$ column density of $N$ = (2.6 $\pm$ 0.3) $\times 10^{12}$ cm$^{-2}$, this yields a HNCOH$^+$/H$_2$NCO$^+$ ratio of 3.8$^{+0.5} _{-0.4}$. This result is consistent with recent kinetic calculations that favour the formation of HNCOH$^+$ over H$_2$NCO$^+$. The detection of HNCOH$^+$ in the ISM broadens the inventory of molecules known to contain the isocyanate functional group and reinforces recent works showing that isomer abundances in the ISM do not necessarily correlate with thermodynamic stability.

</details>


### [232] [Dust growth in the early Universe: insight from the NewCluster spin-off simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39683)
*J. K. Jang,Yohan Dubois,Francisco Rodriguez Montero,San Han,Jinsu Rhee,Diana Ismail,Gyeong-Hwan Byun,Changyeon Yu,Seyoung Jeon,Sukyoung K. Yi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过宇宙学zoom-in模拟研究了z~5星系中的尘埃演化，发现其遵循三个金属丰度依赖的阶段，并指出当前模型在解释高红移星系尘埃质量与尘埃-金属比方面存在不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释高红移（z~5）星系中观测到的尘埃质量和尘埃-金属比偏低现象，探究早期宇宙尘埃形成与演化的物理机制。

Method: 基于NC宇宙学zoom-in模拟的衍生运行，采用实时双组分（硅酸盐/碳质）、双尺寸尘埃模型，分析尘埃随金属丰度演化的规律及反馈效应。

Result: 识别出三个金属丰度依赖的尘埃演化相：低金属丰度下以恒星抛射为主（Phase I），中等金属丰度下硅酸盐吸积主导（Phase II），较高金属丰度下碳质增长也显著（Phase III）；超新星反馈显著抑制局部尘埃-金属比；模拟结果整体低于当前观测推断值。

Conclusion: 固定成分（如铁橄榄石）的尘埃模型可能不适用于化学年轻的高红移星系，需引入金属丰度依赖的尘埃成分模型以更好解释早期宇宙尘埃含量。

Abstract: We investigate dust evolution in galaxies at $z\sim5$ using a spin-off run of the \NC\ cosmological zoom-in simulation with a live two-species, two-size dust model. We find that dust evolution follows three metallicity-dependent phases. At $\log(Z/Z_\odot)<-1$, dust is dominated by stellar ejecta and produces a shallow, featureless UV extinction curve whose normalized wavelength dependence is similar to that of the Calzetti attenuation law (Phase I). At $-1\leq \log(Z/Z_\odot)<-0.5$, efficient silicate accretion drives rapid dust growth, producing silicate-rich dust and SMC-like extinction curves (Phase II). At $\log(Z/Z_\odot)\geq-0.5$, carbonaceous grain growth also becomes efficient, yielding a more balanced grain mixture and Milky-Way-like extinction curves (Phase III). In addition, local dust-to-metal ratios are strongly suppressed in regions affected by recent clustered supernova feedback, further complicating the interpretation of high-$z$ observations. Despite some successes, our galaxies lie below current observationally inferred dust masses and dust-to-metal ratios for high-redshift galaxies. The resulting depletion patterns suggest that the fixed Fe-bearing olivine composition assumed in our current dust model may be too restrictive for chemically young galaxies, and that metallicity-dependent grain compositions may be required to explain the dust content of the early Universe.

</details>


### [233] [Feeding or Fleeing: The Impact of Enhanced Early Black Hole Growth on Circumgalactic Clumps at $z > 6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39692)
*Floor van Donkelaar,Lucas Tortora,Debora Sijacki*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过对比两种宇宙学模拟（Fable和Fable-SE），研究高红移大质量星系周围星系际介质（CGM）中致密气体团块的形成与演化，发现增强早期黑洞增长（Fable-SE）导致更多金属丰度高、向外运动的致密CGM团块，表明强AGN反馈可驱动冷而致密的富金属气体外流至晕外。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解高红移大质量星系CGM中致密气体团块的起源及其受星系反馈（尤其是早期AGN活动）的影响机制。

Method: 采用两个针对同一大质量暗晕的高分辨率宇宙学zoom-in模拟（Fable与Fable-SE），后者引入轻度超爱丁顿吸积以增强早期黑洞增长；识别并追踪CGM中致密气体团块的气体质量、尺寸、金属丰度、恒星含量及径向运动演化。

Result: 两模型团块基本性质（质量、尺寸、恒星质量）分布相似，但Fable-SE产生显著更多金属丰度高、向外运动的团块，尤其在z≲7强AGN吹出相期间；部分团块可运动至晕外；这些外流团块关联HI丰富、高密度、相对低温气体。

Conclusion: 致密CGM团块的空间与化学分布是纤维吸积与AGN反馈共同塑造的结果；早期强AGN活动可驱动冷、致密、富金属气体结构向外迁移，甚至脱离暗晕，对高红移星系演化和重子循环具有重要影响。

Abstract: The circumgalactic medium (CGM) of massive high-redshift galaxies contains inflowing filamentary gas, recycled material, and feedback-driven outflows. We investigate the formation and evolution of dense CGM clumps in two cosmological zoom-in simulations of the same massive halo at $z > 6$: the fiducial Fable model and the modified Fable-SE model, which includes enhanced early black hole growth through mild super-Eddington accretion. We identify dense gas clumps in the CGM and follow their gas properties, stellar content, and radial evolution across cosmic time. At the resolution probed here, the two simulations show broadly similar distributions of clump gas mass, gas size and associated stellar mass. Instead, the clump populations differ mainly in their abundance, metallicity, and radial evolution. Fable-SE produces substantially more metal-enriched clumps, including an outward-moving population during the strong AGN-driven blow-out phase at $z \lesssim 7$. These systems are associated with disturbed HI-rich gas and relatively high gas densities, linking the increased prevalence of dense, metal-enriched CGM structures in Fable-SE to its stronger AGN-driven blow-out phase. Although inward migration dominates overall, Fable-SE also contains an outward-moving clump population during the blow-out phase, with some clumps moving beyond the virial radius. Our results therefore indicate that the distribution of dense CGM clumps around massive high-redshift galaxies reflects both filamentary accretion and feedback-driven CGM evolution, with strong early AGN activity associated with the outward migration of dense, metal-enriched gas structures containing relatively cool gas.

</details>


### [234] [The stellar orbit distribution of 24 late-type galaxies observed by MUSE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39788)
*Le Zhang,Ling Zhu,Jesús Falcon-Barroso*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MUSE档案数据，对24个近邻晚型星系进行轴对称Schwarzschild建模，按轨道圆度分类恒星成分，并分析其光度占比与形态特征，发现低质量旋涡星系中普遍存在动力学温成分，表明其盘更厚、更暖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究晚型星系中恒星轨道结构的质量依赖性趋势，并验证CALIFA和IllustrisTNG的已有结果。

Method: 从MUSE档案选取24个近邻晚型星系，使用GIST+PPXF提取恒星运动学，采用DYNAMITE构建轴对称Schwarzschild模型，按圆度将轨道分为冷、温、热及逆旋转成分，并计算各成分在有效半径内的光度分数与边缘投影扁率。

Result: 确认了轨道结构的质量依赖趋势；发现低质量晚型旋涡星系中普遍存在动力学温成分，其盘更厚、更暖；温成分在低质量星系中较显著。

Conclusion: 动力学温成分在低质量晚型旋涡星系中常见，暗示其盘的垂直结构更厚、动力学更暖；该结论受限于样本量，属初步结果。

Abstract: We selected 24 nearby late-type galaxies from the MUSE archive with kinematic coverage extending to at least one effective radius. Stellar kinematics were extracted with the GIST pipeline using pPXF, and axisymmetric Schwarzschild models were constructed with \textsc{dynamite}. The stellar orbits of each galaxy were classified by circularity into dynamically cold, warm, hot, and counter-rotating components, and we calculated the corresponding luminosity fractions of each component within $R_{\mathrm{e}}$. Our results confirm the mass-dependent trends in orbital structure previously identified from CALIFA galaxies and reproduced by IllustrisTNG. We further checked the morphology of each component and calculated the edge-on flattening of the cold, warm, and hot+CR components. With the high-quality MUSE data, we find that the dynamically warm components are common in low-mass late-type spiral galaxies, suggesting that their discs are dynamically warmer and vertically thicker than those of more massive spirals. We regard this as a tentative result, given the limited sample size.

</details>


### [235] [On the Proper 3D Modeling of Orbits in Wide Binary Gravity Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40388)
*Kyu-Hyun Chae*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文探讨了利用宽双星系统（wide binary stars）来重建开普勒轨道并推断引力常数G的方法，特别关注在低加速度环境下检验牛顿引力与MOND理论的可行性；作者提出并验证了一种允许半长轴a自由变化的贝叶斯MCMC算法，证明其与原有隐式a方法等价，并指出先前关于Chae样本支持牛顿引力的结论可能源于人为扩大位置/速度误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验引力理论（尤其是牛顿力学与MOND）在低加速度尺度下的有效性，利用宽双星系统的精确三维位移和速度数据直接约束引力参数G。

Method: 提出并实现一种贝叶斯MCMC算法，使轨道半长轴a作为显式自由参数；结合α-Cen AB-Proxima系统与Chae等人36个宽双星样本，对比显式a与隐式a建模方法；通过内部一致性关系（如天球面分离s）与观测约束进行轨道拟合与G推断。

Result: 显式a的MCMC算法与隐式a方法在引入观测约束下完全等价；对α-Cen AB-Proxima系统给出了牛顿与MOND框架下的轨道解；指出Chae样本中声称支持牛顿引力的结论可能源于不恰当地扩大r/v误差。

Conclusion: 宽双星是探测低加速度下引力行为的有力工具，但需谨慎处理参数先验、误差建模与个体化轨道分析；贝叶斯MCMC方法及后续观测对异常系统至关重要。

Abstract: A gravitationally-bound system of wide binary stars with precise 3D relative displacement $\mathbf{r}$ and velocity $\mathbf{v}$ can allow a Keplerian orbit reconstruction for an assumed gravitational parameter $G$. A sufficiently large sample of such systems can also be used to directly infer $G$ specific to the sample. When the sky-plane angular coordinates are (essentially) exact and the distances to the binary stars are sufficiently precise, the orbit modeling can be readily done using two internal consistency relations provided by the ratio of angular separations in R.A.\ and decl.\ and the physical sky-plane separation $s$ that must be satisfied by any Keplerian orbits. A recent study argued that treating $a$ explicitly free with an empirical prior imposed on it can dramatically change the inference of $G$ in the Chae et al.\ sample of 36 wide binaries. Here I introduce and test a Bayesian MCMC algorithm that allows $a$ to be explicitly free, and find using the $α$-Cen AB - proxima system and the Chae et al.\ sample that the explicit-$a$ algorithm with an observational constraint is fully equivalent to the implicit-$a$ algorithm with an internal consistency relation involving $s$. For the $α$-Cen AB - proxima system, I present orbits in Milgromian (MOND) as well as Newtonian gravity models obtained with the verified algorithms. I show that a recent claim of Newtonian gravity from the Chae et al.\ sample may be a consequence of using arbitrarily enlarged errors for some of $\mathbf{r}$ and/or $\mathbf{v}$. I discuss how wide binaries can be properly analyzed and modeled to probe gravity at low acceleration and test gravity models, including the necessity of deriving individual-specific parameters, the importance of follow-up observations for anomalous systems, and the relevance of Bayesian MCMC approaches.

</details>


### [236] [A MUSE and MeerKAT study of dynamics and star formation in HCG 16](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39918)
*Ixaka Labadie-García,Enrique Pérez,Julián Garrido,Roger Ianjamasimanana,Lourdes Verdes-Montenegro*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析HCG 16的MUSE和MeerKAT观测数据，揭示其星暴活动始于约4–5亿年前、与NGC 848近距离经过有关，但当前剧烈恒星形成需由多次成员星系相互作用共同驱动，而非单次遭遇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深入理解致密星系群HCG 16的演化过程，特别是中性氢（HI）分布与恒星形成之间的关联，并重建该星系群的相互作用历史。

Method: 结合MUSE光谱数据（经pyPipe3D处理获得Hα图像与恒星种群合成结果）与高分辨率MeerKAT HI数据，开展Hα与HI的二维及三维结构与动力学分析，并构建空间分辨的恒星形成历史以对比星系内外区域。

Result: 识别出约400–500 Myr前开始的主要恒星形成事件，持续至50–60 Myr前；HI分布高度扰动，其中HCG 16a保持近似规则盘，而HCG 16b具有Hα潮汐尾；NGC 848的经过触发了星暴，但无法单独解释HCG 16c和16d当前的剧烈恒星形成。

Conclusion: HCG 16的演化更可能由多个星系间的反复相互作用主导，而非单一强引力遭遇；NGC 848的经过是重要触发因素，但非唯一原因。

Abstract: Context. Hickson Compact Groups (HCGs) present an ideal environment for studying galaxy interactions. HCG 16 is a group of five galaxies in an intermediate stage of evolution (Phase 2), with several members displaying signs of interaction, including distorted morphologies, tidal features, and enhanced star formation rates.
  Aims. We aim to better understand the evolution of HCG 16 and its member galaxies, focusing on the relationship between neutral hydrogen and star formation, while reconstructing the interaction history of the group.
  Methods. We analysed MUSE data processed with pyPipe3D to extract H$α$ images and stellar population synthesis results, and reduced MeerKAT data to produce a high-resolution HI cube. We examined the structure and dynamics of the H$α$ and HI lines in both 2D and 3D, and produced spatially resolved star formation histories to compare the inner and outer parts of the galaxies.
  Results. We identify a major star formation episode in HCG 16 beginning ~400-500 Myr ago, coinciding with the passage of NGC 848 close to the other member galaxies, and that continued at least until 50-60 Myr ago in some galaxies. The HI distribution is highly perturbed in the group, although HCG 16a retains a nearly regular disk, while HCG 16b has associated H$α$ tails.
  Conclusions. The passage of NGC 848 probably activated star formation in HCG 16, but cannot by itself explain the current starburst activity in HCG 16c and 16d. The evolution of the group is better explained by multiple interactions among its members than by a single strong encounter.

</details>


### [237] [Channelled versus Isotropic Accretion of Galaxy Groups onto Clusters: Kinematics, Spin Alignment, and Assembly Bias](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39987)
*Ankit Singh,Frazer Pearce,Meghan Gray,Ulrike Kuchner,Julian Onions,Gustavo Yepes,Alexander Knebe*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MDPL2宇宙学模拟，分析了212,704个星系群-星系团下落对，发现星系群的自转轴取向、速度方向及轨道参数强烈依赖于其沿宇宙网丝状结构（特别是主通道与次通道）还是各向同性路径落入星系团；存在一个特征质量M_flip≈10^13.1 h⁻¹ M⊙，在此质量上下自转轴相对于丝状结构发生平行—垂直翻转；丝状结构线密度越高，M_flip越大；各向同性下落群速度更高、更径向，导致更小的近心点距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究星系群沿不同宇宙网拓扑路径（如丝状结构连接或各向同性）落入星系团时，其运动学、自转和轨道是否呈现系统性差异，此前尚无大样本定量测量。

Method: 基于MDPL2 N体模拟（体积(1 h⁻¹ Gpc)³），识别27,029个星系团及其212,704个伴生星系群，利用DisPerSE算法构建宇宙网丝状结构，并将群-团对分类为：主通道（直接连通）、次通道（支流）、主通道外围和各向同性四类；统计分析各类中群自转轴取向、速度-丝状结构夹角、轨道参数（如近心点）随质量、距离和丝状结构线密度的变化。

Result: （i）主/次通道类群存在自转轴'翻转质量'M_flip≈10^13.07–13.12 h⁻¹ M⊙，低于此质量平行于丝状结构，高于则垂直；各向同性类无翻转；M_flip在R<7R₂₀₀内径向恒定。（ii）主通道中M_flip随宿主丝状结构线密度升高而增大（12.96→13.44），该趋势在匹配群质量、团质量和距离后仍显著但幅度不确定（0.07–0.58 dex）。（iii）各向同性群在2R₂₀₀内显著偏离随机取向（41%在60°内，4.3σ缺失）；主通道群在2R₂₀₀处85%速度平行于丝状结构；各向同性群下落速度更快（快150–180 km/s）、更径向，导致其轨道近心点比主通道群小最多10%（距离匹配时）。

Conclusion: 星系群进入星系团的拓扑路径（尤其是是否经由丝状结构）及其宿主丝状结构的线密度，共同塑造其自转、运动学与轨道特性；该效应可观测，有望通过大面积光谱巡天验证。

Abstract: Galaxy clusters grow by accreting groups from the cosmic web, but whether the route of infall (along a cluster-connected filament or from the open field) imprints a group's kinematics, spin, and orbit remains unmeasured at scale. We classify $212{,}704$ group-cluster infall pairs across $27{,}029$ clusters in the $(1\,h^{-1}\,\mathrm{Gpc})^3$ MDPL2 simulation into primary (cluster-connected), secondary (tributary), primary-outskirt, and isotropic channels using DisPerSE filaments. (i) In both channelled classes, group spin axes lie mainly parallel to the filament below a `spin-flip' mass and perpendicular above it, $\log_{10} M_\mathrm{flip}\;[h^{-1}\,M_{\odot}] = 13.07$ and $13.12$, a $0.05$-dex offset consistent with zero, lying $1.3-1.4$ dex above the field, while isotropic groups show no flip; $M_\mathrm{flip}$ stays radially flat out to $7\,R_{200}$. (ii) Within the primary class $M_\mathrm{flip}$ rises with the linear mass density of the host filament, from $12.96$ (thin tendrils) to $13.44$ (dense bridges); matching group mass, cluster mass and distance keeps the rise positive but leaves its size uncertain ($0.07-0.58$ dex). (iii) Isotropic groups fall below random alignment at $2\,R_{200}$ ($41$ per cent within $60^\circ$, a $4.3σ$ deficit). Primary groups show $85$ per cent velocity-filament alignment at $2\,R_{200}$, and isotropic groups infall $150-180\,\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$ faster, and more radially, out to $5\,R_{200}$. At matched distance isotropic groups are therefore predicted to reach orbital pericentres up to $10$ per cent smaller than primary groups, whose smaller pooled pericentres reflect only their proximity to the cluster. The topological route of accretion, and within it the host filament's linear density, shapes how groups enter clusters, testable with wide-area spectroscopic surveys.

</details>


### [238] [SDSS-V LVM: Mapping of plasma temperatures in the Lagoon Nebula reveals the origin of long-standing chemical abundance discrepancies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40045)
*Amrita Singh,Guillermo A. Blanc,Kathryn Kreckel,J. E. Méndez-Delgado,Christophe Morisset,Sebastián F. Sánchez,Oleg Egorov,Niv Drory,Nimisha Kumari,Fu-Heng Liang,Enrico Congiu,Alfredo Mejía-Narváez,Evelyn J. Johnston,José G. Fernández-Trincado,Hemanth Bommireddy,Abhinna Sundar Samantaray,Thomas Hilder,Rodolfo de J. Zermeño,Ivan Katkov,Dhanesh Krishnarao,Juna A. Kollmeier*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过首次对银河系H II区（Lagoon星云）进行空间分辨的O^{++}再复合线（RLs）和禁戒线（CELs）积分视场单元（IFU）观测，证实电子温度（Te）涨落是导致长期存在的丰度差异（abundance discrepancy）的根本原因，并提出以Te差异作为热涨落幅度的观测代理，从而统一了两种丰度测量方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决自上世纪40年代以来一直存在的、由碰撞激发线（CELs）与再复合线（RLs）推导出的重元素丰度系统性不一致（即‘丰度差异’）这一长期难题。

Method: 利用首套针对Lagoon星云（M8）的空间分辨积分视场单元（IFU）观测数据，同时获取O^{++}的再复合线（RLs）与碰撞激发线（CELs），并采用等离子体温度扰动模型进行分析；引入经典CEL法与混合RL/CEL法所得Te之差作为热涨落幅度的观测代理。

Result: 发现丰度差异与两种方法所得Te之差存在紧密相关性；该Te差异可直接表征热涨落强度；采用由该相关性约束得到的平均电子温度后，CEL与RL推导的丰度完全一致，丰度差异被消除。

Conclusion: 电子温度涨落是Lagoon星云中丰度差异的物理根源；Te差异可作为量化热涨落影响的新观测指标；该结果为校正星云丰度、理解星系化学演化提供了关键依据。

Abstract: Knowing the chemical composition of interstellar gas is essential for understanding the chemical evolution of galaxies. Ionized nebulae provide key laboratories for this purpose, where chemical abundances are commonly derived from emission lines of heavy ions such as doubly ionized oxygen, $O^{++}$. Abundances can be inferred either from collisionally excited lines (CELs) or from recombination lines (RLs). However, for more than 80 years, the former have yielded systematically lower abundances than the latter. We map this ''abundance discrepancy'' using the first spatially resolved integral field unit (IFU) observations of $O^{++}$ RLs and CELs across a Milky Way H II region: the Lagoon Nebula (a.k.a. M8). Modeling the data using a plasma temperature perturbation model, we present unequivocal evidence that electron temperature ($T_e$) fluctuations drive the abundance discrepancy in M8. We find a tight correlation between the abundance discrepancy and the difference between $T_e$ derived from the classical CEL method and a hybrid RL/CEL approach. We identify this Te discrepancy as an observational proxy for the amplitude of the thermal fluctuations, providing a new, direct way to quantify their effect on nebular abundances. The correlation constrains the mean electron temperature of the gas. Adopting this mean temperature reconciles CEL and RL abundances, effectively eliminating the abundance discrepancy.

</details>


### [239] [Can the $\sim 5$ kpc starburst ring in MW create eROSITA bubbles?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40049)
*Mukesh Singh Bisht,Kartick C. Sarkar*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过流体动力学模拟，反驳了eROSITA气泡源于5 kpc恒星形成环的观点，证实其起源于银河系中心的致密能量注入，即核外流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定eROSITA气泡（eRBs）的起源对理解费米气泡成因及银河系反馈机制至关重要；近期有观点认为其可能源自5 kpc处的恒星形成环而非银心，需验证。

Method: 采用详细的流体动力学数值模拟，对比分析恒星形成环与银心致密能量注入两种机制能否产生观测到的对称eROSITA气泡。

Result: 模拟表明，仅靠5 kpc恒星形成环无法产生对称的eROSITA气泡；必须引入来自银河系中心的致密能量注入才能复现观测特征。

Conclusion: eROSITA气泡确为银河系中心核外流的直接表现，支持其与银心活动（如SMBH或剧烈星暴）相关，而非外围恒星形成环所致。

Abstract: eROSITA bubbles (eRBs) in our Galaxy are thought to be due to the energy injection by the central star-forming region or the central Super Massive Black Hole. Identifying the origin of these bubbles is crucial in constraining the origin of the Fermi Bubbles and the nature of the Galactic feedback. However, some recent opinions suggest that the eRBs may not even have been produced by any central source, but rather a result of a star-formation ring at $R\sim 5$ kpc. In this letter, we perform detailed hydrodynamic simulations to refute that claim and show that such a ring of star formation alone is not able to produce the symmetric eROSITA bubbles - one needs a compact energy injection from the Galactic Center to produce these bubbles. Our study, therefore, suggests that the eROSITA bubbles truly represent the nuclear outflow from our Galaxy.

</details>


### [240] [What kinds of strong star--black-hole encounters in AGN disks occur, and how often?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40144)
*R. O'Shaughnessy,Z. Rosenberg,J. Nordhaus*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了活动星系核周围吸积盘内恒星与黑洞之间的相遇过程，引入了由盘内随机扰动引起的初始相对速度、考虑单体和双体介导的强相互作用截面，并建立了包含恒星相遇能量损失及超大质量黑洞潮汐效应的重复相遇模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积盘中大量恒星与黑洞不可避免地发生相遇，其结果依赖于相遇距离与相对速度；此前研究未充分考虑盘内随机性引发的速度弥散及多体相互作用的贡献。

Method: 扩展已有模型：1）纳入由盘内密度涨落和邻近天体引力扰动导致的初始相对速度；2）采用基于直接二体（Hill）或三体（孤立）数值模拟得到的强相遇截面，涵盖单-单、单-双与双-双相互作用；3）构建重复相遇模型，同时计入恒星间动力学摩擦与中心超大质量黑洞的潮汐俘获效应；4）将模型与不同初始双星比例和周期分布的恒星种群参数化族进行卷积。

Result: 在基准盘模型中：单-单相遇数量最多，但双星介导的相遇贡献了68%的并合事件；绝大多数相遇可近似为抛物线轨道；由随机效应驱动的相对速度显著且不可忽略。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了更完备的吸积盘内致密天体相遇物理框架，所开发的工具具备良好可移植性，支持下游应用（如引力波源率预测），并给出了若干关键现象的半解析近似表达式。

Abstract: Disks surrounding active galactic nuclei contain many stars and black holes, which will inevitably encounter one another. The outcome of these encounters depends on how closely and how rapidly the bodies meet.
