Extending previous studies, we account for initial relative velocities seeded by stochastic effects in the disk; incorporate a strong-encounter cross-section accounting for single- and binary-mediated interactions computed via direct two-body (Hill) or three-body (isolated) interactions; and present a repeated-encounter model accounting both for energy loss due to stellar encounters as well as the SMBH tidal field. We convolve our encounter model with a parametric family of natal stellar and binary populations, focused on varying binary fraction and period distribution. In our fiducial disk model, even though single-single interactions supply most encounters, binary-mediated interactions supply 68% of collisions; most encounters are well-approximated as parabolic; and stochastically-driven relative velocities are comparatively substantial and driven by gravitational perturbations from disk fluctuations and neighboring bodies. Our tools are ready to use in downstream applications, including several easily-expressed semianalytic approximations to key interaction phenomena.

</details>


### [241] [Celia: Catalog Embeddings Learned from cosmologIcal simulAtions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40218)
*Xiaowen Zhang,Matthew Ho,Anshul Kumar,Tiziana Di Matteo,Rupert A. C. Croft,Barnabás Póczos*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: Celia是一个自监督基础模型，用于将宇宙学模拟中的子晕及星系属性目录映射为嵌入向量，实现天体物理与大尺度结构信息的压缩与组织；它在多个模拟套件上预训练，能高精度恢复宇宙学参数（如Ω_m、σ_8）和反馈参数，并具有良好的跨模拟泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙学流体动力学模拟计算昂贵且不同代码建模方式差异大，亟需一种统一、高效、可迁移的表示方法来压缩、组织和比较模拟数据。

Method: 提出自监督基础模型Celia，基于IllustrisTNG、SIMBA和Astrid（含N-body对照）等CAMELS模拟套件，通过掩码子晕属性重建和教师网络表征预测进行无标签预训练。

Result: 线性模型在嵌入空间中可高精度恢复Ω_m（R²=0.999）、σ_8及超新星反馈强度（R²≥0.97）；对未见过的Swift-EAGLE模拟也表现优异（Ω_m预测r=0.985）；嵌入主成分可用模拟参数的简单幂律描述。

Conclusion: Celia为模拟星系目录提供了共享坐标系，支持基于模拟的推断、代理建模、星系形成模型比较、物理简并检测及新天体物理探针发现。

Abstract: Cosmological hydrodynamical simulations connect observed galaxies to cosmology and the physics of galaxy formation. Each simulation is expensive, and different codes model galaxy formation in different ways. We present Celia (Catalog Embeddings Learned from cosmologIcal simulAtions), a self-supervised foundation model that maps catalogs of subhalos and their galaxy properties to an embedding vector for the purposes of compression and organization of astrophysical and large-scale structure information. Celia is pretrained without parameter labels on the IllustrisTNG, SIMBA and Astrid suites of CAMELS and their N-body counterparts, by reconstructing masked subhalo properties and predicting the representations of a teacher network. Simple linear models fitted to the embeddings recover $Ω_{\mathrm m}$ with $R^2=0.999$, and $σ_8$ and both supernova-feedback amplitudes with $R^2\geq0.97$, in all three suites. The embeddings translate well to out-of-distribution simulations such as Swift-EAGLE, a code that Celia never saw. A linear model fitted on the three training codes predicts its $Ω_{\mathrm m}$ with a correlation coefficient $r=0.985$, and linear models fitted on 20 labeled simulations reach $r\geq0.94$ for $Ω_{\mathrm m}$, $σ_8$ and a supernova-feedback amplitude. We decompose the variation among the simulations of each suite into principal components of the embedding, and simple power laws of the simulation parameters describe the leading components. Celia gives simulated galaxy catalogs a shared coordinate system for simulation-based inference, emulation, the comparison of galaxy-formation models, the detection of physical degeneracies and the discovery of new astrophysical probes.

</details>


### [242] [A compact NH$_2$D source without NH$_3$ counterpart in the S187 high-mass star-forming region](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40219)
*E. A. Trofimova,I. I. Zinchenko,C. Henkel,A. G. Pazukhin,A. F. Punanova,P. M. Zemlyanukha*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文研究了S187高质星形成区中一个邻近IRAS 01202+6133、缺乏显著NH₃对应体但富含NH₂D的致密源，通过OSO 20米射电望远镜观测与非LTE建模分析，发现其为一低温（~20–30 K）、亚/跨声速湍流、高氨氘化（NH₂D/NH₃ ≈ 1）的冷致密核，可能代表未受扰动的原始气体，但仍需更高分辨率观测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究S187区域中一个异常高氨氘化（NH₂D丰富但NH₃缺失）的致密源，因其在温暖大质量恒星形成环境中出现高氘化现象尚属罕见，可能揭示原始冷核的存在。

Method: 使用OSO 20米射电望远镜在72–112 GHz频段开展观测（低频区成图、高频区定点观测），结合非局部热动平衡（non-LTE）模型分析分子谱线数据，并利用多分子谱线、尘埃连续谱及温度/湍流诊断工具进行物理参数反演。

Result: 确认存在一个尺度≲0.2 pc的致密NH₂D源；检测到约20种分子，仅DCO⁺(1–0)峰与之空间重合；无对应NH₃峰或已知天体，但尘埃连续谱显示同位置增强；气体动能温度约20–30 K；NH₂D线宽~0.4 km/s，表明亚/跨声速湍流；o/p-NH₂D强度比支持丰度比为3；NH₂D/NH₃丰度比可能达~1。

Conclusion: 该NH₂D致密源很可能是一个嵌入温暖环境中的冷、致密、引力稳定的原始气体核，具有高氘化特征，暗示其尚未经历显著恒星形成活动；但当前角分辨率不足，需更高分辨率观测进一步验证其结构与演化状态。

Abstract: Our goal is to investigate a so far only tentatively detected compact NH$_2$D source devoid of a notable NH$_3$ counterpart, located rather close to the luminous IRAS 01202+6133 source (the projected distance is $\lesssim$0.3 pc) in the S187 high-mass star-forming region. A high ammonia deuteration in such an environment is unusual. We performed observations of this area with the 20-m OSO radio telescope in several frequency ranges from $\sim$72 to $\sim$112 GHz. The area was mapped at the lower frequencies while at the higher frequencies only the position of the NH$_2$D emission peak was observed. The data were analyzed using non-LTE modeling. The data confirm a compact NH$_2$D source with a size of $\lesssim$0.2 pc. In addition, emission from about 20 molecules was detected, but only the DCO$^+$(1-0) peak coincides with the NH$_2$D clump. The clump is not associated with any known astronomical source or an NH$_3$ emission peak but the dust continuum map shows an enhancement at approximately the same position. The gas kinetic temperature derived from various indicators is $\sim$20-30 K, which possibly characterizes the surrounding material. The intrinsic NH$_2$D line width ($\sim$0.4 km/s) implies a clump with subsonic or transonic turbulence. The ortho/para NH$_2$D line intensity ratio is consistent with an abundance ratio of 3, expected from the spin statistics. The NH$_2$D/NH$_3$ abundance ratio may be $\sim$1. In general, the data hint at a compact cold gravitationally stable dense core within the warm environment, which may represent pristine material. However this picture still needs a verification with observations at a higher angular resolution.

</details>


### [243] [Playing CHESS with stars II. Homogeneous analysis of high-resolution optical spectra for a sample of 135 giants in 14 bulge globular clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40332)
*S. Özdemir,R. Smiljanic,J. E. Martínez Fernández,P. R. T. Coelho,F. Primas,E. Valenti*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文对14个银河系核球球状星团中的135颗巨星进行了高分辨率光谱的均一化学丰度分析，揭示了核球的化学演化特征，包括较低的α元素丰度、Sc元素异常偏低、以及重元素（如Ba/Eu和Ce/Nd）比值所反映的r/s过程贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核球球状星团是研究银河系核球复杂形成历史的关键示踪体，其化学丰度保留了早期演化信息；需通过均一化分析厘清其与晕、盘星族的化学差异及多星族现象。

Method: 利用UVES存档高分辨率光谱，采用CHESS流程确定恒星大气参数，并通过谱合成法测定Li、O、Na等14种元素丰度；对Na、Mg等8种元素考虑非局部热动平衡（NLTE）效应；识别Li富集巨星并分析多星族特征。

Result: 发现NGC 6723中一颗经3D NLTE校正后A(Li)=1.68的Li富集巨星；核球星团在低金属丰度下[α/Fe]平台低于晕族，且下降更平缓；Sc丰度低于太阳及同金属度盘星；[Ba/Eu]≈0.0表明s-与r-过程共同作用；[Fe/H]<-0.5星团呈负[Ce/Nd]，支持r-过程主导。

Conclusion: 核球球状星团的化学丰度特征表明其形成环境与晕不同，可能经历更早、更持续的恒星形成与气体吸积；Sc异常和重元素比值为理解相关核合成过程提供了关键约束；多星族普遍存在，但重元素无显著弥散，支持原初富集模型。

Abstract: Bulge globular clusters are key tracers of the complex formation history of the Galactic bulge, as they retain information on its past star-formation episodes. Their chemical abundances serve as empirical `fossil records'' of the early evolution of the bulge. We present a homogeneous analysis of archival high-resolution UVES spectra for 135 giants in 14 Galactic bulge globular clusters. We explore the results in the context of the phenomenon of multiple stellar populations and discuss what the abundances reveal about the chemical evolution of the bulge. We employed the CHEmical Survey analysis System (CHESS) pipeline to derive atmospheric parameters on an accurate scale defined by reference stars. Abundances for Li, O, Na, Mg, Al, Si, Ca, Sc, Y, Zr, Ba, Ce, Nd, and Eu were derived by spectrum synthesis. Deviations from local thermodynamic equilibrium (i.e., non-LTE or NLTE) were taken into account for Na, Mg, Al, Si, Ca, Y, Ba, and Eu. We serendipitously discovered a Li-rich giant with A(Li) = 1.68 (after 3D NLTE corrections) in NGC 6723, which was hidden in the archival data. Our sample includes both first- and second-generation stars, as indicated by the observed spread in Na abundances. Compared to halo clusters, the bulge clusters show a lower [α/Fe] plateau at low metallicity and a more gradual decline in [α/Fe] toward higher metallicities. The Sc abundances are below solar and lower than in disk stars of the same metallicity. This difference could be important for solving open questions about the nucleosynthesis of this element. None of the clusters exhibit any significant spreads in heavy elements. Most clusters exhibit [Ba/Eu] \sim 0.0, indicating a contribution from both s- and r-processes. Clusters with [Fe/H] < -0.5 show negative [Ce/Nd] ratios, supporting the idea that these elements were predominantly produced by the r-process in these clusters.

</details>


### [244] [Complex Organic Molecules in Protostars with ALMA Spectral Surveys (COMPASS) VIII. First interstellar detection of fully deuterated methyl cyanide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40365)
*A. Belloche,J. K. Jørgensen,M. N. Drozdovskaya,S. Spezzano,A. Coutens,J. -E. Lee,F. Cruz-Sáenz de Miera,D. Harsono,J. -H. Jeong,S. Maret,B. A. McGuire,P. Nazari,A. L. Plunkett,Y. -L. Yang,H. -S. Yun*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文报告了星际介质中甲基氰（CD3CN）全氘化形式的首次探测，利用ALMA对L1551 IRS5原恒星系统的高灵敏度观测，结合柱密度分析与氘/氢比趋势，推断甲基氰主要在低温前恒星阶段尘埃表面形成，其氘化行为与甲醇相似但存在差异，暗示可能涉及气相温升过程或更早的形成时机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索星际介质中甲基氰全氘化形式CD3CN的存在，以揭示其在恒星形成区中的分子形成路径。

Method: 利用ALMA COMPASS大型项目对L1551 IRS5的高灵敏成像光谱巡天数据，在局域热动平衡假设下推导甲基氰及其多种同位素体（包括氘代物）的柱密度。

Result: 首次在星际介质中探测到CD3CN；检测到甲基氰全部三种氘代同位素体；D/H比随氘代数增加约升高0.5个数量级，与甲醇趋势相似但数值低约因子2。

Conclusion: 甲基氰主要在冷的前恒星阶段尘埃表面形成；其氘化模式支持与甲醇类似但不完全相同的形成机制，可能涉及气相温升贡献或更早的形成时间；目前尚无模型涵盖至全氘化甲基氰的化学演化。

Abstract: Deuteration is a powerful metric to gain insight into the formation pathways of interstellar molecules in star forming regions. Our primary goal is to search for the fully deuterated form of methyl cyanide, CD3CN, in the ISM. We used a sensitive imaging spectral survey of the protostellar system L1551 IRS5 in the Taurus molecular cloud performed as part of the Large Program carried out with the Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array (ALMA) called Complex Organic Molecules in Protostars with ALMA Spectral Surveys (COMPASS). We derived the column densities of methyl cyanide and several of its less abundant isotopologs, including deuterated ones, under the assumption of local thermodynamic equilibrium. All three deuterated isotopologs of methyl cyanide were detected toward the northern binary component of L1551 IRS5. In particular, we report the first interstellar detection of CD3CN. The measured D/H ratio increases by about half an order of magnitude from one deuterated isotopolog to the next, in a similar way as what was measured for methanol in several other low-mass protostars. This suggests that methyl cyanide predominantly forms like methanol on the surface of dust grains during the cold prestellar phase. However, the measured factor of 2 difference between the D/H ratios of methyl cyanide and methanol may either indicate that gas-phase processes during the warm-up stage contribute approximately equally to the production of methyl cyanide, or that methyl cyanide and its deuterated isotopologs are formed during the prestellar phase at a somewhat earlier time than methanol. Multiply deuterated isotopologs are crucial to understand the formation and inheritance of molecules in the ISM by coupling observations and astrochemical models. Unfortunately, no model has so far investigated the deuteration of methyl cyanide up to the fully deuterated form. [Abridged]

</details>


### [245] [The IRAC Distant Cluster Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40534)
*Karolina Garcia,Anthony H. Gonzalez,Mark Brodwin,Peter Eisenhardt,Tyler George,Nadia Hansen,S. A. Stanford,Daniel Stern,Khunanon Thongkham,Ariane Trudeau,Michael J. I. Brown,Buell T. Jannuzi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文介绍了红外阵列相机（IRAC）遥远星系团巡天（IDCS）星表，包含825个基于12波段光学与红外测光红移概率分布识别出的星系团/星系群候选体，覆盖红移范围0.08 < z < 2.0，其中147个位于z ≥ 1.5；通过模拟验证了选择函数（z < 1.5时纯度93%，完备率50%对应质量log(M₂₀₀c/M⊙)=13.74），并利用光谱和X射线观测确认了80个系统，包括38个DESI DR1新确认系统；该星表为大尺度结构、致密环境中星系演化及未来巡天（如LSST、Roman、Euclid）的星团探测校准提供了重要资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建一个具有明确选择函数、覆盖宽红移范围（特别是高红移z≥1.5）的可靠星系团样本，以支持大尺度结构研究、星系环境演化分析，并为当前及未来宽视场巡天（如LSST、Roman、Euclid）中的星团探测方法提供标定基准。

Method: 使用PZWav算法，在Boötes场十二波段光学与红外成像数据中，基于测光红移概率分布识别星系团/群候选体；通过模拟评估纯度与完备率；利用DESI DR1光谱、X射线及文献中独立确认的星团进行验证与红移精度评估（σ_z/(1+z)=0.017）；对已确认系统测量速度弥散与晕质量。

Result: 获得包含825个候选体的IDCS星表，覆盖0.08 < z < 2.0（中位红移z=0.93），含147个z≥1.5系统；z<1.5时模拟显示S/N≥4下纯度93%，完备率50%对应log(M₂₀₀c/M⊙)=13.74；80个系统获光谱/X射线确认，其中38个由DESI DR1确认（z=0.12–0.93，各≥10个成员，中位速度弥散~490 km/s，对应中位晕质量1.1×10¹⁴ M⊙）；与文献确认星团的位置比对显示中位质心偏移211 kpc。

Conclusion: IDCS星表是一个红移覆盖广、选择函数明确、经多手段验证的高质量星系团候选体样本，可有效支撑星系演化、宇宙大尺度结构及下一代巡天项目中星团探测技术的标定与发展。

Abstract: We present the Infrared Array Camera (IRAC) Distant Cluster Survey (IDCS), a catalog of 825 galaxy cluster and group candidates identified in the Boötes field using the PZWav detection code. Candidates are selected using photometric redshift probability distributions derived from twelve-band optical and infrared imaging. The catalog spans $0.08 < z < 2.0$ (median $z = 0.93$) and includes 147 systems at $z \geq 1.5$. For $z < 1.5$, simulations indicate a purity of $93\%$ at the adopted S/N $\geq 4$ threshold and $50\%$ completeness at $\log(M_{200c}/M_\odot) = 13.74 \pm 0.01$. We validate the cluster photometric redshifts through comparison with spectroscopic data, finding $σ_z/(1+z)=0.017$. Including 18 previously published IRAC Shallow Cluster Survey (ISCS) systems, a total of 80 IDCS cluster candidates are now confirmed through spectroscopy and/or X-ray observations. DESI DR1 spectroscopy provides redshifts for 38 systems, including 23 newly confirmed clusters. These DESI-confirmed systems span $0.12 < z < 0.93$ and have at least 10 spectroscopic members each, from which we measure a median velocity dispersion of $\sim 490$ km s$^{-1}$, corresponding to a median halo mass of $1.1 \times 10^{14} M_\odot$. A positional comparison with 39 independently confirmed literature clusters yields a median centroid offset of 211 kpc. The IDCS catalog provides a cluster sample spanning a wide redshift range, with a selection function characterized through simulations and is a valuable resource for studies of large-scale structure, galaxy evolution in dense environments, and the calibration of cluster detection techniques for current and upcoming wide-field surveys, including LSST, Roman, and Euclid.

</details>


### [246] [From Dispersion to Metallicity: Probing the Milky Way Circumgalactic Medium with Fast Radio Bursts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40609)
*Manami Roy,Sanskriti Das,Smita Mathur,Anjali Gupta,Yair Krongold*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新统计方法，利用25个快速射电暴（FRB）的色散量（DM）推断银河系周围介质（CGM）金属丰度，发现其显著高于星系际介质（IGM）预期值，表明大量金属富集反馈物质被保留在CGM中，并呈现显著视线方向差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CGM在星系演化中起关键作用，但其金属丰度观测困难；需通过新方法直接约束银河系CGM热相金属丰度，并检验金属富集反馈物质是否被有效保留。

Method: 结合氧吸收线提供的金属丰度依赖型DM估计与FRB观测得到的金属丰度无关DM，对25条FRB视线方向进行统计分析，并采用自举法，在不同弥散重子分数（fd=0.0, 0.6, 0.8）假设下推断CGM金属丰度。

Result: 推得CGM金属丰度中位数为0.12–0.39 Z⊙，显著高于IGM吸积预期（≲0.1 Z⊙）；部分视线方向出现超太阳金属丰度（fd=0.8时），不合理；fd=0.0保守下限仍远超IGM预期；且视线间金属丰度差异显著。

Conclusion: 结果提供强观测证据：银河系CGM高效保留了大量金属富集的反馈物质；CGM具有高度各向异性和多相结构，由反馈外流与宇宙吸积复杂相互作用塑造。

Abstract: Circumgalactic Medium (CGM) plays a critical role in galactic evolution, serving as the primary reservoir for accretion and outflows. In this work, we present a novel statistical analysis to derive CGM metallicity from the dispersion measure (DM) of 25 Fast Radio Burst (FRB) sightlines. By combining metallicity-dependent oxygen absorption DM estimates with metallicity-independent FRB DMs, we directly constrain the metallicity of the hot phase of the Milky Way (MW) CGM. Considering different diffuse baryon fractions ($f_{\text{d}} = 0.0, 0.6, 0.8$) in the Intergalactic Medium (IGM) and intervening halos, the median metallicities of CGM are $Z_{\text{CGM}} = 0.12_{-0.04}^{+0.09} \, Z_{\odot}$, $0.26_{-0.12}^{+0.18} \, Z_{\odot}$, and $0.39_{-0.20}^{+0.26} \, Z_{\odot}$, respectively, by the bootstrapping method. Super-solar metallicity is seen in multiple sightlines for $f_d=0.8$, which is implausible for MW CGM metallicities. Even under conservative lower-bound estimates ($f_{\text{d}} = 0.0$), the inferred CGM metallicity significantly exceeds expectations for IGM accretion ($Z_{\text{IGM}} \lesssim 0.1 \, Z_{\odot}$), providing strong empirical evidence that a large fraction of the metal-enriched galactic feedback material is efficiently retained within the Milky Way CGM. Furthermore, we observe significant sightline-to-sightline variation in inferred metallicities, pointing toward a highly anisotropic multi-phase CGM shaped by complex interactions between feedback outflows and cosmic accretion.

</details>


### [247] [PRISMS GHZ8: Evidence for a Young Burst and Potential Merger Activity at z=10.23](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40633)
*Ruqiu Lin,Jorge Zavala,Javier Álvarez-Márquez,Luis Colina,Marco Castellano,Adriano Fontana,Paola Santini,Lorenzo Napolitano,Sarah Kendrew,Alejandro Crespo Gómez,Victoria Salazar,Abdurro'uf,Stefano Carniani,Tiger Hsiao,Yuichi Harikane,Mahmoud Hamed,Takuya Hashimoto,Akio Inoue,Danial Langeroodi,Rui Marques-Chaves,Ikki Mitsuhashi,Yurina Nakazato,Borja Pérez-Díaz,Pierluigi Rinaldi,Carlota Prieto-Jiménez,Ana Varo-O'Ferrall,Fang-Ting Yuan,Shuairu Zhu,Zhen-Ya Zheng*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST观测到z=10.23的UV亮星系GHZ8，发现其由两个致密成分组成，其中东南部成分（GHZ8-SE）具有极蓝紫外连续谱，暗示极高电离光子逃逸率（f_esc=0.6–1.0）；主成分（GHZ8-main）显示爆发式或短时持续恒星形成活动（<50 Myr），为早期宇宙中UV亮星系的爆发式恒星形成提供了直接观测证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UV亮星系对理解早期宇宙恒星质量快速增长及再电离源至关重要，但其高UV光度的物理机制尚不明确。

Method: 利用JWST/MIRI LRS光谱和NIRCam成像对z=10.23的放大星系GHZ8进行观测与形态分解，并结合光谱测光SED拟合分析其恒星形成性质。

Result: 首次在z>10星系中探测到Hα和Hβ-[OIII]发射线；识别出GHZ8由两个空间分离仅≈600 pc的成分（GHZ8-main和GHZ8-SE）构成；GHZ8-SE具有极蓝UV谱（β_UV≈−3.45），要求极年轻、ISM贫乏且高f_esc的恒星种群；GHZ8-main的UV与Hα光度表明其处于短时爆发末期或<50 Myr的持续恒星形成阶段。

Conclusion: GHZ8为早期宇宙中UV亮星系存在爆发式恒星形成的首个直接观测证据，可能正经历并合驱动的星暴，但需更高分辨率数据确认。

Abstract: UV-bright galaxies in the early Universe provide important constraints on the rapid build-up of stellar mass and the sources responsible for cosmic reionization. However, the physical processes that drive their high UV luminosities remain unclear. Here we report JWST/MIRI LRS spectroscopy of GHZ8, a UV-bright galaxy at z=10.23 with a magnification $μ=1.23$. The spectrum shows a $\sim 5σ$ detection for the $\rm Hβ$-[OIII] complex and $\sim 4σ$ for $\rm Hα$ line, with fluxes of $3.2\pm0.9\times10^{-18}$ and $\rm 1.7\pm0.4\times10^{-18}\,erg\,s^{-1}\,cm^{-2}$, respectively. Based on JWST/NIRCam imaging, we find that GHZ8 consists of two components separated only by 0.16" ($\approx600$ pc): a bright, extended main component and a faint, compact southeastern clump, hereafter referred to as GHZ8-main and GHZ8-SE. Through morphological decomposition, we find demagnified $M_{UV}=-19.91\pm0.10$ and $-18.03\pm0.15$ mag for GHZ8-main and GHZ8-SE, with blue UV slopes of $β_{UV}=-2.53\pm0.22$ and $-3.45\pm0.49$, respectively, indicating that both components host young stellar populations. In particular, the extremely blue UV continuum of GHZ8-SE requires an extremely young, ISM-poor stellar population with a high ionizing photon escape fraction, $f_{esc}= 0.6-1.0$. Combining the UV and $\rm Hα$ luminosities with the results from the spectro-photometric SED fitting, we constrain the star-formation activity of GHZ8-main, and find either a recent, short-lived burst of star formation that is now in its final phases, or continuous star formation over a relatively short period ($<50\,{\rm Myr}$). GHZ8 therefore provides observational evidence for bursty star formation in a UV-bright galaxy. Globally, GHZ8 may be undergoing a merger-induced starburst, although deeper imaging or spatially resolved kinematic information is required for a more definitive interpretation.

</details>

### [248] [A GLIMPSE of Dark Matter: JWST Constraints on Early Structure Formation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37751)
*Julian B. Muñoz,John Chisholm,Seiji Fujimoto,Tiger Yu-Yang Hsiao,Kristen B. W. McQuinn,Priyamvada Natarajan,A. Saldana-Lopez,Hakim Atek,Arghyadeep Basu,Danielle Berg,Ryan Endsley,Lukas J. Furtak,Damien Korber,Rohan P. Naidu,Richard Pan,Daniel Schaerer*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST GLIMPSE、哈勃和斯巴鲁观测数据，通过高红移（z~7）星系紫外光度函数检验暗物质小尺度结构形成，结果支持冷暗物质模型，并对热遗迹温暗物质质量给出m_WDM > 3.2 keV（95%置信度）的新限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中间红移时期（再电离期z~7）暗物质行为尚不清楚，而第一代星系的形成提供了探测其小尺度性质的关键窗口。

Method: 结合JWST GLIMPSE透镜巡天、哈勃与斯巴鲁的紫外光度函数观测数据，在边缘化天体物理不确定性后，拟合物质功率谱，并对热遗迹温暗物质模型施加质量限制。

Result: 在k=3–27 Mpc⁻¹尺度上，观测到的功率谱与冷暗物质预测一致；对温暗物质质量给出95%置信度下限m_WDM > 3.2 keV，且该限制独立于低红移探针。

Conclusion: 结果首次在再电离时期对暗物质小尺度行为进行了直接检验，证实冷暗物质模型在亚Mpc尺度仍成立，并展示了JWST在暗物质本质研究中的强大潜力。

Abstract: While we have a solid understanding of how dark matter (DM) behaves during recombination ($z\sim 10^3$) and in the late universe ($z\lesssim$ a few), much less is known about intermediate times, when the first galaxies formed. These galaxies provide a powerful handle on DM through structure formation, as halos have to exist to host luminous sources. Here we use recent determinations of the UV luminosity function from the James Webb Space Telescope (JWST) GLIMPSE lensing program, along with legacy Hubble and Subaru data, to test the small-scale behavior of DM during the epoch of reionization ($z\sim 7$). Marginalizing over astrophysical uncertainties, we find the matter power spectrum to be consistent with the cold-DM prediction down to sub-Mpc scales, with 1$σ$ uncertainties of 0.2 dex at $k=3-9 \, \rm Mpc^{-1}$ and 0.5 dex at $k=9-27 \, \rm Mpc^{-1}$, the smallest scales probed at these early times. For thermal-relic warm DM we constrain $m_{\rm WDM} > 3.2\, \rm keV$ (95% CL), independent of lower-$z$ probes. Our results test small-scale structure formation in a new redshift window and demonstrate the promise of JWST to constrain the nature of DM.

</details>


### [249] [Disentangling constraints on $S_8$ from the high and low redshift CMB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37752)
*Joann Jones,Ualype de Andrade Uchôa,Scott Dodelson*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新方法，将CMB数据中的高红移与低红移信息分离，发现去除引力透镜效应后，CMB推得的S8与DES观测结果一致，缓解了所谓的S8张力问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CMB与星系巡天在S8参数上存在的张力问题，传统方法混淆了CMB中高、低红移成分（尤其是透镜效应带来的低红移信息），难以准确比较不同时期的结构增长。

Method: 采用主成分分析（PCA）对CMB透镜信息进行边缘化处理，从而分别提取原初CMB（高z）与透镜CMB（低z）所含的宇宙学参数约束。

Result: 仅用原初CMB（Planck 2018+ACT DR6+SPT-3G D1）得到S8 = 0.795±0.022，与DES的0.789±0.012高度一致；而包含透镜信息时S8偏高，且该偏差依赖于中微子质量和∑mν先验。

Conclusion: S8张力可能主要源于CMB透镜信号（即低红移结构增长），而非原初CMB本身；去除透镜贡献后，ΛCDM框架下CMB与晚期探针之间的一致性显著增强。

Abstract: The clustering of matter in the universe is quantified by $σ_8$, the RMS fluctuations today in linear theory in spheres of radius $8h^{-1}$ Mpc, where $h$ parametrizes the Hubble constant. Surveys of gravitational lensing and galaxy clustering best constrain $S_8\equiv (Ω_m/0.3)^{0.5} σ_8$, where $Ω_m$ is the energy density of matter in units of the critical density. Comparing this late time measurement to the value of $S_8$ inferred from the cosmic microwave background (CMB), one can test the $Λ$CDM prediction for the growth of clustering from the primordial universe to now. Several analyses report hints that CMB measurements of $S_8$ are larger than those from galaxy surveys. However, CMB measurements have information from both high and low redshift due to lensing, and these tests traditionally conflate the two. Here, we present a technique to extract constraints on the cosmological parameters from each redshift component separately, enabling a high-z vs. low-z clustering comparison that was not previously possible. We marginalize over lensing information using a principal component analysis, and show that the primordial CMB from combined Planck 2018, ACT DR6, and SPT-3G D1 prefers $S_8= 0.795\pm0.022$, in agreement with the recent determination from the Dark Energy Survey (DES) of $S_8=0.789\pm0.012$. The clustering inferred from CMB lensing is larger. However, both the level of excess lensing and increase in agreement with DES depends on the prior on the sum of neutrino masses, $\sum m_ν$. We find that removing lensing brings the CMB towards agreement with DES in any case.

</details>


### [250] [The Redshift-Space Cluster-Galaxy Cross-Correlation as a Probe of Gravity on Cosmic Scales](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37782)
*Nicole Marcelina Gountanis,David H. Weinberg,Andrés N. Salcedo,Claire Lamman*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI BGS巡天数据，通过星系团-星系红移空间交叉相关函数，对广义相对论（GR）在宇宙学尺度上的偏离进行Fisher矩阵预测，以参数FG量化引力偏离，并获得高精度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在宇宙学尺度上检验广义相对论需要直接对引力动力学敏感的探针，而星系团的运动学信息（如自由落体速度场）提供了这一可能。

Method: 基于AbacusSummit N体模拟构建模拟星表，采用HOD模型描述星系分布、对数正态质量-可观测量关系刻画星系团选择；用单参数FG重标晕块自由落体速度以表征对GR的偏离；通过Fisher矩阵分析ξ^s_cg(r_p, r_π)对FG及其他参数的约束能力，并考虑参数退化影响。

Result: DESI BGS可在z~0.3处将FG约束至σ(FG)=0.033，足以以>4σ区分典型f(R)与Galileon引力模型；主要限制来自FG与质量-可观测量散射的简并；若完全知晓星系-晕关联，约束可提升至σ(FG)≈0.015；同时可高精度测得Ω_m和σ_8（均为0.010），且与FG弱相关。

Conclusion: DESI BGS的星系团-星系相关性是检验准线性尺度引力理论的有力新探针，可与弱透镜星系团质量剖面等现有方法互补。

Abstract: Testing General Relativity (GR) on cosmological scales requires probes that are directly sensitive to gravitational dynamics. We present a Fisher matrix forecast of how well the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) Bright Galaxy Survey (BGS) can constrain departures from GR through the cluster infall velocity field, using the redshift-space cluster-galaxy cross-correlation function $ξ^{\rm s}_{\rm cg}(r_p, r_π)$ as the primary observable. We construct mock catalogs from the AbacusSummit N-body simulations, using a Halo Occupation Distribution (HOD) model for galaxies and a log-normal mass-observable relation for cluster selection. We parameterize departures from GR with a single multiplicative factor $F_{\rm G}$ that rescales halo infall velocities relative to the GR prediction. Marginalizing over the HOD, mass-observable, and cosmological parameters, we find that DESI BGS can constrain $F_{\rm G}$ to $σ(F_{\rm G}) = 0.033$ at $z \sim 0.3$. This precision is sufficient to distinguish representative $f(R)$ and Galileon gravity models from GR at $\gtrsim 4σ$. The strongest limitation is a degeneracy between $F_{\rm G}$ and the scatter in the cluster mass-observable relation, while the constraint shows no strong degeneracy with any individual HOD parameter. Perfect knowledge of the galaxy-halo connection would improve the constraint to $σ(F_{\rm G}) \approx 0.015$, and including the galaxy autocorrelation function in the data vector could recover part of this improvement. The same data vector yields tight constraints on the cosmological parameters, $σ(Ω_m) = 0.010$ and $σ(σ_8) = 0.010$, only moderately correlated with $F_{\rm G}$. These results demonstrate the potential of DESI BGS cluster-galaxy correlations to test gravity on quasi-linear scales, complementary to existing modified gravity probes and to weak-lensing cluster mass profiles.

</details>


### [251] [Probing Cosmology with Diffusion Models: Memorization, Generalization, and Parameter Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37825)
*Jiaming Pan,Nicholas Kern*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型作为宇宙学模拟替代品的可靠性，发现即使模型通过了视觉、单点统计和功率谱收敛测试，仍可能导致宇宙学参数估计过度自信；训练数据集大小对模型泛化能力和参数密度校准至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简单重建保真度等指标无法保证生成模型在科学推断中的可信性，需探究其在宇宙学推断任务中的实际表现。

Method: 在CAMELS IllustrisTNG模拟的HI场数据上训练扩散模型，系统分析训练集大小对样本记忆性、一阶/二阶统计量一致性（像素值分布与功率谱）及宇宙学参数估计的影响。

Result: 小训练集导致记忆化并引发参数后验密度校准失准；存在一个临界训练集规模，使模型能良好泛化并实现校准的后验估计；模型可通过传统统计检验却仍产生过自信的参数推断。

Conclusion: 在采用神经代理模型的宇宙学推断流程中，除常规统计检验外，必须增加模型泛化能力的评估作为必要验证环节。

Abstract: Diffusion models are increasingly used as surrogates for expensive cosmological simulations, but simple metrics like reconstruction fidelity do not guarantee a surrogate can be trusted for scientific inference. To explore this, we train diffusion models on HI fields from the CAMELS IllustrisTNG simulations and test how three properties change with training-set size: memorization of individual training examples, agreement with one- and two-point statistics (the pixel-value distribution and the power spectrum), and estimation of cosmological parameters from the generated fields. At fixed model size, generative models trained with smaller, less diverse training data produced memorized samples. While this is a well known phenomenon, we show that this leads to miscalibrated parameter densities for cosmological inference tasks. At fixed size, there is a training set size with enough information content to enable a model to generalize, at which point posterior density estimation with neural surrogates can be well-calibrated. We demonstrate that a neural surrogate can therefore pass visual, one-point and power-spectrum convergence tests while still resulting in overconfident parameter estimation in a cosmological inference pipeline. Thus, tests of model generalization should be an additional check for inference pipelines adopting neural surrogates.

</details>


### [252] [Early Dark Energy Crowds Out Interacting Dark Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37836)
*William Giarè,Jeremy Sakstein*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在存在早期暗能量（EDE）的情况下，暗能量与冷暗物质之间的相互作用是否能进一步缓解哈勃常数（H₀）张力。结果表明，观测数据强烈倾向于非相互作用极限，即最佳拟合模型退化为标准EDE模型；引入相互作用带来的H₀提升非常有限，且会显著恶化对低红移观测（如Ia型超新星）的拟合，尤其因CMB对早期物质密度的强约束导致当前物质密度被压得过低，从而被数据强烈排斥；该限制在EDE已提升H₀时尤为严重，且适用于一大类再复合后相互作用模型；即使允许暗能量状态方程动力学演化（CPL形式），结论不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解哈勃常数（H₀）观测值在不同宇宙学探针间存在的张力，特别是探索在已有早期暗能量（EDE）模型基础上，引入暗能量与冷暗物质的晚期相互作用能否提供额外改善。

Method: 构建包含早期暗能量（EDE）、晚期独立暗能量组分（w ≈ −1）、宇宙学常数及暗物质-暗能量相互作用的扩展ΛCDM模型；通过联合宇宙微波背景（CMB）、Ia型超新星等观测数据进行参数约束与模型拟合分析；检验相互作用强度、能量流向及状态方程动力学（CPL形式）的影响。

Result: 数据强烈支持非相互作用极限；最佳拟合模型实质回归标准EDE模型；虽能量从暗物质流向暗能量可略微提升H₀，但导致低红移数据（尤其SNe Ia）拟合显著变差；CMB对早期物质密度的紧约束迫使当前Ωₘ过低，被SNe Ia强烈排除；该限制在EDE已提升H₀时加剧；CPL参数化不改变核心结论。

Conclusion: 在EDE框架下引入暗能量与冷暗物质的晚期相互作用无法有效缓解哈勃张力，反而因与低红移观测冲突而被数据强烈抑制；该失效机制具有普适性，适用于多种再复合后相互作用模型；因此，单纯叠加晚期相互作用并非解决H₀张力的可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate whether interacting dark energy can provide additional relief to the Hubble tension once early dark energy (EDE) is present. Considering a cosmology containing EDE, an independent late-time dark energy fluid with an equation of state fixed at $w\simeq-1$ interacting with cold dark matter, and a cosmological constant, we find that the data strongly drive the model toward the non-interacting limit. The best fit effectively reduces to the standard EDE cosmology, where a non-zero EDE component provides most of the required increase in $H_0$, leaving little statistical gain for the interaction. Although energy transfer from dark matter to dark energy can raise $H_0$ further, this limited gain is rapidly outweighed by the deterioration of the fit to low-redshift observations. In particular, since the CMB tightly constrains the early-time physical matter density, any additional energy transfer requires a lower present-day matter abundance. Such low values are strongly disfavored by Type Ia supernovae. We show that this limitation becomes particularly severe once EDE has already raised $H_0$ and that the same mechanism extends to a broad class of post-recombination interactions. Allowing a dynamical dark energy equation of state of the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder form does not alter our conclusions.

</details>


### [253] [Core-collapse universality for inelastic self-interacting dark matter halos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37852)
*Evan Batteas,Alexander Brisebois,Maheen Hemani,Maximilian Heyne,J. Leo Kim,Lukas Lehmann,Laura Sagunski,Aster Schnell,Sean Tulin*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有速度依赖的弹性和耗散自相互作用的非弹性暗物质晕的引力热坍缩，推导了通过激发态上散射和衰变导致的有效冷却率，并数值求解了引力热流体方程；发现非弹性暗物质晕演化具有隐藏的参数普适性，并据此提出了无需模拟即可计算坍缩时间的半解析公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非弹性暗物质晕在自相互作用下的引力热演化行为，特别是其与纯弹性情形不同的动力学多样性及潜在普适规律。

Method: 基于引力热流体框架，推导非弹性过程（上散射至激发态并衰变）的有效冷却率；数值求解球对称引力热流体方程，模拟核心增长与坍缩全过程；通过参数分析挖掘演化中的隐藏普适性，并构建半解析坍缩时间公式。

Result: 发现非弹性暗物质晕演化不具普遍轨迹，但存在隐藏的参数普适性；成功导出适用于任意速度依赖弹性与非弹性截面的半解析坍缩时间公式。

Conclusion: 非弹性自相互作用显著改变暗物质晕的引力热演化路径，但其坍缩动力学仍受基本参数控制，可通过半解析方法高效预测，为观测约束非弹性暗物质模型提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We study the gravothermal collapse of inelastic dark matter halos with velocity-dependent elastic and dissipative self-interactions. Within the gravothermal fluid framework, we derive the effective cooling rate for dark matter upscattering to an excited state followed by decay back to the ground state, with emitted states carrying off energy from the halo. We solve the gravothermal fluid equations numerically to simulate spherically symmetric halos over the core-growing and collapse phases of their evolution. While halos with purely elastic self-interactions have universal evolution trajectories, inelastic dark matter halos exhibit much more diversity in their evolution. Nevertheless, inelastic halo dynamics contain a hidden parametric universality from which we derive a semi-analytic formula for calculating the halo collapse time for arbitrary velocity-dependent elastic and inelastic cross sections without the need for simulations.

</details>


### [254] [Simulating and Characterizing Non-Gaussian Small-Scale Polarized Emission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.37942)
*Mark A. Bishop,Brandon S. Hensley,Krzysztof M. Gorski,Charles Lawrence,Graça Rocha*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种生成随机小尺度银河系前景辐射（以偏振同步辐射为例）的新框架，能够产生多个独立实现，并继承大尺度形态信息，包含各向异性、多重分形和被输送的非高斯特征，参数少且具物理意义；通过高阶统计量（如闵可夫斯基泛函和结构函数）验证其非高斯特性，为构建含随机非高斯结构的前景模拟套件奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前前景模拟无法以随机方式再现星际介质（ISM）中观测到的纤维状、非高斯结构，而这类结构对CMB B模探测构成关键干扰，亟需更真实的模拟方法。

Method: 构建基于偏振同步辐射观测的随机小尺度前景发射生成框架，引入具有物理意义的少量参数，刻画各向异性、多重分形与对流型非高斯结构，并采用高阶统计量（而非仅功率谱）评估合成场的结构复杂性。

Result: 成功生成多个独立且形态上继承大尺度信息的随机前景实现；验证了Minkowski泛函和结构函数能部分表征其可调的非高斯程度。

Conclusion: 该框架为发展新一代含随机非高斯ISM结构的CMB前景模拟提供了必要基础，有望提升B模宇宙学分析的稳健性。

Abstract: Galactic foregrounds present a major challenge for cosmic microwave background (CMB) cosmology as their amplitudes exceed the target primordial $B$-mode signal. While small-scale interstellar medium (ISM) emission is expected to be highly complex, current foreground simulations cannot reproduce the filamentary, non-Gaussian features of ISM structure in a stochastic way. To address this gap, we introduce a framework for generating stochastic small-scale foreground emission, using polarized synchrotron observations as an example use case. This method is capable of producing many independent realizations that additionally inherit morphological information from large angular scales. The underlying structural complexity incorporates anisotropic, multifractal, and advected non-Gaussian features that are tunable with a small number of parameters that carry physical interpretations. Because standard power spectra do not describe the complex phase coherence required for the structural complexity, we evaluate the synthetic fields using higher-order statistics, demonstrating that the Minkowski functionals and structure functions partially capture varying degrees of non-Gaussianity. This work lays the necessary foundation for new foreground simulation suites with stochastic realizations of possible non-Gaussian structure.

</details>


### [255] [The scale of homogeneity in DESI Data Release 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38083)
*Cullan Howlett,L. Giani,Elizabeth G. O'Brien,A. Whitford,Tamara M. Davis,J. Aguilar,S. Ahlen,U. Andrade,E. Armengaud,S. BenZvi,F. Beutler,D. Bianchi,D. Brooks,R. Canning,A. Carnero Rosell,F. J. Castander,E. Chaussidon,T. Claybaugh,A. Cuceu,A. de la Macorra,Arjun Dey,Biprateep Dey,P. Doel,D. Forero-Sánchez,J. E. Forero-Romero,E. Gaztañaga,Satya Gontcho A Gontcho,G. Gutierrez,C. Hahn,K. Honscheid,D. Huterer,M. Ishak,S. Juneau,T. Karim,D. Kirkby,A. Kremin,O. Lahav,A. Lambert,C. Lamman,M. Landriau,L. Le Guillou,M. Manera,A. Meisner,R. Miquel,J. Moustakas,S. Nadathur,J. A. Newman,H. E. Noriega,E. Paillas,N. Palanque-Delabrouille,W. J. Percival,C. Poppett,F. Prada,I. Pérez-Ràfols,C. Ravoux,G. Rossi,R. Ruggeri,L. Samushia,E. Sanchez,C. Saulder,E. F. Schlafly,D. Schlegel,M. Schubnell,J. Silber,M. Siudek,G. Tarlé,B. A. Weaver,H. Zou*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI第一版数据（DR1）测量宇宙均匀性尺度，通过近500万个星系和类星体的计数-球体估计器，确定了不同红shift区间下的同质性尺度RH，并验证了ΛCDM模型预测，为宇宙学原理提供了强有力支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验宇宙学原理，即宇宙在大尺度上是均匀且各向同性的，这是现代宇宙学的基石假设，需通过观测数据进行严格验证。

Method: 使用DESI DR1数据，采用计数-球体（counts-in-spheres）估计器，计算相关维数D2(r)，定义同质性尺度RH为D2(r)进入均匀渐近值1%范围内的尺度；结合不同红移区间、星系偏差及建模方法，并校正南北银冠系统偏差。

Result: 所有示踪体均明确过渡到同质性状态，星系同质性尺度为93.8–142.6 h⁻¹Mpc；物质同质性尺度随红移单调下降，与ΛCDM高度一致；识别并校正了约10%的南北银冠系统偏差；精度显著优于WiggleZ和SDSS先前结果。

Conclusion: 在0.1 < z < 2.1全红移范围内，观测结果强有力支持宇宙学原理；DESI数据为宇宙大尺度结构和基础宇宙学假设的精密检验树立了新标杆。

Abstract: We present measurements of the scale of cosmic homogeneity using the first data release (DR1) of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), the largest publicly available spectroscopic extragalactic survey to date. Using a counts-in-spheres estimator applied to nearly 5 million galaxies and quasars across six independent redshift bins spanning $0.1 < z < 2.1$, we measure the correlation dimension $D_2(r)$ and define the homogeneity scale $R_H$ as the point at which the galaxy distribution reaches within 1\% of the homogeneous asymptotic value. We find that all tracers clearly transition to homogeneity, with the galaxy homogeneity scale ranging from $93.8^{+0.9}_{-0.7}$ to $142.6^{+1.4}_{-1.4}$ $h^{-1}$Mpc depending on the tracer redshift, galaxy bias, and modelling used to extract the scale. Using a cosmology-dependent model, we reconstruct the underlying matter homogeneity scale, finding it decreases monotonically with redshift in excellent agreement with $Λ$CDM predictions. We identify a systematic offset of up to $\sim 10\%$ between measurements in the North and South Galactic Caps, largely due to fiber assignment effects reproduced in our simulations. Accounting for this, and with a complementary demonstration using toy models that our methodology correctly identifies inhomogeneous universes in all but rather finely-tuned observer configurations, this hence provides strong evidence in favour of the cosmological principle across our full redshift range. Our matter homogeneity scale measurements are consistent with, and substantially more precise than, previous constraints from WiggleZ and SDSS, demonstrating the power of DESI data for precision tests of the cosmological principle.

</details>


### [256] [Gravitational Wave Background from Metastable Current-Carrying Cosmic Strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38140)
*Ivan Rybak,Rinku Maji,Qaisar Shafi*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了携带电流的亚稳态宇宙弦（MSCSs）网络产生的随机引力波背景（SGWB），引入涡旋子（vorton）形成现象，并构建了适用于脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）频段的解析谱模板，同时推导了LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）频段的尺度不变SGWB约束；结果表明部分参数空间虽可解释PTA信号，却与LVK约束存在张力，暗示需额外机制抑制高频谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）观测到的潜在引力波信号，同时协调其与地面探测器（如LVK）的高频频段约束之间的张力。

Method: 扩展以往宇宙弦SGWB模型，引入涡旋子形成的唯象处理；构建适用于PTA频段的解析引力波谱模板，并推导LVK频段的尺度不变谱解析表达式。

Result: 获得PTA频段的快速准确谱模板及LVK频段的尺度不变谱约束；发现部分参数能拟合PTA信号但违反LVK上限，揭示高频抑制机制的必要性。

Conclusion: 仅靠标准电流携带亚稳态宇宙弦模型难以同时满足PTA和LVK观测约束，需引入额外物理机制（如涡旋子更高效捕获或弦网络演化修正）来压制高频引力波谱。

Abstract: We study the stochastic gravitational wave background (SGWB) generated by networks of current-carrying metastable cosmic strings (MSCSs). Extending previous studies, we include the possibility of vorton formation phenomenologically. We construct an analytical template that provides a fast and accurate approximation to the spectrum in the frequency range probed by pulsar timing arrays (PTAs). We also show how the parameters of cosmic string networks determine the low-frequency slope. For the frequency range relevant for LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK), we derive an analytical expression that constrains the scale-invariant part of the SGWB. Finally, we confront the model with current constraints from PTAs and the LVK collaboration. We identify regions of the parameter space that can produce a signal consistent with PTA observations but are in tension with LVK constraints, suggesting that additional mechanisms to suppress the high-frequency spectrum are required for a consistent interpretation of the PTA signal

</details>


### [257] [Machine Learning Based Classification of Type 1 Active Galactic Nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38280)
*Jie Li,Xu-Liang Fan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于随机森林的机器学习方法，利用测光数据区分I型类星体和I型赛弗特星系，适用于大天区巡天，并能获得较完整的赛弗特星系候选体样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为在大规模测光巡天中有效区分I型类星体与I型赛弗特星系，并构建相对完整的赛弗特星系候选样本。

Method: 采用主成分分析（PCA）探索区分性，并构建基于随机森林（RF）的监督分类器；在DESI DR1交叉样本上进行应用验证，并与光谱分类结果对比。

Result: RF分类器对I型类星体F1得分为0.967，对赛弗特星系为0.791；提高阈值可提升赛弗特星系样本纯度；应用于DESI DR1后，发现大量低光度源被光谱误判为类星体，而本方法能更好恢复赛弗特星系的统计特征（如SED中位数和红移分布占比）。

Conclusion: 该RF分类器能有效识别与训练样本相似的赛弗特星系，在大样本测光数据中具有实用价值，有助于弥补光谱分类对低光度赛弗特星系的遗漏。

Abstract: Aims. We aim to provide a machine learning based method to distinguish type 1 quasars and type 1 Seyfert galaxies based on photometric data, which could be applied to large all-sky surveys. The method can also be used to obtain relatively complete samples of Seyfert galaxy candidates when broad-line AGNs have been selected.
  Results. The results of PCA show that quasars and Seyfert galaxies are partial distinguishable. The supervised classifier based on random forest (RF) achieves good performances for type 1 quasars with a F1 score of 0.967. The performance for classifying Seyfert galaxies is worse, with a F1 score of 0.791. Increasing the classification threshold can help to derive a sample of Seyfert galaxies with high purity. The classifier are then applied to the cross-matched sample with DESI DR1. When compared with the results of spectroscopic classification, quasars also show high precision and recall. For Seyfert galaxies identified by RF classifier, there are plenty of less luminous sources who are classified as quasars by spectroscopic method. If they are regarded as Seyfert galaxies, our classifier also performs well to predict Seyfert galaxies. Both the median SEDs and the fraction of Seyfert galaxies derived by our classifier are similar with those for the training sample, while the fraction of Seyfert galaxies for spectroscopic classification are lower at all redshift. These results indicate that our classifier can recover the features of Seyfert galaxies, at least those similar to the Seyfert galaxies in the training sample.

</details>


### [258] [Gravitational Dark Matter Production from Spectator Field in $R^2$-Higgs Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38397)
*Arnab Samanta,Rajesh Mondal,Tanmoy Modak*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多场R²-Higgs暴胀模型中，仅通过非最小耦合ξχχ²R与引力相互作用的旁观者场χ作为暗物质候选者的宇宙学引力产生机制，并发现其有效质量平方存在抵消区域，可实现正确的暗物质剩余丰度；此外，Higgs与戈德斯通粒子的预加热过程对解释观测到的暗物质密度起关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质在暴胀时期的引力产生机制，特别是通过仅与引力耦合的旁观者场实现，以规避传统粒子物理机制的限制。

Method: 在爱因斯坦框架下，采用双重协变形式主义推导背景场及线性涨落的运动方程，分析χ场的有效质量对其产额和剩余丰度的影响，并考察Higgs与Goldstone预加热的作用。

Result: 发现χ场有效质量平方存在抵消区域，可自然给出正确暗物质丰度；预加热过程对最终暗物质密度有显著贡献。

Conclusion: 仅靠引力耦合的旁观者场可在R²-Higgs暴胀中成功产生符合观测的暗物质，其丰度由有效质量结构和预加热动力学共同决定。

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological gravitational dark matter production of spectator field $χ$ in the multifield $R^2$-Higgs inflation. The spectator field $χ$ couples to gravity only through a non-minimal coupling $ξ_χχ^2R$ and serves as a dark matter (DM) candidate. Working consistently in the Einstein frame, we derive the equation of motion of the background fields and fluctuations at linear order using the doubly covariant formalism. We show the dark matter production and the subsequent comoving relic abundance heavily depend on this effective mass of the field $χ$. Specifically, we find that there exists cancellation regions in the effective mass-squared of $χ$, which ensures the correct relic abundance is achieved. Further, we show that the preheating from Higgs and Goldstone may play critical role in accounting the observed relic density.

</details>


### [259] [Testing Extended Exponential Infrared Teleparallel Gravity with CMB, DESI, and PantheonPlus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38684)
*Mahmoud Hashim,Jackson Levi Said,Waleed El Hanafy,Eleonora Di Valentino*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个扩展的指数红外f(T)引力模型（EExpIR），通过结合CMB、BAO和Ia型超新星等观测数据对其参数进行限制，并发现该模型与ΛCDM模型在统计上难以区分，但完整数据集下ΛCDM略占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为探索暗能量本质及解决哈勃常数争议，拓展f(T)引力理论并检验其对宇宙学观测的拟合能力。

Method: 采用相空间分析确定物理合理的解分支并设定参数先验；联合Planck/ACT/SPT（CMB）、DESI BAO、PantheonPlus（SNe Ia）及SH0ES（H₀校准）数据进行贝叶斯参数估计与模型比较。

Result: 得到新参数b=0.573^{+0.016}_{-0.060}，H₀=70.67^{+0.31}_{-0.35} km/s/Mpc（68%置信度）；贝叶斯证据显示：仅用CMB时两模型无显著差异，加入全部数据（含SH0ES）后ΛCDM有弱偏好。

Conclusion: EExpIR f(T)模型能有效描述宇宙演化，其 torsion 动力学可充当平滑过渡的暗能量成分，但当前观测尚不足以显著优于标准ΛCDM模型。

Abstract: We place cosmological constraints on an extended exponential infrared (EExpIR) $f(T)$ teleparallel gravity model using current observational datasets. The extension is characterized by an additional parameter, $b$, which governs the late-time cosmological dynamics. Owing to the existence of two solution branches, phase-space analysis is employed to select the cosmological branch and to establish an appropriate prior range for the new parameter. In this framework, the torsion sector acts as an effective dark-energy component, exhibiting a smooth evolution from a quintessence-like phase to a phantom regime, while recovering the standard cosmological behavior at early times. To constrain the model parameters, we combine early-Universe observations from Planck, ACT, and SPT measurements of the CMB with late-time probes from DESI BAO and PantheonPlus Type Ia supernovae. Using the full dataset, including the SH0ES calibration, we obtain $b=0.573^{+0.016}_{-0.060}$ and $H_0=70.67^{+0.31}_{-0.35}~{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$ at 68\% CL. Model comparison based on Bayesian evidence reveals that the EExpIR $f(T)$ and the $Λ$CDM models are statistically indistinguishable when only CMB data are considered, whereas the inclusion of the full cosmological dataset with SH0ES leads to a weak preference in favor of $Λ$CDM.

</details>


### [260] [Towards constraining cosmology with HI--galaxy cross-correlations from MeerKLASS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38733)
*G. Autieri,M. Spinelli,Z. Chen,M. Viel,M. Barberi-Squarotti,P. Bull,S. Camera,I. P. Carucci,S. Cunnington,J. Fonseca,K. Grainge,M. O. Irfan,Y. Li,A. Pourtsidou,M. G. Santos,J. Wang,L. Wolz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用MeerKLASS 2019 L波段中性氢（HI）强度映射数据与WiggleZ星系巡天在z≈0.43处的交叉功率谱，验证了从前景去除到似然分析的完整宇宙学参数推断流程在真实数据上的可行性，并得到与标准宇宙学一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证端到端分析流程（从前景清洁到似然分析）在真实HI强度映射数据上的可行性，并确认其能否给出符合共识宇宙学的参数结果。

Method: 基于MeerKLASS HI强度映射与WiggleZ星系的交叉功率谱，构建包含单口径望远镜观测效应的理论功率谱模型；采用贝叶斯似然分析，分别固定/采样HI振幅等天体物理烦扰参数，并通过模拟数据检验忽略观测效应的影响。

Result: 在最乐观设定下（固定烦扰参数），得到H₀ = 65.3⁺⁵·⁸₋₁₀ km s⁻¹ Mpc⁻¹、Ωₘ = 0.44⁺⁰·¹²₋₀·₃₀（68%置信度）；HI振幅使用信息先验时结果变化小于1σ；忽略观测效应会导致参数偏移达2.5σ且低估不确定性；改变前景模态数和k范围引起的偏差小于0.5σ。

Conclusion: 该分析流程对HI强度映射数据建模具有鲁棒性，为未来MeerKLASS和SKAO的宇宙学研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present an inference of cosmological parameters from the cross-correlation power spectrum between the MeerKLASS 2019 L-band HI intensity maps and the overlapping WiggleZ galaxies at $z \simeq 0.43$. The analysis is intended as a proof of concept to verify that the full pipeline, from the foreground cleaning of the maps to the likelihood analysis, can be applied end-to-end to real data and can return results consistent with the concordance cosmology. The model of the observed power spectrum includes observational effects that are relevant to a single-dish experiment. In the most optimistic scenario in which we hold the astrophysical nuisance parameters fixed, we obtain $H_0 = 65.3^{+5.8}_{-10}\text{ km s}^{-1}\text{ Mpc}^{-1}$ and $Ω_{\text{m}} = 0.44^{+0.12}_{-0.30}$ at the 68% confidence level. We find that sampling the HI amplitude with an informative prior retains most of the constraining power and changes these results by less than 1 sigma. Moreover, using synthetic data, we show that neglecting the observational effects in the model biases the inferred parameters by up to 2.5 sigma and also underestimates their uncertainties. Finally, we show that varying the number of foreground modes removed and the range of scales included changes the inferred parameters by less than 0.5 sigma. These results demonstrate the robustness of our pipeline in modelling HI intensity mapping data, and set the foundation for future cosmological inference with intensity mapping using MeerKLASS and SKAO.

</details>


### [261] [Probing the local primordial non-Gaussianity with clustering of FRB dispersion measure and galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38735)
*Indranil Das,Gilbert Holder*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用FRB与星系联合巡天的3×2点统计（DM自功率谱、DM-星系交叉功率谱、星系自功率谱），预测其对局域型原初非高斯性参数f_NL的限制能力，发现大样本FRB（如SKA时代10^7个暴）可将σ(f_NL)提升至约2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局域型原初非高斯性会在自由电子场（由FRB色散量DM探测）和星系场中引入尺度依赖的偏置，因此联合分析FRB与星系巡天有望提升对其幅度f_NL的测量精度。

Method: 采用DESI类亮红星系样本与覆盖35%天区的FRB样本，基于全3×2点角功率谱进行 Fisher 信息矩阵预报，并在不同参数集（含3个天体物理偏置参数和6个ΛCDM参数）上做边缘化处理。

Result: 在Planck 2018宇宙学框架下，10^4或10^5个FRB时σ(f_NL)≈5；当FRB数达10^7时，σ(f_NL)≈2；星系自功率谱主导当前限制，而DM相关谱随FRB数增加贡献显著增强。

Conclusion: FRB-星系联合分析是探测局域型原初非高斯性的有力新探针，尤其在SKA等下一代FRB巡天时代具备突破性潜力。

Abstract: We forecast the sensitivity of a joint fast radio burst (FRB) and galaxy survey to local primordial non-Gaussianity using the full $3\times2$-point statistic: the angular dispersion-measure (DM) auto-spectrum, the DM-galaxy cross-spectrum, and the galaxy auto-spectrum. Local primordial non-Gaussianity imprints a scale-dependent bias on both the free-electron field probed by FRB dispersion measures and the galaxy field. Hence, all three spectra carry information on the local non-Gaussianity amplitude $f_{\rm NL}$. Adopting a DESI-like luminous-red-galaxy sample and an FRB population over 35% of the sky, we forecast $σ(f_{\rm NL}) \simeq 5$ for both $N_{\rm FRB} = 10^4$ and $10^5$ at fixed Planck 2018 cosmology, marginalised over three astrophysical bias parameters $(b_e^0, z_{\rm fb}, b_g^{\rm amp})$. The $f_{\rm NL}$ constraint is robust to marginalising over the six $Λ$CDM parameters $(ω_b, ω_{\rm cdm}, h, A_s, n_s, τ)$ with Planck priors. At these sample sizes, the galaxy auto-spectrum sets the constraint, while the DM sector's contribution grows with the burst count and becomes decisive for the larger samples anticipated in the SKA era, reaching $σ(f_{\rm NL}) \simeq 2$ at $10^7$ bursts.

</details>


### [262] [Running cosmological constant in light of recent CMB-BAO data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.38973)
*A. Racioppi,M. Vijar*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了由跑动宇宙常数驱动的单场慢滚暴胀模型，并利用最新的CMB-BAO数据对其进行了检验。研究发现，当相对辐射修正δ为正时，模型在包含BAO数据时具有良好兼容性；而δ为负时，原始模型被排除，但引入有效质量修正后可变为类山顶模型，从而与CMB数据兼容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验跑动宇宙常数驱动的慢滚暴胀模型在最新观测数据下的可行性，特别是分析辐射修正参数δ正负取值对模型预测和观测约束的影响。

Method: 基于单场慢滚暴胀框架，构建含对数辐射修正的宇宙常数势函数，将相对修正δ作为自由参数；分别考虑δ>0和δ<0情形，结合CMB-only与CMB+BAO联合数据进行参数约束和预测分析；对δ<0情形引入有效质量项以恢复慢滚条件并改变势函数形态。

Result: δ>0时，模型在CMB+BAO数据下广泛兼容，但在纯CMB数据中仅在2σ边界附近窄带内可行；δ<0原始模型因慢滚破缺被排除，引入有效质量后形成类山顶势，可在CMB-only下兼容，CMB+BAO下仅达2σ置信水平。

Conclusion: 跑动宇宙常数暴胀模型在适当修正（如引入有效质量）下仍具观测可行性，δ的符号及大小显著影响其与当前宇宙学数据的兼容性，凸显理论细节与观测约束间的紧密关联。

Abstract: We revisit single-field slow-roll inflation driven by a running cosmological constant, i.e. a cosmological constant receiving a logarithmic radiative correction, in light of the latest CMB-BAO data. The relative radiative correction $δ$ is treated as a free parameter of the model and allowed to be positive or negative. For positive $δ$, the predictions interpolate between a logarithmic regime and a linear inflation one. Compatibility with CMB-only data is confined to a narrow stripe near the $2σ$ border where $δ$ is relatively large. On the other hand, the predictions fit easily within the constraints including BAO data in a wide range of parameters. For negative $δ$, the slow-roll approximation breaks down at small field values and the predictions are excluded by data. However, once the effective mass contribution inside the logarithmic correction is taken into account, the negative $δ$ potential develops a local maximum and behaves like a hilltop model, achieving compatibility with CMB-only data, while agreement with CMB-BAO data is possible only at $2σ$.

</details>


### [263] [Cosmic birefringence tomography with radio galaxies I: Testing birefringence solutions to the CMB-BAO tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39449)
*Toshiya Namikawa,Fumihiro Naokawa,Richard Battye,Salvatore Samuele Sirletti*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用射电星系独立检验了轴子类粒子（ALPs）在低红移引发的宇宙双折射效应，聚焦于可缓解BAO与CMB观测间张力的模型，并对nπ相位模糊和冷暗物质能量注入两种机制进行了限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解近期重子声学振荡（BAO）测量与宇宙微波背景（CMB）观测之间的轻微张力，该张力可能由再电离时期产生的大宇宙双折射角解释。

Method: 基于Battye、Jackson & Browne（2026）提供的射电星系喷流形态与偏振角数据，拟合宇宙双折射角，分别约束nπ相位模糊模型和冷暗物质向ALPs能量注入模型。

Result: 对于nπ模型，在log10(mφ/eV) ≲ −30.7时，|n| ≥ 1被2σ以上排除；对能量注入模型，数据与零注入一致，但略微偏好非零注入。

Conclusion: 当前约3000个样本受限于统计与天体物理不确定性，未来需借助大量低红移射电星系开展层析宇宙双折射研究，以实现对宇宙双折射的独立检验。

Abstract: We present an independent test of cosmic birefringence induced by axionlike particles (ALPs) at low redshift using radio galaxies. We focus on models that can alleviate the mild tension between recent baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) measurements and observations of the cosmic microwave background (CMB). This tension can be explained by a large cosmic birefringence rotation angle generated during the reionization epoch. Such a large rotation can arise if the recent isotropic cosmic birefringence measurements are subject to an $nπ$ phase ambiguity, with $n\in\mathbb{Z}$, or if cold dark matter injects energy into ALPs. We constrain these two scenarios by fitting the cosmic birefringence angles estimated from the jet morphologies and polarization angles of radio galaxies obtained by Battye, Jackson & Browne (2026). For the $nπ$ model, the radio-galaxy data exclude $|n|\geq 1$ at more than $2σ$ for $\log_{10}(m_φ/{\rm eV})\lesssim -30.7$. For the energy injection model, the data are consistent with no energy injection, but with a slight relative preference for a nonzero energy injection compared to the no-injection case. Since our samples are limited to $\sim 3000$ and there could still be several astrophysical uncertainties and statistical fluctuations, further exploration of tomographic cosmic birefringence using a large number of low-redshift radio galaxies is important for independent tests of cosmic birefringence.

</details>


### [264] [Cosmological axion-photon interactions with a nonminimal dark gauge sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39473)
*Hao Jiao,Dong-Won Jung,Nils A. Nilsson*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了非最小耦合的轴子-光子-暗光子相互作用对宇宙学中辐射和暗辐射演化的影响，分析其导致的CMB谱畸变与宇宙双折射现象，并利用CMB单极谱限制能量转换比例，同时指出φF	ilde{B}相互作用可部分缓解标准轴子-光子耦合的观测限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解轴子类粒子在暗区与可见区之间的Chern-Simons型耦合如何影响宇宙微波背景（CMB）的观测特征，特别是谱畸变与双折射，以探索新物理参数空间。

Method: 基于轴子场与光子、暗光子的非最小Chern-Simons耦合构建宇宙学演化模型，计算辐射与暗辐射间能量转换及偏振旋转效应，并结合CMB单极谱观测数据进行参数约束。

Result: 利用CMB单极谱限定了光子-暗光子能量转换分数；发现φF\tilde{B}混合相互作用可弱化由当前宇宙双折射角观测限所施加的标准轴子-光子耦合（φF\tilde{F}）约束。

Conclusion: 非最小轴子-规范场耦合对CMB具有可观测效应，为轴子及暗光子参数提供新的宇宙学探针，且不同耦合项之间存在补偿效应，需联合分析。

Abstract: We study the cosmological evolution of radiation and dark radiation with nonminimal axion-photon-dark photon couplings. The Chern-Simons-type axion-gauge field interactions between the dark and visible sectors can induce energy conversion between photons and dark photons as well as polarization rotation during the propagation of radiation, leading to CMB spectral distortions and cosmic birefringence. Using the observed CMB monopole spectrum, we constrain the photon-dark photon energy conversion fraction, which is determined by the coupling constants and the evolution of the axion field. We further show that the parity-odd mixing interaction $φF\tilde{B}$ can partially relax the constraints on the standard axion-photon coupling, $φF\tilde{F}$, derived from the current observational limits of the cosmic birefringence angle.

</details>


### [265] [A deep learning approach to member identification and mass estimation in galaxy clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39546)
*Zicheng Wang,Liping Fu,Wei Du,Wentao Luo,Wenxiang Pei,Zhu Chen,Zhijian Luo,Yicheng Li,Jiachen Qiu,Haoran Zhao,Kaiwen Mai,Weiming Ren*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度学习的概率框架，用于星系团成员识别与动力学质量估计，通过LSTM网络分配成员概率，并用贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）估计质量；该方法在模拟数据上验证了对中心偏移和径向不完备性的鲁棒性，显著降低了质量估计的残差散射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团动力学质量的准确估计对星系团形成演化研究及宇宙学应用至关重要，但前景/背景星系（interloper）会引入偏差并增大不确定性。

Method: 构建两阶段深度学习模型：第一阶段使用LSTM网络根据投影相空间邻近性为星系分配成员概率；第二阶段利用这些概率加权构建投影相空间密度图，输入贝叶斯神经网络（BNN）以推断星系团质量及预测不确定性。

Result: LSTM输出的成员概率校准良好，p_mem=0.5等概率轮廓能有效区分成员与非成员；采用p_th=0.5阈值可在完备性与纯度间取得平衡；加权后BNN质量估计残差散射σ_ε降至0.120（降低19.5%）；使用最亮星系（BCG）代替引力中心会导致质量低估最高达47.6%；移除40%非中心星系仅使质量低估6.1%。

Conclusion: 该概率深度学习框架能有效抑制interloper影响，提升动力学质量估计精度与鲁棒性，尤其对中心偏移敏感，凸显精确定位星系团中心的重要性。

Abstract: Accurate dynamical mass estimates of galaxy clusters are essential for studies of cluster formation and evolution and for cosmological applications. However, interloper galaxies, which are not gravitationally bound to the cluster but are incorrectly identified as members, can bias dynamical mass measurements and increase uncertainties. We developed a probabilistic deep-learning framework for cluster member identification and dynamical mass estimation and tested sensitivity to cluster miscentring and radially dependent incompleteness in controlled mock experiments. We used mock galaxy cluster catalogues to train a two-stage model. A long short-term memory (LSTM) network assigns membership probabilities to galaxies arranged by projected phase-space proximity. These probabilities construct membership-weighted projected phase-space density maps, from which a Bayesian neural network (BNN) infers cluster masses and predictive uncertainties. The LSTM membership probabilities are well calibrated and form an isoprobability envelope in projected phase space, with the $\langle p_{\rm mem}\rangle = 0.5$ contour broadly tracing the empirical member--interloper transition. A threshold of $p_{\rm th} = 0.5$ balances member completeness and purity, with high completeness within $R_{\rm proj} < r_{200}$. Membership weighting lowers the residual scatter of the fiducial BNN mass estimator, $\mathrm{BNN}^{\mathrm{Mem}}_{\mathrm{2D}}$, to $σ_ε= 0.120$, a 19.5% reduction relative to the unweighted baseline, although predictive uncertainties are not uniformly calibrated across BNN models. Using the brightest cluster galaxy rather than the gravitational centre lowers the median inferred mass by up to 47.6%, with larger reductions at larger centring offsets. By contrast, removing 40% of eligible non-central galaxies within $r_{200}$ lowers it by 6.1% relative to complete catalogues.

</details>


### [266] [Low-variance renoising for weak gravitational lensing shear estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39554)
*Xiangchong Li*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的噪声校正方法——谱重噪声（spectral renoising），相比传统旋转重噪声（rotation renoising）能显著降低噪声方差，在保持亚百分之一级剪切偏差的同时提升有效星系数密度约20%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Metacalibration和AnaCal使用的“重噪声”（renoising）方法虽能消除剪切响应引入的噪声各向异性，但会加倍噪声方差，限制弱光星系的测量精度与统计效率。

Method: 推导了重噪声需满足的一般条件：总噪声（原始噪声经像素剪切响应变换后加所添加噪声）在剪切一阶下应与剪切无关；在此框架下，提出新方案‘谱重噪声’，即添加一个经特定滤波器（由噪声功率谱除以点扩散函数得到）处理的缩放版原始噪声，并为其指定相应剪切依赖性。

Result: 谱重噪声在均匀高斯噪声下仅需添加原始噪声方差的一小部分；图像模拟表明其乘性剪切偏差控制在10^{-3}量级，且在暗端提升有效星系数密度约20%。

Conclusion: 谱重噪声是一种更优的噪声校正策略，在维持高精度剪切测量的同时显著提升观测效率，适用于下一代弱引力透镜巡天。

Abstract: The "renoising" noise-bias correction used by Metacalibration and AnaCal adds to every galaxy image an independent noise field with the statistics of the original noise rotated by 90 degrees. The rotated field cancels, to first order in shear, the anisotropy that the shear-response operation induces in the original noise, at the cost of doubling the noise variance. We derive the general condition that an added noise field must satisfy for this cancellation: the distribution of the total noise -- the original noise transformed with the pixel shear-response operator, plus the added noise carrying an assigned shear dependence that we are free to choose -- must be independent of shear to first order. Under this condition the shear response of the renoised ellipticity is estimated from the known realization of the added noise alone. The rotated noise field, which we call rotation renoising, is one solution of the condition and yields the analytical response estimator of AnaCal. We then construct a second solution, spectral renoising, which adds only a small fraction of the original noise variance: for homogeneous Gaussian noise the added field is an independent, scaled-down copy of the image noise, and its assigned shear response is the same field passed through a fixed filter set by the noise power spectrum deconvolved by the point spread function. Image simulations show that spectral renoising keeps the multiplicative shear bias at the $10^{-3}$ level and raises the effective galaxy number density by about 20 per cent at the faint end.

</details>


### [267] [Cosmological constraints on extra radiation and early dark energy with lepton asymmetry and varying quark masses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39579)
*Yo Toda*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了额外辐射和类轴子早期暗能量（EDE）对宇宙学参数的限制，结合CMB、BAO、超新星及原初氦/氘丰度等多组观测数据，发现轻子不对称性可缓解Hubble张力并放宽中微子质量上限，而夸克质量变化则更有利于增大EDE分数和提高H0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hubble常数张力问题，并检验额外辐射、早期暗能量、轻子不对称性及基本粒子质量变化等新物理对原初核合成与宇宙微波背景联合约束的影响。

Method: 联合分析Planck CMB、DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon+超新星以及原初氦和氘丰度数据，在不同扩展模型（含Neff、ξ、EDE、δmq/mq、∑mν）中进行贝叶斯参数估计。

Result: 在额外辐射+轻子不对称模型中得Neff=3.27±0.16、ξ=0.017±0.011、H0=69.24±0.92 km/s/Mpc；轻子不对称可缓解H0张力且使∑mν 95%上限从0.0640 eV升至0.0777 eV；夸克质量增加可补偿EDE导致的氘减少，从而允许更大EDE分数和更高H0。

Conclusion: 原初轻元素丰度是区分不同新物理模型的关键探针：轻子不对称主要影响氦丰度与H0推断，而夸克质量变化对氘丰度更敏感，能有效缓释EDE模型中的参数张力；多观测联合分析显著提升了对早期宇宙物理的甄别能力。

Abstract: We investigate cosmological constraints on extra radiation and axionlike early dark energy (EDE), allowing primordial lepton asymmetry or light-quark-mass variation during big bang nucleosynthesis (BBN). We combine Planck CMB, DESI DR2 BAO, Pantheon+ supernovae, and primordial helium and deuterium measurements. When both extra radiation and lepton asymmetry are allowed, we obtain $N_{\rm eff}=3.27\pm0.16$, $ξ=0.017\pm0.011$, and $H_0=69.24\pm0.92\,{\rm km\,s^{-1}\,Mpc^{-1}}$. A positive lepton asymmetry compensates the helium increase caused by extra radiation, allowing a higher inferred Hubble constant even with the LBT helium measurement, which agrees with standard BBN. For EDE, primordial-abundance measurements instead restrict the higher baryon densities associated with larger EDE fractions, which reduce the predicted deuterium abundance. Allowing lepton asymmetry does not relax this restriction. In contrast, a positive light-quark-mass variation increases deuterium and compensates this reduction, permitting larger EDE fractions and a higher inferred Hubble constant. The same variation leaves the extra-radiation constraint nearly unchanged. We also find that allowing lepton asymmetry relaxes the 95\% upper limit on the summed neutrino mass from $0.0640\,{\rm eV}$ to $0.0777\,{\rm eV}$ in the extra-radiation model.

</details>


### [268] [Efficient GLS map-making and assessment of $1/f$ noise impact for future CMB satellite missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39581)
*Marta Monelli,Guillaume Patanchon,Avinash Anand*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出STITCH方法，通过统计填充时间序列数据块来缓解循环噪声协方差近似引入的边缘效应，从而提升CMB偏振B模测量中GLS制图的精度，同时保持计算效率；该方法显著抑制边缘伪影、不引入显著E→B泄漏，并给出了典型巡天策略下1/f时域噪声到角功率谱的映射经验模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间相关的1/f噪声是CMB偏振测量（尤其是原初B模探测）中大尺度系统误差的主要来源；现有基于循环噪声协方差近似的广义最小二乘（GLS）制图法虽高效，但因截断数据导致边界人工效应，影响噪声建模精度。

Method: 提出STITCH（Statistical Inpainting for Timeline CHunks）预处理方法：在原始时间序列数据块两端添加符合循环噪声模型统计特性的合成样本，使扩展后序列适配标准循环GLS求解器；结合数值模拟评估其对噪声功率谱、信号恢复保真度（如E→B泄漏）的影响，并拟合不同观测策略（LiteBIRD/Planck/PICO）下时域1/f噪声到角功率谱的映射模型。

Result: STITCH显著压制了循环近似引起的边界残差，使角噪声功率谱接近理想循环假设下的结果；对真实信号（包括大尺度偏振模式）恢复无明显偏差，E→B泄漏可忽略；获得了三种典型巡天策略下时域噪声参数到角噪声谱的经验拟合模型。

Conclusion: STITCH是一种兼顾计算效率与噪声建模精度的有效预处理方案，适用于未来高灵敏度CMB偏振实验；所建立的时-角噪声映射模型为 correlated noise 建模与系统误差预算提供了实用工具和理论基础。

Abstract: Time-correlated $1/f$ noise is a major source of large-scale contamination in measurements of the polarized cosmic microwave background (CMB) that can limit sensitivity to primordial $B$ modes. Generalized least-squares (GLS) map-making is optimal for stationary Gaussian noise when its covariance is known, but practical implementations rely on approximations to make the computation tractable. In particular, approximating the noise covariance as circulant enables efficient map-making using fast Fourier transform, but introduces artificial edge effects when applied to finite data chunks. We introduce STITCH (STatistical Inpainting for Timeline CHunks), a preprocessing method that mitigates these effects by extending time-ordered data chunks with synthetic samples that make the extended timelines statistically consistent with the assumed circulant noise model. They can then be processed with standard circulant GLS solvers, preserving their computational efficiency while reducing residuals associated with chunk boundaries. Simulations show that STITCH substantially suppresses these edge effects, bringing the resulting noise power spectra close to those obtained when the circulant approximation holds. We demonstrate negligible impact of our procedure on the recovered signal, such as $E\to B$ leakage, even at large scales. Finally, we investigate how temporal $1/f$ noise projects onto angular scales for representative LiteBIRD-, \emph{Planck}-, and PICO-like scanning strategies. Using noise simulations spanning a range of temporal noise parameters, we fit an effective model to the resulting angular noise spectra. These results provide an empirical characterization of the temporal-to-angular noise mapping for the configurations studied and a basis for further investigations of correlated noise in future CMB polarization missions.

</details>


### [269] [Dwarf Galaxy Reticulum II's MeerKAT L-Band Observation: Constraining WIMP Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39752)
*Phillip Badenhorst,Michael Sarkis,Yin-Zhe Ma,Wen-Qing Guo,Geoff Beck,Qiang Yuan,Gyula I. G. Jozsa,Steven G. Murray*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用MeerKAT射电望远镜对矮椭球星系Reticulum II进行L波段观测，结合WIMP湮灭理论模型，给出质量在10–200 GeV范围内的WIMP湮灭截面95%置信度上限（≈10⁻²⁶ cm³/s），并强调矮星系中磁场建模对间接探测暗物质的关键影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WIMP是暗物质主要候选者之一，其在矮椭球星系中湮灭产生的高能电子/正电子可在磁场中产生可探测的同步辐射射电信号；但现有射电限制高度依赖于不确定的磁场假设，亟需更可靠的磁场表征。

Method: 使用MeerKAT阵列在1.3–1.5 GHz频段对Reticulum II进行射电观测，测量其表面亮度分布，并结合WIMP湮灭产生电子/正电子、扩散及同步辐射的理论模型，在给定均匀磁场与扩散常数假设下推导湮灭截面上限。

Result: 获得WIMP质量m_χ ≈ 10–200 GeV区间内95%置信度的湮灭截面上限⟨σv⟩ ≲ 10⁻²⁶ cm³ s⁻¹；该结果受限于磁场模型假设，凸显磁场精确刻画的重要性。

Conclusion: 矮椭球星系是间接探测暗物质的重要目标，但未来如SKA等新一代射电设施要充分发挥灵敏度，必须加强对矮星系中磁场环境的实测与建模。

Abstract: The nature of dark matter remains one of the most profound open questions in modern physics, with weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs) among the leading candidates proposed to account for the observed anomalies across cosmic scales. If WIMPs annihilate or decay in the dense dark matter haloes of nearby galaxies, the resulting high-energy electrons/positrons can produce synchrotron radiation in the presence of ambient magnetic fields, yielding a potentially detectable radio signature. Dwarf spheroidal galaxies are particularly promising targets for indirect dark matter searches due to their high mass-to-light ratios and low astrophysical foreground contamination. While radio observations have been widely used to constrain WIMP properties in these systems, the resulting limits remain highly sensitive to poorly constrained magnetic field assumptions. Here we utilize L-band ($1.3$--$1.5$ GHz) observations from the MeerKAT array directed towards dwarf galaxy Reticulum II and measure the surface brightness profile across the dwarf galaxy. Combining these measurements with theoretical models of WIMP annihilation and yield 95% confidence level upper limits on the WIMP annihilation cross-section $\langleσv\rangle \lesssim 10^{-26}$ cm$^3$ s$^{-1}$ for masses $m_χ\sim 10$-$200$ GeV (with the assumed uniform magnetic field model and diffusion constant). As next-generation radio facilities such as the Square Kilometre Array come online, robust characterisation of magnetic environments in dwarf galaxies will be essential to fully exploit their sensitivity to the particle nature of dark matter.

</details>


### [270] [Mapping cluster angular momentum with the rotational kinetic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39977)
*Jack Y. L. Kwok,Bernardita Ried Guachalla,William R. Coulton,Selim C. Hotinli,Emmanuel Schaan*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用微波背景图（CMB）重建星系团内电离气体角动量投影的最优多频匹配滤波器（MMF），用于探测旋转型动能SZ效应（rkSZ），并构建了描述星系团内气体相干旋转的简化模型，验证其在TNG模拟中的适用性，进而预测Simons Observatory对不同质量与红移星系团的信噪比，并讨论其在星系团质量校准、自旋取向及成团性偏差等方向的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系团内介质若存在相干旋转，将产生旋转型动能SZ效应（rkSZ），该信号可反映星系团角动量分布；但rkSZ远弱于热SZ（tSZ）及其他前景污染，亟需新方法从CMB数据中有效提取。

Method: 提出一种最优多频匹配滤波器（MMF），在微波图中分离rkSZ信号，抑制miscentered tSZ、CMB各向异性及红外背景；构建含差速旋转壳层的rkSZ剖面模型，并用TNG-Cluster与TNG300-1模拟验证；基于该模型进行Simons Observatory单簇及累积信噪比预测。

Result: 模型显示内介质以恒定赤道切向速度差速旋转，切向与200c半径处圆周速度比为v_tan/V_c,200c=0.18（与质量和红移无关）；理想质量限制星表下，log10(M_200c/M⊙)>14、14.5、15的累积SNR分别达≈53、25、6。

Conclusion: 本工作为CMB观测测量星系团内气体旋转提供了实用框架，有望改进星系团质量校准中非热压修正，并助力研究自旋取向关联与成团性偏差。

Abstract: Galaxy clusters carry angular momentum. If the intracluster gas rotates coherently, the kinetic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (kSZ) effect imprints a dipole on the microwave sky centered on the cluster. We show that microwave maps alone can be used to reconstruct a noisy vector field tracing the projected angular momentum of the ionized gas. We present an optimal multifrequency matched filter (MMF) that isolates the rotational kSZ (rkSZ) signal from the much larger miscentered thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich (tSZ) effect, cosmic microwave, and infrared background contributions, improving on both real-space oriented stacking and matched filtering of maps cleaned with full-sky multifrequency weights. For the rkSZ profile, we introduce a simple model for coherent intracluster gas rotation and validate it against TNG-Cluster and TNG300-1. The model consists of differentially rotating concentric shells with constant equatorial tangential velocity, characterized by a mass- and redshift-indepedent tangential-to-circular velocity ratio of $v_{\rm tan}/V_{c,200c}=0.18$. Using this model, we forecast single-cluster signal-to-noise (SNR) as a function of mass and redshift for Simons Observatory; cross-correlation with an external catalog of rotation proxies yields a cumulative detection SNR. For an idealized mass-limited catalog with perfectly known spin directions, the cumulative SNR of our optimal MMF reaches $\approx 53$, $25$, and $6$ when including all clusters above $\log_{10}(M_{200c}/M_\odot)>14$, 14.5, and 15, respectively. This work provides a practical framework for CMB measurements of intracluster gas rotation, with applications to the rotational contribution to non-thermal pressure in cluster mass calibration, as well as spin alignments and clustering biases.

</details>


### [271] [Benchmarking Quantum Neural Networks Developed for MBHB Coalescence in the Context of LISA Space Mission](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.39980)
*Maria-Catalina Isfan,Laurentiu-Ioan Caramete,Ana Caramete*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文探讨了量子、混合量子-经典和经典机器学习模型在LISA引力波数据分析中的应用，特别是在信号检测与并合预测任务中；结果显示部分量子模型在特定任务上表现优于经典模型，但未观察到普适的量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着引力波数据分析需求增长及量子计算发展，探索量子机器学习在LISA数据处理中的潜力成为重要方向。

Method: 基于LISA数据挑战Sangria数据集，对比评估多种量子模型（如VQC、QLSTM、量子核方法等）与经典模型（如SVM、MLP、RNN、LSTM）在信号检测和并合预测任务上的性能。

Result: 在信号检测任务中，部分量子模型（尤其是后VQC混合架构）达到或超过经典基线；在并合检测与并合时间估计中，标准VQC表现最稳健且定位误差最低。

Conclusion: 尽管未发现普适量子优势，但特定量子模型在关键引力波分析任务中展现出实用价值，有望融入未来LISA数据分析流程。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) techniques are increasingly explored to analyse gravitational wave (GW) data expected from the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). At the same time, advances in quantum computing have stimulated interest in quantum machine learning as a potential alternative to conventional approaches. We investigate the applicability of quantum, hybrid quantum-classical, and classical ML models to two tasks relevant for LISA observations of massive black hole binaries: GW signal detection and merger prediction. Using the Sangria dataset from the LISA Data Challenge, we benchmark several quantum architectures, including variational quantum classifiers (VQCs), post VQC hybrid models, quantum kernel methods, data reuploading classifiers, and quantum long short-term memory (QLSTM) networks, against classical support vector machines, multilayer perceptrons, recurrent neural networks, and LSTM networks. The models are trained on labelled data derived from the Sangria training dataset and evaluated on the corresponding blind dataset. For signal detection, several quantum models match or outperform classical baselines. In particular, the best performing hybrid post VQC architecture correctly identifies all signal samples in the blind dataset, while the standard VQC and data reuploading classifier achieve competitive detection rates. For merger detection and coalescence time estimation, however, the standard VQC emerges as the most reliable model, successfully localising all detected merger events with the lowest localisation errors. We further analyse the trade-offs between predictive performance, model complexity, and quantum circuit resources. Our results indicate that, although no universal quantum advantage is observed, selected quantum models can provide practical benefits for specific GW analysis tasks and represent promising candidates for future LISA data analysis pipelines.

</details>


### [272] [Phenomenological implications of cosmic (super)strings with time-varying tension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40020)
*Anastasios Avgoustidis,Edmund J. Copeland,Adam Moss,Juhan Raidal,Filippo Revello,Gonzalo Villa*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了宇宙弦和超弦张力随时间变化的模型，分析其对引力波（GW）和宇宙微波背景（CMB）的影响，并通过MCMC方法比较不同时间依赖张力模型与传统恒定张力模型的观测效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙弦/超弦张力可能因模场演化或与标量场耦合而随时间变化，但其观测后果尚未系统研究；需评估该时间依赖性是否带来可区分的引力波或CMB特征。

Method: 构建五种不同形式的时间依赖张力模型，计算其引力波谱和CMB扰动贡献，并采用MCMC方法进行参数拟合与模型比较。

Result: 发现时间依赖张力模型在PTA与干涉仪频段间产生有别于恒定张力的GW谱特征，但这些特征非唯一；模型与当前GW数据兼容性不劣于其他主流解释。

Conclusion: 宇宙弦张力的时间演化虽未提供唯一可观测标识，但仍为合理且与数据一致的候选机制，值得在下一代引力波和CMB实验中进一步检验。

Abstract: Cosmic strings and cosmic superstrings could have formed in the early universe. In a particular class of models, the tension associated with these defects is time dependent, either because the strings themselves are composed of evolving moduli fields, or because they are directly coupled to evolving scalar fields leading to an effective time dependence in the string tension. Here we take seriously the possibility of this time dependence and investigate the phenomenological consequences of such an evolution, comparing the results to the more traditional case of a fixed time independent tension. In particular, for five different time varying models, motivated by different possible forms of evolution of the string tension, we evaluate their Gravitational Wave (GW) contributions as well as their impact on the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB). Performing an MCMC analysis of the models we find there are indeed interesting spectral signatures in the GW emission at frequencies between the current PTA and interferometer data, but the signatures are not unique to these strings, making them hard to pin down using current data. However, we do find cosmic strings with evolving tension fit the GW data as well as other favoured models.

</details>


### [273] [Extracting non-Gaussian information of line-intensity maps with weighted skew spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40063)
*Eleonora Vanzan,Azadeh Moradinezhad Dizgah,Ely D. Kovetz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出并应用了最优加权偏斜谱（skew spectra）方法来压缩线强度映射（line-intensity maps）的三谱（bispectrum），在不依赖发射体物理模型的前提下，实现对非线性偏置比、宇宙学参数和噪声参数的高精度约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三谱虽含丰富宇宙学与天体物理信息，但其高维数据向量难以处理；偏斜谱可将其压缩为类功率谱的少量可观测量，但此前未在发射线强度映射（尤其含未知平均强度和光度加权噪声/关联项）中系统应用。

Method: 构建并应用最优加权偏斜谱（含四个分量，其一匹配泊松噪声），结合功率谱进行联合分析；开展理想化实空间Fisher矩阵预测，针对SPHEREx类巡天的Hα强度映射（z≈0.2–4.6），分别模拟深场与全天两种观测配置。

Result: 1）四维偏斜谱可在微扰尺度下以几百分比误差复现三谱对偏置、噪声及宇宙学参数的约束；2）功率谱+偏斜谱联合分析可无模型地测量各红移bin内二阶偏置与线性偏置之比（消除平均强度简并），从而探测偏置比的红移演化；3）在z≲3的全天配置下，可3σ探测偏置比约0.1量级的偏离。

Conclusion: 偏斜谱是线强度映射中高效提取非高斯信息、突破功率谱局限（如无法获取偏置比）、且摆脱发射体建模依赖的关键工具，为未来巡天（如SPHEREx）提供了新分析范式。

Abstract: The bispectrum of line-intensity maps carries cosmological and astrophysical information beyond the power spectrum, but its large data vector hampers the analysis of survey data. Skew spectra address this by compressing the bispectrum into a handful of power-spectrum-like observables, cross-spectra of the observed field with filtered squares of it. We present the first application of optimally weighted skew spectra to line-intensity mapping of galaxy-hosted emission, where all clustering and shot-noise terms are luminosity weighted and the mean intensity is unknown. We perform idealized real-space Fisher forecasts for a SPHEREx-like survey of H$α$ emission at $0.2 \lesssim z \lesssim 4.6$, in its deep fields and in a noisier all-sky configuration. First, the compression carries over to intensity maps: on perturbative scales and for a fixed redshift evolution of the emitters, four skew spectra, one of them matched to the shot noise, recover the bispectrum constraints on biases, shot noise and cosmological parameters to within a few percent. Second, the joint analysis of the power spectrum and the skew spectra measures the ratios of the second-order biases to the linear bias in each redshift bin. Within a bin the biases are degenerate with the unknown mean intensity, but their ratios are not, so their redshift evolution is measured without a model for the emitters. For emission that depends on halo mass alone, these ratios are determined by luminosity-weighted averages of the halo biases, so their evolution can test the halo-emission connection, including dependence on assembly history or environment. The bias ratios are not accessible to the tree-level real-space power spectrum alone. At $z \lesssim 3$ an all-sky SPHEREx-like configuration can detect departures of order $0.1$ in the bias ratios at $3σ$, while deeper fields perform better in the highest-redshift bins.

</details>


### [274] [At the EDGES of MADness: phenomenology of relic millicharged atomic dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40064)
*Eleonora Vanzan,Kimberly K. Boddy,Ely D. Kovetz*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于原子暗物质（atomic dark matter）的millicharged暗物质（MAD）模型，通过暗重组过程自然产生极低丰度的millicharged成分，并利用修改版CLASS代码分析其在CMB、大尺度结构和超新星数据下的可观测参数空间，发现其可同时满足EDGES信号所需电荷范围与多类观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释EDGES观测到的21-cm吸收异常信号，同时调和其与CMB等宇宙学观测对millicharged暗物质丰度的严格限制；探索该类粒子能否自然源于合理的暗区物理机制。
