Method: 构建原子暗物质模型，引入暗氢类似结构及暗重组过程，结合动能混合机制生成millicharged组分；修改宇宙学Boltzmann求解器CLASS以纳入该模型，并联合CMB、LSS和超新星数据进行参数空间扫描与约束分析。

Result: 发现存在与CMB、大尺度结构、超新星及实验室/天体物理限制相容的MAD参数区域，且部分区域与EDGES建议的电荷范围（ε~10⁻⁶–10⁻⁴）重叠；MAD具有独特宇宙学印记，有望被下一代21-cm实验探测。

Conclusion: millicharged暗物质可自然源于原子暗物质框架，无需精细调节；尽管EDGES信号本身存疑，MAD仍是一个有观测前景的可行暗物质候选者，其宇宙学效应为未来21-cm观测提供了关键检验途径。

Abstract: Millicharged dark matter lives in a corner of parameter space that has been considerably shrinking over the past decades, due to constraints from cosmology, astrophysical systems, and ground-based experiments. A particularly interesting probe is the 21-cm absorption signal at Cosmic Dawn, which attracted interest in light of the EDGES claim: the reported excess absorption can be explained by millicharged dark matter with a fractional electric charge of~$10^{-6} \lesssim ε\lesssim 10^{-4}$, but it must be a subpercent of the total dark matter abundance to remain consistent with the cosmic microwave background (CMB). We show that very low fractions of millicharged dark matter naturally arise from atomic dark matter: a dark sector model that replicates the physics of standard atomic hydrogen. Dark ions undergo the process of dark recombination, leaving a relic ionized component that, in the presence of kinetic mixing, is naturally millicharged. We modify the cosmological Boltzmann solver CLASS to incorporate this scenario, and we investigate the parameter space of millicharged atomic dark matter (MAD) that is allowed by CMB, large-scale structure, and supernovae data. We find there are cosmologically viable regions of MAD parameter space that are consistent with laboratory and astrophysical bounds and also overlap with the EDGES-defined window. Although the EDGES result is likely not a Cosmic Dawn signal, MAD leaves distinctive cosmological signatures, accessible to upcoming 21-cm observations.

</details>


### [275] [Theoretical Consistency and Observational Constraints on Entropic Cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40205)
*Yunliang Ren,Xiaofeng Yang,Xuwei Zhang,Shuangnan Chen,Yangjun Shi,Hoernisa Iminniyaza*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了三种熵宇宙学模型（A、B、C），通过观测数据（Pantheon+、DESI DR2、Planck）和理论约束（早期宇宙一致性、TCC、Quintom-B穿越）进行分析，发现模型B在拟合与理论自洽性上表现最优，而模型A和C分别存在理论或观测适配问题，表明熵宇宙学仍需进一步发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI DR2结果暗示暗能量可能随时间演化，促使重新审视熵宇宙学；同时需检验不同熵驱动项对宇宙演化的影响及其理论与观测兼容性。

Method: 构建熵宇宙学一般框架，分析三种含不同Hubble参数及其导数的驱动项（H²、H²+Ḣ、H²+Ḣ+H⁴）的模型；结合早期宇宙一致性、Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture（TCC）和Quintom-B穿越等理论检验，并用Pantheon+、DESI DR2 BAO和Planck 2018 CMB距离先验进行观测约束；对模型C采用一阶微扰处理以保证数值稳定性。

Result: 模型A最简单但难以同时满足早期一致性与TCC；模型B加入导数修正后，数据拟合略优于A且更符合理论约束；模型C虽含更高阶项，但未改善拟合效果；观测偏好参数区域与理论自洽区域不完全重叠。

Conclusion: 当前熵宇宙学模型尚不能在全宇宙演化历史中同时满足观测拟合与理论自洽，亟需进一步理论完善。

Abstract: Recent DESI DR2 results suggest that dark energy may evolve with time, which motivates a re-examination of entropic cosmology. In this work, we study three representative entropic models, denoted as Models A, B, and C, corresponding to the driving terms $H^2$, $H^2+\dot H$, and $H^2+\dot H+H^4$, respectively. We establish a general framework for entropic cosmology and examine these models through early universe consistency, the Trans-Planckian Censorship Conjecture (TCC), and the realization of Quintom-B crossing. We constrain these models using the Pantheon+ sample, DESI DR2 BAO measurements, and Planck 2018 CMB distance priors, and compare their fits to the data. For Model C, we adopt a first-order perturbative treatment in the full data analysis to maintain numerical stability in the presence of the higher-order term. We find that Model A is the simplest among the three models, but it is difficult to make it compatible with both early universe consistency and the TCC condition. Model B, which includes a derivative correction term, shows a small difference in the fit to observational data compared to Model A, and is more consistent with the theoretical constraints. Model C includes more correction terms, but does not provide a better fit to the data. The parameter region favored by observations does not fully overlap with that preferred by theoretical consistency, which suggests that entropic cosmology may require further development to achieve a self-consistent description across different cosmic epochs.

</details>


### [276] [Going off the (momentum) grid: A simple implementation of noncold relics in linear Boltzmann solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40275)
*Arad Nasiri,Murali Saravanan,Caio B. de S. Nascimento*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种改进线性玻尔兹曼求解器的方法：色散流体近似（DFA）和广义玻尔兹曼层级（GBH），显著提升了非冷遗迹（如大质量中微子）的功率谱计算精度，同时保持较低计算开销，并已在CLASS中开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提高非冷遗迹（如大质量中微子、热暗物质组分）在宇宙学模拟中功率谱计算的精度与效率，尤其在小尺度上克服传统流体近似的局限性。

Method: 引入色散流体近似（DFA）以刻画非冷成分尺度依赖的声速；提出并实现广义玻尔兹曼层级（GBH），通过完全积分动量依赖来简化非冷遗迹建模；二者均集成于CLASS代码中。

Result: DFA显著优于CLASS默认流体近似，在中微子及交叉功率谱中表现更优，冷暗物质功率谱误差降低达一个数量级；GBH结合DFA可实现亚千分之一的物质功率谱精度和<0.05σ的参数约束精度，计算代价与标准实现相当。

Conclusion: DFA与GBH是高效高精度建模非冷遗迹（尤其是非热遗迹）的有力工具，已开源，有望成为未来精密宇宙学分析的标准组件。

Abstract: Linear Boltzmann solvers typically evolve the perturbations of noncold relics, such as massive neutrinos or hot subcomponents of the dark matter, on a momentum grid, often switching to a fluid approximation on small scales for efficiency. We implement in CLASS the Dispersive Fluid Approximation (DFA), a fluid scheme that captures the scale-dependent sound speed of these species. Within the standard ncdm module, the DFA significantly outperforms the default CLASS fluid approximation in the cross and neutrino power spectra, and reduces errors in the cold dark matter power spectrum by up to an order of magnitude, while adding only 1-4% to the total runtime. We also present the first open-source implementation of the Generalized Boltzmann Hierarchy (GBH), which simplifies the implementation of noncold relics by integrating out the momentum dependence of their perturbations altogether. Combined with the DFA and tested on massive neutrinos, the GBH reproduces the matter power spectrum to better than permille accuracy and cosmological parameter constraints to better than 0.05 sigma, at a computational cost comparable to that of the standard implementation. We argue that the GBH is particularly well suited to non-thermal relics whose distribution function varies with time or across parameter space. Our implementation of both the DFA and the GBH in CLASS is publicly available at https://github.com/aradn97/class_GBH.

</details>


### [277] [Toward a Complete Interpretation of Global 21 cm Absorption Signals Using the Radio CMB](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40300)
*Hannah Fronenberg*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出结合CMB氢光学深度与全球21 cm信号测量，以无模型方式打破自旋温度与射电背景温度的简并性，从而独立确定射电背景温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球21 cm信号虽能探测宇宙黎明，但其亮度温度依赖于自旋温度和射电背景温度的组合，导致如EDGES吸收特征存在冷气体或额外射电背景两种解释，需打破该简并。

Method: 利用高红移中性氢对CMB各向异性的共振21 cm吸收产生的光学深度（仅依赖自旋温度），将其与全球21 cm信号联合分析；通过模拟四种干涉仪构型进行灵敏度预测。

Result: 一个由55×55台6米天线组成的致密阵列、积分4000小时，可对EDGES类光学深度实现4.2σ探测；典型宇宙黎明模型则需约十倍集光面积。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种不依赖天体物理建模、可普适应用于任何全球21 cm探测结果的模型无关解读路径。

Abstract: The global 21 cm signal is among the most direct probes of cosmic dawn, encoding information about when the first stars formed and how efficiently they heated their surroundings. However, such measurements are limited by bright Galactic foregrounds, ionospheric effects, and instrumental systematics. Even a confident detection would face a further obstacle: the brightness temperature depends on the spin temperature of the gas and the temperature of the radio background only through a single combination, so anomalous signals such as the EDGES (Bowman et al. 2018) absorption trough admit both cold gas and excess radio background interpretations. Recently, Holder et al. 2026 showed that resonant 21 cm absorption by high-redshift neutral hydrogen imprints an excess optical depth on CMB anisotropies at radio wavelengths, which depends on the spin temperature alone. Here we propose combining a measurement of the CMB hydrogen optical depth with the global signal to break this degeneracy, isolating the radio background temperature without the need for astrophysical modelling. Forecasting four interferometric configurations, we find that a compact $55 \times 55$ array of 6 m dishes observing for 4000 hours can establish an EDGES-like optical depth at $4.2σ$, while fiducial cosmic dawn histories require roughly an order of magnitude more collecting area. This constitutes a model-independent route to interpreting any global 21 cm detection.

</details>


### [278] [Cosmological lensing as a jump process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40341)
*Galymzhan Baltabay,Ville Vaskonen*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于跳跃过程的引力透镜场概率密度函数（PDF）形式化方法，推导了收敛率κ和剪切γ的联合PDF，并用于改进FLUMEN模拟器以支持基于似然的宇宙学参数推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现基于完整放大率分布的高精度宇宙学参数推断（如σ₈），需精确建模引力透镜效应引起的放大率分布，尤其关注高放大率尾部，且避免采样噪声。

Method: 在Born近似下将透镜效应建模为红移空间中的跳跃过程；对独立透镜导出带补偿项的Kolmogorov-Feller方程并在傅里叶空间解析求解；通过傅里叶-汉克尔逆变换获得κ与剪切模γ的联合PDF；进一步导出放大率PDF；最后用于训练更新版FLUMEN emulator。

Result: 获得了无采样噪声的κ、γ及放大率PDF解析表达式；成功训练FLUMEN emulator以支持跨宇宙学模型的似然推断；预估了Roman、Rubin、Einstein Telescope和LISA观测样本对σ₈的约束精度（分别为4.8%、5.1%、8.5%、19%）。

Conclusion: 该跳跃过程框架为引力透镜统计建模提供了严格而高效的解析工具，显著提升了利用强透镜放大率分布进行宇宙学参数约束的能力与精度。

Abstract: We develop a jump-process formalism for the probability density function (PDF) of gravitational lensing fields. In the Born approximation, each lens adds a jump to the convergence $κ$ and the two components of the shear, making lensing a jump process in redshift. For independent lenses, the joint PDF of the lensing fields obeys a compensated Kolmogorov-Feller equation, which we solve in closed form in Fourier space and extend to clustered lenses using their two-point correlation function. A Fourier-Hankel inversion then yields the joint PDF of $κ$ and the shear magnitude $γ$, from which the magnification PDF follows, including its high-magnification tail, without sampling noise. We use these PDFs to train an updated version of the FLUMEN emulator, which makes likelihood-based inference from the full magnification distribution feasible across cosmologies. To motivate such analyses, we estimate that samples of $10^4$ Roman supernovae, $10^5$ Rubin supernovae, 300 Einstein Telescope standard sirens, and 12 LISA standard sirens could constrain the matter fluctuation amplitude $σ_8$ respectively to 4.8\%, 5.1\%, 8.5\%, and 19\%, with the other cosmological parameters fixed.

</details>


### [279] [Cosmological tests of chameleon dark energy with dark sector coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40347)
*Jia-Qi Wang,Zong-Kuan Guo,Shao-Jiang Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究了变色龙暗能量模型，该模型通过标量场与暗物质的非最小耦合驱动宇宙晚期加速膨胀，并能解释DESIBAO与PlanckCMB观测间Ωm值的差异，同时产生穿越w=-1的有效暗能量状态方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决DESIBAO与PlanckCMB观测对Ωm值的不一致，以及联合超新星数据暗示的暗能量状态方程穿越w=-1的问题。

Method: 构建一个由规范标量场非最小耦合暗物质驱动的变色龙暗能量模型，分析其在早期和晚期宇宙中的演化行为，并通过χ²拟合与ΛCDM模型比较。

Result: 该模型在暗能量向暗物质传递能量时，相对ΛCDM有Δχ²=-7.25的优度提升；双向耦合联合采样显示非零相互作用在3.3σ置信水平显著；模型无需引入幻影场即可使有效暗能量状态方程在z≈0.5处穿越w=-1。

Conclusion: 变色龙暗能量模型可统一解释多个宇宙学观测张力，特别是Ωm差异与w=-1穿越现象，且具有统计显著的相互作用证据。

Abstract: Recent DESI measurements of baryon acoustic oscillations favor a value of $Ω_{\mathrm m}$ different from that preferred by Planck cosmic microwave background observations. Combined with supernova data, these observations suggest an evolving dark energy equation of state that crosses $w=-1$. As a potential solution to multiple cosmological problems, chameleon dark energy has attracted increasing attention in recent years. In this work, we constrain a chameleon model in which late-time cosmic acceleration is driven by a canonical scalar field nonminimally coupled to dark matter. At early times, the scalar field evolves under the combined influence of its potential and the nonminimal coupling, and its effective mass greatly exceeds the Hubble parameter. The chameleon model with energy transfer from dark energy to dark matter yields a best-fit improvement of $Δχ^2=-7.25$ relative to $Λ\mathrm{CDM}$, comparable to that for energy transfer in the opposite direction. Joint sampling over both coupling directions favors a nonzero interaction at $3.3σ$. Although no phantom field is introduced, the modified dark matter evolution produces an effective dark energy equation of state that crosses $w=-1$ at $z\simeq0.5$.

</details>


### [280] [UNIONS: Red and blue alignments of DESI DR1 galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40411)
*R. Paviot,C. Murray,M. Kilbinger,C. Daley,F. Hervas-Peters,A. Corinaldi,T. de Boer,S. Guerrini,M. J. Hudson,G. S. H. Paek,L. Van Waerbeke,J. Yun*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文利用DESI DR1光谱样本与UNIONS弱引力透镜形状样本的交叉相关，测量了不同颜色、恒星质量和晕质量下星系的内禀对齐（IA）信号，并比较了NLA和TATT模型对数据的拟合效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系内禀对齐（IA）是弱引力透镜宇宙学分析中主要的系统误差来源，需依赖光谱样本的直接测量来设定其振幅先验。

Method: 通过交叉关联DESI DR1光谱星表（BGS、LRG、ELG）与UNIONS弱透镜形状星表，在2400 deg²天区上测量IA；按颜色与恒星质量分组，拟合三维星系-星系与星系-形状多极矩，采用NLA与TATT模型；利用星系-星系透镜效应测定BGS红样本晕质量，并通过恒星-晕质量关系外推至LRG样本。

Result: 红星系呈现强径向对齐，其对齐振幅在6×10¹¹–3×10¹³ h⁻¹M⊙晕质量范围内并非单幂律：低质量段近似平坦，高于1.3×10¹³ h⁻¹M⊙后陡增；大质量蓝BGS星系首次以3.3σ显著性探测到切向对齐；小质量蓝BGS与ELG则与零对齐一致；当前精度下数据未显示TATT优于NLA。

Conclusion: IA振幅具有复杂的晕质量依赖性，不能简单用单幂律描述；蓝星系存在显著切向对齐，挑战传统认知；NLA模型仍足以描述当前数据，暂无需更复杂模型。

Abstract: Intrinsic alignments (IA) of galaxies are a dominant source of systematics in weak-lensing cosmological analyses, and direct measurements on spectroscopic samples are the primary source of priors on their amplitude. We measure the IA of galaxies from the first data release (DR1) of the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI) as a function of colour, stellar mass, and halo mass, by cross-correlating the DESI spectroscopic catalogues with the weak-lensing shape catalogue of the Ultraviolet Near-Infrared Optical Northern Survey (UNIONS) over up to 2400 deg$^2$. We fit the three-dimensional galaxy-galaxy and galaxy-shape multipoles of the DESI BGS, LRG, and ELG samples, split by colour and stellar mass, with the non-linear linear alignment (NLA) and the tidal alignment and tidal torquing (TATT) models. Halo masses are measured for the BGS red sample from galaxy-galaxy lensing and extended to the LRGs through the resulting stellar-to-halo mass relation. Red galaxies are strongly and radially aligned, but their alignment amplitude is not a single power law of halo mass between $6\times10^{11}$ and $3\times10^{13}\,h^{-1}M_\odot$: it is nearly flat at low mass and rises steeply above a break at $1.3\times10^{13}\,h^{-1}M_\odot$. Massive blue BGS galaxies instead show a tangential alignment, detected at $3.3σ$, to our knowledge the most significant detection of a non-zero alignment of blue galaxies to date, while the low-mass blue BGS galaxies and the ELGs are consistent with zero. At our current precision, the data do not favour TATT over NLA.

</details>


### [281] [OCCAMS: A Multi-Task Learning-Based Investigation of the CMB Power Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40415)
*Ankit Mandal,Rahul Shah,Md. Wali Hossain,Somasri Sen,Anjan A Sen,Supratik Pal*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文使用无模型的变分自编码器（VAE）分析CMB温度与极化功率谱，发现6个潜在变量即可实现与标准6参数宇宙学模型相当的拟合效果，且部分潜在变量可对应标准宇宙学参数（如τ和As），表明深度学习可提供数据驱动的紧凑参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在不预设宇宙学模型的前提下，神经网络拟合CMB功率谱所需的最少潜在变量数，并评估这些变量与标准宇宙学参数的对应关系。

Method: 采用多任务学习的变分自编码器（VAE），在CLASS生成的TT/TE/EE谱（2≤ℓ≤2500）上训练；系统比较L=5,6,7个潜在变量的性能；使用χ²、AIC、BIC和贝叶斯证据评估拟合优度；通过归一化互信息衡量潜在变量与宇宙学参数的对应程度；引入半自动（SA）和全自动（CA）两种解耦权重策略。

Result: L=6在多数指标下最优，与标准ΛCDM自由参数数量一致；SA策略解耦更清晰，CA策略贝叶斯证据更高；τ与As对应的潜在变量互信息极低，与标准分析中大尺度极化分离二者一致。

Conclusion: 深度学习方法可在无先验宇宙学模型下，以6个潜在变量实现对CMB谱的紧凑、数据驱动参数化，且部分变量具有物理可解释性。

Abstract: How many latent variables are needed to fit the cosmic microwave background (CMB) temperature and polarisation power spectra when a neural network is given no cosmological model, and to what extent do they map onto the standard cosmological parameters? We use multi-task learning with variational autoencoders trained on CLASS-generated spectra (TT, TE, EE) over $2\le\ell\le2500$. No cosmological model is built into the network, and the number of latent variables, $L$, is varied over $L=5$, $6$ and $7$. Their fits are compared to the Planck 2018 data using $\hatχ^2$, AIC, BIC and Bayesian evidence. Their correspondence with cosmological parameters is measured with normalised mutual information post-training. The loss weights are set within a single training run by uncertainty weighting, with the weight of the disentanglement term either fixed (semi-automated, SA) or learned (completely automated, CA). The reconstruction loss is split over multipole ranges in three configurations that separate the roles of temperature and polarisation and of the low- and high-$\ell$ ranges. In four of the six combinations of configuration and strategy, the fit improves from $L=5$ to $L=6$ and not beyond, and $L=6$ is the best, or close to the best, in five of the six cases. So, six latent variables are needed, as many as the free parameters of the standard, model-dependent approach. SA gives clearer disentanglement, while CA needs no a priori choice of the disentanglement weight and has the higher evidence at $L=5$ and $6$: the two strategies are points on a trade-off between interpretability and fit to the data. Notably, the latent variables for $τ$ and $A_s$ share almost no information in any model, consistent with large-scale polarisation separating the two, as in standard analyses. A deep-learning approach can thus give a compact, data-driven parametrisation of the CMB spectra.

</details>


### [282] [An Empirically Calibrated [C II] Luminosity-Halo Mass Relation for Line Intensity Mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40550)
*Oliver Jeong,Martin White*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过仅使用[C II]观测数据校准双幂律形式的晕质量-光度关系L(M_h,z)，以更准确地预测高红移(z>4)下[C II]线强度映射的功率谱，评估其探测大尺度结构聚类信号而非散粒噪声的能力。结果表明聚类信号显著高于散粒噪声，且主要来自当前星系巡天可及的暗晕。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有[C II]功率谱预测多依赖于外推或模拟得到的晕质量-光度关系，缺乏对不确定性的量化；本文旨在仅基于[C II]观测数据直接校准该关系，提升高红移宇宙学探测的可靠性。

Method: 采用双幂律形式L(M_h,z)建模[C II]晕质量-光度关系，利用0.4<z<4.2的层析交叉相关测得的平均[C II]强度、以及z≈0和4.3≤z≤8的光度函数测量与限制进行单独（z=4.5, 5.5）和联合（0<z<7）拟合，并分析不同建模假设对不确定性的影响。

Result: 在z=4.5和5.5处，聚类信号比散粒噪声高出约45倍（23–93）和64倍（38–112），二者在k_c≈0.37和0.44 h Mpc⁻¹处相交；聚类功率幅度不确定性为0.5–0.6 dex，散粒噪声为0.7–0.9 dex；信号主要来自可被当前星系巡天（如LBG、Lyα发射体）探测的暗晕，光度加权偏置b≈4–5。

Conclusion: 现有[C II]观测数据已足以确定功率谱形状，且高红移[C II]强度映射具备清晰的大尺度结构聚类探测能力；未来实验应聚焦于降低幅度不确定性，并利用已知暗晕种群开展协同分析。

Abstract: Millimeter-wave line intensity mapping aims to observe the high-redshift universe through the 158 $μ$m fine-structure line of [C II], the brightest far-infrared line of star-forming galaxies. Whether a [C II] experiment at $z>4$ measures the clustering of large-scale structure, which carries rich cosmological information, rather than the shot noise of the emitters depends on the amplitude and the shape of the [C II] power spectrum. Forecasts often build the power spectrum from a halo mass--luminosity relation, $L(M_h)$, chained from scaling relations measured in other regimes or read off simulations, without a quantified uncertainty. We instead calibrate a double power-law $L(M_h,z)$ on [C II] data alone: the mean [C II] intensity from tomographic cross-correlation at $0.4<z<4.2$ and the luminosity-function measurements and limits at $z\simeq0$ and $4.3\lesssim z\lesssim8$. The four parameters of the double power law are fitted at $z=4.5$ and $5.5$ separately, with no assumption about their evolution, and then jointly across $0<z<7$, and we examine how the uncertainty depends on the modeling assumptions. The shape of the power spectrum is settled by the existing data. The clustering peak stands a factor of $45\,(23$-$93)$ and $64\,(38$-$112)$ above the shot-noise floor at $z=4.5$ and $5.5$, and the two cross at $k_c\simeq0.37$ and $0.44\,h$Mpc$^{-1}$. The amplitude is known less well, the clustering power to $0.5-0.6$ dex and the shot noise to $0.7-0.9$ dex. The results show that the signal comes from halos accessible by current galaxy surveys, those that host the Lyman-break galaxies and Ly$α$ emitters at these redshifts. The luminosity-weighted bias is relatively high, with values of $4-5$.

</details>


### [283] [Field-level inference under pressure: the information content of the thermal Sunyaev-Zel'dovich field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.40617)
*Carmen Embil Villagra,Adrian E. Bayer,William R. Coulton,Alina Sabyr,Francisco Villaescusa-Navarro,Blake D. Sherwin,Anthony Challinor,J. Colin Hill,Nicholas Battaglia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文使用卷积神经网络（CNN）和可解释的统计量（如Minkowski泛函和功率谱）在模拟数据上对热Sunyaev-Zel'dovich（tSZ）场进行基于仿真的推断（SBI），比较其对宇宙学参数（Ω_c、σ_8）及天体物理参数（P_0、β）的约束能力；结果表明CNN在无噪情况下更优，但在Simons Observatory级别噪声下，组合Minkowski泛函与功率谱可达到与CNN相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tSZ场高度非高斯，其角功率谱无法完全捕获其中的宇宙学信息；需探索更有效且可解释的信息提取方法。

Method: 采用基于仿真的推断（SBI）框架，利用10,000张模拟Compton-y图，联合变化Ω_c、σ_8、P_0和β四个参数；对比CNN与三种Minkowski泛函（单独及组合）以及角功率谱的约束能力；涵盖无噪与含Simons Observatory实际噪声两种情形。

Result: 在无噪条件下，CNN给出比传统统计量更紧致的参数约束；在SO级噪声下，Minkowski泛函与功率谱的组合可达到与CNN相近的约束精度。

Conclusion: Minkowski泛函等可解释统计量结合功率谱，在真实观测噪声下具有与深度学习模型相媲美的性能，支持其在真实tSZ数据分析中的应用。

Abstract: The thermal Sunyaev--Zel'dovich (tSZ) field is highly non-Gaussian and contains cosmological information that cannot be fully captured by its angular power spectrum. In this work, we investigate the information content of this field using a convolutional neural network (CNN), and compare to simple and interpretable summary statistics, in a simulation-based inference (SBI) framework. We perform the first field-level analysis of the tSZ field, using a suite of $10{,}000$ simulated Compton-$y$ maps, where we jointly vary the cosmological parameters $Ω_c$ and $σ_8$ (the density fraction for cold dark matter and the matter clustering amplitude, respectively) and two parameters describing the amplitude and shape of the electron pressure profile, $P_0$ and $β$, that aim to capture astrophysical uncertainties. We consider both noiseless simulations and simulations including realistic Simons Observatory (SO) noise after component separation. We use SBI to compare the constraints obtained from the CNN with those from the three Minkowski functionals (MFs), individually and in combination, as well as the angular power spectrum. This is the first application of SBI to the MFs of the tSZ field. We find that the CNN provides tighter constraints than conventional summary statistics in the noiseless case. However, at SO-like noise levels, the combination of MFs and the power spectrum recovers constraints comparable to those obtained with the CNN. Our results highlight the potential of these interpretable summary statistics, as well as field-level inference, for extracting information from the tSZ field, motivating efforts toward their application to real data.

</details>
